## Class resources, 2024 chassis and small gaps - S4 (2026-10-09)
- **S4 build-time option choices from feature text (done, decision D10 = build-time only).** `reference_data.FEATURE_TEXT_CHOICES` (feature id -> pick count) lists exactly four imported ids, each verified by script against the real class files: `totem-warrior-phb-3-totem-spirit`, `totem-warrior-phb-6-aspect-of-the-beast`, `totem-warrior-phb-14-totemic-attunement` (count 1 each) and `storm-herald-xge-3-storm-aura` (the raw block has no `count`, default 1). Scan of the 27 `options`-block features: the rest are optional-feature refs (maneuvers, shots, runes, disciplines, Swords fighting style), grant-all (Psionic Power, Soul Blades, Telekinetic Adept, Genie's Vessel, Tipsy Sway, Kensei), per-rest (The Third Eye, Master Transmuter), follow-the-first-pick (Storm Soul, Raging Storm: derived text only, no card), or SRD Hunter features (already `subfeature_options`). None of those were added.
- **Converter** (`official/subclasses.py::convert_subclasses`, `_text_options`): for an allowlisted feature, the first top-level `options` block becomes `feature_specific.text_options = {"count", "options": [{"id", "name", "desc"}]}` from its `refSubclassFeature` children; the text is still inlined into `desc`. Option id = `<featureId>--<slug(option name)>`, e.g. `totem-warrior-phb-3-totem-spirit--bear` (slice R5 keys the Bear resistance off it).
- **Resolve**: `_feature_choice_templates` has a `text_options` branch (`_text_options_template`), cid `class:<cls>:<lvl>:feature:<featureId>`, so `sheet_features` lists the pick (sheet feature "Totem Spirit" has choice ["Bear"]) and the generic pass prunes picks when the level drops (6 -> 5 prunes the Aspect pick; the Totem Spirit pick stays). Option objects are `{id, label, description}` with no name prefix in the description. No frontend change (generic choice card).
- **Option counts (real data).** Totem Spirit, Aspect of the Beast and Totemic Attunement each have 5 options: Bear, Eagle, Elk, Tiger, Wolf. The plan expected Elk/Tiger as separate SCAG entries; in the data they are SCAG-sourced children inside the SAME options block, so they are in the same card. They are not hidden when SCAG is excluded in `draft.sources` (disclosed; the optional-progression pools are not source-filtered either). Storm Aura: Desert, Sea, Tundra.
- Tests: `tests/test_feature_text_choices.py` (12). Server suite 1292 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## Class resources, 2024 chassis and small gaps - S5 (2026-10-09)
- **S5 Dueling as a separate field (done, decision D3 = b).** `engine/attacks.weapon_attack` no longer bakes the Dueling +2 into `damage`. New `duelingDamage` (one-handed dice + mod + 2) is present only for melee, non-two-handed weapons when `styles["one_handed_melee_damage"]` is set (Dueling fighting style, Swords bard Dueling, etc.); otherwise `null`. The note "Dueling: only with no other weapon" is kept. There is still no hands model, so the "no other weapon" check stays a note.
- **Versatile weapons:** they get `duelingDamage` (Dueling applies to the one-handed grip: longsword `damage "1d8+3"`, `duelingDamage "1d8+5"`); `versatileDamage` (two-handed grip) never gets the bonus, as before. Two-handed and ranged weapons get none.
- **Behavior change:** `damage` for a Dueling character is now the plain value; tests that asserted Dueling inside `damage` (`test_engine_attacks.py`, the Swords bard test in `test_subclass_import.py`) were updated in the same commit.
- Exports/UI: `Attack.duelingDamage` in `types.ts`; Markdown/PDF "Атаки" bullet adds ", с Дуел <dmg>" after the versatile text; ExportPage adds ", with Dueling <dmg>" (plain text, no CSS). YAML carries the field automatically via `derived.attacks`.
- Tests: server 1295 passed (+3), root 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend build green.


## Class resources, 2024 chassis and small gaps - R1 (2026-10-09)
- **R1 resource framework, Barbarian and Rogue (done, decisions D1 = `derived.classResources`, D2 = mixed uses SRD numbers).** `derived.classResources` is a flat list in class order (a class's subclass entries follow its own). Shape: `{id "<class>:<name-slug>", classId, source {kind class|subclass, id, label}, name, uses, unlimited? (only when true; uses is then null), recharge long|short|short-or-long|null, die?, dice?, pool?, bonus?, dc?, details?}`; optional fields that are None are omitted. Barbarian gives `Rage` (`uses`, `recharge "long"`, `details {damageBonus, resistances [bludgeoning, piercing, slashing]}`); Rogue gives `Sneak Attack` (`dice "NdF"`, `uses` null, `recharge` null).
- **Loader.** `official/subclasses.load_class_2024_tables(class_dir)` -> `{class: {column slug: [20 values]}}` from the XPHB `classTableGroups` and `load_subclass_2024_tables` -> `{subclass id: {...}}` from `subclassTableGroups` (ids use `_sub_id`, e.g. `psi-warrior-xphb`). Groups without exactly 20 `rows` (the `rowsSpellProgression` slot tables) are skipped, so are columns whose label has a `|spells|` filter, "Spell Slots" and "Slot Level". Label slug: `{@filter X|..}` / `{@tip X|..}` -> X. Values: digit strings -> int, `{type: bonus|bonusSpeed}` -> value, `{type: dice}` -> `"NdF"`, `{@dice D6}` -> `"d6"`. `Catalog.class_2024_tables` / `subclass_2024_tables` (default `{}`) are filled next to `class_2024_progressions`. Real columns: barbarian rages / rage-damage / weapon-mastery; bard bardic-die; cleric, paladin channel-divinity; druid wild-shape; fighter second-wind / weapon-mastery; monk martial-arts / focus-points / unarmored-movement; ranger favored-enemy; rogue sneak-attack; sorcerer sorcery-points; warlock invocations. Subclass tables exist only for Psi Warrior and Soulknife XPHB (`die-size`, `number`).
- **Engine.** New pure `engine/class_resources.py`: `resources_for(catalog, resolved_classes, modifiers, prof_bonus, ruleset, choices=None, warnings_out=None)`. `_BUILDERS[class_id] -> builder(ctx, rc)` (and an empty `_SUBCLASS_BUILDERS[subclass_id]` for R6). A builder reads SRD numbers from `rc["level_row"]["class_specific"]` and asks `ctx.table(class, column, level)` for the 2024 value (None unless ruleset is exactly "2024" and the column exists, in which case the builder keeps the SRD number). To add a class (R2-R6): write `_name(ctx, rc)` with the `_resource(...)` helper and register it. `resolve()` calls it once, just before the `derived` dict, with the `warnings` list.
- **Verified by script (real data).** XPHB Rage Damage by level: 2 (1-8), 3 (9-15), 4 (16-20), equal to the SRD `rage_damage_bonus` at every level. XPHB Rages equal the SRD `rage_count` at levels 1-19; the only difference is level 20 (SRD 9999 = unlimited, 2024 = 6; level 17-19 are 6 in both). Rogue 2024 Sneak Attack equals the SRD `sneak_attack` at all 20 levels (ceil(level/2) d6). Result: unlimited Rage at 20 under 2014 and mixed, 6 under 2024.
- **Degrade.** Under "2024" with an empty `class_2024_tables` the SRD numbers are used and the warning `class-2024-tables-missing` is added once (only when a class with a table-reading builder is present). Mixed and 2014 never read the tables or warn.
- **Exports/UI.** `DERIVED_FIELDS` has `classResources` (YAML carries it); `extra_blocks` adds "Ресурси на класа" with `export_fields.resource_line` ("Rage: 3 (дълга почивка), щета +2", "Sneak Attack: 2d6", unlimited as "без ограничение"; recharge words "кратка почивка", "дълга почивка", "кратка или дълга почивка"). Frontend: `ClassResource` and optional `Derived.classResources` in `types.ts`, new `components/ClassResourcesList.tsx` (plain `<ul>`, same wording), used in each ClassPage block (filtered by classId) and on ExportPage; no CSS.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, level 20, human/acolyte, mean of 20): Fighter mixed 2.68 ms before, 2.78 ms after; Barbarian mixed 2.04 -> 1.98 ms; 2024 ruleset Fighter 1.72 ms, Barbarian 1.38 ms after (limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_class_resources.py` (60). Server suite 1355 passed (1295 + 60), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend build green.

## Class resources - R2 Fighter and Monk, attacks per action, Monk unarmed strike (2026-10-09)
- **Fighter** (`_fighter`): `Second Wind` (`recharge "short-or-long"`, `details.heal "1d10+<fighter level>"`; uses 1 under 2014/mixed, the XPHB `second-wind` table under 2024: 2 at 1-3, 3 at 4-9, 4 at 10-20), `Action Surge` (SRD `action_surges`, 1 at 2 and 2 at 17, short-or-long) and `Indomitable` (SRD `indomitable_uses`, 1/2/3 at 9/13/17, long); the last two are omitted while 0. The 2024 table has no Action Surge/Indomitable column, so the SRD rows serve every ruleset.
- **Monk** (`_monk`): `Martial Arts` (`die "d6"`), plus `Ki` (2014/mixed, `ki_points`) or `Focus Points` (2024 table `focus-points`), `recharge "short"`, `dc = 8 + PB + WIS` (resource omitted at level 1, where both are 0). Martial Arts die verified by script: 2014 d4 (1-4), d6 (5-10), d8 (11-16), d10 (17-20); 2024 d6, d8, d10, d12 at the same breakpoints (XPHB column `martial-arts`, "1d6" etc.). Focus Points 0 at level 1 then equal to level, as the plan said. `export_fields.resource_line` and `ClassResourcesList` now also print `details.heal` ("лекуване 1d10+5").
- **Attacks per action** (`class_resources.attacks_per_action`, `derived.attacksPerAction`): 1 + the largest extra-attack count of any source (PHB 164, no stacking). Fighter reads `extra_attacks` from the level row (1/2/3 at 5/11/20, the only class with that column). The other sources are the feature ids in `EXTRA_ATTACK_FEATURES` (verified: these are exactly the catalog features named "Extra Attack" besides the Fighter's `extra-attack-1/2/3`): `barbarian|monk|paladin|ranger-extra-attack` (level 5), bard `valor-phb|swords-xge|valor-xphb` and wizard `bladesinging-tce|bladesinger-frhof` (level 6, only with that subclass). Example: Fighter 5 / Barbarian 5 = 2, Fighter 11 / Monk 5 = 3, Fighter 20 = 4. Not affected by ruleset.
- **Unarmed strike** (`attacks.unarmed_strike`, id `unarmed-strike`): appended after the weapon rows by `resolve()` when Monk level >= 1; better of STR/DEX (tie STR), always proficient, `damage "<1dN>+mod"` from the Monk's Martial Arts resource die, `bludgeoning`, range "5", note "Martial Arts". Verified that no catalog item has that id or "unarmed" in its name. Known limit: the row is not changed by the Monk Weapon Mastery/Fighting Style, nor by two-weapon rules.
- **Exports/UI.** `attacksPerAction` is in `DERIVED_FIELDS`; the Markdown/PDF "Атаки" block starts with "Атаки на действие: N" (the block is also printed when there are no attack rows but N > 1). ExportPage shows "Attacks per action: N" as the first plain list item; `Derived.attacksPerAction?` in `types.ts`. No CSS; the 375px width check was not run in a browser (plain `<li>` lines only).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, level 20, human/acolyte, mean of 20): Fighter mixed 2.65 ms, 2024 1.67 ms; Monk mixed 1.93 ms, 2024 1.31 ms (limit 50 ms; R1 Fighter was 2.78 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_class_resources_fighter_monk.py` (67, rule matrix in the docstring); the R1 test that used a Fighter as the "no builder" class now uses a Paladin. Server suite 1422 passed (1355 + 67), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend build green.

## Class resources - R3 Cleric, Paladin, Druid, Channel Divinity multiclass (2026-10-09)
- **Cleric** (`_cleric`): `Channel Divinity` (2014/mixed: SRD `channel_divinity_charges` 1/2/3 at 2/6/18, `recharge "short-or-long"`; 2024: XPHB `channel-divinity` column 2/3/4 at 2/6/18, `recharge "long"` with `details.shortRestRegains 1`, because XPHB regains one use on a short rest and all on a long rest) and, 2014/mixed only, `Destroy Undead` (`details.maxCr` "1/2","1","2","3","4" at 5/8/11/14/17 from `destroy_undead_cr`; no uses). The 2024 Cleric has Sear Undead, which has no CR column, so no entry.
- **Paladin** (`_paladin`): `Lay on Hands` (`pool = 5 x level`, long rest, both editions, verified in the SRD and XPHB text); `Divine Sense` only under 2014/mixed (`uses = max(1, 1 + CHA mod)`, long rest; in 2024 it is a Channel Divinity effect); `Channel Divinity` from level 3: 2014/mixed 1 use (hardcoded `reference_data.PALADIN_2014_CHANNEL_DIVINITY`, the SRD level rows have no column and the feature text is empty), 2024 table 2 (3-10), 3 (11-20).
- **Druid** (`_druid`): `Wild Shape` from level 2. 2014/mixed: 2 uses (hardcoded `WILD_SHAPE_2014_USES`, from the feature text), short-or-long, `details {maxCr, swim, fly}` from `wild_shape_max_cr/swim/fly`; `unlimited` at level 20 (SRD feature `archdruid`). Verified SRD rows: CR 1/4 (2-3), 1/2 with swim (4-6), 1 with swim (7), 1 with swim and fly (8+); note the SRD row already gives CR 1 at level 7 (the feature text only changes at 8, where flying is added), so the plan's "1 at 8" is the fly step. 2024: XPHB `wild-shape` column 2 (2-5), 3 (6-16), 4 (17-20), not unlimited at 20 (XPHB Archdruid regains a use on initiative); `details {maxCr, fly, knownForms, shortRestRegains 1}` from the prose-only Beast Shapes table, hardcoded as `WILD_SHAPE_2024_BEAST_SHAPES` (CR 1/4 no fly at 2, 1/2 at 4, 1 with fly at 8; 4/6/8 known forms; no swim restriction in 2024). Level 1 has no entry.
- **Channel Divinity multiclass** (PHB 164, "...getting the feature again doesn't give you an additional use of it"): `resources_for` ends with `_merge_channel_divinity`; one entry, `uses` = max over classes, `classId` = the class giving the max (first class on a tie), `details.classes` lists every class that grants it (only when more than one). Cleric 6 / Paladin 3 = one entry, 2 uses (cleric); 2024 Cleric 6 / Paladin 3 = 3, not 5. Applied under every ruleset. Open question: the XPHB data has no multiclassing rules text, so the 2024 multiclass sentence could not be verified; the XPHB class text says "this class's Channel Divinity" (separate pools?). Needs a user decision (see the report).
- **Exports/UI.** `resource_line` and `describeResource` now also print `details.maxCr` ("макс. CR 1/2"); `maxCr?` in `types.ts`. Otherwise unchanged. Verified-by-script facts and the multiclass case matrix are in the test docstring.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20): Cleric 20 mixed 2.55 ms, 2024 1.92 ms; Cleric 6 / Paladin 3 mixed 1.29 ms, 2024 1.15 ms (limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_class_resources_cleric_paladin_druid.py` (92); the R1 "no builder" test now uses a Ranger. Server suite 1514 passed (1422 + 92), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend build green.

## Class resources - R4 Bard, Sorcerer, Wizard, 2024 Ranger (2026-10-09)
- **Bard** (`_bard`): `Bardic Inspiration`, `die` d6/d8/d10/d12 at 1/5/10/15 (2024: XPHB `bardic-die` column, checked level by level against SRD `bardic_inspiration_die`: identical; 2014/mixed: the SRD row), `uses = max(1, CHA mod)` in both editions, `recharge` long below level 5 and `short-or-long` from 5 (Font of Inspiration; same text in SRD and XPHB). 2024 adds `details.spellSlotRegainsUse` from level 5 (XPHB Font of Inspiration: spend a slot to regain one use). Without the 2024 tables the SRD die is used.
- **Sorcerer** (`_sorcerer`): `Sorcery Points` from level 2 (uses = level, long rest; SRD `sorcery_points` / XPHB column, identical). `details.slotCosts` = {slot level: cost}. 2014/mixed: SRD `creating_spell_slots` (1-5 = 2/3/5/6/7, all from level 2, the 2014 text has no minimum level). 2024: same costs but only slots whose minimum sorcerer level (2/3/5/7/9, prose table in the XPHB Font of Magic text) is reached; hardcoded as `reference_data.SORCERER_2024_CREATING_SLOTS`. Dict keys are ints in the resolved dict (strings after JSON). Not added (not in the R4 plan): Innate Sorcery (2024, 2 uses) and Sorcerous Restoration.
- **Wizard** (`_wizard`): `Arcane Recovery`, `pool = ceil(level/2)`, `uses None`, long rest, `details {maxSlotLevel 5, onShortRest}`. SRD `arcane_recovery_levels` equals ceil(level/2) at all 20 levels (script-checked); XPHB has no column, so 2024 uses the formula (`ARCANE_RECOVERY_MAX_SLOT_LEVEL` in reference_data). Same text in both editions.
- **Ranger** (`_ranger`): 2024 only, `Favored Enemy (Hunter's Mark)`, XPHB `favored-enemy` column 2/3/4/5/6 at 1/5/9/13/17, long rest, id `ranger:favored-enemy-hunter-s-mark`. 2014/mixed: no resource (the choice is already modelled). With the 2024 tables empty: no entry plus the `class-2024-tables-missing` warning.
- **After R4** only Warlock has no builder (R7/R8); the R1 "no builder" test and the "no resources block" export test now use a Warlock.
- **Exports/UI.** No change: `describeResource`/`resource_line` already print die, uses, pool and recharge; `slotCosts` and the other new details are not printed. Frontend untouched (no build needed).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, level 20, CHA 16, mean of 20): Bard mixed 2.77 ms, 2024 2.03 ms; Sorcerer mixed 2.75 ms, 2024 2.06 ms (limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_class_resources_bard_sorcerer_wizard_ranger.py` (138, rule matrix in the docstring). Server suite 1652 passed (1514 + 138), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## Class resources - R5 Saves, speed and rage effects folded into derived (2026-10-10)

Decision D5: always-on numbers change `derived` itself.
- **Aura of Protection** (Paladin 6): `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS["aura-of-protection"] = {save_bonus_ability: CHA, save_bonus_min: 1}`; `features.save_bonus` adds max(CHA mod, 1) to every save (proficient or not). PHB and XPHB text checked by script: same number, "minimum bonus of +1". Several auras never add up (one bonus). Not modelled: must be conscious / not Incapacitated, range.
- **Diamond Soul** (Monk 14, XPHB name Disciplined Survivor): `{save_proficiency: "all"}`; the six abilities are appended to `sheet.proficiencies.savingThrows` without duplicates.
- **Slippery Mind** (Rogue 15) is ruleset-dependent, so it is NOT in the static table: `features.RULESET_SAVE_PROFICIENCIES`. PHB: Wisdom. XPHB (verified in the imported data): Wisdom and Charisma. 2014 and mixed = WIS, 2024 = WIS + CHA.
- **Fast Movement** (Barbarian 5): `{speed_bonus_unless_heavy_armor: 10}`; +10 to every speed mode unless an equipped armor has category Heavy (shield, Light, Medium do not turn it off). It is a different feature from Unarmored Movement, so a Barbarian 5 / Monk 2 gets both when unarmored (50), only Fast Movement in Medium armor (40), neither in Heavy (30).
- **Rage resistances**: `resources_for` now receives the resolved choices; with the S4 option `totem-warrior-phb-3-totem-spirit--bear` the Rage resource lists every damage type except psychic (`reference_data.DAMAGE_TYPES`). Other totems and no pick keep bludgeoning/piercing/slashing. **Not modelled:** the 2024 Path of the Wild Heart per-rage choices (Rage of the Wilds etc.); under 2024 only the base resistances are shown.
- **Attack notes** (`class_resources.annotate_attacks`): "Rage: +N damage" on non-ranged rows whose ability is STR of a Barbarian with Rage (the Monk unarmed row has no item and gets none); "Sneak Attack Xd6" on finesse or ranged rows of a Rogue. Information only; damage numbers are unchanged.
- No new sheet/derived keys, so `types.ts` and the export-coverage test are unchanged; frontend untouched.
- **Existing tests:** none needed changing (no existing draft sat at Paladin 6+/Monk 14+/Barbarian 5+ with asserted saves or speed).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20): Paladin 6 1.09 ms, Barbarian 5 (greataxe, hide armor) 0.77 ms, Barbarian 3 Bear 0.43 ms (limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_save_speed_rage_effects.py` (32, rule matrix in the docstring; 26 fail without the implementation). Server suite 1684 passed (1652 + 32), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## Class resources - R6 Subclass resources (2026-10-10)
- **Ids verified in `catalog.subclasses`:** `psi-warrior-tce` / `psi-warrior-xphb` (Fighter), `soulknife-tce` / `soulknife-xphb` (Rogue), `battle-master-phb` / `battle-master-xphb`, `arcane-archer-xge`. Registered in `_SUBCLASS_BUILDERS`; entries have `source.kind "subclass"` and follow their class's own entries. All start at class level 3 (`reference_data.SUBCLASS_RESOURCE_MIN_LEVEL`); below it there is no entry even if the subclass is set.
- **Psionic Energy Dice** (`fighter:psionic-energy-dice`, `rogue:psionic-energy-dice`). TCE text checked: dice = 2 x proficiency bonus (total-level PB), d6 at 3, d8 at 5, d10 at 11, d12 at 17 (`PSIONIC_DIE_BY_LEVEL_2014`), recharge long, `details.bonusActionRegainsOne`. XPHB: `subclass_2024_tables` `number`/`die-size`, checked against the text table (3: 4xd6, 5: 6xd8, 9: 8xd8, 11: 8xd10, 13: 10xd10, 17: 12xd12); recharge long, `details.shortRestRegains 1` (XPHB: one die back on a short rest). The plan's "d8x8 at 9-10" and the other plan values matched the table.
- **Ruleset choice (documented):** a 2024 table is read only under ruleset "2024" (`Ctx.subclass_table`, as for classes). The XPHB record uses its table under 2024 and falls back to the 2014 formula under 2014/mixed (a test proves the table attribute is never read there); the TCE record always uses the formula, also under 2024 (its own edition). With empty `subclass_2024_tables` under 2024 the formula is used and the non-blocking warning `subclass-2024-tables-missing` is added once.
- **Battle Master** (`fighter:superiority-dice`): 4 d8, 5 at 7, 6 at 15, d10 at 10, d12 at 18 (PHB `combat-superiority` / `improved-combat-superiority`; XPHB "Improved"/"Ultimate Combat Superiority": identical numbers, so one builder for both and no ruleset dependence); recharge short-or-long; `dc = 8 + PB + max(STR mod, DEX mod)` (PHB `abilityDc` str/dex, XPHB "Strength or Dexterity modifier (your choice)"). Not modelled: the Relentless differences (PHB regain one die at initiative; XPHB free d8 once per turn).
- **Arcane Archer** (`fighter:arcane-shot`): 2 uses, short-or-long (XGE text: "two uses ... short or long rest").
- **Optional tail skipped (disclosed):** Rune Knight rune uses, Bladesinging (PB uses), Twilight Sanctuary, Psi Warrior/Soulknife power-specific uses (Telekinetic Movement, Psi-Powered Leap, Bulwark of Force, Psychic Whispers, Soulknife Rend Mind), Battle Master Know Your Enemy, and every other subclass resource.
- **Exports/UI:** no change (generic resource printing already shows uses, die, dc, recharge); `types.ts` unchanged, frontend untouched.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20): Psi Warrior TCE 11 1.36 ms, Psi Warrior XPHB 11 (2024) 0.99 ms, Battle Master 15 1.92 ms (limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_class_resources_subclass.py` (110, rule matrix in the docstring). Server suite 1794 passed (1684 + 110), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## Spells - R7 Warlock Mystic Arcanum (2026-10-10)
- **Decision D11.** Four ordinary single-pick choices `class:warlock:{11|13|15|17}:mystic-arcanum-{6|7|8|9}` (count 1, option `group` `spell-level-N`, source `{kind: class, id: warlock, level: 11|13|15|17}`), built by `resolve._mystic_arcanum_templates(catalog, rc, sources, ruleset, class_choices)`. It is called for every class in the second (spell) loop of `resolve()`, before the caster branch, and returns nothing unless the class is Warlock.
- **Gate (verified by script).** A template exists when `class_specific.mystic_arcanum_level_N` >= 1 in the Warlock level row: SRD columns flip at Warlock 11/13/15/17, and the XPHB Mystic Arcanum features are also at 11/13/15/17, so the same level row serves all rulesets (no separate 2024 table needed).
- **Options.** `_spell_options(catalog, "warlock", N, sources, ruleset, keep)`, so `sources` and ruleset/twin visibility behave like other spell options and an already stored pick stays offered. Verified counts at level 6: mixed 18, 2014 17, 2024 15, `sources=["srd"]` 7 (all 7 SRD ones incl. `true-seeing`; 2024 offers `true-seeing-xphb`). A multiclass (Warlock 11 / Wizard 5) offers Warlock-list spells only because the list id is fixed to "warlock".
- **Pruning.** Nothing new: on level-down the cids no longer exist, so the generic stale-cid pass reports them in `pruned`. The ids are not count-variable (count is always 1), so an over-count would be a blocking `over-selected` as for other choices.
- **Sheet.** The Warlock entry of `sheet.spells` gets `mysticArcanum: [{level, id, name}]` (empty list when nothing is picked; other classes have no such key). Note: a Warlock always has a cantrip choice from level 1, so the entry always exists.
- **Exports.** Markdown `- **Мистичен арканум:** Name (ниво 6), ...` and PDF body line `Мистичен арканум: ...` (suffix with the class name for multi-caster sheets), both after the class's known spells; mirrored in `markdown_export.py` and `pdf_export.py`, read with `.get` so old fixtures work. No `export_fields` change.
- **Frontend.** `ClassPage.isSpellChoiceId` also matches `:mystic-arcanum-` (cards go on the Spells tab); `types.ts` `SheetClassSpells.mysticArcanum?`. No CSS.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50): Warlock 17 2.3 ms, Warlock 10 1.5 ms, Warlock 17 / Paladin 3 2.5 ms (limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_mystic_arcanum.py` (21) plus one arcanum case each in the Markdown and PDF export tests. Server suite 1819 passed (1794 + 25), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green.

## Invocations - R8 Eldritch Invocation effects, SRD set (2026-10-10)
- **Table.** `reference_data.INVOCATION_EFFECTS`, keyed by SRD feature id `eldritch-invocation-*`, same effect-dict style as `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS` (`innate_spell {spell, uses, level}`, `skills`, `ac_formula`, `senses`, `resource_bonus`, `note`). Every entry was checked against the SRD feature text and SRD spell ids; `tests/test_invocation_effects.py` re-checks that each innate entry's text says "cast <spell>" plus "at will" or "once using a warlock spell slot ... long rest" and that the spell level matches. Slot-cast invocations (Thief of Five Fates, Mire the Mind, Sign of Ill Omen, Bewitching Whispers, Dreadful Word, Sculptor of Flesh, Minions of Chaos) are `"1/long rest"` at the spell's own level; the rest are `"at-will"`. Chains of Carceri is at-will with a note (celestial/fiend/elemental, once per creature per long rest).
- **Plumbing.** `engine/features.py`: `invocation_effects(choices, class_levels)` returns the same `(class, level, effect)` triples as `active_effects`, built from the selected ids AFTER `_gate_invocation_choices`; resolve concatenates them to `effects`, so `ac_formulas` (D6: Armor of Shadows 13+DEX, shield allowed, best formula wins, never stacked with Unarmored Defense) works unchanged. Ordering fix: the invocation gate now runs BEFORE the expertise gate so Beguiling Influence (Deception + Persuasion) counts as proficiency for expertise; skills join `proficient_skills` right after the gate. `senses.devilsSight 120` (higher wins; darkvision untouched; `_SENSE_NAMES` has the label). Innate entries use `_innate_entry_to_sheet` plus `source: "Eldritch Invocation"` (and `note`), ability CHA.
- **Resources.** New `_warlock` builder in `engine/class_resources.py`: `warlock:agonizing-blast` (`bonus` = CHA mod, `details.spell eldritch-blast`) and note entries (`details.note`) for Thirsting Blade, Repelling Blast, Book of Ancient Secrets. `resource_line` and `describeResource` print the note. `types.ts`: `SheetInnateSpell.source/note`, `ClassResource.details.note`.
- **Skipped (text not modelled):** Eldritch Spear, Eyes of the Rune Keeper, Gaze of Two Minds, Voice of the Chain Master, One with Shadows, Lifedrinker, Witch Sight. XPHB invocations (C7) not covered. **Superseded: the XPHB invocations (C7a-c, 2026-10-11) are covered under ruleset 2024, see the "C7a", "C7b" and "C7c" sections; the SRD invocations listed here stay as written for mixed and 2014.**
- **Known limitation (D3), lifted by G2 via `equipment.worn.armor = null`.** The SRD Warlock's fixed starting kit contains leather armor and without `worn` every granted item counts as worn, so a Warlock built from the default kit gets no Armor of Shadows AC until the player marks no armor worn; tests use a catalog copy without the kit. Agonizing Blast uses the raw CHA modifier (a negative one shows as a negative bonus).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50): Warlock 17 with 9 invocations 1.4 ms (limit 50 ms). Tests: `tests/test_invocation_effects.py` (13). Server 1832 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green.

## 2024 chassis - C1 XPHB class data import (2026-10-10)

Data only; nothing in `resolve()` reads the new fields yet (no behaviour change).

- **Loader.** `official/subclasses.load_class_2024_records(class_dir)` -> `{class: {feat_progression (raw), features [{name, level, ref}] (the `classFeatures` refs in order, placeholders included), starting_proficiencies, multiclassing, starting_equipment}}` for the 12 XPHB SRD-class records. `load_raw_class_features_2024` reads the XPHB `classFeature` records and `convert_class_features_2024(raw, records)` returns `(features, level_features)`. `Catalog.class_2024_records`, `class_2024_level_features` and `weapon_masteries` (all default `{}`) are filled in `_merge_official_subclasses` / `_merge_official_items`; an SRD-only catalog leaves them empty (test).
- **Class features.** `catalog.features["xphb-<class>-<level>-<slug(name)>"]` = `{index, name, level, class {index, name}, desc, source "XPHB", ruleset "2024"}`. Script-verified: 283 XPHB `classFeature` records for the 12 classes, 110 placeholders skipped by name ("Subclass Feature" 35, "<Class> Subclass" 12, "Ability Score Improvement" 51, "Epic Boon" 12), **173 imported**. Not every imported record is a per-level feature: 150 are named by a class record's `classFeatures` refs and make up `class_2024_level_features[class][level]` (list of ids); the other 23 are option/child records (Protector, Thaumaturge, Turn Undead, Flurry of Blows, Poison (Cost: 1d6) ...) kept in `catalog.features` only, so later slices can resolve entry references to them. No id collides with an SRD feature (the loader raises on a clash).
- **Weapon mastery.** `items.attach_masteries` sets `item["mastery"] = "<Name>"` on weapons whose normalized name (lowercase; "crossbow, light" -> "light crossbow") equals an XPHB base item's, using the first entry of its `mastery` list ("Sap|XPHB" -> "Sap"). Of the 42 catalog weapons (37 SRD + 5 imported), 38 get one (Musket Slow, Pistol Vex included); Net, Double-Bladed Scimitar, Hoopak and Yklwa have no XPHB base item and get none. `catalog.weapon_masteries` = `{name: [desc paragraphs]}` for the 8 XPHB `itemMastery` records (Cleave, Graze, Nick, Push, Sap, Slow, Topple, Vex). Ids: `longsword` Sap, `crossbow-light` Slow.
- **Test counts updated.** `test_loader.test_load_returns_expected_counts` features +173; `test_class_resources_fighter_monk.test_every_extra_attack_feature_in_the_catalog_is_in_the_table` now ignores `xphb-*` ids (2024 Extra Attack features are chassis data, not in the 2014 table). Item counts are unchanged.
- **Cost.** Catalog `load()` about 0.102 s before, about 0.122 s after (3 runs each, +20 ms from two more passes over the class files and the items file). `resolve()` untouched. Server suite 1832 -> 1842 (10 new tests in `tests/test_class_2024_chassis.py`), root suite 405 passed, 8 skipped.

## 2024 chassis - C2 Weapon Mastery (2026-10-10)

- **Rule (XPHB feature text, read from the 5etools class files).** Level 1 feature of Barbarian ("two kinds of Simple or Martial Melee weapons"), Fighter ("three kinds of Simple or Martial weapons"), Paladin, Ranger, Rogue ("two kinds of weapons ... with which you have proficiency"). Counts: Barbarian/Fighter from `catalog.class_2024_tables[cls]["weapon-mastery"]` (Barbarian 2, 3 at L4, 4 at L10; Fighter 3, 4 at L4, 5 at L10, 6 at L16); Paladin/Ranger/Rogue have no table column, fixed 2 (`_MASTERY_FIXED_COUNT`). The Rogue's own weapon proficiency is simple + martial Finesse/Light, which is the filter used. Multiclass: a level 1 class feature, so every listed class in the character gets its own card (follows the text; the books' multiclass chapter is not in the data). The same weapon may be picked on two cards (not prevented); `sheet.weaponMasteries` is deduped.
- **Template.** `resolve._weapon_mastery_template(catalog, rc, ruleset)`: ruleset "2024" only, cid `class:<cls>:weapon-mastery`, options = every catalog weapon with `mastery` (so D9: Musket Slow and Pistol Vex are in; Net, Hoopak, Yklwa, Double-Bladed Scimitar have none), label "Longsword (Sap)". Class filter disables with a reason ("melee weapons only" for Barbarian, "martial weapons need Finesse or Light" for Rogue). No card when the catalog has no mastery weapons or the table is missing (SRD-only catalog: no card, no crash). `_is_count_variable_choice` treats it as count-by-level, so level-down keeps the first N picks and reports "now allows N" in `pruned`.
- **Proficiency decision: build early, gate late.** The weapon-proficiency set is only final deep in `resolve()`, so the template is built with the class-filter reasons in the normal template pass (stale-id detection, count pruning and status work unchanged) and `_gate_mastery_choices(choices, catalog, proficient_weapons)` runs right after the proficiency block, disabling "not proficient" options and dropping stale picks (same pattern as the expertise and invocation gates). Proficiency test is `attacks.weapon_is_proficient` (shared with the attack math).
- **Output.** `sheet.weaponMasteries: [{itemId, name, mastery}]` (always present, `[]` outside 2024); `derived.attacks[i].mastery` (name or null, set by `attacks.annotate_mastery`). Exports: `weaponMasteries` in `SHEET_TAIL_FIELDS`, extra block "Майсторство с оръжия" (one bullet `Longsword: Sap`) and the "Атаки" bullet gets the suffix `, майсторство Sap`. Frontend: `Attack.mastery`, `WeaponMastery`, `SheetData.weaponMasteries` in `types.ts`; ExportPage prints a plain list and the attack suffix. ClassPage shows the card with no change (cid prefix `class:<cls>:`, not a spell id).
- **Known limits.** The SRD chassis proficiencies are used, so under 2024 a Rogue cannot master Scimitar or Whip ("not proficient"); the 2024 Rogue proficiency list is C9. "Change one choice after a long rest" is not modelled (the choice is simply editable). Mastery effects themselves (Sap, Topple...) are not applied, only named.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50, Fighter 20): 1.7 ms under 2024, 2.6 ms mixed (limit 50 ms). Tests: `tests/test_weapon_mastery.py` (31). Server 1842 -> 1873 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green.

## 2024 chassis - C3 Fighting Style as a feat choice, FS feats in ASI pools (2026-10-10)

Resolves U3. Plan: C3 in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-09-class-resources-2024-chassis-small-gaps.md`.

- **Verified by script (real catalog).** 13 FS-category feats: FS 11 (10 XPHB: Archery, Blind Fighting, Defense, Dueling, Great Weapon Fighting, Interception, Protection, Thrown Weapon Fighting, Two-Weapon Fighting, Unarmed Fighting; plus Arcane Warrior AU, `ruleset` 2014), FS:P Blessed Warrior, FS:R Druidic Warrior. Prerequisites: all FS feats `[{feature: ["Fighting Style"]}]` except Blessed / Druidic Warrior, whose only prerequisite is an unverifiable `otherSummary` ("When Gaining the Level 2 Paladin/Ranger Fighting Style Feature"). XPHB `featProgression` (C1): Fighter FS at 1, Paladin FS+FS:P at 2, Ranger FS+FS:R at 2. XPHB Champion has `champion-xphb-7-additional-fighting-style` ("You gain another Fighting Style feat of your choice"; it has no `feature_specific`, so before C3 it produced no card).
- **The card.** Under ruleset `2024` only, `_fighting_style_slots(catalog, rc, sources, ruleset)` replaces the features `fighter/paladin/ranger-fighting-style` (and the Champion's `additional-fighting-style` at 10 and `champion-xphb-7-additional-fighting-style`) with a feat card `class:<cls>:<lvl>:fighting-style` (count 1, options `feat:<index>` from `_feat_options`, no `group`, categories Fighter FS / Paladin FS+FS:P / Ranger FS+FS:R). `_feature_choice_templates(skip_features=)` skips the replaced SRD cards, so a stored SRD pick under 2024 and a stored feat pick under 2014/mixed fall to the generic stale-cid `pruned` pass (both tested). If the catalog offers no FS feat for the class (SRD-only data, sources without XPHB/AU) the slot list is empty and the SRD subfeature card stays (no empty card). Option states as for ASI feats: prerequisite via `_feat_prerequisite_unmet_reason` (the holder is treated as having the feature), duplicate via the S2 `taken` map (background, species, earlier fighting-style cards; a same-name feat of another edition reads "already taken (Name, SRC)"). A stored pick hidden by sources stays selected (`hiddenByFilters`, warning `feat-hidden-by-filters-<cid>`); a duplicate gives the problem `feat-duplicate-<index>-<cid>`. "Replace the feat when you gain a level" is not modelled (the card is simply editable).
- **Picked feat = a normal feat.** `_resolve_fighting_style_feats` returns shallow catalog copies tagged `_asi_origin = "<cid>:feat:<index>"`, `_asi_level` and `_fs_cid`, so `_feat_cid_prefix`, `_feat_instance_key`, cascaded choices (Blessed / Druidic / Arcane Warrior cantrip picks, cid `class:<cls>:<lvl>:fighting-style:feat:<index>:...`) and Blind Fighting's blindsight (feat `senses`) work unchanged. They join `resolved_feats` after species feats and before ASI feats (precedence background, species, fighting style, ASI; first pick wins) and `grantedFeats`. `sheet.feats[].origin` gains `"fighting-style"` (`feats_summary`; `types.ts` `SheetFeat.origin`). Ties are decided by that precedence, not by level: a Champion's level 7 card can block an ASI pick of the same feat at level 4 (the ASI card shows it "already taken").
- **Effects.** `_class_feature_list(..., feats=resolved_feats)` appends every resolved FS-category feat (card or ASI pick) under its feat index, so `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS` keys `archery-xphb` (ranged attack +2), `defense-xphb` (AC +1 in armor, chain mail 16 -> 17), `dueling-xphb` (`duelingDamage`), `two-weapon-fighting-xphb` (offhand damage + modifier), `great-weapon-fighting-xphb` (new flag `great_weapon_fighting_2024`, note "treat 1s and 2s as 3"; the 2014 note keeps "reroll"), `thrown-weapon-fighting-xphb` (`damage_bonus.thrown 2`, new `attack_styles` key `thrown_damage`, shown as the note "Thrown Weapon Fighting: +2 damage when thrown" on weapons with the Thrown property) apply. Blind Fighting uses the feat record's own `senses`. **Skipped (no verifiable engine expression):** Interception and Protection (reactions), Unarmed Fighting (the engine has no generic unarmed strike for non-Monks), the cantrip grants' spell-list effects beyond the ordinary feat spell choice.
- **ASI pools (U3).** `_HeldFeatState.fighting_style` is set once for the final character when any class has a feature named "Fighting Style" up to its level (`_class_has_fighting_style`; the Bard Swords feature counts). `_is_asi_eligible_feat(..., fighting_style)` admits the three FS categories under 2024 and mixed only when it is set (never 2014); the pool is cached per `(epic, fighting_style)`, `_resolve_asi_selected_feats` honors a pick with the same flag, so a Wizard's stored `archery-xphb` pick lands in `pruned`. The 5etools `feature: ["Fighting Style"]` prerequisite is now verifiable (`_KNOWN_PREREQUISITE_FEATURES`, `_feature_prerequisite_met`; Spellcasting / Pact Magic behave as before). Note (RESOLVED by F1, 2026-10-10): FS:P and FS:R feats were first admitted to any character with a Fighting Style feature; their own prerequisite ("when gaining the level 2 Paladin / Ranger feature") is not met by an ASI pick, so F1 removed them from the ASI pools (`_FIGHTING_STYLE_CATEGORIES = {"FS"}`); they come only from the Paladin / Ranger fighting-style cards. Mixed keeps the SRD subfeatures for the class feature itself (U9).
- **A1 pool sizes after C3** (Fighter, no sources; the combos add 21): 2014 slot 4/19 123/123 (unchanged); 2024 129 -> **142** at slot 4, 161 -> **174** at slot 19; mixed 237 -> **250**, 269 -> **282**; with sources `["srd","xphb"]` 2024/mixed slot 4 52 -> **64**, slot 19 64 -> **76** (12 = 13 minus Arcane Warrior AU). Paladin and Ranger equal the Fighter; a Wizard is unchanged (129 / 237). AU Arcane Warrior is offered under 2024 (not only 10 XPHB feats as the plan expected): `ruleset_allows` keeps 2014 feats visible under 2024 and the A1 2024 pool already holds the AU "G" feats, so the Fighter card shows 11 options (10 with sources excluding AU), Paladin and Ranger 12 (11).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, Fighter 20, mean of 50): 2024 1.9 ms without and 2.5 ms with a fighting-style pick, mixed 2.7 ms, 2014 1.6 ms (limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_fighting_style_feats.py` (33); `test_asi_feat_eligibility.py`, `test_asi_feat_groups.py`, `test_feat_cross_edition_duplicates.py`, `test_resolve.py` counts updated on purpose (pool sizes above; the first mixed feat ids now `abjuration-adept-au, actor-xphb, arcane-warrior-au`). Server suite 1873 -> 1907 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green.

## C4 Wizard spellbook (2026-10-10, both rulesets, D7)

- **Rule (quoted from XPHB class-wizard.json).** "It starts with six level 1 Wizard spells of your choice." / "Whenever you gain a Wizard level after 1, add two Wizard spells of your choice to your spellbook. Each of these spells must be of a level for which you have spell slots." Prepared: "choose four spells from your spellbook. The chosen spells must be of a level for which you have spell slots." The PHB wording (6 at level 1, +2 per level) is not in the bundled data (the SRD feature only says the wizard has a spellbook); the plan's statement is used for 2014/mixed.
- **Prepared counts (unchanged).** 2014/mixed: `max(1, INT mod + level)` (INT 16: 4 at level 1, 8 at level 5). 2024: XPHB `preparedSpellsProgression` (4 at level 1, 9 at level 5, 25 at level 20), label "Prepared Spells".
- **Template.** `resolve._wizard_spellbook_template`, called from `_spell_choice_templates` only when `cls_id == "wizard"` (Eldritch Knight and Arcane Trickster belong to fighter/rogue and keep their B-series known-spells card on the whole wizard list; tested). cid `class:wizard:spellbook`, label "Spellbook", count `6 + 2*(level-1)`, options = Wizard spells of level 1..highest slot level with the usual visibility/kept rules (`_spell_options_up_to`), grouped by spell level. Emitted between the cantrip and the prepared card. `_is_count_variable_choice` matches `:spellbook`, so level-down or a lost slot level gives a `pruned` entry ("now allows N selection(s)"), never a blocking problem. Level 20 book: 44 spells.
- **Prepared card.** Options are the effective book: the stored book picks that are valid (offered, deduplicated) truncated to the book count, i.e. what the book card itself resolves to. Description "Choose N ... to prepare from your spellbook."; with an empty book it is "Choose spells for your spellbook first." and the card has zero options. VERIFIED: `build_choice_template` returns `None` for an empty option list, so the empty-book prepared card is built by hand in that one case; a stored prepared pick outside the options is reported by the existing count-variable pass (`dropped` = raw selection minus resolved selection), so "option not present" is handled with no new code (non-blocking `pruned`, tested for a pick outside the book, an empty book, and a book truncated by level-down). The prepared card stays `pending` until the book holds enough spells (blocks export, as any pending choice). `expanded_spell_ids` are ignored for the Wizard (no Wizard subclass uses them).
- **Sheet / exports.** `sheet.spells[wizard].spellbook` = `[{id, name, level}]` (only on the wizard entry; empty list when nothing is chosen); `types.ts` `SheetClassSpells.spellbook?`. Markdown/PDF print "Книга със заклинания: Name (ниво N), ..." when non-empty (same mirrored line, before the prepared list). ClassPage `isSpellChoiceId` matches `:spellbook` and sorts the spellbook card first; no CSS.
- **Not modelled.** Copying spells into the book for gold/time, free spells found in play, Spell Mastery / Signature Spell picks, the 2024 level-up replacement rule.
- **Migration impact.** Existing drafts that stored Wizard prepared picks without a spellbook now see those picks reported in `pruned` ("now allows N selection(s) ...") and the prepared card empty until a spellbook is chosen; the stored `class:wizard:spells-known` value is untouched in the draft, so choosing the same spells in the book makes the old picks valid again (no data loss until "Clear stale selections" is used).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_wizard_spellbook.py` (17). Updated because they picked Wizard prepared spells without a book: in `test_resolve.py` `test_selected_cantrips_and_known_spells_appear_in_the_sheet`, `test_single_class_spellcaster_spells_appear_as_a_list_of_one`, `test_secondary_class_spellcaster_spells_appear_in_the_sheet`, `test_pruning_a_multiclass_choice_does_not_disturb_the_other_class` (give the Wizard a book), `test_spell_options_expand_with_level` (reads the spellbook card), `test_leveling_a_wizard_down_prunes_excess_spells_instead_of_a_problem`, `test_leveling_down_drops_a_now_out_of_range_spell_level_as_pruned` (extra `spellbook` pruned entry), `test_lowering_the_spellcasting_ability_prunes_excess_prepared_spells`; in `test_spell_import.py` `test_existing_srd_spell_pick_resolves_unchanged_under_every_ruleset`. Added one Markdown and one PDF spellbook-line test. `resolve()` for a Wizard 20 with a full book: 3.1 ms (limit 50). Server suite 1926, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## C5 XPHB class-feature list under ruleset 2024 (2026-10-10)

- **Scope.** `sheet.features` only (and so the Markdown/PDF/YAML exports, which read `sheet.features` through `export_fields.extra_blocks`; `test_markdown_and_pdf_share_the_same_blocks` stays green). Under `ruleset == "2024"` the `source.kind == "class"` rows come from `catalog.class_2024_level_features[cls]` (XPHB name, XPHB level, XPHB order; levels above the class level are cut; a class with no XPHB list, i.e. an SRD-only catalog, falls back to the SRD refs). `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical to before. Subclass rows are untouched. Placeholders and ASI rows are not in the XPHB list, so they stay out; the 23 child/option records are not listed per level and so do not appear. Effects (`_class_feature_list`, `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS`, resources, choice templates) still read the SRD refs: only the display list changed. No frontend change: the SPA never lists `sheet.features` (ClassPage's features tab shows choice cards), the type is unchanged.
- **Plumbing.** `sheet_features.build_features(..., ruleset="mixed")`; `resolve()` passes its `ruleset`.
- **Choices follow a small explicit map** `sheet_features.SRD_TO_XPHB_FEATURE`, `(class, SRD feature id) -> (XPHB name, n-th XPHB feature of that name)`, matched by name and ordinal (never level, the levels differ): bard-expertise-1/2 -> Expertise #0/#1 (SRD cards 3/10, XPHB rows 2/9); rogue-expertise-1/2 -> Expertise #0/#1 (1/6, same); metamagic-1/2/3 -> Metamagic #0/#1/#2 (3/10/17 vs 2/10/17); warlock eldritch-invocations -> Eldritch Invocations (2 vs 1); ranger favored-enemy-1-type / -2-types / -3-enemies -> the single Favored Enemy row (picks merged); fighter/paladin/ranger-fighting-style -> Fighting Style. 14 SRD ids mapped. The cid families outside the SRD ids attach by cid: C3 feat cards `class:<cls>:<lvl>:fighting-style` -> the XPHB Fighting Style row of that level (Fighter 1, Paladin 2, Ranger 2; the Champion's extra card, level 7 XPHB or 10 SRD, goes to its subclass row "Additional Fighting Style"), C2 `class:<cls>:weapon-mastery` -> Weapon Mastery (labels "Longsword (Sap)").
- **Could not map (kept, not dropped).** Pact Boon (no 2024 equivalent), Natural Explorer (1/2/3 terrain types; XPHB has Deft Explorer, which has no card here): they keep their SRD row (SRD name and level) with the choice attached, listed in `SRD_CHOICE_FEATURES_WITHOUT_XPHB`. The same fallback row is used for any card whose XPHB row is missing. A test checks that every non-subclass SRD feature with `feature_specific` is either mapped or in that list. Not choices today and not added: Mystic Arcanum cards (never attached under 2014 either), the Ranger's XPHB Expertise (level 9) and Warlock/Wizard XPHB picks (Divine/Primal Order, Scholar, Signature Spells) which have no card. **Superseded by C6b-1 / C6b-2: Natural Explorer now lives in `SRD_CHOICE_FEATURES_REPLACED_2024` (not shown under 2024) and Deft Explorer has an Expertise and a language card; only Pact Boon is still unmapped (and it has an XPHB counterpart in the C7a invocation card, Pacts as invocations).**
- **Known differences visible to users.** The XPHB list names `Spellcasting` / `Pact Magic` without the class suffix, shows Weapon Mastery / Fighting Style for Fighter/Paladin/Ranger and Barbarian/Rogue, has no "Subclass" rows, and e.g. Fighter 20 lists Indomitable three times and Action Surge twice (separate XPHB records). Ranger under 2024 shows "Natural Explorer (n terrain types)" rows next to the XPHB ones while terrain picks exist (the SRD card is still offered). **Superseded: the Ranger sentence (Natural Explorer rows next to the XPHB ones, the SRD card still offered) is no longer true under 2024 since C6b-1 (the SRD Ranger cards are dropped when the XPHB Ranger exists).**
- **Tests.** `tests/test_class_2024_feature_list.py` (28). No existing test changed (all use default/mixed/2014 rulesets). `resolve()` mean of 50: Fighter 20 1.4 ms (2024) / 1.35 ms (mixed), Wizard 20 1.9 ms / 1.9 ms (limit 50 ms). Server 1926 -> 1954 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## C6a Divine Order and Primal Order (2026-10-10, ruleset 2024 only)

- **Text verified** in the imported `catalog.features` (C1): `xphb-cleric-1-divine-order` / `xphb-druid-1-primal-order` ("You have dedicated yourself to one of the following sacred roles of your choice.") and the child records. Protector: "proficiency with Martial weapons and training with Heavy armor". Warden: the same with Medium armor. Thaumaturge: "one extra cantrip from the Cleric spell list" plus a bonus to Intelligence (Arcana or Religion) checks "equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of +1)". Magician: the same with the Druid list and Arcana or Nature. The plan's summary matched the text.
- **Data.** `reference_data.CLASS_ORDER_CHOICES` (source comment with the quoted text): per class the slug, the feature record, and per option `proficiencies`, `extra_cantrips`, `skill_bonus {skills, ability, minimum}`. Option ids are the catalog feature indexes (`xphb-cleric-1-protector`, ...); labels and descriptions come from the catalog.
- **Card.** `resolve._class_order_template`: cid `class:cleric:1:divine-order` / `class:druid:1:primal-order`, count 1, `source.level` 1, so it is `pending` (required) until chosen. Emitted only under ruleset `2024` and only when the catalog holds the XPHB feature record (an SRD-only catalog gives no card, no crash). Every Cleric / Druid entry of a multiclass character gets its own card (level 1 class feature, as Weapon Mastery). Under 2014 / mixed there is no card, so a stored pick falls into the generic stale-cid `pruned` pass (tested); an invalid stored option is ignored and the card stays pending.
- **Cantrip.** In the class loop the 2024 spellcasting dict gets `cantrips_known + 1` before `_spell_choice_templates` (no change to that function). The bump uses the first valid stored option, the same pick the generic pass resolves. The cantrip card is already count-variable, so switching to Protector or a level-down reports the dropped extra cantrip in `pruned` (non-blocking), never a problem.
- **Proficiencies.** The resolved order cards feed the existing `_categorize_proficiency` right before the class proficiency loop, so `sheet.proficiencies.weapons/armor` gain `martial-weapons` + `heavy-armor` (Protector) or `martial-weapons` + `medium-armor` (Warden). The C2 Weapon Mastery gate runs after that block and sees the shared set, so Rogue 1 / Cleric 1 (Protector) can pick a martial finesse weapon such as the Rapier; Cleric and Druid have no mastery themselves.
- **Skill bonus.** After `derived_mod.skill_scores`, for each resolved order with `skill_bonus` the amount `max(minimum, final WIS modifier)` is added to the listed skills. It is folded into `derived.skills[skill]` (no new key, so `types.ts` and the export-key test are unchanged); proficiency / expertise stay inside the base value (Religion for an Acolyte, WIS 16: 0 + 2 + 3). No passive score exists for these skills. Two orders in one character (Cleric Thaumaturge + Druid Magician) stack on Arcana (decision: different named features, no stacking rule in the text); reported as an open point.
- **Feature row.** `sheet_features` attaches the chosen option as `choice` to the C5 XPHB row "Divine Order" / "Primal Order" (fallback: a level 1 row of that name if the XPHB list lacks it).
- **Not modelled.** Changing the order on level up (the card is editable), the Protector's heavy armor Strength requirement beyond the existing armor warnings.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50, Cleric 20 Thaumaturge, WIS 16): 1.9 ms (Druid 20 Magician 2.0 ms; limit 50 ms). Tests: `tests/test_class_order.py` (25). Server 1954 -> 1979 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, no frontend change (generic card renderer; skills are an existing `Record<string, number>`), no existing test changed.

## S6 Export field-level sweep, kitchen sink (2026-10-10)

- **Tests only, no export code changed.** `server/tests/kitchen_sink.py` builds 12 fully chosen real-catalog drafts (Barbarian 20 in 2014 and 2024, Monk 14, Paladin 6, Cleric 6, Warlock 17 with Mystic Arcanum and invocations, Fighter 10 2024 with masteries and Dueling, Wizard 5 in both rulesets with a spellbook, Psi Warrior 11, Totem Bear 6, Cleric 1 Thaumaturge); each runs a pick-the-first-options filler with per-draft overrides and is skipped when the 5etools files are missing. `test_export_fields.py` checks, per draft: every `sheet`/`derived` key in the YAML, every class resource, weapon mastery, arcanum and spellbook spell, innate spell, attacks per action, "с Дуел", mastery suffix, Devil's Sight and feature choice (e.g. Divine Order) in both the Markdown and the pypdf text, no `?` and no fpdf2 "missing glyphs" warning (a separate test draws "×", "’" and "–"), and non-vacuity guards; `test_app.py` exports all drafts through `/api/export/{yaml,markdown,pdf}` with status 200. No export bug was found, so there is no fix commit. `tests/test_chargen_isolation.py` gained `kitchen_sink` in `SERVER_LOCAL_MODULES` (the helper is a server test module). Server 1979 -> 2041 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## Review fixes F1 (2026-10-10)

Logic bugs from the whole-branch review of 33027dd..HEAD. One commit per bug (`fix(...) (F1-1..F1-6)`).

1. **Fighting-style cards vs ASI picks (C3).** Both kinds of feat pick now resolve in ONE pass ordered by (class index, class level), a fighting-style card before the ASI of the same level. `_resolve_asi_selected_feats` drives the walk and takes a `before_level(rc, upto_level_or_None, seen)` hook plus a `taken_before_cid` out-map; `_FightingStyleResolver` (replaces `_resolve_fighting_style_feats`) is that hook. The shared `seen` map (S2 name-key semantics unchanged) starts as background + species. Each card's and each ASI card's "already taken" state reads `seen` as it was before that position, so the LATER pick is the duplicate: Champion 8 with `4:asi = archery` and `7:fighting-style = archery` blames the level-7 card (`feat-duplicate-archery-xphb-class:fighter:7:fighting-style`), and the level-4 ASI card no longer shows Archery as taken. This replaces the C3 sentence "a Champion's level 7 card can block an ASI pick of the same feat at level 4". Multiclass order is class order, then level (as the ASI walk already was).
2. **FS:P / FS:R not in ASI pools.** `_FIGHTING_STYLE_CATEGORIES = {"FS"}` for `_is_asi_eligible_feat`; the new `_FS_FEAT_CATEGORIES` (all three) still drives the class-feature list/effects for picked cards. Blessed Warrior and Druidic Warrior come only from the Paladin / Ranger fighting-style cards (a stored ASI pick of them lands in `pruned`). A1 pool sizes (Fighter, no sources; 21 ASI combos excluded): 2024 slot 4 142 -> 140, slot 19 174 -> 172; mixed 250 -> 248 and 282 -> 280; sources `srd`+`xphb` 2024/mixed 64 -> 62 (slot 19 76 -> 74); 2014 (123) and Wizard (129 / 237) unchanged; Paladin/Ranger = Fighter. The A1 rule table and the C3 "open question" text above are updated.
3. **2024 Rage and Second Wind recharge.** XPHB text (verified in the catalog): "You regain one expended use when you finish a Short Rest, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest." When the 2024 table value is used both are `recharge "long"` with `details.shortRestRegains 1` (same pattern as Channel Divinity / Wild Shape); 2014 / mixed unchanged (Second Wind short-or-long, Rage long).
4. **Rogue 2024 weapon proficiency (C2).** XPHB Rogue `startingProficiencies.weapons` (verified in the 5etools class file): Simple + "Martial weapons that have the Finesse or Light property"; its `multiclassing.proficienciesGained` has no weapons, so a secondary Rogue gets none. `reference_data.RULESET_CLASS_WEAPON_PROFICIENCIES[("2024","rogue")]` drops the SRD specific grants (longswords, rapiers, shortswords, hand-crossbows) of the PRIMARY class and adds the pseudo-id `martial-weapons-finesse-or-light` (listed in `sheet.proficiencies.weapons`, exports print "Martial weapons finesse or light"); `attacks.weapon_is_proficient` resolves it by category + properties, so attack rows and the mastery gate agree. 2014 / mixed unchanged. Not the C9 overhaul: other classes' 2024 lists are still the SRD ones. A Rogue loses Longsword proficiency under 2024 (it is not Finesse/Light). **Superseded by C9a: `RULESET_CLASS_WEAPON_PROFICIENCIES` was removed and the Rogue's Simple + `martial-weapons-finesse-or-light` list (and every other class's 2024 list) now comes from the XPHB overlay `Catalog.classes_2024`; F3a-8 also removed the Finesse/Light filter from the Rogue's Weapon Mastery card (proficiency alone limits it).**
5. **Mastery gate before truncation.** The generic pass no longer truncates `:weapon-mastery` picks; `_gate_mastery_choices` drops non-proficient picks first, then cuts to the count. Prune reasons: "...is no longer a proficient weapon" (gate), "...is not an available weapon for this class" (class filter / unknown id), "...now allows N selection(s)" (real over-count only).
6. **Speed bonuses are walk-only.** `_raise_walk_speed` is used for Fast Movement (R5) and Unarmored Movement; swim / fly / climb / burrow keep their own values (Triton Barbarian 5: walk 40, swim 30).

Timing (`resolve()`, Fighter 20, mean of 50): 2024 1.9 ms, mixed 2.7 ms (limit 50 ms). Tests: new/changed in `test_fighting_style_feats.py`, `test_asi_feat_eligibility.py`, `test_feat_cross_edition_duplicates.py`, `test_resolve.py`, `test_class_resources.py`, `test_class_resources_fighter_monk.py`, `test_weapon_mastery.py`, `test_class_order.py` (Rogue mastery gate test now uses a secondary Rogue), `test_save_speed_rage_effects.py`. Server suite 2041 -> 2049 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## Class resources (R) overview (2026-10-10)

| Stage | What | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| R1 | `derived.classResources` framework, Barbarian (Rage) and Rogue (Sneak Attack) | 2024 numbers only under ruleset 2024 (U9) |

## G1 rules-accuracy fixes (2026-10-10)

Source for the rulings: the 2024 Player's Handbook text supplied by the user (Multiclassing section; feat rule "a feat can be taken only once unless its description has a Repeatable subsection"; chapter 5 feat list). One commit per item (`G1-1`..`G1-7`).

1. **Channel Divinity (R3).** The 2024 Multiclassing chapter has no merge rule and each class text says "You can use this class's Channel Divinity twice". `resources_for` now skips `_merge_channel_divinity` under ruleset `2024`: `cleric:channel-divinity` and `paladin:channel-divinity` stay separate (own uses, `recharge "long"` with `shortRestRegains 1`, no `details.classes`). 2014 and mixed keep the PHB-2014 merge (one resource, uses = max, options combined). Cleric 6 / Paladin 3 in 2024: 3 + 2 uses (was one entry of 3).
2. **Elemental Adept (S2).** PHB text in the catalog: "You can select this feat multiple times. Each time you do so, you must choose a different damage type." `reference_data.REPEATABLE_FEAT_OVERRIDES = {"elemental-adept-phb"}`, applied in `official/feats.py`. PHB Magic Initiate and Skilled have no repeat sentence (verified) and stay non-repeatable. The engine has no damage-type choice for the feat, so the "different type" rule is player-enforced (documented, not modelled). **Superseded (FE7):** the damage type is now a card per instance and `_gate_distinct_choices` enforces "a different damage type" across instances, so the rule is modelled, not player-enforced. Outcomes: EA in any edition order is allowed (the feat being picked is repeatable); XPHB Magic Initiate/Skilled then PHB version stays blocked; PHB twice stays blocked.
3. **Fighting Style duplicates, mixed.** SRD fighting-style subfeature cards (`Fighting Style`, `Additional Fighting Style`; option name minus "Fighting Style: " is the S2 name key) now take part in the F1 ordered walk (`_FightingStyleResolver` gets SRD slots). A held SRD pick records `name:<key>` ("Dueling, SRD"); later FS feat options read "already taken (Dueling, SRD)" and a stored duplicate gives the existing `feat-duplicate-*` problem. The other direction: SRD options whose name key is already held (earlier feat, or an earlier SRD style of another class, e.g. Fighter 1 + Paladin 2, or Champion Additional Fighting Style) are disabled with "already taken (...)", a stored duplicate is not applied (card pending) and reports `fighting-style-duplicate-<optionId>-<cid>`. Position decides (class order, then level), as in F1. Not modelled: the Bard Swords "Fighting Style" feature (no options in the data).
4. **Feature-text options and sources (S4).** The converter adds `source` to a `text_options` option only when it differs from the subclass source (Elk, Tiger = SCAG in Totem Spirit / Aspect / Attunement; Storm Aura options have none). `_text_options_template(..., sources, keep)` hides an option whose source is not enabled unless it is already stored (then kept, `hiddenByFilters`). `_feature_choice_templates` takes `sources` and `class_choices`. The optional-progression pools (maneuvers etc.) are still not source-filtered.
5. **Arcane Warrior (AU).** AU is a selectable source (the picker's `/api/catalog` source list includes AU, 42 books). An empty `draft.sources` means "everything", so Arcane Warrior used to be offered under 2024 by default. Following the book (chapter 5 lists exactly ten Fighting Style feats, plus Blessed Warrior / Druidic Warrior from class text): `_feat_offered` now requires, under ruleset `2024`, that a Fighting Style feat of a book other than XPHB has its book in an explicit source list. Applies to the 2024 card and the 2024 ASI pools; `mixed` is unchanged (it still shows Arcane Warrior unfiltered), a stored pick stays selected with `hiddenByFilters`. Pool sizes (Fighter, no sources; 21 ASI combos excluded): 2024 slot 4 140 -> **139**, slot 19 172 -> **171**; FS card Fighter 11 -> **10**, Paladin and Ranger 12 -> **11**; Paladin 2024 139. Unchanged: 2014 123, mixed 248 / 280, `srd`+`xphb` 62 / 74, Wizard 129 / 237.
6. **Slot note exactness (S1).** New `derived.slotGrantingClasses` (class ids whose own table grants shared-pool slots at their level; Pact Magic never; same test `spell_slots_for` uses, via `spells._slot_granting`). In `DERIVED_FIELDS` (YAML), `Derived.slotGrantingClasses?` in `types.ts`; `SpellSlotsSummary` shows "(общи за всички класове)" when more than one class is listed and shows the shared line on a class tab when that class is listed (Warlock tab and the export page unchanged). Not re-checked in a browser at 375px (plain `<p>` text as before).
7. **Wild Heart per-rage options (R5).** Text found in the catalog (`wild-heart-xphb-3-rage-of-the-wilds`: "Whenever you activate your Rage, you gain one of the following options of your choice." Bear / Eagle / Wolf; `wild-heart-xphb-14-power-of-the-wilds`: Falcon / Lion / Ram). Under ruleset 2024 with `wild-heart-xphb` the Rage resource gets `details.perRageOptions = [{name, effect}]` (effect = catalog text after "Name."; level 3 three entries, level 14 six). Printed by `export_fields.resource_detail_parts` and `ClassResourcesList` (mirrored): "при всяка ярост една от опциите: Bear: ...; Eagle: ...". The Bear resistance is not folded into the Rage resistances (the pick is per rage).

Tests: server 2059 -> 2087 passed; root 405 passed / 8 skipped; `npm run build` green.

## G2 Worn / wielded equipment model (2026-10-10, decision D3 option c)

Why: every granted, chosen and `equipped` added item counted as worn, so the default Warlock kit's leather armor blocked Armor of Shadows (Mage Armor: base AC 13+DEX, ends when armor is donned), any armor in a Barbarian/Monk pack blocked Unarmored Defense (RAW: "not wearing any armor"), and Dueling's "no other weapons" could not be checked (PHB/SRD: "wielding a melee weapon in one hand and no other weapons"; XPHB: "holding a Melee weapon in one hand and no other weapons"; checked in the catalog text).

- **Draft (both optional, `engine/normalize.py` coerces them; wrong types give `malformed-equipment-worn|wielded` problems and the bad part is dropped).** `equipment.worn = {armor: <id|null>, shield: <bool|null>}`: a missing `armor` key = auto (best armor as before), `null` = no body armor, an id = only that suit; `shield` missing/`null` = auto, `true` = the inventory's shield, `false` = none. `equipment.wielded = {main, off}`: the dict being present means the hands are fully specified (missing key = empty hand). With neither key the engine is untouched (golden test `tests/golden/g2_resolve_baseline.json.gz`, written from the pre-G2 engine: 5 SRD drafts and 4 kitchen-sink drafts resolve identically except the additive keys below).
- **`engine/worn.py` (pure).** `apply(equipped_items, equipment, inventory, lookup)` returns the effective equipped list (non-armor items, chosen/auto body armor, shields, plus wielded items, so a wielded weapon always has an attack row and a worn suit need not be flagged `equipped`), the resolved worn/wielded state, `hands` for the attack math, and warnings/pruned. The inventory is granted + chosen + added (any `equipped` flag). resolve() uses the effective list for AC, `armor_warnings` (so a suit left in the pack raises no proficiency/strength warning), stealth disadvantage, Unarmored Movement and Fast Movement.
- **Dueling.** With `wielded` set, `weapon_attack(..., hands)` puts the +2 into `damage` only for the main-hand weapon, when it is melee and not two-handed (versatile held in one hand counts; `versatileDamage` never gets it), and the off hand is empty or not a weapon (a shield or other gear is fine). `duelingDamage` is then None, the note is "Dueling: +2 damage", rows get `wielded: bool`. Without `wielded` the S5 behavior (`duelingDamage` plus the "only with no other weapon" note, no `wielded` key) is unchanged. Two-Weapon Fighting's `offhandDamage` was NOT changed (it is per light weapon, not hand-aware; not trivial to improve).
- **Warnings (non-blocking, ids):** `worn-armor-unknown-item`, `worn-armor-not-in-inventory`, `worn-armor-not-body-armor` (shield or weapon in the armor slot), `worn-shield-missing`, `wielded-{main,off}-unknown-item`, `wielded-{main,off}-not-in-inventory`, `wielded-{main,off}-not-holdable` (body armor in a hand), `wielded-main-shield`, `wielded-two-handed-conflict` (a two-handed weapon while the other hand holds anything), `wielded-two-handed-shield` (only when `worn.shield` is explicitly true), `wielded-same-item-twice` (one copy), `wielded-shield-not-worn`. A not-in-inventory id also adds a `pruned` entry (`equipment:worn:armor`, `equipment:wielded:main|off`) and is ignored (worn armor falls back to auto, a hand becomes empty), so removing an item from the inventory cleans the slot. A shield in the off hand counts as a worn shield in auto-shield mode.
- **Output.** `sheet.equipment.slots = {armor, shield, hands, current}` is always present (what the selectors offer; `current` = what the engine counts now), `sheet.equipment.worn {armor, shield, manual}` and `.wielded {main, off, manual}` only when the draft sets them (resolved values after pruning). No new top-level sheet/derived key, so the export-key test needs no change; YAML carries them inside `equipment`. Markdown and PDF print the same plain line "Носено: броня: ..., щит; в ръце: основна ..., свободна ..." (`export_fields.worn_wielded_line`, in the Equipment block, only when set).
- **Frontend.** `EquipmentPage` section "Носено и държано": a checkbox turns on manual worn (seeded from `slots.current`) with a select (no armor + inventory body armors) and a shield checkbox; a second checkbox turns on manual hands with main/off selects (off also lists shields). Reducer actions `SET_WORN` / `SET_WIELDED` (undefined removes the key), `Draft.equipment.worn?/wielded?`, `malformed-equipment-worn|wielded` mapped to the equipment step. No CSS. Checked in the browser pane at 375px: `scrollWidth` 375; Fighter with Dueling, leather armor, shield, longsword: worn none + shield + main longsword + off shield gave AC 14 and longsword damage `1d8+5`.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_worn_wielded.py` (41): golden compare, Warlock default kit with/without `worn.armor=null` (15 vs 13), Barbarian with chain mail in the pack (14 vs 16), Dueling matrix, validation, stale ids, slots, Markdown/PDF line, YAML. Server 2087 -> 2128 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green. `resolve()` Fighter 20 with worn/wielded about 1.0 ms (auto 1.0 ms).
- **Decisions to note:** a not-in-inventory id produces both a warning and a `pruned` entry; the Markdown/PDF line appears only in manual mode; items of any kind besides armor may be held (a torch in the off hand is allowed, it is not a weapon).

## F0 Golden baseline for mixed and 2014 (2026-10-10)

Tests only, no engine or frontend change. `server/tests/test_c_series_golden.py` plus `server/tests/golden/c_series_mixed_2014_baseline.json.gz` (about 0.7 MB) guard "mixed and 2014 output never changes" for the whole 2024 chassis series.

- **Cases (228).** Per ruleset (`mixed`, `2014`) and per `sources` (`[]`, `["srd"]`): the 12 SRD classes at levels 1, 3, 11, 20 (first SRD subclass from level 3) and five multiclass drafts (Wizard 1/Fighter 1, Rogue 3/Bard 2, Warlock 5/Sorcerer 3, Cleric 1/Druid 1, Barbarian 3/Monk 2); plus every non-2024 kitchen-sink entry (8) under both `sources` values. Every pending choice is filled with the kitchen-sink "first option" filler.
- **What is stored.** The FILLED draft and its `resolve()` result, so later changes to the filler or to the pending cards cannot move the input. The test re-resolves the stored draft and compares after a JSON round trip (exact equality).
- **Allowed drift.** The module-level `STRIP` tuple (dotted paths, `[]` = every list element) is empty. A later slice may list an additive key there only with a note; any other difference fails.
- **Skip.** The module skips when `has_official_classes`, `has_official` or `has_official_spells` is false (local 5etools data absent). Kitchen-sink cases with missing marks are left out of the golden at generation time.
- **G2 golden.** `test_worn_wielded.py` now excludes golden names ending `-2024` (`srd-monk5-2024`); 2024 output evolves through the C-series. The file entry stays.
- **Regenerate on purpose** (only when mixed/2014 output is agreed to change): from `server/`, `../.venv/bin/python tests/test_c_series_golden.py --regenerate`, then review the diff of the behaviour change and commit the new gz.
- **Totals.** Server 2356 passed (2128 + 228 golden cases, the file-coverage test and the `-2024` exclusion net out: 2128 + 229 - 1); root 405 passed, 8 skipped.
- **Superseded by RP22 (2026-10-23): the golden now has 241 cases and a rebaseline policy.** `STRIP` is `("derived.featResources",)`. Deliberate mixed / 2014 changes go through `--rebaseline <SLICE>` (RP6) with an append-only `REBASELINES` entry (`RP7`, `RPF`, `RP15`, `RP16`, `RPX1`, `RPX2`, `RPX3`), never `--regenerate`; new cases come only through `--append`. The full log (cases, paths, hashes) is in "RP overview" at the end. The statement above that `STRIP` is empty and that the golden is regenerated on purpose is history.

## C9a 2024 class chassis: starting and multiclass proficiencies, skill and tool choices (2026-10-10)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, never regenerated).

- **Data.** `load_class_2024_records` now also keeps `saving_throws` (XPHB `proficiency`) and `primary_ability` (XPHB `primaryAbility`). New `catalog/official/class_chassis.py`: `convert_class_chassis_2024(records, srd_classes)` returns full merged class dicts `{**srd_class, proficiencies, proficiency_choices, multi_classing}` (SRD dicts never mutated; SRD multiclass prerequisites and the SRD equipment keys are kept, C9b replaces the equipment). `Catalog.classes_2024` (default `{}`) is filled by `loader._merge_class_chassis_2024` after `_merge_official_items`; `official_source` re-exports the converter. Mapping is strict (unmapped armor, weapon filter or tool string raises ValueError).
- **Proficiency lists (XPHB).** Primary: Fighter/Paladin light, medium, heavy, shields (explicit ids, no `all-armor`); Bard, Cleric, Druid, Sorcerer, Wizard, Warlock simple only; Monk simple + new `martial-weapons-light`; Rogue simple + `martial-weapons-finesse-or-light` + Thieves' Tools; Druid Herbalism Kit and no medium armor. Multiclass: no class grants Simple weapons; Barbarian shields + martial; Fighter/Paladin/Ranger light, medium, shields + martial; Cleric the same armor; Druid light + shields; Rogue light + Thieves' Tools; Warlock light; Bard light; Monk, Sorcerer, Wizard nothing. Skill picks: Fighter 2 of 9 (Persuasion), Rogue 4 of 10 (no Performance), Wizard 2 of 7 (Nature), Bard any 3 + 3 instruments; Monk tool card = one pick of 27 (17 Artisan's Tools + 10 instruments, the raw two-alternative list merged into one options_array, so the card now resolves; the SRD Monk card never did). Multiclass picks: Bard 1 skill (any) + 1 instrument, Ranger 1 of 8, Rogue 1 of 10. Saves equal the SRD for all 12 (test). Multiclass prerequisites equal XPHB `primaryAbility` (test).
- **Engine.** In `resolve()`'s class loop `entry_cls` becomes `catalog.classes_2024[id]` when the raw `draft["ruleset"] == "2024"` and the overlay exists, so the feat pre-walk, class/multiclass choice templates, proficiency block and equipment templates read it unchanged. `reference_data.RULESET_CLASS_WEAPON_PROFICIENCIES` and its branch are deleted (F1-4 Rogue override folded into the overlay; `MARTIAL_FINESSE_LIGHT_PROFICIENCY_ID` kept). `attacks.weapon_is_proficient` handles `martial-weapons-light` (Martial + `light` property; constant `MARTIAL_LIGHT_PROFICIENCY_ID`). `reference_data.proficiency_label` has an override map ("Martial weapons with Finesse or Light", "Martial weapons with Light"). `/api/catalog/class/<id>?ruleset=2024` spreads the overlay and computes `levelFeatureCounts` from `class_2024_level_features` (removes the C5 ClassPage drift; ASI/subclass placeholder rows are not counted, as in C5). No frontend change.
- **Degrade.** SRD-only catalog: `classes_2024 == {}`, SRD proficiencies (so a 2024 Rogue there keeps the SRD list; there are no masteries either), no crash (test).
- **Observations, not changed.** A stored pick that is no longer an offered option (Rogue `skill-performance` after switching to 2024) is dropped by the generic option filter with no `pruned` entry (existing behaviour, same under any ruleset); stored picks therefore shrink silently, the card goes back to pending. Existing 2024 tests needed no edit except the key-set assertion in `test_class_2024_chassis.py`.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50): mixed Fighter 20 2.7 ms, Monk 20 2.0, Warlock 17/Paladin 3 2.7, Wizard 20 3.0; 2024 1.9, 1.4, 2.3, 2.2 (limit 50 ms). Tests: `tests/test_class_chassis_2024.py` (99). Server 2356 -> 2455 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched.

## C6b-1 2024 expertise cards and removal of the SRD Ranger cards (2026-10-10)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated).

- **Data.** `reference_data.EXPERTISE_2024` (source comment quotes the XPHB texts read from `catalog.features`): per XPHB feature id `{class, level, count, skills, lacks_expertise}` for Bard 2/9, Rogue 1/6, Ranger Deft Explorer (2) and Expertise (9), Wizard Scholar (2, six skills: Arcana, History, Investigation, Medicine, Nature, Religion). `reference_data.SRD_CARDS_REPLACED_2024` lists the SRD cards that go away.
- **Helper module.** New `engine/class_choices_2024.py` (pure): `expertise_entries`, `expertise_templates(catalog, rc, ruleset)`, `replaced_srd_features(catalog, cls_id, ruleset)`. resolve() only wires: the class loop appends `expertise_templates` after `_feature_choice_templates` and adds `replaced_srd_features` to `skip_features`. Cids `class:<cls>:<lvl>:expertise-choice:<xphb feature id>`, label = XPHB feature name ("Expertise", "Deft Explorer", "Scholar"), options = the 18 skills (never tools), required count. A card exists only if the XPHB feature is in `catalog.features` (SRD-only catalog: nothing, the SRD cards stay).
- **Skipped SRD cards (2024).** `bard-expertise-1/2`, `rogue-expertise-1/2` when the replacing XPHB cards exist; the six Ranger `favored-enemy-*` / `natural-explorer-*` cards whenever `class_2024_records["ranger"]` exists (2024 Favored Enemy is the R4 resource, Deft Explorer replaces Natural Explorer). Stored picks of those cids land in `pruned` through the generic stale-cid pass.
- **Gate.** `_gate_expertise_choices(choices, proficient, fixed_expertise, class_order)`: the proficiency rule is unchanged ("not proficient"). A template flag `lacksExpertise` (Deft Explorer, Ranger 9 only, decision E1; superseded by E1 revised, see "Expertise duplicates" 2026-10-11 below) also disables skills that already have Expertise ("already has Expertise") and drops stored picks of them (a `pruned` entry). Walk order: fixed feat expertise, feat cards, then class cards by (class order, class level); the later pick is the duplicate. (Superseded 2026-10-11: under 2024 unflagged cards forbid it too.) `pruned` entries are still reported in `choices` order. `lacksExpertise` is stripped from `resolve()`'s output like `count_by_level`.
- **Generic attach (sheet_features).** `_CLASS_CHOICE_ID` gained `languages` (for C6b-2); `_xphb_class_rows` also returns the XPHB feature id; any class card whose cid ends in an XPHB feature id of that class attaches to that row (`by_fid`), so later cards need no `sheet_features` work. `SRD_CHOICE_FEATURES_WITHOUT_XPHB` is now `("pact-boon",)`; the Natural Explorer ids moved to `SRD_CHOICE_FEATURES_REPLACED_2024` (unreachable under 2024 with the XPHB Ranger; this closes open question 9: Ranger 2024 no longer shows Natural Explorer rows). The mapping entries for the SRD Expertise / Favored Enemy ids stay (harmless, used only by an SRD-only 2024 catalog).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_expertise_2024.py` (22). Two existing tests in `tests/test_class_2024_feature_list.py` were edited: `test_unmapped_choice_features_keep_their_srd_row` (the Ranger part now asserts no SRD cards under 2024 and the SRD fallback on an SRD-only catalog) and the two unmapped-list tests (include `SRD_CHOICE_FEATURES_REPLACED_2024`). Server 2455 -> 2477 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched.
