- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50): mixed / 2024 Fighter 20 2.75 / 1.94 ms, Wizard 20 3.0 / 2.2, Bard 9 / Rogue 6 2.1 / 4.5, Ranger 9 1.7 / 1.2 (limit 50 ms).
- **Not changed.** The expertise-related Rogue "thieves' tools" option exists only in the SRD card (mixed / 2014); under 2024 the Rogue's Thieves' Tools proficiency itself comes from the C9a overlay.

## C6b-2 2024 class languages and Primal Knowledge (2026-10-10)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated). Tests were written first (collection failed on the missing data names), then the implementation.

- **Data.** `reference_data.LANGUAGES_2024_STANDARD` (10) / `LANGUAGES_2024_RARE` (9) from PHB 2024 ch. 2 (incl. Common Sign Language, Druidic, Thieves' Cant); `LANGUAGE_NAMES` = `LANGUAGES` + both tables, now read by `markdown_export` / `pdf_export` (`LANGUAGES` itself is not extended, it still drives every mixed/2014 language choice). `CLASS_LANGUAGES_2024` keyed by XPHB feature id (Thieves' Cant: fixed `thieves-cant` + 1 pick; Druidic: fixed `druidic`; Deft Explorer: 2 picks). `PRIMAL_KNOWLEDGE_2024`. Source comments quote the XPHB text.
- **Builders** (`engine/class_choices_2024.py`, pure): `language_templates` (cid `class:<cls>:<lvl>:languages:<fid>`, options = the 19-entry pool, E2), `fixed_class_languages`, `gate_languages`, `primal_knowledge_templates` (cid `class:barbarian:3:feature:xphb-barbarian-3-primal-knowledge`, 6 options read from `classes_2024["barbarian"]` level 1 skill choice), `gate_primal_knowledge`. No card without the XPHB feature records (and, for Primal Knowledge, the chassis overlay): SRD-only catalog degrades to nothing.
- **resolve() wiring.** Two template calls after the expertise templates; Primal Knowledge picks are kept out of the first `proficient_skills` loop, gated against all other sources (class cards, background, species, feats), then added; the language gate runs right after the expertise gate with `fixed_languages` (moved up, now including class fixed languages), and `chosen_languages` accepts class language cards.
- **Language gate.** Known = fixed (species/background/feat/class) + picks of background/species/feat language cards (those keep priority); class cards then walk by (class order, class level). Known options are disabled "already known", stored duplicates are dropped with a `pruned` entry and the card goes pending. Primal Knowledge: "already proficient" (a background grant shows the existing generic "already granted by <background>" reason instead).
- **Sheet.** Picks attach to their XPHB rows through the C6b-1 generic by-fid rule (Thieves' Cant, Deft Explorer with expertise + languages, Primal Knowledge); Druidic is a fixed grant with no card. No `sheet_features` change, no frontend change, no new sheet key.
- **Not modelled.** C8 (origin languages) still pending: `common` is enabled in the pool unless some source already grants it. Primal Knowledge's Strength-check clause is text only. **Superseded by C8 (2026-10-11): origin languages, Common as a fixed 2024 language and the `origin:languages` card exist; see "C8" below.**
- **Tests.** `tests/test_class_languages_2024.py` (23). Server 2477 -> 2500 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50): Rogue 1 0.4 ms, Druid 1 0.7, Ranger 2 (Elf) 0.65, Barbarian 3 0.5, Rogue 6 / Ranger 2 1.0, Fighter 20 2024/mixed 1.9/2.8, Wizard 20 2024/mixed 2.2/3.1 (limit 50 ms).

## C6c-1 XPHB optional-feature import (EI + MM) and 2024 Metamagic (2026-10-10)

Ruleset `2024` only for behaviour; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated). Tests were written first, then the implementation.

- **Import.** `official/subclasses.convert_class_optional_features_2024(raw_options, resolve_spell)` converts the 38 XPHB records of featureType EI (28) and MM (10) to `catalog.features["opt-<slug>-xphb"]` = `{index, name, desc, source "XPHB", ruleset "2024", feature_types, repeatable, prerequisite_2024 {level, optional_features [opt ids], cantrip "damage"|"attack"|None}, spells {innate [{spell, uses "at-will"|"1/long rest"|"ritual"}], known_choose / prepared_choose [{filter, count}], ability?}, senses {truesight: 30}, feat_progression, cost}`. `OPTION_FEATURE_TYPES` and `convert_optional_features` are untouched (subclass path unchanged); `loader._merge_official_subclasses` merges them before the early return for missing subclass data and raises on an id clash (none). `load_class_2024_records` keeps `optional_feature_progression` (Sorcerer `{2:2,10:4,17:6}`, Warlock list). Repeatable (4 records) is detected from the named "Repeatable" sub-entry. Spell names that do not resolve are dropped; bad input never raises.
- **Spells.** `official/spells._spell_from_5etools` adds `damage_inflict` / `spell_attack` (only when present; additive, visible in `GET /api/spells/<id>` only). `engine/spells.spell_deals_damage` / `spell_attack_roll` read them or the SRD `damage` / `attack_type` (used by C7).
- **Metamagic card.** `class_choices_2024.metamagic_templates` (resolve() only wires it after the Primal Knowledge call): Sorcerer under 2024, cid `class:sorcerer:2:feature:xphb-sorcerer-2-metamagic` (attaches to the C5 Metamagic row through the generic by-fid rule; no sheet_features change), count = progression at the highest reached level (none at level 1), `count_by_level` so a level-down is a non-blocking `pruned` entry, options = the 10 MM records labelled "Careful Spell (1 SP)" ... "Quickened Spell (2 SP)". `SRD_CARDS_REPLACED_2024["sorcerer"]` = `metamagic-1/2/3`, skipped only when the XPHB feature, progression and records exist (SRD-only catalog keeps the SRD cards). Stored SRD picks land in `pruned`.
- **Resource.** `class_resources._sorcerer` adds `details.metamagic = [{name, cost}]` under 2024 for the picks; `export_fields.resource_detail_parts` and `ClassResourcesList.tsx` print "метамагия: Quickened Spell (2)". No new sheet key; `types.ts` details is already an open record.
- **Edited tests (count/shape only).** `test_loader` features +38, `test_class_2024_chassis` record keys (+`optional_feature_progression`), `test_expertise_2024` replaced-card classes (+sorcerer), `test_subclass_import::test_real_option_records` now counts the subclass family (records with `prerequisite_level`).
- **Not done here.** Invocations are only imported (C7a builds the card); `prerequisite_2024.cantrip` and `spells` are unused until then.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_metamagic_2024.py` (25). Server 2500 -> 2525 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` ok.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50): Sorcerer 2 mixed/2024 0.63/0.74 ms, Sorcerer 17 2.33/1.94, Fighter 20 2.78/1.95, Wizard 20 2.99/2.22 (limit 50 ms).

## C6c-2 Blessed Strikes and Elemental Fury (2026-10-10)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated). Tests were written first (all new tests failed), then the implementation.

- **Generalised C6a.** `reference_data.CLASS_ORDER_CHOICES` is now `CLASS_OPTION_CHOICES_2024`, keyed `(class, slug)` with `feature`, `label`, `level`, `cid` and `options {catalog feature index: effect}`; the source comment quotes the XPHB texts. Divine Order (cid `class:cleric:1:divine-order`) and Primal Order (`class:druid:1:primal-order`) keep their cids and effects (no migration, `test_class_order.py` unchanged and green). New cards: `class:cleric:7:feature:xphb-cleric-7-blessed-strikes` (Divine Strike / Potent Spellcasting) and `class:druid:7:feature:xphb-druid-7-elemental-fury` (Potent Spellcasting / Primal Strike), count 1, required (pending until picked), shown from class level 7 on. The `_class_order_*` helpers left `resolve.py` and became `class_choices_2024.class_option_options / class_option_templates / class_option_effect / class_option_extra_cantrips / selected_class_options / selected_class_option_effects` (resolve() only wires them). The cids end in the XPHB feature id, so the C6b-1 generic attach puts the pick on the C5 "Blessed Strikes" / "Elemental Fury" rows with no `sheet_features` change. Below level 7, under mixed/2014, and for an SRD-only catalog there is no card; a stored pick is a stale cid and lands in `pruned`.
- **Resources (decision E3).** `class_resources._class_strikes` (called from `_cleric` and, through the new `_wild_shape` split, `_druid`), only under 2024 with the pick: `cleric:divine-strike` / `druid:primal-strike` with `dice` (1d8, 2d8 from Cleric 14 / Druid 15), `details.note` ("once per turn, when you hit with a weapon [or a Wild Shape Beast form]") and `details.damageTypes`; `cleric:potent-spellcasting` / `druid:potent-spellcasting` with `bonus` = final WIS modifier (not floored: a negative modifier shows negative, as Agonizing Blast) and a note (Cleric 14 adds "temporary hit points equal to twice WIS modifier (N) within 60 feet", Druid 15 adds "+300 feet of range"). `annotate_attacks` adds "Divine Strike: +1d8 necrotic or radiant (once per turn)" / "Primal Strike: +1d8 cold, fire, lightning, or thunder (once per turn)" to every catalog-weapon row (not the Monk's unarmed strike, not for Potent Spellcasting). `details.note` is already printed by `resource_line` and `describeResource`, so exports and the SPA need no change; no new sheet key.
- **Older-subclass rule.** The text "if you get either option from a Cleric subclass in an older book, use only the option you choose" needs no code: no subclass Divine Strike / Potent Spellcasting effect is modelled in any ruleset, so nothing can duplicate. The rows keep their subclass `sheet.features` entries without a choice.
- **Not modelled.** Cantrip damage and range itself (no cantrip damage rows exist), the temp-HP and range effects (information only), and the Wild Shape Beast-form attack row.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_blessed_strikes_2024.py` (35). No existing test changed. Server 2525 -> 2560 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched (no build needed).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50, mixed / 2024, ms): Cleric 7 1.19 / 1.18, Cleric 14 1.9 / 1.65, Druid 15 2.09 / 1.76, Cleric 7 + Druid 7 2.16 / 1.93, Fighter 20 2.89 / 1.96, Wizard 20 3.16 / 2.29 (limit 50 ms).

## C6d 2024 class spell grants: always prepared, Magical Secrets, Spell Mastery, Signature Spells (2026-10-10)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated). Tests were written first (35 of the 50 failed), then the implementation.

- **Data.** `load_class_2024_records` keeps the raw `additional_spells`. New `class_chassis.convert_class_spell_grants_2024(records, resolve_spell)` (re-exported by `official_source`, called from `loader._merge_class_chassis_2024`) fills `Catalog.class_2024_spell_grants = {class: {"always": {class level: [spell ids]}, "expanded": {XPHB level key: parsed filter}}}`; `|xphb` names resolve to `-xphb` ids. Result: Bard 20 Power Word Heal/Kill, Druid 1 Speak with Animals, Paladin 2 Divine Smite and 5 Find Steed, Ranger 1 Hunter's Mark, Warlock 9 Contact Other Plane; Bard `expanded` keys `10` (levels 1-5) and `s6`..`s9` (Cleric, Druid, Wizard). Decision E9: Druid 2 Find Familiar is dropped (`_NOT_ALWAYS_PREPARED`, comment quotes the Wild Companion text "you can expend a spell slot or a use of Wild Shape"); Druid 1 Speak with Animals stays (Druidic: "you always have the Speak with Animals spell prepared"). SRD-only catalog: `{}`.
- **Helpers** (`engine/class_choices_2024.py`, pure): `always_prepared_class_spells`, `magical_secrets_filters`, `label_magical_secrets`, `effective_picks`, `wizard_spell_pick_templates`; data `reference_data.WIZARD_SPELL_PICKS_2024` (source comment quotes Spell Mastery / Signature Spells). resolve() only wires.
- **Always prepared.** In the spell loop the class grants at `class level <= rc_level` (Paladin 1 has none; multiclass uses the class level) are merged into `always_prepared` with the class name as reason ("always prepared (Ranger)"), so the prepared card disables them like subclass spells (a stored pick of one is dropped). `sheet.spells[i].alwaysPrepared` lists them after the subclass spells (no duplicates); the "Винаги подготвени заклинания" export block already prints that list. Under `sources=["srd"]` the sheet lists the `-xphb` id while the picker disables its visible twin (existing twin handling).
- **Magical Secrets (Bard >= 10).** The `expanded` filters whose key is a class level reached ("10") or a spell slot level reached ("s6".. via `_highest_spell_slot_level`) go through `_spell_filter_options` (ruleset/source visibility, stored picks kept) and are passed as `expanded_spell_ids` to `_spell_choice_templates` (the existing Fiend-style mechanism: only spells up to the highest slot level are added). The prepared card keeps its level `group` tags; options that are not on the Bard list are labelled "Fireball (Magical Secrets)". Below 10 the picks fall into the count-variable `pruned` pass. Approximation: the text lets you choose such spells only as new prepared spells or replacements; the engine has no level-up history, so the whole prepared list is open to the extra lists (same as the plan).
- **Spell Mastery / Signature Spells (Wizard, 2024).** `_spell_choice_templates` (wizard branch) builds, from the effective spellbook (what the spellbook card resolves to), cards `class:wizard:18:spell-mastery-1` / `-2` (count 1, "Spell Mastery (level 1|2)", book spells of that level with `casting_time == "1 action"`) and `class:wizard:20:signature-spells` (count 2, level 3 book spells, no casting condition); they are appended after the prepared card. Needs the XPHB feature records. An empty book (or none eligible) gives an option-less pending card ("Choose spells for your spellbook first." / "No spell in your spellbook is eligible ..."). Valid stored picks are added to the Wizard's `always_prepared` (reason "Spell Mastery" / "Signature Spells"), so the prepared card disables them. `_is_count_variable_choice` matches the three new ids: a pick that leaves the book, a level-down below 18/20, or an over-count is a non-blocking `pruned` entry, never a problem.
- **Sheet / exports.** Wizard `sheet.spells` entry gets `spellMastery` (from level 18) and `signatureSpells` (from level 20) as `[{id, name, level}]` under 2024 (empty list while unpicked; absent at lower levels and in other rulesets); the picks are also in `alwaysPrepared`. `sheet_features` attaches the pick names to the C5 "Spell Mastery" / "Signature Spells" rows (`_spell_pick_labels`, by feature name because the cids have no feature-id tail). Markdown/PDF print "Майсторство със заклинания: ..." and "Запазени заклинания: ..." (shared `export_fields.SPELL_PICK_LINES`, after the Mystic Arcanum line). Frontend: `ClassPage.isSpellChoiceId` matches `:spell-mastery-` and `:signature-spells` (cards go to the Spells tab), `types.ts` `SheetClassSpells.spellMastery?` / `signatureSpells?`. No CSS.
- **Not modelled.** The free casts (Paladin's Smite, Faithful Steed, Contact Patron, Hunter's Mark uses beyond the R4 resource, Spell Mastery at-will, Signature once per rest) are resources for R9; Spell Mastery's "replace one after a Long Rest" is just editing the card. The 2014 counterparts (SRD Magical Secrets, 2014 Spell Mastery / Signature Spells) stay out (E11). **Superseded by R9 (2026-10-11): Paladin's Smite, Faithful Steed and the other free casts are class resources, see "R9" below (Contact Patron is in C7b; Spell Mastery / Signature Spells are the sheet picks).**
- **Edited test.** `test_class_2024_chassis::test_twelve_class_records` key set (+`additional_spells`).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_class_spell_grants_2024.py` (50). Server 2560 -> 2610 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` ok.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50, mixed / 2024, ms): Fighter 20 3.03 / 2.02, Wizard 20 (picks) 3.26 / 2.90, Wizard 20 2.99 / 2.40, Bard 10 1.70 / 2.45, Bard 20 2.88 / 4.76, Paladin 5 1.21 / 1.19, Ranger 5 1.06 / 1.06, Druid 2 0.71 / 0.74, Warlock 9 1.71 / 1.47 (limit 50 ms).

## C7a 2024 Eldritch Invocation card, counts, prerequisites, Pact as invocation (2026-10-10)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated). Tests first, then the implementation; a previous executor's uncommitted partial work was reviewed, kept (it was correct) and completed.

- **Card.** `class_choices_2024.invocation_templates` (resolve() only wires it next to `metamagic_templates`): cid `class:warlock:1:invocations:xphb-warlock-1-eldritch-invocations`, count = `class_2024_tables["warlock"]["invocations"][level-1]` (fallback: the record's `optional_feature_progression`), options = the 28 XPHB EI records sorted by name plus repeat instances `<id>~2..~count` (decision E4: Agonizing Blast, Eldritch Spear, Repelling Blast, Lessons of the First Ones), each directly after its base, labelled "Agonizing Blast (2)". The `:invocations:` marker keeps R8 count-variable pruning, `selected_invocation_ids` and `class_resources._warlock` working. The Pacts are plain invocations in the card.
- **SRD cards.** `SRD_CARDS_REPLACED_2024["warlock"] = (eldritch-invocations, pact-boon)`, applied only when the XPHB card can exist (`_invocation_ready`: feature record, count table and EI records), so an SRD-only catalog keeps the SRD cards.
- **Gate.** `_gate_invocation_choices` takes a 2024 branch into `class_choices_2024.gate_invocations` (pure): level, `optional_features` (base id selected in the same card), damaging / attack-roll Warlock cantrip (`spells.spell_deals_damage` / `spell_attack_roll` over `class:warlock:spells-cantrips`), and repeat instance k needs k-1; run to a fixed point (Blade -> Thirsting -> Devouring). Unmet options are disabled with reasons ("requires warlock level N", "requires Pact of the Blade", "requires a Warlock cantrip that deals damage / requires an attack roll", "take Agonizing Blast first"); dropped picks give a non-blocking `pruned` entry.
- **Helpers.** `features.selected_invocation_ids` strips `~k` (effect lookup); new `selected_invocation_instances` keeps it (hook for C7c per-instance dependents); `class_resources._warlock` strips it too. The sheet "Eldritch Invocations" row (level 1) lists the picks through the C6b-1 generic attach.
- **Hooks for C7b/C7c.** Effects are still keyed by the SRD `INVOCATION_EFFECTS`; XPHB ids have no effects until C7b. Tome cantrips counting towards the cantrip prerequisite is C7c (the gate already takes a `cantrips` list).
- **Edited tests (shape only).** `test_expertise_2024` replaced-card classes (+warlock); `test_class_2024_feature_list::test_unmapped_choice_features_keep_their_srd_row` now asserts no Pact Boon card under 2024 and keeps the Pact Boon row check on an SRD-only catalog.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_invocations_2024.py` (14). Server 2610 -> 2624 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 10, mixed / 2024, ms): Warlock 17 + Paladin 3 2.8 / 2.7, Warlock 20 2.9 / 2.6, Fighter 20 2.9 / 2.0, Wizard 20 3.0 / 2.3; Warlock 17/20 with 9-10 picks under 50 ms (asserted in test).

## C7b 2024 Eldritch Invocation effects, Magical Cunning, Contact Patron (2026-10-11)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated). Tests first (`tests/test_invocation_effects_2024.py`, 42; all but 5 failed before the implementation), then the implementation.

- **Table.** `reference_data.INVOCATION_EFFECTS_2024`, keyed by `opt-<slug>-xphb`, holds only what the imported record does not carry: Armor of Shadows `ac_formula 13+DEX` (shield allowed), Devil's Sight `senses.devilsSight 120`, Gift of the Depths `swim_equals_walk` + note, `resource_bonus` for Agonizing Blast, and notes (Eldritch Mind, Fiendish Vigor, One with Shadows, Pact of the Chain "Magic action", Pact of the Blade, Thirsting Blade incl. the PHB 2024 Multiclassing rule that it adds nothing to Extra Attack (verified in the PHB markdown), Devouring Blade, Eldritch Smite, Lifedrinker, Gaze of Two Minds, Gift of the Protectors `{cha_names}` = max(1, CHA mod), Investment of the Chain Master, Eldritch Spear `{spear_range}` = 30 x Warlock level, Repelling Blast). Texts quoted in the source comment.
- **Data-carried effects.** `class_choices_2024.invocation_effect(catalog, id)` (pure): SRD ids read `INVOCATION_EFFECTS`; XPHB ids merge the record's innate spell (frequency `at-will` / `1/long rest`; the data's `ritual` for Pact of the Chain is mapped to at-will, decision E6; level from the catalog spell; dropped when the spell is missing) and `senses` (Witch Sight truesight 30) with the table (table wins). 10 innate spells: Armor of Shadows (Mage Armor), Ascendant Step (Levitate), Fiendish Vigor (False Life), Mask of Many Faces (Disguise Self), Master of Myriad Forms (Alter Self), Misty Visions (Silent Image), One with Shadows (Invisibility), Otherworldly Leap (Jump), Visions of Distant Realms (Arcane Eye), Whispers of the Grave (Speak with Dead), Pact of the Chain (Find Familiar), all at will; Gift of the Depths Water Breathing 1/long rest.
- **Plumbing.** `features.invocation_effects(choices, levels, catalog=None)` uses that function (so `ac_formulas`, senses and innate entries work unchanged); `features.swim_equals_walk`; resolve sets `speeds["swim"] = max(swim, walk)` after Fast Movement / Unarmored Movement. The innate fallback name drops the `-xphb` suffix.
- **Resources.** `class_resources._warlock` now builds one note/bonus resource per invocation INSTANCE ("Agonizing Blast (2)", unique ids) via `invocation_effect`. Agonizing Blast under 2024 has `bonus` = raw CHA modifier and `details.note` (the target cantrip is C7c). Under 2024, when the XPHB feature record exists: `warlock:magical-cunning` (level 2, 1 use, long rest, note "half your maximum Pact Magic slots (N) rounded up = M"; Warlock 2/5 -> 1, 11 -> 2, 17 -> 2) and `warlock:contact-patron` (level 9, 1 use, long rest). Contact Other Plane being always prepared is C6d.
- **Skipped / deferred.** Pact of the Tome and Lessons of the First Ones (C7c); no entry is half-modelled (test). Not modelled: Pact of the Blade weapon row, Thirsting Blade attack count, Eldritch Smite / Lifedrinker damage on rows (notes only), Eldritch Spear's per-cantrip range (needs the C7c target pick). **Superseded by C7c (2026-10-11): the Pact of the Blade weapon row, Pact of the Tome, Lessons of the First Ones and Eldritch Spear's per-cantrip range (target pick) are done, see "C7c"; still notes only: Thirsting Blade's attack count, Eldritch Smite / Lifedrinker damage on rows.**
- **Exports / types.** No new top-level keys; `details.note` is already printed.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 30, ms): Warlock 20 (2024, 10 invocations) 2.8, Fighter 20 mixed/2024 3.05/2.16, Wizard 20 mixed/2024 2.96/2.34 (limit 50 ms).
- **Gate.** Server 2624 -> 2666 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched (no build needed).

## C7c 2024 Eldritch Invocation dependents: targets, Pact of the Tome, Pact of the Blade, Lessons of the First Ones (2026-10-11)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated). Built in three commits in the plan's order (C7c-1 targets + Tome, C7c-2 Blade, C7c-3 Lessons). Tests first (rule matrices quote the XPHB text), then the implementation. Decisions E4, E5, E7 as approved.

- **Dependent cards.** `class_choices_2024` section "C7c" (pure) builds cards for SELECTED invocation instances only, from the RAW stored selection (`raw_invocation_instances`: the card's valid picks, first `count`, minus Warlock level and repeat-chain failures; the cantrip-based gate still runs late). cids contain `:invocation:` (singular), so R8 plumbing ignores them: `class:warlock:invocation:<instance>:cantrip | :feat`, `...:opt-pact-of-the-tome-xphb:cantrips | :rituals`, `...:opt-pact-of-the-blade-xphb:weapon`. `resolve()` wires them (`invocation_dependent_templates` at the end of the Warlock spell block, because the target options are the picked Warlock cantrips). They are count-variable (`_is_count_variable_choice`), so a stored pick that stops being valid is a non-blocking `pruned` entry ("... is no longer available and was ignored"), never `over-selected`. After the invocation gate `prune_invocation_dependents` drops the cards (and cascades) of any instance the gate removed.
- **Targets (E4).** One card per Agonizing Blast / Eldritch Spear / Repelling Blast instance. Options = the known Warlock cantrips plus the Tome cantrips; a cantrip that does not qualify is disabled with its reason ("does not deal damage", "does not require an attack roll", "range is under 10 feet" - `spell_range_feet` parses `^N feet$`, so Touch / Self / Self (emanation) never qualify for Spear); a cantrip another instance of the same invocation holds is disabled ("already chosen for Agonizing Blast (2)"), the lower instance wins a duplicated stored pick. `class_resources._warlock`: Agonizing `details.spell` = the pick (+ note), Spear note "Eldritch Blast: range 120 ft + 150 ft (30 x Warlock level 5) = 270 ft", Repelling note names the cantrip; without a pick the C7b generic notes stay.
- **Pact of the Tome.** Cantrips card (3) and rituals card (2, `ritual` level 1) over `_spell_filter_options` (any class, ruleset / sources aware); spells the Warlock already has (picked cantrips, picked prepared spells, class and patron always-prepared) are disabled "already prepared". `sheet.spells[warlock].pactOfTheTome` (cantrips then rituals; only present while the Tome is selected), printed as "Книга на сенките: ..." in Markdown and PDF (`export_fields.SPELL_PICK_LINES`), `types.ts`, and `ClassPage.isSpellChoiceId` sends the cards to the Spells tab. Tome cantrips are not "known" cantrips (the cantrip card count is unchanged). `gate_invocations(..., tome_cantrips)`: they count towards the damaging / attack-roll cantrip prerequisites while the Tome is selected, inside the same fixed point, so removing the Tome can drop Agonizing Blast and, with it, its target card.
- **Pact of the Blade (E5).** The card is built early with every Simple / Martial Melee weapon and narrowed late (`gate_pact_weapon`, right after the G2 inventory is built) to the weapons of the inventory (granted + chosen + added); a pick that left the inventory is pruned. The pact weapon is appended to the equipped items when it is only in the inventory (it is conjured in hand), so it always has an attack row. `attacks.weapon_attack(..., pact=True)`: proficient, ability CHA when the CHA modifier is strictly greater than the weapon's normal ability (a tie keeps STR / DEX), note `attacks.PACT_WEAPON_NOTE`; `reference_data.PACT_WEAPON_ROW_NOTES_2024` adds short Thirsting Blade / Devouring Blade / Eldritch Smite / Lifedrinker notes to that row (the numbers are unchanged). Works with `equipment.wielded` (row `wielded: true`).
- **Lessons of the First Ones (E7).** `_lessons_slots`, `_LessonsResolver` and `_lessons_templates` in `resolve.py` (they need the feat helpers). The resolver is hooked into the F1 ordered walk next to the fighting-style resolver (`walk_hook`): position (Warlock class index, level 2), against the same `seen` map, so a Lessons pick beats a later ASI pick and loses to an earlier class's ASI pick (tests for both). Two Lessons instances never hold the same feat, repeatable or not (Magic Initiate included); a feat already held by the background / species / an earlier pick is "already taken" (S2 name map, cross-edition); a repeatable background feat (Magic Initiate) may be taken again. Picked feat dict: `_inv_cid`, `_asi_level` 2, `_asi_origin` = `<card cid>:<feat index>` (e.g. `class:warlock:invocation:opt-lessons-of-the-first-ones-xphb:feat:magic-initiate-xphb`); the feat's own choices cascade through `_feat_choice_templates` / `_feat_spell_choice_templates` with that origin (Skilled picks reach the proficient skills, Magic Initiate spells reach `innateSpells`). `feats_summary` origin `"invocation"` (`types.ts`). A duplicate gives the usual `feat-duplicate-<feat>-<cid>` problem; a non-Origin stored feat is ignored; a feat hidden by sources stays with a warning and `hiddenByFilters`. Removing the invocation or levelling below 2 prunes the pick and its cascade.
- **Not modelled / deviations.** The engine models no feat's mechanical effect in `derived` (Alert's initiative bonus, Tough's HP are not applied for ANY source); the plan's "initiative changes" test therefore checks the Skilled proficiencies and the Magic Initiate spells instead. A Lessons feat's fixed ability bonuses / ability-bonus choices are not applied (no 2024 Origin feat has any; same as the fighting-style feats). Tome picks are only checked against the Warlock's own spells, not against other classes' prepared spells. E5 follows the approved wording (weapon taken from the inventory); the XPHB text lets the Warlock conjure any Simple / Martial Melee weapon, so a Warlock without a melee weapon in the kit has to add one to equipment first. **Superseded (FE1-FE12): feat effects now reach `derived` (initiative, HP, speed, AC, attacks, resources, notes); see the FE overview.**
- **Edited / added tests.** New: `tests/c7c_helpers.py` (shared drafts), `test_invocation_targets_tome_2024.py` (22), `test_pact_of_the_blade_2024.py` (10), `test_lessons_of_the_first_ones_2024.py` (16); kitchen sink gains `warlock7-2024` (Tome + Blade + Agonizing + Lessons) with `_EXPECT` / `_expected_strings` in `test_export_fields.py`; `tests/test_chargen_isolation.py` lists `c7c_helpers` among the local server test modules. `test_invocation_effects_2024.py` is untouched (Tome and Lessons deliberately stay out of `INVOCATION_EFFECTS_2024`; their effects come from the dependent cards).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50, ms): Warlock 20 (2024, 10 invocations incl. Tome, Blade, Thirsting, 2x Agonizing, Spear, Repelling and 2x Lessons) 4.9 (same draft under mixed 3.9), Fighter 20 mixed / 2024 4.2 / 2.8, Wizard 20 mixed / 2024 3.9 / 2.9 (limit 50 ms, asserted in tests).
- **Gate.** Server 2666 -> 2719 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` ok (frontend: `types.ts` + `ClassPage.isSpellChoiceId` only; no CSS, the Features tab already lists every `class:warlock:` card including feat-sourced cascades, so no browser pass at 375px was needed for a routing-only change).

## C9b 2024 starting equipment packages (2026-10-11)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated). The tests (`tests/test_class_equipment_2024.py`, 95) were written first with the rule matrix (the XPHB `entries` line of every class) in the docstring.

- **Data.** `class_chassis._class_equipment_options_2024(raw)` converts `defaultData` A/B (Fighter A/B/C) into one SRD-shaped envelope per class, flagged `keep_counts: True`; `convert_class_chassis_2024` puts it in `classes_2024[cls]["starting_equipment_options"]` and sets `starting_equipment: []` (a record without usable `defaultData` keeps the SRD equipment). Member mapping: `reference_data.CLASS_2024_EQUIPMENT_ITEM_MAP` (`arrows (20)` -> ONE `arrow` (F3a-6: the catalog item is already a 20-pack, quantity 20, weight 1), label "Arrows (20)", `robe` -> `robes`, `druidic focus` -> `quarterstaff`, label "Druidic Focus (Quarterstaff)"); `{"special": "Spellbook"}` -> `spellbook`; "holy symbol" -> nested `holy-symbols` pick (Amulet / Emblem / Reliquary); `equipmentType instrumentMusical` -> nested `musical-instruments` pick (10); the Monk's plural `equipmentTypes` -> sentinel `reference_data.MONK_TOOL_SENTINEL` (`monk-tool`). Everything else goes through the background converter's member function. A leftover `unlisted-` / `category-` / `flavor-` member raises ValueError (strict, like C9a). The background converter itself is unchanged and still raises on the Monk record (E12 scope). The envelope `desc` is the record's `entries` line with the 5etools tags stripped.
- **Gold.** `gp-N` members as before; the amount comes from the `entries` text when it exists (text wins). Defect found: the Cleric A record says `value: 7000` (70 GP) while its line says "7 GP"; the overlay uses 7. A test compares every package's gold with its text.
- **Quantities (E8).** `choices.build_choice_options` / `build_choice_template` / `build_equipment_choice` add `quantities: {item id: n}` (counts other than 1) to a bundle option when the envelope has `keep_counts`. `_chosen_equipment` reads it (`qty`), so the inventory, weight (`carryingCapacity.carried`) and G2 slots count 4 Handaxes / 8 Javelins / 20 Arrows. SRD, mixed and 2014 envelopes never carry the flag. `ChoiceOption.quantities?` added to `types.ts`.
- **Monk.** `resolve._monk_tool_pick` finds the tool proficiency pick (`class:monk:1:prof-choice-*` with tool options); `_chosen_equipment` hands out that item for the sentinel, `_selected_gold_and_narrative_items` shows the option label as a narrative item while no tool is picked.
- **G2 / armor.** Package items enter `chosen_equipment` and therefore the worn / wielded model unchanged (default: best armor + shield worn, every weapon an attack row; explicit `worn` / `wielded` override as before). Cleric A with Chain Shirt + Shield gives 13 + DEX (max 2) + 2; a test runs package A of all 12 classes and asserts no `armor-*` warning.
- **Observations, not changed.** (1) The catalog packs (`explorers-pack`, ...) are the SRD 2014 items, so their `contents` differ from the 2024 PHB (e.g. Explorer's Pack: SRD has a Mess Kit, the PHB 2024 has 2 flasks of Oil); `contents` is not read by the engine or the SPA, so nothing on the sheet is wrong, but a 2024 item catalog would be needed to show them. (2) A 2024 Wizard no longer gets the SRD fixed `spellbook` item automatically: it comes with package A only (B is gold, as in the book). The Spellbook class feature and spellbook spells are independent of that item. (3) Same for the 2024 Barbarian, who lost the SRD fixed Explorer's Pack and javelins. (4) A 2024 character that has not picked a package has no class equipment (and no automatic kit); the package card stays pending like every other equipment card. (5) Existing Warlock 2024 tests (`c7c_helpers._draft`, `test_invocation_effects_2024._draft`) relied on the automatic SRD Warlock kit (leather armor, daggers) and now select package A explicitly; `test_class_chassis_2024::test_twelve_overlays_full_merged_dicts` no longer asserts that the equipment keys equal the SRD ones.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50, mixed / 2024): Fighter 20 2.84 / 1.86 ms (with package A 3.01 / 1.90), Wizard 20 2.94 / 2.27, Cleric 1 with package A and nested pick 0.62 / 0.58, Monk 1 package A 0.39 / 0.35 (limit 50 ms).
- **Totals.** Server 2719 -> 2814 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green (types only, no UI change).

## C8 2024 origin languages (2026-10-11)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated). Tests were written first (12 of 19 failed), then the implementation. Decision E10.

- **Rule** (PHB 2024 ch. 2 "Choose Languages", quoted from the user's markdown): "Your character knows at least three languages: Common plus two languages you roll or choose from the Standard Languages table. ... Your class and other features might also give you languages."
- **Fixed.** `class_choices_2024.fixed_origin_languages(ruleset)` returns `["common"]` under 2024; resolve() appends it to `fixed_languages` (the same list the C6b-2 gate and the sheet read). Under 2024 every character knows Common even with no background chosen.
- **Card.** `origin_language_templates(bg, bg_id, ruleset)`: cid `origin:languages`, source kind `background` (id/label = the chosen background), count 2, options = the 9 Standard languages without Common (reference tables from C6b-2). Picks are stored under `background.choices`, so BackgroundPage (filter: kind background|feat, not `class:` / `species:`) renders it with no frontend change, and the export gate (`_pending_export_problems`) blocks until both are chosen. No card without a chosen background (nowhere to store picks); Common is still known. `chosen_languages` already accepted background `:languages` ids (the cid ends `:languages`), so the picks reach `sheet.proficiencies.languages` untouched.
- **Gate.** `gate_languages` now also handles the origin card. Priority: fixed languages, picks of every other background/species/feat language card (e.g. SRD Human's extra language), the origin card, then class cards (Thieves' Cant, Deft Explorer) by (class order, class level). Known options are disabled "already known"; stored duplicates are dropped into a non-blocking `pruned` entry. Consequence: the Rogue Thieves' Cant card now shows `common` disabled (as the C6b-2 plan anticipated).
- **Data check.** No 2024 species and no 2024 background record carries `languages` / `language_options` (60 backgrounds, 18 species checked), so by the book text the origin card is the only 2024 origin language source. The only 2024-ruleset record with a fixed language is the feat Cult of the Dragon Initiate (FRHoF, Draconic; via Dragon Cultist): it stays and makes Draconic "already known" on the card. 2014 records used under 2024 (SRD Human: Common + 1 pick; SRD races' fixed languages) still apply on top, nothing is removed.
- **Not catalog-gated.** The card depends on reference data only (language tables), so an SRD-only catalog under `2024` still gets Common and the card (unlike the XPHB-feature-backed cards); tested for no crash.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_origin_languages_2024.py` (19, one skip-guarded by data). No existing test changed. Server 2814 -> 2833 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched (no build).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 30, mixed / 2024, ms): Rogue 1 0.47 / 0.5, Fighter 20 4.0 / 2.6, Wizard 20 3.8 / 3.2, Monk 20 2.9 / 1.9, Warlock 17 3.2 / 3.1 (limit 50 ms).

## R9 2024 resource tail (2026-10-11, decision E14)

Ruleset `2024` only; `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden `test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated). Tests first (`tests/test_class_resources_tail_2024.py`, 72; 38 failed before the implementation), then the implementation.

- **Data.** `reference_data.RESOURCE_TAIL_2024` (one dict per resource: class, XPHB feature id, name, uses, recharge, note, optional `extras`) and `SIGNATURE_SPELLS_2024`; every entry quotes the XPHB feature text in its source comment, and `test_reference_values_match_catalog_text` pins each quote against the imported record. No XPHB class table column carries any of these numbers (the table columns are listed in the test docstring), so all are prose-only. `uses` is an int, `"wis_min1"` (max(1, WIS modifier)) or None (note only).
- **Engine.** `class_resources._tail_2024(ctx, rc)` is called from `resources_for` after the class builder and before the subclass builder (so Sorcery Points stay first). An entry exists only when ruleset == "2024", the XPHB feature record is in `catalog.features` and the class level has reached that record's own `level` (no level duplicated in code); an SRD-only catalog gives nothing. Notes grow with companion feature records (Sorcery Incarnate 7 and Arcane Apotheosis 20 on Innate Sorcery; Greater Divine Intervention 20).
- **Resources** (ids `<class>:<slug>`): Innate Sorcery (Sorcerer 1, 2 uses, long), Sorcerous Restoration (5, 1, long, note "half your Sorcerer level rounded down (N)"), Uncanny Metabolism (Monk 2, 1, long, `details.heal` "1<Martial Arts die>+<Monk level>"), Divine Intervention (Cleric 10, 1, long), Paladin's Smite (Paladin 2, 1, long), Faithful Steed (5, 1, long), Tireless (Ranger 10, uses max(1, WIS), long, note 1d8 + max(1, WIS)), Nature's Veil (14, max(1, WIS), long), Wild Companion (Druid 2, note only: no uses, no recharge, decision E9), Wild Resurgence (5, 1, long: the once-per-Long-Rest Wild Shape use to level 1 slot; the per-turn half is in the note), Nature Magician (Archdruid, 20, 1, long), Stroke of Luck (Rogue 20, 1, short-or-long), Persistent Rage (Barbarian 15, 1, long), Signature Spells (Wizard 20: uses = number of picked spells, one cast each, short-or-long, names in the note, no entry while nothing is picked).
- **Scanned** (all 173 `xphb-*` class features: rest/uses/times/Initiative/expend text, then the flagless names read): every feature above, plus read and **skipped** because the text gives no use count or recharge to model: Relentless Rage (rising DC counter, resets on any rest), Superior Inspiration, Font of Inspiration (R4), Perfect Focus, Stunning Strike, Superior Defense, Abjure Foes and Restoring Touch (spend Channel Divinity / Lay on Hands), Divine Spark, Turn Undead, Tactical Mind, Eldritch Master, Spell Mastery (at will, already on the sheet), Memorize Spell, Words of Creation, Countercharm, Slow Fall, Foe Slayer, Precise Hunter, Relentless Hunter, Magician, Brutal Strike family, Cunning Strike options, Evasion, Auras. Already modelled elsewhere: Rage, Second Wind, Action Surge, Indomitable, Channel Divinity, Lay on Hands, Wild Shape, Bardic Inspiration, Sorcery Points, Arcane Recovery, Favored Enemy, Focus Points, Magical Cunning, Contact Patron, Mystic Arcanum (R7). Subclass XPHB resources other than the R6 ones are still the disclosed optional tail.
- **Exports / UI.** Nothing new: the notes and `details.heal` print through the existing `resource_line` / `describeResource`, no new `details` key (the Signature Spells names are in the note), so `types.ts` and the frontend are untouched (no build needed) and the export-key test is unchanged.
- **Edited test.** `test_blessed_strikes_2024::test_resource_order_follows_the_class`: Druid 7 now also lists Wild Companion and Wild Resurgence after Wild Shape / Primal Strike.
- **Gate.** Server 2833 -> 2905 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped.
- **Timing** (`resolve()` level 20, mean of 30, mixed / 2024, ms): Sorcerer 2.89 / 2.19, Paladin 2.64 / 1.95, Druid 2.77 / 2.03, Rogue 2.42 / 1.64 (limit 50 ms; also asserted in the tests).

## F3a Whole-branch review fixes (2026-10-11)

Mixed and 2014 are byte-identical (F0 golden green, never regenerated). Tests in `server/tests/test_f3a_fixes.py` (written first, each fails before its fix) and `test_types_drift.py`. Server 2905 -> 2935 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` ok.

- **1 Pruned entries for picks a card no longer offers.** The general choice pass reports a non-blocking `pruned` entry for stored ids that are not among the card's options at all (a 2014 equipment bundle under the 2024 packages, which reuse the cid). Disabled-but-offered options (e.g. an ASI that would exceed 20) are still dropped silently, as before; ASI feat picks keep their own entry. Test fixed: `test_resolve::test_clearing_a_subclass_prunes_...` used fighting-style ids that were never offered.
- **2a Invocation repeat instances.** Agonizing Blast, Eldritch Spear and Repelling Blast instance k are disabled ("needs another eligible cantrip") unless k distinct known cantrips pass `_target_reason` (Spear: range 10+; Tome cantrips count). Instance 1 with no eligible target says "needs an eligible cantrip".
- **2b Unavailable cards.** `class_choices_2024.mark_unavailable` (called last in `resolve()`, ruleset 2024 only): a pending new-chassis card (`expertise-choice`, `languages`, `feature:xphb-*`, Wizard Spell Mastery / Signature Spells, the Warlock invocation dependents) with no enabled option gets `status: "unavailable"` + `unavailableReason`. Only `status == "pending"` blocks export (server `_pending_export_problems`, ExportPage, StepNav), so it never blocks. `types.ts`: `ChoiceStatus` + `Choice.unavailableReason`; ChoiceCard prints the reason. An empty spellbook still blocks through the pending spellbook card.
- **2c Pact of the Blade.** The card offers every catalog Simple / Martial Melee weapon; the inventory no longer matters. `gate_pact_weapon` is removed; the pact weapon row is built from the catalog item when it is not owned (proficient, CHA when strictly better, works with `equipment.wielded`).
- **3 Expertise order.** ASI feat Expertise cards are walked at their (class, ASI level) position (after the class feature of the same level); origin feats stay first. Under 2024 Skill Expert's Expertise card is `lacksExpertise` (XPHB: "proficiency but lack Expertise"). Test inverted: `test_expertise_2024` Deft Explorer (level 2) now wins over Skill Expert (ASI 4).
- **4 Primal Knowledge.** It is checked against sources at or before Barbarian 3 only; later skill cards (feat or class) see its skill as "already proficient" (disabled, stored pick dropped with a `pruned` entry).
- **5 Languages.** `gate_languages(..., positioned_fixed)`: class fixed languages and Lessons / ASI feat languages count from their (class position, level) on; ASI feat language picks too. Rogue's Druidic pick survives a later multiclass Druid; an origin Draconic pick survives a later Cult of the Dragon Initiate. Lessons feats sit at Warlock 2.
- **6 Arrows.** `arrows (20)` -> ONE catalog `arrow` (the item is already a 20-pack: quantity 20, weight 1), label "Arrows (20)". Ranger A + Soldier carries 35 lb. `crossbow-bolt` is also a 20-pack; no XPHB class package uses bolts (backgrounds: E12). The C9b claim above is corrected.
- **7** Magical Cunning at Warlock 20 (Eldritch Master): "regain ALL your expended Pact Magic slots".
- **8** Rogue Weapon Mastery: no Finesse/Light filter, proficiency alone limits it.
- **9** The 2024 invocation list is cut to its count AFTER `gate_invocations` (overflow is a `pruned` entry); `raw_invocation_instances` applies the structural check before its count cut. Limit: a spell-prerequisite drop that happens only in the late gate can still leave a picked-behind invocation without its dependent cards for that resolve.
- **10 Types.** `Attack.ability` allows "CHA"; `SheetInnateSpell.featId`. `test_types_drift.py` parses `types.ts` interfaces and checks the keys of sheet, derived, choices (+ source, options), attacks, classResources, features, feats, spells and innateSpells from the kitchen-sink drafts and every class at 5 / 11 / 20 (mixed and 2024) plus a 2024 multiclass. `UNTYPED_ALLOWLIST` is empty. `kitchen_sink.fill` only fills `pending` cards.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 30, mixed / 2024, ms): Fighter 20 4.05 / 2.75, Wizard 20 3.51 / 3.17, Warlock 20 3.09 / 4.00, Bard 20 3.00 / 5.04, Warlock 20 with 10 invocations (2024) 4.51 (limit 50 ms).

## F3b Final consolidation (2026-10-11)

Docs, tests, a browser check and one small frontend fix. Mixed and 2014 are untouched (F0 golden green, never regenerated).

- **Docs.** The stale statements (headline status, "What's implemented", R and C overviews, C5, F1-4, C6b-2, C6d, C7b, Known gaps, Open design questions) now carry "Superseded by ..." pointers or the final status; the open-questions list was rewritten with owners. The older plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-09-class-resources-2024-chassis-small-gaps.md` got a note at the top (its C6b / C6c / C7 / C9 "skipped" statements were superseded by `2026-10-10-2024-chassis-remaining-stages.md`).
- **Kitchen-sink sweep.** `tests/kitchen_sink.py` has 7 new 2024 drafts with a Human / Soldier (XPHB) origin so the origin language card is present: Rogue 6 (two Expertise cards, Thieves' Cant, masteries), Sorcerer 17 (Metamagic, Innate Sorcery, Sorcerous Restoration), Cleric 7 (Blessed Strikes: Divine Strike), Wizard 20 (Scholar, Spell Mastery level 1 + 2, Signature Spells), Ranger 9 (Deft Explorer, Expertise, two languages), Barbarian 3 (Primal Knowledge), Fighter 4 (package A with 8 Javelins). `_sub(..., origin=)` and `fill` now fills proficiency cards before the rest, so an Expertise card sees the proficiencies picked in the same pass. `test_export_fields.py` checks, for every draft, every language name, Spell Mastery / Signature lines, equipment names with their `x<qty>` and the Expertise heading in Markdown and the PDF text, plus per-draft expectations and `test_kitchen_sink_2024_stage_lines_reach_every_export` (the book's words, YAML languages and equipment equal the sheet). No export gap was found, so no export code changed. Server 2935 -> 2971 passed (7 drafts x 5 parametrized tests + 1), root 405 passed / 8 skipped.
- **375 px browser check** (Vite dev server + Flask, drafts loaded through `localStorage["dnd-builder-draft-v2"]`): `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` was 375 on ClassPage (features and Spells tabs), BackgroundPage, EquipmentPage and ExportPage for Warlock 7 (invocations card 6/6, Lessons, Agonizing Blast cantrip, Pact of the Blade weapon, Pact of the Tome cantrips / rituals, Pact weapon note on the Longsword row in the export preview), Rogue 1 (Expertise, Thieves' Cant card with Common disabled, Weapon Mastery, origin Languages on BackgroundPage), Wizard 5 (spellbook 14/14, Scholar, prepared spells), Cleric 7 (Divine Order, Blessed Strikes, nested holy symbol card, Divine Strike on attack rows) and Fighter 4 (Fighting Style, Weapon Mastery 4/4, package A). A Wizard 20 whose spellbook has no level 3 spell shows the Signature Spells card as unavailable with "No spell in your spellbook is eligible (level 3)." and the page still says "Всичко е готово за експорт" with the three download buttons enabled (`/api/export/markdown` 200). No console errors, no server errors. One functional gap found and fixed: the Equipment page listed chosen package items without their quantity (Javelin x8); it now prints `x<qty>` (the sheet, exports and granted list already did).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50, ms, fully chosen drafts, mixed / 2024): Fighter 20 4.20 / 2.72, Monk 20 2.14 / 2.05, Wizard 20 3.09 / 3.12, Bard 20 2.96 / 5.01, Sorcerer 20 3.20 / 2.83, Warlock 20 3.07 / 4.01; Warlock 20 (2024) with 10 invocations (Tome, Blade, Thirsting Blade, 2x Agonizing Blast, Eldritch Spear, Repelling Blast, 2x Lessons, Devil's Sight, four cantrips) 4.87. All far under the 50 ms limit.

## Expertise duplicates (2026-10-11, decision E1 revised)

User decision, stricter than RAW on purpose: XPHB only forbids a repeated Expertise skill on Deft Explorer, Ranger 9 and Skill Expert, but the app never lets a pick be wasted, so under ruleset 2024 NO Expertise card may pick a skill that already has Expertise. Mixed and 2014 are byte-identical (F0 golden green, never regenerated); the SRD expertise cards keep their old behavior there.

- **Gate.** `resolve._gate_expertise_choices`: under `ruleset == "2024"` every feat- or class-sourced Expertise card behaves as `lacksExpertise` (Bard 2/9, Rogue 1/6, Wizard Scholar, Deft Explorer, Ranger 9, and every feat Expertise card in a 2024 build: Skill Expert, Boon of Skill, and the TCE / XGE feats when picked). The `SKILL_EXPERT_2024` special case is removed; the `lacksExpertise` flag still gates outside 2024 (no card carries it there). Walk order is unchanged: fixed feat Expertise, origin feat cards, then class cards and ASI feat cards by (class index, class level; ASI feat after the class feature of the same level). The later pick is the duplicate: option disabled with reason "already has Expertise", a stored duplicate is dropped with a `pruned` entry.
- **Unavailable.** A card left with no enabled option (e.g. a multiclass Wizard whose only proficient Scholar skill, Religion, already has Rogue Expertise) becomes `unavailable` through `mark_unavailable` (F3a-2b), non-blocking.
- **Consequence.** Total Expertise picks are capped by the number of distinct proficient skills; later cards stay pending or become unavailable.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_expertise_2024.py`: `test_unflagged_cards_allow_a_duplicate_expertise` replaced by Rogue 1/6, Bard 2/9, Rogue 6 + Ranger 9 (both class orders), Scholar vs Rogue (both orders), level order, Skill Expert after a class card, unavailable, and a mixed/2014 guard. Edited to disjoint picks: `test_bard_9_rogue_6_has_four_cards_attached_to_their_own_xphb_rows` and `tests/test_class_2024_feature_list.py::test_expertise_metamagic_invocations_favored_enemy_attach_by_ordinal`.

## FE0 Feat effects: scan table, coverage ratchet and data guards (2026-10-11)

First slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Data and tests only: `resolve()` and every output are unchanged, the F0 golden is green and was not regenerated (`STRIP` stays empty).

- **`server/catalog/feat_effects_data.py`** (pure data, imports nothing, nothing reads it until FE1). `FEAT_EFFECT_KEYS` (the effect vocabulary of plan 4.1), `SCAN_KINDS`, `FEAT_KINDS` (index -> scan kinds, one entry per catalog feat), `FEAT_EFFECTS` (classified; FE0 lists only the 7 Fighting Style feats already done through `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS` / Blind Fighting's `senses`, as `{"data_only": True}`), `FEAT_PENDING = FEAT_KINDS - FEAT_EFFECTS` (298 indexes), `FE10_DONE = False`.
- **`server/tests/test_feat_effects_coverage.py`** (28 tests). Ratchet: every catalog feat is in exactly one of `FEAT_EFFECTS` / `FEAT_PENDING`; effect keys are catalog feats with known vocabulary; `len(FEAT_PENDING) <= PENDING_CEILING` (298; each FE slice lowers the constant, FE10 sets it to 0 and flips `FE10_DONE`). Scan table covers the catalog exactly and its per-kind counts and 16 spot checks are pinned. Data guards: V2 (all 32 Epic Boons carry `ability[].max` 30, nobody else), V3 (no Origin / FS / FS:P / FS:R feat has an ability record), B5 (Keenness of the Stone Giant is the only `bonusSenses` holder), B3 (Echoing Soul data says one skill, the text says two), B4 (Harper Agent's Thieves' Cant is dropped), V18 (the golden holds 0 feat cases, `GOLDEN_FEAT_CASES`, raised additively by FE12). The Epic Boon cap is not in the catalog body text, only in the data, so that guard reads the raw `feats.json`.
- **Scan numbers (verified).** 305 feats: numeric 19, choice 22, attack 32, resource 82 (union of those four 144; overlaps numeric/attack: Dragon Hide, Dual Wielder PHB, Revenant Blade; resource overlaps numeric/choice/attack: Keenness, Spell Resistant, Portal Jumper, Martial Adept, Metamagic Adept, Orcish Fury, Transmuted Anatomy, Strike of the Giants), notes-only 154, done Fighting Style 7.
- **Deviation.** The plan's "161 notes feats, after removing 7 done FS feats" is 154 after removing them (305 - 144 = 161 still includes the 7). The three spell-card FS feats (Arcane Warrior, Blessed Warrior, Druidic Warrior), Interception and Protection are scanned as notes; Unarmed Fighting as attack. They are not marked done.
- **Totals.** Server 2979 -> 3007 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped (no isolation allowlist change needed: `catalog` is already a server-local module).

## FE1 Feat effects: initiative, hit points, walk and climb speed (2026-10-11)

Second slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). The first behaviour change of the series. Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (entries are keyed by feat index, no `draft.ruleset` branching), so the 2014 feats now take effect under `2014` and `mixed` (user-approved exception). Kept-but-hidden picks (A2) keep their effect. The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration.

- **Data.** `FEAT_EFFECTS` gained 12 entries (`alert-phb` initiative 5, `alert-xphb` initiative "pb", `tough-phb` / `tough-xphb` hp_per_level 2, `boon-of-fortitude-xphb` hp_flat 40, `mobile-phb` / `speedy-xphb` walk 10, `boon-of-speed-xphb` walk 30, `squat-nimbleness-xge` / `mark-of-passage-efa` / `transmuted-anatomy-au` walk 5, `athlete-xphb` climb_equals_walk). `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES` pins the governing catalog sentence of each (tags stripped); a per-entry test checks it against the catalog text. `FEAT_PENDING` 298 -> 286, `PENDING_CEILING` lowered to 286.
- **`server/engine/feat_effects.py`** (pure, no catalog import): `instances`, `initiative_parts`, `hp_bonus`, `walk_bonus`, `climb_equals_walk`. Numeric effects are deduplicated by the edition-independent feat name key, so a stray duplicate never doubles Alert / Tough.
- **`derived.initiative(..., bonus=0, proficient=False)`**: `DEX + (PB if proficient else JoaT / Remarkable Athlete half bonus) + bonus`. Alert PHB (flat +5) stacks with the half bonus; Alert XPHB (add the Proficiency Bonus) replaces it. Defaults reproduce the old numbers.
- **HP.** `+2 x total character level` (Tough, retroactive: twice the level when gained plus 2 per later level) and +40 flat (Boon of Fortitude), added after Dwarven Toughness / class `hp_bonus`; applies in manual HP mode too.
- **Speed order.** species walk -> Unarmored Movement -> Fast Movement -> feat walk bonus (walk only, F1-6) -> Gift of the Depths swim -> Athlete climb = max(existing climb, final walk).
- **No new output keys**, so `types.ts`, exports and the frontend are untouched.
- **Known limit.** Mark of Passage (EFA) is a Dragonmark feat that no ASI gate or species grant in the app can offer, so its entry is only checked on the pure engine with the real catalog record.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_effects_numeric.py` (44 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); a mutation run without the resolve/derived wiring fails 24 of them. Kitchen-sink and export expectations did not move (no draft asserts an initiative that includes Alert).
- **Totals.** Server 3007 -> 3051 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped. Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms, feat-holding drafts): Fighter 20 mixed 4.0, Fighter 20 2024 2.6, Wizard 20 3.0 (< 50 ms).

## FE2 Feat effects: ability-score caps (2026-10-11)

Third slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Fixes B1, B2 and B9. The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration. No new output keys, so `types.ts`, exports and the frontend are untouched.

- **Catalog.** `catalog.feats[*]["ability_cap"]` (`_feat_ability_cap_from_5etools`): the largest `ability[].max` of the raw record, else 20. 32 feats carry 30 (every Epic Boon, Boon of Terror's fixed Charisma included); the other 273 carry 20. The cap is not in the feat body text, only in the data (and in Resilient's `entry` sentence for 20).
- **B1 (Epic Boon cap 30).** `asi.asi_exceeds_cap(deltas, scores, cap=ASI_CAP)`; `_apply_asi_progression` passes the picked feat's `ability_cap`, combos stay at 20. A Boon at STR 20 now gives 21 (up to 30). New optional out-parameter `cap_skipped_feat_cids` reports which ASI feat deltas did not fit.
- **B2 (origin half-feats).** Background and species feat deltas are clamped, feat by feat in background-then-species order, to `min(delta, max(0, cap - running))` where `running` = base + species + background bonuses plus the earlier origin feats (`asi.clamped_delta`, `origin_running` in `resolve()`). Fixed increase first, then the feat's own chosen increase. Only the feat's delta is clamped, never species / background bonuses (Custom Lineage's +2 on a 19 still gives 21). A mixed Variant Human with an Athlete (PHB) STR pick at STR 20 stays 20 (was 21): the one intended mixed/2014 change. Every feat in the catalog carries 0 or 1 increase of +1 (verified), so the ASI skip rule and the clamp agree.
- **B9 (option reasons).** `_feat_ability_bonus_choice_templates(feats, origin=None, scores=None)`: with `scores`, an option whose ability would pass the feat's cap is disabled "already at maximum (20)" / "(30)". Origin feats pass the running pre-feat scores; ASI feats pass `scores_by_level[feat level]` of their class walk. The early validity pass in `_resolve_asi_selected_feats` passes no scores (the walk clamps anyway). A stored pick of a closed option is dropped (it never applies) and now reported: the generic pass adds a `pruned` entry for feat `:ability-bonus` cards ("<label>: STR is already at maximum (30) and was ignored."). Other disabled-but-offered picks elsewhere still drop silently as before.
- **Prerequisite check.** `_feat_prerequisite_problems(..., applied_fixed_deltas_by_instance)` subtracts the fixed increase that was really applied (clamped for an origin feat; nothing for an ASI feat skipped at its cap) instead of the raw `ability_bonuses`; the chosen deltas it receives are the applied ones too. Removes the false "prerequisite unmet" for a delta that never landed. Not observable on shipped data for a fixed bonus (the lowest score at which clamping happens, 20, never straddles a real prerequisite), so it is covered by a synthetic unit test.
- **Unchanged.** The 1-30 range problem (checked on the pre-feat scores); fixed Primal Champion-style feature bonuses; FS / Lessons feats (no ability records, V3).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_ability_caps.py` (25 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); against the unmodified `resolve.py` 10 of them fail. `FEAT_PENDING` is unchanged (FE2 classifies no feat: the cap lives in catalog data), so `PENDING_CEILING` stays 286.
- **Totals.** Server 3051 -> 3076 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped. Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms, feat-holding drafts): Fighter 20 mixed 4.1, Fighter 20 2024 2.6, Wizard 20 3.0 (< 50 ms).

## FE3 Feat effects: notes, `derived.featResources`, Lucky (2026-10-11)

Fourth slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index; kept-but-hidden picks keep notes and resources). Decision F-D1 applied.

- **`derived.featResources`** (new key, always present, `[]` for feat-less drafts): the `classResources` entry shape minus `classId`, plus `featId`, with `source: {kind: "feat", id: <feat index>, label: <feat name>}`. The id is `<instance cid prefix>:<slug of name>` (`feat:lucky-xphb:luck-points`, `class:fighter:4:asi:feat:...:luck-points` for an ASI pick), so a repeatable feat never collides. Entry keys implemented: `name`, `uses` (int or `"pb"`), `recharge`, `note` (goes to `details.note`). Seven resources: Lucky PHB (3, long), Lucky XPHB (PB, long), Mage Slayer XPHB Guarded Mind, Ritual Caster XPHB Quick Ritual (1, long), Second Chance (also regained on rolling initiative, in the note), Fade Away, Orcish Fury (1, short-or-long).
- **`sheet.feats[].notes`** (only when non-empty): note templates filled with the character's numbers. `engine/feat_effects.py` gained `increased_ability`, `numbers_for`, `render`, `notes`, `resources`; `instances(..., choices)` now also carries `increased` (the single fixed increase, else the selected `<prefix>:ability-bonus` pick, else None). Templates: `{expr}` is safe integer arithmetic (`+ - * //`, `max`, `min`) over `pb`, `level`, `<abl>_mod`, `<abl>_score`, `inc_mod`; `{sum:a,b}` prints `8 + 3 = 11`. A template that needs `inc_mod` renders "(choose the ability)" until the ability is chosen. Unknown names / other syntax raise (caught by a data guard test over every template, with and without a chosen ability). Numbers are the final modifiers/scores.
- **Data.** 44 entries with notes and/or resources (all PHB / XPHB General and Origin feats scanned as notes: Actor, Athlete PHB, Chef, Crafter, Defensive Duelist, Durable, Dungeon Delver, Grappler, Healer, Heavy Armor Master, Inspiring Leader, Keen Mind PHB, Linguist, Mage Slayer, Mounted Combatant, Musician, Ritual Caster, Sentinel, Shield Master, Skulker, Spell Sniper, Telekinetic, Telepathic, War Caster; plus Lucky and the three XGE racial feats) and 15 `data_only` entries (Heavily / Lightly / Moderately Armored, Martial Weapon Training, Resilient, Skilled, Magic Initiate, Fey Touched, Shadow Touched: already applied through catalog proficiency, save, spell fields; a test checks each carries such a field). `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES` pins one or more governing sentences per entry (tuple for several) against the catalog text. `FEAT_PENDING` 286 -> 227, `PENDING_CEILING` lowered to 227. Skulker XPHB's Blindsight 10 is already a sheet sense from catalog `senses` (the note says so; no double count).
- **Not in FE3.** Orcish Fury's attack note stays with FE5 (the entry only has its resource and the Relentless Endurance note so far); Lucky's per-roll rules are plain notes. Interception / Protection (FS category), Martial Adept and Metamagic Adept resources (FE9), Epic Boons and the other books (FE10) are untouched.
- **Surfaces.** `export_fields`: `DERIVED_FIELDS` has `("featResources", [])` after `classResources`; `extra_blocks` prints an h2 "Ресурси от таланти" with `resource_line` bullets and appends ` — note; note` to the feat bullet (Markdown and PDF mirror it). `types.ts`: `ResourceCore` (shared), `ClassResource` / `FeatResource extends ResourceCore`, `Derived.featResources?`, `SheetFeat.notes?`; `ClassResourcesList` / `describeResource` accept both; `ExportPage` lists "Ресурси от таланти" and the feats with their notes (plain markup). `test_types_drift` CONTAINERS gained `derived.featResources[]`; the export sweep `_expected_strings` now includes feat resource names and notes.
- **Golden.** `test_c_series_golden.STRIP = ("derived.featResources",)` with its note (additive, user-approved, both sides stripped); nothing else changed and the golden was not regenerated. The separate G2 baseline (`test_worn_wielded.strip_g2`) also drops `derived.featResources` (same additive key). `test_feat_effects_coverage`: the FS data_only test now selects the 7 FS feats by scan kind (other data_only entries exist from FE3).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_resources.py` (100 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); with the resolve wiring removed 25 fail.
- **Totals.** Server 3076 -> 3176 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped. Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms, note/resource-holding drafts): Fighter 20 mixed 3.8, Fighter 20 2024 2.6, Wizard 20 mixed 5.3 (< 50 ms). `npm run build` green.

## FE4 Feat effects: AC, passive scores, Stealth, senses (2026-10-11)

Fifth slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index; kept-but-hidden picks keep their effect). Decision F-D3 applied. The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration. No new output keys, so `types.ts`, exports and the frontend are untouched.

- **Data.** 7 entries (`FEAT_PENDING` 227 -> 220, `PENDING_CEILING` lowered to 220), governing sentences pinned in `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES`: `dragon-hide-xge` (`ac_formula` 13 + DEX, shield allowed, plus a claws note), `medium-armor-master-phb` (`medium_armor_dex_cap` {3, DEX score 16} and `medium_armor_stealth_ok`), `medium-armor-master-xphb` (cap only: the 2024 text has no Stealth clause, V11), `dual-wielder-phb` and `revenant-blade-erlw` (`ac_bonus_if` +1 with a static note), `observant-phb` (`passive_bonus` +5 Perception and Investigation, lip-reading note), `keenness-of-the-stone-giant-bgg` (`senses_additive` darkvision 60). Observant (XPHB) stays pending for FE6 (its text has no passive bonus).
- **AC.** `equipment.armor_class_from_gear(..., medium_dex_cap=None)`: the cap replaces `max_bonus` of Medium body armor only when higher (Dexterity SCORE rule: `feat_effects.medium_armor_dex_cap(insts, final DEX score)`, so XPHB's own +1 DEX counts). Feat formulas (`feat_effects.ac_formulas`) are appended to `features_mod.ac_formulas(effects)` and compete best-of, never summed (Dragon Hide vs Unarmored Defense / Draconic Resilience / Armor of Shadows); they apply only without body armor and a shield is kept. Conditional bonuses (`feat_effects.ac_bonus(insts, gear.hands, equipped_items)`) are added to the result.
- **F-D3 conditions, proven only by `equipment.wielded`** (`gear.hands`; without it nothing is proven and the static note states the rule). Dual Wielder (PHB): main and off hand each hold a one-handed melee weapon (a two-handed weapon, a shield or an empty hand fails; the same item in both hands passes, the existing `wielded-same-item-twice` warning covers a single copy). Revenant Blade: the double-bladed scimitar is the only thing held (main or off hand, the other empty; V12: the item is Two-Handed in the catalog). Its Finesse clause is a note only (attack rows are FE5).
- **Stealth.** `equipment.armor_warnings(..., medium_armor_stealth_ok=False)`: only Medium armor is exempt (Medium Armor Master PHB); heavy armor keeps its disadvantage and the XPHB feat changes nothing.
- **Passive.** `derived.passivePerception` / `passiveInvestigation` add the +5; `derived.skills` and `passiveInsight` are untouched. Duplicates deduplicated by feat name key.
- **Senses.** `feat_effects.add_senses(senses, insts)` runs last in `sheet.senses` (after species, feats, invocations): darkvision 60 for a species without, else existing + 60 (Dwarf 120); Devil's Sight (`devilsSight`) and other senses untouched. The catalog `bonus_senses` field of the plan was not needed (the table carries the value; a test checks it against the raw `bonusSenses`).
- **Not in FE4.** Keenness's Stone Throw resource (FE10), the unarmed claws row and Finesse on the scimitar row (FE5), Dual Wielder's non-Light two-weapon fighting (FE5).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_defense.py` (53 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); with the `resolve.py` wiring reverted 16 fail. `test_feat_resources.FE3_WITH_TEXT` now excludes the 7 FE4 entries.
- **Totals.** Server 3176 -> 3229 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped (frontend untouched, no build). Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms, feat-holding drafts): Fighter 20 mixed 3.9, Fighter 20 2024 2.4, Wizard 20 mixed 3.5 (< 50 ms).

## FE5 Feat effects: attack rows (2026-10-11)

Sixth slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index; kept-but-hidden picks keep their effect). Decisions F-D2, F-D5, F-D6 applied. The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration. No new output keys, so `types.ts`, exports and the frontend are untouched (attack rows already carry `notes`, `offhandDamage`, `damage`).

- **Data.** 28 newly classified feats (`FEAT_PENDING` 220 -> 192, `PENDING_CEILING` lowered to 192); with Dragon Hide, Dual Wielder PHB, Revenant Blade and Orcish Fury (classified earlier) all 32 attack feats of the scan have an entry. `attack_notes` entries are `{when, text}`; `when` is a predicate or a list of predicates (any matches) over the row: `melee`, `ranged`, `weapon`, `properties {all, any}`, `lacks`, `damage_types`, `items`, `ability_in`, `proficient`, `damaging`. Text placeholders: `{pb}`, `{inc_mod}`, `{inc_score}`, `{mod}`, `{die}`, `{dtype}`, `{dmg:1d4}` and the FE3 `{expr}` arithmetic. Governing sentences pinned in `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES` (tuples where an entry merges FE4 quotes).
- **F-D2.** Optional or conditional riders never touch `damage`: Savage Attacker (PHB melee weapon rows, XPHB every weapon row), Great Weapon Master (PHB -5/+10 on proficient Heavy melee; XPHB "+PB damage" on any Heavy weapon row, ranged Heavy included, plus Hew on melee), Sharpshooter (PHB -5/+10 on proficient ranged; XPHB no number), Charger (+5 / 1d8), Crossbow Expert (loading, melee range, hand crossbow bonus attack), Piercer / Slasher / Crusher by the row's damage type (the Unarmed Strike and Dragon Hide claws included), Polearm Master (PHB glaive, halberd, quarterstaff, spear; opportunity attack also pike; XPHB quarterstaff, spear and Heavy+Reach melee; butt-end damage `1d4 + the row's modifier`), Gunner, Elven Accuracy (rows whose ability is DEX, INT, WIS or CHA), Orcish Fury (names the extra die), Poisoner (DC 14, or 8 + increased ability modifier + PB, "(choose the ability)" until picked), Bomber (Thrown rows), Boon of Irresistible Offense (ignore resistance; extra damage = the increased score).
- **Numbers on rows.** `attacks.weapon_attack` reads style flags merged from `feat_effects.attack_flags`: `offhand_non_light` (Dual Wielder PHB) and `offhand_dw2024` (XPHB) give `offhandDamage` to melee weapons without Two-Handed that are not Light (dice + min(mod, 0); with the Two-Weapon Fighting style, + mod: F-D5); `offhand_light_crossbow_mod` (Crossbow Expert XPHB) gives the Light hand crossbow `offhandDamage` with the modifier (the Light property now also allows a ranged extra attack, but only for this feat; the 2024 Light property without it is the pre-existing melee-only row); `finesse_items` (Revenant Blade) makes the double-bladed scimitar use the better of STR/DEX. Row `ability` therefore changes only through that flag.
- **F-D6 Unarmed Strike.** `feat_effects.unarmed_spec` returns `{die, type, notes}`; `attacks.unarmed_strike(modifiers, pb, martial_arts_die, feat=None, monk=True)` builds the row. Non-Monk: Strength only, always proficient, bludgeoning unless the feat says otherwise (Dragon Hide: slashing). Tavern Brawler PHB/XPHB 1d4, Dragon Hide 1d4, Unarmed Fighting 1d6 (1d8 when `equipment.wielded` proves both hands hold neither a weapon nor a Shield; a held torch does not count; unknown hands keep the d6 and a note). Monk: one row; the best die wins, Martial Arts first on a tie, the others listed as "Alternative: ..." notes; the Monk keeps the better of STR/DEX for the strike. Without such a feat the row is byte-identical to R2.
- **Order in `resolve()`.** gear attacks -> unarmed row (Monk or feat) -> class annotations (Rage, Sneak Attack, Divine Strike) -> pact notes -> `feat_effects.annotate_attacks` -> `annotate_mastery`. `feat_numbers` moved up so the notes can use it.
- **Gunner (side fix).** Gunner's `firearms` proficiency id fell into the tools bucket; `reference_data.FEAT_WEAPON_PROFICIENCY_IDS` now files it under weapons and `attacks.weapon_is_proficient` maps it to the DMG firearms (`musket`, `pistol`; a test pins that these are the only DMG ranged weapons), so a Gunner's pistol row is proficient. Only feat holders are affected.
- **Not in FE5 / known limits.** Crossbow Expert PHB's hand crossbow bonus action attack is a note, not an `offhandDamage` (its text does not say whether the modifier is added). Revenant Blade's Finesse is not seen by the Sneak Attack note of `class_resources.annotate_attacks` (it reads item properties). A feat held with no matching weapon row shows nothing (the rules are on the rows). Weapon Master, Tavern Brawler's improvised-weapon proficiency beyond the catalog id, and Poisoner's resistance rule for poison-damage rows are untouched. **Superseded by FE12: Crossbow Expert PHB's hand crossbow bonus attack now has `offhandDamage` with the ability modifier (official Sage Advice: the no-modifier clause belongs to two-weapon fighting only); same flag pattern as the XPHB variant (`offhand_hand_crossbow_mod`).**
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_attacks.py` (120 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); with the three resolve wirings removed 37 fail. No kitchen-sink, export or types expectation moved (no existing draft asserts attack-row notes for these feats).
- **Totals.** Server 3229 -> 3349 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped (frontend untouched, no build). Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms, feat-holding drafts): Fighter 20 mixed 4.6, Fighter 20 2024 3.0, Wizard 20 mixed 4.1 (< 50 ms).

## FE6 Feat effects: skills and Expertise (2026-10-12)

Seventh slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index). Decision F-D4 applied (position-aware, like F3a and Primal Knowledge). The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration. No new output keys, so `types.ts`, exports and the frontend are untouched.

- **Data.** 8 newly classified feats (`FEAT_PENDING` 192 -> 184, `PENDING_CEILING` lowered to 184): `keen-mind-xphb`, `observant-xphb` (`prof_or_expertise`), `skill-expert-tce`, `prodigy-xge`, `skill-expert-xphb`, `boon-of-skill-xphb` (`lacks_expertise`), `echoing-soul-rhw`, `harper-agent-frhof` (`data_only`, carried by catalog fields). Governing sentences pinned in `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES`.
- **B3.** `reference_data.FEAT_SKILL_PICK_COUNT_OVERRIDES = {"echoing-soul-rhw": 2}`: the feat text says two skills, the 5etools data one; `official/feats.py` rewrites the count of the `any_skill` envelope. The Expertise pick (one skill, changeable on a Long Rest: F-D7, the card stays editable) is unchanged.
- **B4.** `reference_data.FEAT_FIXED_LANGUAGE_OVERRIDES = {"harper-agent-frhof": ["thieves-cant"]}` adds the fixed language (the id of the C6b-2 rare-language table) to `catalog.feats[...]["languages"]`. It reaches `sheet.proficiencies.languages` and the C6b-2 language gate through the existing origin-feat path: Deft Explorer's card shows `thieves-cant` disabled "already known" and a stored pick is pruned.
- **B6 (proficiency or Expertise).** `_feat_choice_templates` marks the feat's skill card `profOrExpertise` (stripped from the output like `lacksExpertise`). `resolve._prof_or_expertise_priors` computes, per card, the skills the character is already proficient in at the card's position: fixed origin grants (background, species, origin feats), cards at or before (class order, class level) (an ASI feat after the class feature of its level), fixed skills of earlier ASI feats, and earlier flagged cards. A pick in that set is Expertise (added to `sheet.expertiseSkills`, still listed once as a proficiency); otherwise it is a plain proficiency. A later grant never converts the pick. `class_choices_2024.gate_after_primal_knowledge` skips flagged cards (they convert instead of being disabled "already proficient"). Under 2024 the conversion is also gated by "already has Expertise" through `_gate_expertise_choices(..., poe_priors)`: the flagged card takes part in the Expertise walk at its position (option disabled, stored pick dropped with a `pruned` entry, its Expertise blocks a later card). Outside 2024 there is no such gate (the feat text has no "lack Expertise" clause; the sheet lists the skill once).
- **B7.** `lacks_expertise` feats (Skill Expert TCE / XPHB, Prodigy, Boon of Skill) set the template flag `lacksExpertise` on their Expertise card under every ruleset (the gate already honoured the flag). The SRD Rogue / Bard cards stay unflagged under mixed / 2014 (control test).
- **Known limits.** Fixed skills of Lessons-of-the-First-Ones feats are counted with the origin grants (`fixed_origin_skills`, as for Primal Knowledge); no shipped Origin feat grants a fixed skill, so nothing changes. Invocation skills (Beguiling Influence) are not in the prior set (they overlap neither feat's skill list).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_skill_choices.py` (35 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); with the `resolve.py` / `class_choices_2024.py` wiring reverted 13 fail. `test_feat_effects_coverage` B3/B4 guards now assert the fixed catalog data.
- **Edited expectations (intended).** `test_f3a_fixes::test_f3_skill_expert_lacks_expertise_under_every_ruleset` (was "only under 2024": the mixed case now blocks the duplicate, B7), `test_official_source` Echoing Soul count 2 and Harper Agent Thieves' Cant, `test_feat_defense` Observant no longer pending. The golden was not touched.
- **Totals.** Server 3349 -> 3384 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped (frontend untouched, no build). Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms): Fighter 20 mixed 2.9, Fighter 20 2024 2.5, Wizard 20 mixed 2.9, Wizard 8 with Keen Mind 2024 1.6 (< 50 ms).

## FE7 Feat effects: resistance, damage-type and variant picks (2026-10-12)

Eighth slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index). Decision F-D7 applied (choices the text lets you change on a rest stay editable build-time cards). The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration. No new output keys, so `types.ts`, exports and the frontend are untouched (picks show through `sheet.feats[].choice`, `damageModifiers.resistances`, feat notes and `derived.featResources`).
