- **Data.** 8 newly classified feats (`FEAT_PENDING` 184 -> 176, `PENDING_CEILING` lowered to 176): `dragonscarred-frhof` (`resist_choice` 1 of 5), `boon-of-energy-resistance-xphb` (2 of 9, Energy Redirection DC note), `portal-jumper-au` (1 of 3 + Portal Step resource), `spell-resistant-au` (1 of 4 + Magic Resistant resource), `scion-of-the-outer-planes-sato` (`resist_by_variant`, no extra card), `elemental-adept-phb` / `-xphb` (`damage_type_choice`, distinct across repeats), `strike-of-the-giants-bgg` (new vocabulary key `variant_choice`: six strikes, note per type, resource). Governing sentences pinned in `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES`; a test checks Scion's map against the feat's own table. Note placeholder `{choice}` = the instance's pick ("(not chosen yet)" before).
- **Cards.** `resolve._feat_extra_templates` (called from `_feat_choice_templates`, which all five sites already call: background, species, Fighting Style, Lessons, ASI): `:resistance-choice`, `:damage-type`, `:giant-type`, under the same cid prefix as the feat's other cards. Scion reads the existing `:spell-variant` pick.
- **Resistances.** `feat_effects.instances` now carries `resist` and `pick_text`; `feat_effects.resistances` feeds `chosen_resistances` (set union with species / Rage resistances, no duplicates). Elemental Adept grants none (it ignores the target's).
- **Distinct rule.** `resolve._gate_distinct_choices` (internal flag `distinctGroup`, stripped from output): origin cards first, then ASI cards by (class order, level); the later instance's option is disabled "already chosen for Elemental Adept (n)", a stored duplicate is dropped with a `pruned` entry. PHB and XPHB share the group.
- **Pending cards** block export through the generic `_pending_export_problems`.
- **Limits.** The shipped FE7 feats cannot reach the background, Fighting Style and Lessons sites (no background grants one; none is FS category; Lessons offers only XPHB-era Origin feats), so the matrix test patches the effect table of a feat each site does grant. Portal Jumper (AU, Origin) is reached through a species feat grant. Strike of the Giants / Scion prerequisites are not enforced unless the prerequisite preference is on (unchanged engine behaviour).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_resist_choices.py` (44 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); with the wiring removed 25 fail. Edited expectations: `test_feat_resources` (resource count 10, FE7 entries excluded from the FE3 text list), `test_feat_effects_coverage` ceiling.
- **Totals.** Server 3384 -> 3428 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped (frontend untouched, no build). Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms): Fighter 20 mixed 4.1, Fighter 20 2024 2.7, Wizard 20 mixed 3.7 (< 50 ms).

## FE8 Feat effects: weapon picks (2026-10-12)

Ninth slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index). Decision F-D7 applied (Weapon Master XPHB's mastery, changeable on a Long Rest, is an editable build-time card). The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration. No new output keys, so `types.ts`, exports and the frontend are untouched (picks show through `sheet.feats[].choice`, `sheet.proficiencies.weapons`, `sheet.weaponMasteries` and the attack rows).

- **Data.** 2 newly classified feats (`FEAT_PENDING` 176 -> 174, `PENDING_CEILING` lowered to 174): `weapon-master-phb` (`weapon_proficiency_choice` {count 4, simple and martial}) and `weapon-master-xphb` (`mastery_choice` {count 1, simple and martial}). Governing sentences pinned in `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES`. Both vocabulary keys existed since FE0; `categories` is now used by the card builder.
- **Cards.** `resolve._feat_extra_templates` gained an `items` argument (`catalog.items`, passed by the five `_feat_choice_templates` sites): `:weapon-choice` offers every Simple / Martial catalog weapon (42, none disabled: RAW does not forbid a weapon already proficient), `:weapon-mastery` the weapons that have a mastery property (labelled "Dagger (Nick)").
- **PHB proficiencies (B8).** Picks are added to `proficient_weapons` as `reference_data.weapon_proficiency_id(item)` right after the other proficiency merges and before `_gate_mastery_choices`, so attack rows are proficient, `sheet.proficiencies.weapons` lists the id (never the tools bucket: not routed through `_categorize_proficiency`) and a class Weapon Mastery card (2024 Rogue picking a Longsword) sees them.
- **XPHB mastery (B8).** `_is_mastery_choice` now covers class and feat cards with the `:weapon-mastery` suffix (count-variable handling, no generic truncation). `_gate_mastery_choices` walks class cards first, then the feat card: a non-proficient weapon is disabled "not proficient"; a weapon a class card already holds is disabled "already mastered" and a stored duplicate is dropped with a `pruned` entry. `_weapon_masteries` includes the feat card, so `annotate_mastery` marks the row. The class gate's wording differs only in the "not an available weapon for this feat" prune text.
- **Limits.** Mastery works under every ruleset for the feat holder (class mastery cards stay 2024-only). Two class cards of a multiclass character are still not deduped against each other (unchanged). A class pick made after the feat's pick wins the weapon (the feat card is the one disabled).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_weapon_picks.py` (31 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); with the `resolve.py` wiring removed 24 fail. Edited expectations: `test_feat_resources` (FE8 entries excluded from the FE3 text list), `test_feat_effects_coverage` ceiling.
- **Totals.** Server 3428 -> 3459 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped (frontend untouched, no build). Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms): Fighter 20 mixed 2.9, Fighter 20 2024 1.9, Wizard 20 mixed 3.0 (< 50 ms).

## FE9 Feat effects: option-pick feats (2026-10-12)

Tenth slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index; kept-but-hidden picks keep their effect). Decision F-D7 applied (the picks the texts let you change on a level or rest stay editable build-time cards). The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration. No new output keys, so `types.ts`, exports and the frontend are untouched (picks show through `sheet.feats[].choice`, `derived.featResources`, `sheet.innateSpells`, AC and the attack rows).

- **Data.** 4 newly classified feats (`FEAT_PENDING` 174 -> 170, `PENDING_CEILING` lowered to 170): `fighting-initiate-tce`, `eldritch-adept-tce`, `martial-adept-phb`, `metamagic-adept-tce`, all with `option_picks {kind, count}` (kinds fighting-style, invocation, maneuver, metamagic). The resource vocabulary gained `die` and `dc` (an expression over `pb` / `<abl>_mod`, evaluated by the FE3 safe evaluator). Governing sentences pinned in `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES`.
- **Cards.** `resolve._feat_extra_templates` (all five feat sites; it now also receives `catalog`): `:fighting-style` (the 6 SRD fighter styles), `:maneuvers` (the 23 options of the PHB Battle Master list, PHB + TCE; `_optional_feature_options` was extracted from `_optional_progression_template`), `:metamagic` (the 8 SRD options), `:invocation` (the 32 SRD invocations) plus `:invocation-ability` (INT / WIS / CHA, the text says "choose when you select this feat").
- **Effects.** Fighting Initiate: the picked id joins `_class_feature_list` (feat cards with the `:fighting-style` suffix), so `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS` applies Defense (+1 AC in armor), Archery, Dueling, ... Eldritch Adept: `features.invocation_effects` also reads the feat's card, so Armor of Shadows (AC formula and Mage Armor), Devil's Sight and Beguiling Influence work; the innate spell uses the chosen ability and the source label "Eldritch Adept"; `class_resources._warlock` also lists the resource of a Warlock's Eldritch Adept pick. Martial Adept: feat resource "Superiority Die (Martial Adept)" (1 d6, short-or-long, DC 8 + PB + better of STR / DEX, note lists the maneuvers), separate from the Battle Master dice. Metamagic Adept: feat resource "Sorcery Points (Metamagic Adept)" (2, long rest, Metamagic only), separate from the Sorcerer's pool. `{choice}` in a note is the picked labels (`feat_effects._picks`).
- **Rules.** `resolve._gate_feat_invocation_prereqs`: an invocation with any prerequisite is open only to a Warlock who meets it (level, Pact Boon or a 2024 Pact invocation, Eldritch Blast); a non-Warlock has level 0, so only the 11 prerequisite-free ones are open; a stored pick that fails is dropped with a `pruned` entry. `resolve._gate_duplicate_picks`: one option cannot be held twice across the families fighting styles (SRD class cards, 2024 Fighting Style feat cards, Fighting Initiate), maneuvers (Battle Master, Martial Adept), Metamagic (Sorcerer SRD / 2024, Metamagic Adept) and invocations (Warlock, Eldritch Adept). Cards are walked in build order (origin feats, then (class index, level), a class card before the ASI of its level); the later holder is the duplicate (option disabled "already taken (Dueling, SRD)", a stored duplicate dropped with a `pruned` entry); options compare by name, so the 2014 and 2024 editions of one option are the same. The walk runs only for families in which a feat card takes part, and a class card is checked only against feat holders, so a character without these feats is byte-identical (the golden would otherwise have caught Sorcerer drafts that repeat a Metamagic option across their own cards).
- **Limits.** The TCE-only fighting styles (Blind Fighting, Interception, Thrown, Unarmed, Superior Technique), Seeking / Transmuted Spell and any XPHB invocation are not in the pools (not imported; Eldritch Adept offers the SRD list under every ruleset). A 2024 Fighting Style feat card is only a holder: picked after a Fighting Initiate style it does not see it (the feat is already resolved). Eldritch Adept's Agonizing Blast / Thirsting Blade style notes for a Warlock come through the generic class resource entry (no per-instance target cantrip card). Dual-source position uses the (class index, level) convention of F1.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_option_picks.py` (51 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); with the `resolve.py` / `features.py` / `feat_effects.py` / `class_resources.py` wiring reverted 39 fail. Edited expectations: `test_feat_resources` (FE9 entries excluded from the FE3 text list; resource keys `die` / `dc`; 12 resources), `test_feat_effects_coverage` ceiling.
- **Totals.** Server 3459 -> 3510 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped (frontend untouched, no build). Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms): Wizard 20 mixed with all four feats 4.0, Wizard 20 2024 with all four 3.4, Fighter 20 mixed 2.9, Fighter 20 2024 1.9, Wizard 20 mixed 3.0 (< 50 ms).

## FE10 Feat effects: resource and note tail, `FEAT_PENDING` emptied (2026-10-12)

Eleventh slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Every one of the 305 catalog feats now has an entry in `FEAT_EFFECTS`; `FEAT_PENDING` is empty, `PENDING_CEILING = 0`, `FE10_DONE = True`, and the ratchet test asserts `FEAT_PENDING == frozenset()` and `set(FEAT_EFFECTS) == set(catalog.feats)`. Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index; kept-but-hidden picks keep their entries). Decision F-D8 applied (FE11 skipped): the eight Arcane Adept feats of Unearthed Arcana (slot-keyed spell tables), Potent Dragonmark's extra slot, Rune Shaper, Boon of Magic School Mastery and Magic Connoisseur are notes only. The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration (`STRIP` unchanged). No new output keys, so `types.ts`, the exports and the frontend are untouched (no build needed): the notes and resources use the FE3 surfaces (`sheet.feats[].notes`, `derived.featResources`, `die` from FE9).

- **Data.** New `server/catalog/feat_effects_tail.py` (pure data; `feat_effects_data` merges `TAIL_EFFECTS` / `TAIL_QUOTES` at its end). 170 feats classified: 67 with resources (74 resources, `uses` an int, `"pb"` or the new `"inc_mod"`; recharge long, short-or-long), 9 `data_only` (everything is a catalog field: Drow High Magic, Fey Teleportation, Fey Touched (TCE), Shadow Touched (TCE), Initiate of High Sorcery, Outlands Envoy, Strixhaven Initiate, Svirfneblin Magic, Wood Elf Magic; a test checks each carries spell or language fields) and 94 notes only (spell grants are listed with the non-spell benefits; tables are summarised). Notes paraphrase the catalog text with the character's numbers (DCs such as `{8+pb+inc_mod}` and `{13+pb}`, Temporary Hit Points, `{2*level}`, `{pb//2}` runes, Potent Dragonmark's `{min(5, (level+1)//2)}` slot level, Chef's `{4+pb}`); the governing sentence(s) of every entry are pinned in `TAIL_QUOTES` against the catalog text.
- **Completion of earlier entries.** `COMPLETION` appends the text FE1-FE6 left out of 13 entries they had classified only for their number: Alert (both editions), Athlete (XPHB), Boon of Fortitude (the extra Constitution-modifier healing), Boon of Speed (Escape Artist), Keen Mind and Observant (XPHB bonus actions), Keenness of the Stone Giant (Stone Throw resource, PB uses, long rest), Mark of Passage, Mobile, Speedy, Squat Nimbleness and Transmuted Anatomy (Resilient Anatomy resource). The first quote of each stays in `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES` (a string, as FE1 tests expect); the added text is pinned in `FEAT_EFFECT_EXTRA_QUOTES`.
- **Engine.** `engine/feat_effects.resources`: `uses: "inc_mod"` (Spell Subterfuge: uses equal to the modifier of the ability the feat increased; a negative modifier gives 0; before the ability is chosen the resource shows 0 uses and its note ends with "(choose the ability)"). Nothing else in the engine changed.
- **Already carried by catalog fields (verified, no entry needed beyond a note).** Resistances, immunities, condition immunities, senses, Expertise, languages and spells reach the sheet for Boon of Terror (Frightened immunity, Intimidation Expertise), Boon of Truesight, Aberrant Anatomy (Blindsight 15, Perception Expertise), Infernal Constitution, Boon of Poison Mastery, Boon of Blazing Dawn, Soul Drinker, Furious Storm, Ember of the Fire Giant, Fury of the Frost Giant, Mark of Storm, Cult of the Dragon Initiate (Draconic) and Fey Teleportation (Sylvan); a test pins each. Conditional resistances (Soul of the Storm Giant's aura, Boon of Desperate Resilience while Bloodied, Boon of the Night Spirit) are notes, not permanent resistances.
- **Choices that last one rest (F-D7).** Planar Adaptation (Planar Wanderer) and Versatile Merc (Zhentarim Tactics) are notes only.
- **Limits.** Cult of the Dragon Initiate: if you already know Draconic you learn another language; the app lists Draconic only, the note says to add the replacement by hand. Ability Score Improvement (XPHB) as a feat is excluded from every ASI pool by design (the class level offers the ability combos), so its entry is a note. The Arcane Adept tables are listed in the note, not resolved against the character's highest spell slot. Several of the 23 feats that no ASI pool offers (Dragonmark, Dark Gift and Fighting Style Paladin / Ranger feats) are reached through the same patched-catalog pattern as FE7 in the matrix test; their grant path in the app is unchanged.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_tail.py` (399 tests, rule matrix in its docstring): every feat classified, every scan-kind `resource` feat has a resource (two documented spell-table exceptions), quote pins for every tail entry and for all entries together, vocabulary guard, a parametrized resolve over all 69 resource-bearing feats at Proficiency Bonus 2 (Fighter 4) and 4 (Fighter 9), the `inc_mod` rule, a numbers matrix for notes (DCs, Temporary Hit Points, level, Proficiency Bonus), catalog-carried effects reaching the sheet, Markdown / PDF / YAML surfaces, no-feat identity, empty catalog and timing; with the data merge removed the module cannot be imported, with the `inc_mod` engine change reverted 3 tests fail. Edited expectations: `test_feat_effects_coverage` (`PENDING_CEILING` 0, empty-set assertion, every feat has an entry), `test_feat_resources` and `test_feat_effects_numeric` (the FE3 / FE1 data checks exclude the FE10 additions to their entries).
- **Totals.** Server 3510 -> 3910 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped. Timing `resolve()` (mean of 20, ms): Fighter 20 mixed with five resource feats 5.6, Fighter 20 2024 with five 3.7, Wizard 20 mixed with five 5.8, Wizard 20 2024 with five 5.0 (< 50 ms).

## Feat effects (FE) overview (2026-10-12)

Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`. Every feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index; kept-but-hidden picks keep their effect). FE11 (spell-grant tail) was skipped by decision F-D8.

| Slice | What | Commit |
|---|---|---|
| FE0 | scan table, coverage ratchet, data guards | `49e8f1e` |
| FE1 | initiative, hit points, walk and climb speed | `5ecb3de` |
| FE2 | ability-score caps (Epic Boon 30, origin half-feats clamped) | `28c0d07` |
| FE3 | feat notes, `derived.featResources`, Lucky | `e446b84` |
| FE4 | AC, passive scores, Stealth, senses | `1eaeb4f` |
| FE5 | attack rows (off-hand, Finesse, Unarmed Strike, row notes) | `c3a7c5d` |
| FE6 | skills and Expertise | `a4becc2` |
| FE7 | resistance, damage-type and variant picks | `609c01e` |
| FE8 | Weapon Master picks | `ddbb593` |
| FE9 | option-pick feats (Fighting Initiate, Eldritch Adept, Martial Adept, Metamagic Adept) | `d9da9c5` |
| FE10 | resource and note tail, `FEAT_PENDING` emptied | `48b96f1` |
| FE11 | spell-grant tail | skipped (F-D8) |
| FE12 | consolidation, F-D9, Crossbow Expert PHB | `b541792` (+ docs commit) |

**Counts (305 catalog feats, recomputed in FX from `FEAT_EFFECTS`; the FE12 figures 119 / 33 / 153 mixed the categories and are superseded).** Method: an entry is `data_only` when it says so (33: everything it does is already a catalog field: 7 Fighting Style feats, 15 FE3 proficiency / save / spell feats, 9 FE10 spell / language feats, others); otherwise it is *modelled* when it has any key besides `notes` (`resources`, `attack_notes`, a number, a choice, an attack flag; `modifies` alone does not count); the rest are *notes only*. Result: **142 modelled** (60 with a numeric, choice or attack effect only, 74 with resources only, 8 with both; `attack_notes` feats count as modelled: 28 feats, 23 of them with nothing else), **33 `data_only`**, **130 notes only**; 142 + 33 + 130 = 305. Resources: 82 feats (Potent Dragonmark joined in FX6); numeric / choice / attack: 68 feats. 13 entries carry `modifies` (FX3). `FEAT_PENDING` is empty. Suite growth: server 2979 -> 3945 (FE12) -> 3984 (FX), root 405 / 8 skipped.

## FE12 Feat effects: consolidation (2026-10-12)

The F0 golden changed only additively (below). No new output keys; `types.ts` and the frontend are untouched.

- **F-D9 (2024 only; refined in FX4).** XPHB Jack of All Trades: "any ability check you make that uses a skill proficiency you lack and that doesn't otherwise use your Proficiency Bonus" (catalog `xphb-bard-2-jack-of-all-trades`). Under ruleset `2024` `resolve()` adds no Jack of All Trades half bonus to Initiative (the half bonus still applies to non-proficient skill totals and the passives built on them: skill checks only). **Remarkable Athlete is left as it was in every ruleset.** The FE12 version also dropped it under 2024, which was wrong: the XPHB Champion's Remarkable Athlete (`champion-xphb-3-remarkable-athlete`: "you have Advantage on Initiative rolls and Strength (Athletics) checks") never had the half bonus and is not mapped, while the SRD / 2014 `champion` subclass's own `remarkable-athlete` effect (half PB rounded up on STR / DEX / CON checks and Initiative) is that record's own text and now stays. Mixed and 2014 are unchanged. Alert XPHB still replaces the half bonus (FE1). Implemented in `resolve()` with `initiative_jack`; tests `tests/test_half_bonus_2024.py` (8).
- **Crossbow Expert PHB.** Official Sage Advice: the ability modifier is added to the damage of the hand crossbow bonus attack (the "no modifier unless negative" rule is two-weapon fighting's). New flag `offhand_hand_crossbow_mod` (entry `offhand.hand_crossbow_mod`); the hand crossbow row gets `offhandDamage` with the modifier (negative modifiers included), and the row note says so. Tests in `test_feat_attacks.py`; the FE5 limit above is superseded.
- **Kitchen sink.** Four feat-holding drafts in `kitchen_sink.py`: `feats-mixed-fighter14` (Alert, Tough, Great Weapon Master, Lucky, Observant, PHB), `feats-2014-rogue16` (Keen Mind, Weapon Master, Resilient, Elemental Adept x2), `feats-mixed-wizard8` (Martial Adept, Eldritch Adept), `feats-2024-fighter19` (Alert from Criminal, Tough, Great Weapon Master, Lucky, Observant, Keen Mind, Weapon Master, Boon of Fortitude). `test_export_fields._EXPECT` pins feats, resources, initiative, notes and attack notes; `_expected_strings` now also requires every attack-row note and off-hand damage in Markdown and PDF (YAML is checked by the existing key / equality tests).
- **Types drift.** `tests/test_feat_consolidation.py` resolves a Fighter 19 holding each of the 305 feats (patched-catalog route for the feats no ASI pool offers) with weapons on the sheet and runs the `test_types_drift` check on every container; `UNTYPED_ALLOWLIST` stays empty.
- **Golden (decision: add feat cases).** `test_c_series_golden.py --append` (new mode) added exactly three cases and asserted the 228 existing ones unchanged (stored filled draft and stripped result equal a fresh resolve; every entry written back identical): `ks/src-all/feats-mixed-fighter14`, `ks/src-all/feats-2014-rogue16`, `ks/src-all/feats-mixed-wizard8` (feat drafts run under `sources` [] only, since a `srd` filter would hide the picks). The 2024 draft is not in the golden. `GOLDEN_FEAT_CASES = 3`. A future change to a mixed / 2014 feat effect now fails these cases and needs `--append` (new cases) or a documented STRIP / regeneration decision.
- **Isolation.** `tests/test_chargen_isolation.py` allowlists the server-local test modules `test_feat_tail` and `test_types_drift`.
- **Timing** `resolve()` (mean of 20, ms, 5 resource feats, level 20): mixed Fighter 5.6, Monk 3.5, Wizard 5.6, Warlock 4.8, Bard 5.1, Sorcerer 5.2; 2024 Fighter 3.4, Monk 2.7, Wizard 5.0, Warlock 4.7, Bard 16.4, Sorcerer 4.3; Warlock 20 with 10 invocations 4.1 (all < 50 ms).
- **375 px check** (Vite dev server + Flask, drafts via `localStorage["dnd-builder-draft-v2"]`, mixed Fighter 14 with five feats and 2024 Fighter 19 with eight): `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` was 375 on ExportPage (feat bullets with notes, "Ресурси от таланти", attack notes) and ClassPage (all ASI feat cards, the level-4 picker open with 131 options); no element past the viewport outside the nav strip. No frontend change.
- **Totals.** Server 3910 -> 3945 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped; `npm run build` green.


## FX Review fixes for the feat-effects series (2026-10-16)

Batch of fixes from a read-only review of FE0-FE12. Golden `test_c_series_golden.py` untouched and green (no regeneration); mixed / 2014 output changes only where noted (feat effects of feats a character holds).

- **FX1 Performance** (`engine/resolve.py`). `_source_allowed` no longer rebuilds the lowered source set per call (`_lowered_sources`, `lru_cache` keyed on the `sources` tuple, unhashable junk falls back to the old path). `_spell_filter_options` took the spell visibility test (`_spell_visible`, ~100k calls per 2024 Bard 20 resolve) six times per resolve; `_visible_spell_ids` now computes the visible id set once per (spells dict identity and size, sources, ruleset) and the filter reads it (`keep` ids still bypass it; output identical, golden green). Timing, `resolve()` mean of 20, plain Bard 20 2024: `sources []` 5.04 ms -> 4.05 ms, `sources [PHB, XPHB, TCE, XGE]` 7.16 ms -> 3.28 ms (mixed unchanged at 2.4-3.0 ms). The FE12 figure of 16.4 ms for a feat-heavy 2024 Bard 20 came from the same two hotspots. Tests `test_fx_perf_caches.py`.
- **FX2 Poisoner (TCE) note.** The attack note said the creature "fails" the DC 14 Constitution save; the text says it "must succeed on a 14 Constitution saving throw or take 2d8 poison damage and become poisoned". Note reworded; `test_feat_attacks.py` pins it.
- **FX3 Cross-feat modifications.** New optional entry field `modifies` (`FEAT_EFFECT_KEYS`): `{target feat index: {"replace": [[old, new]], "recharge": {resource name: recharge}}}`, applied by `feat_effects.apply_modifies` only while the target feat is also held (notes and resources both go through it). Greater Aberrant Mark (EFA) patches Aberrant Fortitude to 1d6 and "Short Rest or Long Rest" (catalog: "you can roll 1d6 instead of 1d4. You also now regain your use of Aberrant Fortitude whenever you finish a Short Rest or Long Rest"); each of the 12 Greater Marks (EFA) turns the base mark's Intuition note from 1d4 to 1d6 ("you can roll 1d6 instead of 1d4"). The ERLW Aberrant Dragonmark is a different feat and is not patched. The remaining cross-feat texts are listed as open question 21. Tests `test_feat_modifies.py`.
- **FX4 F-D9 refinement.** See the corrected bullet in FE12: only the 2024 Jack of All Trades Initiative bonus is dropped; Remarkable Athlete is untouched in every ruleset.
- **FX5 Frontend keys.** `ExportPage.tsx` feat list used `key={f.id}`, a duplicate for repeatable feats (Elemental Adept, Magic Initiate, Skilled). Key is now `id:origin:level:index`. No other list in `frontend/src` is keyed by a feat id (`ClassResourcesList` keys resources by their instance-scoped `id`, unique). `npm run build` green.
- **FX6 Potent Dragonmark.** The extra spell slot is now the feat resource "Dragonmark Spell Slot" (1 use, Short or Long Rest), its note computes the slot level. Catalog text: "The spell slot's level is half your level (round up), to a maximum of level 5. You regain the expended slot when you finish a Short Rest or Long Rest." Level = total character level, `min(5, (level+1)//2)`; the spell slot table is not changed. Feat resources 81 -> 82 (the overview counts are recomputed above).
- **FX7 Silent clamp.** A fixed +1 (Keen Mind PHB at INT 20, Actor XPHB at CHA 20, via an ASI slot or an origin feat) lost to the cap is now reported as a non-blocking `pruned` entry ("<Feat>: INT is already at maximum (20) and the increase was ignored"; `choiceId` = the ASI card id, or `feat:<index>` / the species origin for an origin feat). The score and the problems list are unchanged.
- **FX8 Docs.** FE overview counts recomputed and the method stated; the G1-2 Elemental Adept line marked "Superseded (FE7)"; the cap-20 / uncapped background-feat statements marked "Superseded (FE2)"; F-D9 corrected; open questions 18-22 added.


## RP1 (X1) 2024 background ability increases capped at 20 (2026-10-22)

Golden `test_c_series_golden.py` untouched and green (not regenerated). Output changes only under ruleset `2024`; mixed and 2014 are unchanged.

- **Rule.** PHB 2024: "None of these increases can raise a score above 20." In `engine/resolve.py`, right after the background choice bundle is applied (species increases are off under 2024, so the step sits on the base scores), each ability's background delta is clamped with `asi.clamped_delta(..., ASI_CAP)`. The lost part is a non-blocking `pruned` entry `{choiceId: background:<id>:ability-bonus, reason: "<Background>: STR is already at maximum (20) and the increase was ignored."}`, appended before the FX7 notes. It is never a problem.
- **Order.** The clamp runs before origin feats (FE2 clamp), species feats and ASI progression, which therefore read the clamped score and keep their own caps (20, or 30 for Epic Boons). The background card's option list is not disabled (a bundle such as `str-2+dex-1` stays selectable; only the over-cap part is dropped).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_background_cap_2024.py` (8): 20+2, 19+2, 18+2, +1/+1/+1 with one at 20, two scores over, a later-book background (carouser-abh), mixed / 2014 guards, ASI after the clamp. Seven of them fail without the fix.
- **Timing** `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms, level 20): mixed Fighter 4.0, Wizard 3.7, Bard 3.7; 2024 Fighter 2.6, Wizard 2.8, Bard 3.9.
- **Totals.** Server 3984 -> 3992 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP2 (B2) 2024 fixed numeric effects of XPHB class and subclass features (2026-10-22)

- **Table.** `reference_data.CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS_2024`, keyed by the imported XPHB feature record id. Each entry carries `quotes` (verbatim sentences of the record, pinned by `test_quotes_match_catalog`) plus the `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS` keys and new ones: `supersedes`, `senses_additive`, `sense_range`, `climb_equals_walk`, `swim_equals_walk`, `speed_bonus_flat`, `resist`, `skills`, `proficiencies`. `CLASS_FEATURE_NOTES_ONLY_2024` lists the conditional or per-rest-choice features that are deliberately not effects (Stride of the Elements, Power of the Wilds, Aspect of the Wilds, Stormborn, Full of Stars, Rage of the Gods, Aura of Warding, Nature's Ward, Fiendish Resilience, Elemental Epitome), each with the conditional wording pinned.
- **Collector.** `features.xphb_effects_2024(catalog, resolved_classes, ruleset)`: ruleset "2024" only; applies an entry when the record is in the catalog, the class level has reached the record's own `level` and, for a subclass feature, the record's subclass is the selected one. `features.class_effects` merges them with the SRD effects; an entry with `supersedes` drops that SRD id only when the XPHB record was found (so an SRD-only catalog keeps the SRD Primal Champion, cap 24). The merged list feeds the existing readers (ability bonuses, AC formulas, HP per level, Fast Movement key, `swim_equals_walk`).
- **Entries.** Body and Mind (DEX/WIS +4, cap 25), Primal Champion (cap 25, replaces the SRD 24), Draconic Resilience XPHB (HP = Sorcerer level, AC 10 + DEX + CHA, shield allowed: the text says "armor", not "armor or a Shield"), Dance Unarmored Defense (10 + DEX + CHA), Shadow and Gloom Stalker Darkvision (60, or +60 on the final range, added after every other source), Thief climb, Sea swim, Glory 7 Speed +10, War 17 b/p/s, psychic (Psi Warrior 10, Aberrant 6, Great Old One 10), Celestial 6 radiant, Mercy Insight + Medicine + Herbalism Kit. Added beyond the plan's list, same kind (unconditional, fixed numbers): Ranger 6 Roving (+10 unless Heavy armor, climb and swim = Speed) and Ranger 18 Feral Senses (Blindsight 30).
- **Cap note.** An XPHB ability increase cut by its cap is a non-blocking `pruned` entry, `choiceId` `feature:<record id>` ("Body and Mind: DEX is already at maximum (25) and the increase was ignored." or "... capped at 25; only +N of +M applied."). The SRD Primal Champion under mixed / 2014 reports nothing, as before.
- **Design note (Dance).** Corrected in RPF: the child record states no condition, but its parent Dazzling Footwork says "While you aren't wearing armor or wielding a Shield, you gain the following benefits.", so `allows_shield` is False (pinned through `parent_quotes`); body armor replaces the formula through `armor_class_from_gear` as for every formula. Mercy's skills are added to the proficiency set (no replacement card when a class pick already took Insight or Medicine; subclass proficiency picks are RP12).
- **Not changed.** No new sheet / derived keys (`types.ts`, exports untouched; frontend untouched). Mixed and 2014 are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20): Fighter 20 2.17 ms (2024) / 3.32 ms (mixed), Wizard 20 2.72 / 3.91, Bard 20 3.83 / 3.23, Monk 20 Shadow 1.76, Sorcerer 6 Draconic XPHB 1.45, Barbarian 20 1.75 (limit 50 ms).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_xphb_feature_effects_2024.py` (37; 24 behaviour tests failed before the wiring). Server suite 3992 -> 4029 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP3 (A1, open question 13) Cult of the Dragon Initiate replacement language (2026-10-22)

Golden `test_c_series_golden.py` untouched and green (not regenerated; no golden case holds the feat). Feat completion, so it follows the FE edition policy: the feat's own text under every ruleset in which the character holds it (mixed, 2014 and 2024 alike).

- **Rule** (catalog `entries`, Dragon's Tongue): "If you already know Draconic when you select this feat, you instead learn one language of your choice from the language tables in the Player's Handbook or Chapter 2 of this book." Table `reference_data.FEAT_LANGUAGE_REPLACEMENT` (feat index -> language, label, quote); the quote is pinned against the catalog text. No FRHoF-specific language table exists in the catalog, so the pool is the PHB 2024 Standard + Rare tables (E2, 19 options), documented here.
- **Card.** `class_choices_2024.feat_replacement_language_template` builds `<feat cid prefix>:languages` (count 1; label "Cult of the Dragon Initiate: Dragon's Tongue") for every holder, from `_feat_choice_templates` (all five call sites). The `:languages` suffix means `chosen_languages` and the sheet language list pick the pick up unchanged. The fixed Draconic still shows.
- **Condition (gate).** `gate_languages` keeps the card only when Draconic is already known at the feat's position, not counting the feat's own grant (removed once from the fixed list or the positioned list). Origin feats (background, species): fixed species / background languages and picks of other species / background / feat language cards; the C8 `origin:languages` card does not count (its Draconic is disabled by the feat's fixed grant, C8). Lessons of the First Ones feats: everything earlier in the build, i.e. fixed species / background languages, the C8 origin card picks, earlier class language cards and fixed class languages at an earlier (class, level). A later class card picking Draconic is the duplicate (F3a-5), so the card does not appear. Known options are disabled "already known"; a stored pick of one is dropped with a `pruned` entry and the card goes pending.
- **Vanishing card.** When the earlier source is removed the card is removed from `choices` and a stored pick becomes a non-blocking `pruned` entry ("... no longer applies because you do not already know Draconic ..."). `_is_count_variable_choice` and `mark_unavailable` are not touched: the gate itself produces the `pruned` entries and the card always has 19 minus known options.
- **Note.** The FE10 "add it by hand" wording is removed from `feat_effects_tail` (Dragon's Tongue note now points at the card).
- **No new output keys** (`types.ts`, exports, frontend untouched).
- **Not done.** Greater/other feats with the same "if you already know" shape do not exist in the data (scan: only this feat).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20): mixed Fighter 4.3, Wizard 4.2, Bard 11.2 (measured while two full suites ran); 2024 Fighter 2.8, Wizard 3.1, Bard 4.3 (limit 50).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_cult_of_the_dragon_language.py` (21, rule matrix in the docstring). Server suite 4029 -> 4050 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP4 (A4, open question 16) 2024 Fighting Style feat card sees a Fighting Initiate style (2026-10-23)

Golden `test_c_series_golden.py` untouched and green (not regenerated). 2024 only (ruleset policy table: A4).

- **Rule** (catalog text): Fighting Initiate "If you already have a style, the one you choose must be different."; XPHB Fighter Fighting Style "gain a Fighting Style feat of your choice". Same style = same name key, so Dueling (TCE fighter style) equals the feat Dueling (XPHB).
- **Change.** `resolve._fi_seen_add(seen, catalog, feat, stored, ruleset)`: under ruleset 2024 a valid stored pick of the six fighter styles on the feat's `:fighting-style` card adds `seen["name:<style>"] = "<Style>, Fighting Initiate"` (setdefault, first holder wins). Called (1) in `_resolve_asi_selected_feats` right after the ASI feat is accepted (so its position is its (class index, level), after the cards of that level), and (2) for species-granted feats (origin position) into the initial `seen`. Later FS feat cards and later ASI picks of the FS feat read `seen` as before: option disabled "already taken (Dueling, Fighting Initiate)", a stored duplicate gives one `feat-duplicate-*` problem, nothing in `pruned` (the FS card is a holder in `_gate_duplicate_picks`, so no double report).
- **Unchanged.** FS first then FI (FE9: the FI card is disabled and pruned), mixed / 2014 (SRD style cards keep the FE9 `pruned` path, no problem).
- **Origin position.** Species feat grants can hold Fighting Initiate (Variant Human offers category-less feats). Background-granted feats do not include it, so backgrounds are not read. Lessons of the First Ones offers only Origin feats, so not Fighting Initiate. A Fighting Initiate whose prerequisite (martial weapon proficiency) fails is still recorded, like every feat name in the walk.
- **No new output keys.**
- **Tests.** `tests/test_fighting_initiate_fs_card_2024.py` (11; 4 fail without the change).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20): mixed Fighter 3.1, Wizard 3.2, Bard 3.1; 2024 Fighter 2.0, Wizard 3.3, Bard 3.7 (limit 50).
- **Totals.** Server suite 4050 -> 4061 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP5 (A5, open question 21) Cross-feat modifications with numbers (2026-10-23)

Golden `test_c_series_golden.py` untouched and green (not regenerated; no golden case holds an affected feat). Feat completion, so it follows the FE edition policy: the feat's own text under every ruleset in which the character holds it.

- **Innate patch.** `modifies[target]` gains `innate: {spell id: {"uses": text with {pb}, "note": text}}`, read by `feat_effects.innate_patches(insts, numbers)` (only while the target feat is held) and applied in `resolve._feat_innate_spells` to the target's innate entry with that `spell_id`. Greater Mark of Healing: Cure Wounds becomes `"<pb>/long rest"` with the note "Improved Healing: treat a 1 or 2 on a healing die as a 3." (one Cure Wounds entry, no "1/day" left; Lesser Restoration stays "1/day"; the Greater resource "Improved Healing (Cure Wounds)" stays the tracker). Greater Mark of Making / Shadow: note "cast as its level 3 version" on Magic Weapon / Invisibility (uses unchanged). Quotes in `feat_effects_tail.RP5_INNATE_QUOTES`, pinned by test.
- **ability_from.** New entry field `ability_from: <base feat index>` (`FEAT_EFFECT_KEYS`). While the base feat is held: `resolve._feat_spell_ability` returns the ability chosen for the base (pending dict while unchosen), and the dependent's own `<prefix>:spell-ability` card is removed from `choice_templates` before the stale-selection pass, so a stored pick there is a non-blocking `pruned` entry. Without the base the dependent keeps its own card (Adept of the Robes) or its previous behaviour. Entries: Adept of the Black / Red / White Robes (Initiate of High Sorcery), Outlands Envoy (Scion of the Outer Planes; previously the ability was null), Warlike Familiar (Familiar Friend; previously "inherit" with nothing to inherit), Magic Connoisseur (Magic Initiate XPHB). Quotes in `RP5_ABILITY_FROM`.
- **Deviations from the plan text.** Magic Connoisseur already had no own card and followed Magic Initiate through the catalog's `sameAsFeat`; it is only registered for consistency. The Marks are held through a House background (category D feats are not offered at an ASI), so tests use `house-*-heir-efa`. Outlands Envoy changed from `data_only` to a notes entry (the `data_only` entry cannot carry `ability_from`); counts of `data_only` entries drop by one.
- **Surfaces.** The sheet innate-spell `note` field already existed (`types.ts`); Markdown and PDF now append the note after the uses. No new output keys.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_cross_numbers.py` (29; 10 fail without the `resolve` wiring). `test_feat_tail` vocabulary set extended with `ability_from`.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20): mixed Fighter 3.0, Wizard 3.0, Bard 2.9; 2024 Fighter 1.9, Wizard 3.0, Bard 3.6 (limit 50).
- **Totals.** Server suite 4061 -> 4089 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched (no build needed).

## RP6 (P0) Golden rebaseline mode (2026-10-23)

Tests only. `server/tests/test_c_series_golden.py` gains `--rebaseline <SLICE> [--dry-run]`, the append-only constant `REBASELINES` (empty today) and the function `rebaseline()`. The real golden gz is byte-identical (no rebaseline was run); `STRIP` stays `("derived.featResources",)`. The module docstring explains the workflow.

- **Entry.** `REBASELINES[slice] = {"cases": [fnmatch over case names], "paths": [regexes over normalized paths], "why": line}`. The `paths` allowlist is written from the slice's rule matrix, never copied from the dry run; the reviewer compares the two.
- **Algorithm (as in the plan, section 1).** Cases not matching `cases` must re-resolve to the stored result (STRIP applied to both sides) and are written back unchanged. Matching cases: `ks.fill` on a copy of the stored draft (only newly `pending` cards get picks, with the kitchen-sink overrides of that case), re-resolve, then both `{draft, result}` sides are normalized and diffed. Normalization turns every list of dicts that all carry a unique string `id` into a dict keyed by id, so an inserted card is one path (`result.choices[<id>]`, options `...options[<id>]`) and does not shift the others. An added or removed subtree is reported once at its root.
- **Rules.** Any changed path outside `paths`, or an unmatched case that moved, raises `RebaselineError` before anything is written. A matching case with no diff prints a warning (pattern too wide). Cases are never deleted or added (`--append` adds). The gz is written only when a matching case changed (gzip headers would otherwise change the bytes for nothing). The printed summary (changed cases, count per path regex, warnings) goes verbatim into the slice's ARCHITECTURE section.
- **`--dry-run`** prints every changed path per case (marking `DISALLOWED` ones) and writes nothing, and does not raise on violations.
- **Tests (9 new, same file).** A synthetic two-case golden in `tmp_path` through the module-level `GOLDEN` override, with a fake resolver and filler (no catalog involved): expected path passes and writes only the matching case; unexpected path fails and leaves the file untouched; an unmatched case changing fails; dry run writes nothing; warning for an unchanged matching case; inserted card at a keyed path; unknown slice; `REBASELINES == {}` and `STRIP` pinned; normalization and `diff_paths` unit test.
- **Totals.** Server suite 4089 -> 4098 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP7 (E11c) SRD Monk tool or instrument choice (2026-10-23)

First slice that deliberately changes mixed / 2014 output, through the RP6 `--rebaseline` policy (never `--regenerate`). Rule: the SRD Monk Proficiencies line "Choose one type of artisan's tools or one musical instrument" (`catalog.classes["monk"]["proficiency_choices"][1]["desc"]`, pinned by test) is now a card under mixed and 2014. Tests first (13 of 19 failed before the change), then the implementation.

- **Cause and fix.** That envelope holds two nested `option_type: "choice"` options (artisan's tools / musical instrument), which `build_choice_options` skipped, so the card never resolved. `choices.build_choice_template` now passes `flatten_nested=True` to `build_choice_options` for `type: "proficiencies"` envelopes only; the bare option builder and every other envelope type keep skipping a nested choice. `_flatten_nested_tool_choice` turns each nested list into flat `{id, label, group}` entries, `group` being the catalog tool category (`reference_data.TOOLS`: "Artisan's Tools", "Musical Instrument", already typed as `ChoiceOption.group` and rendered by `ChoicePicker`). Cid `class:monk:1:prof-choice-1` (the same as the 2024 card, so a pick survives a ruleset switch), label "Tool Proficiencies", count 1, order after `prof-choice-0`. The pick reaches `sheet.proficiencies.tools`. A secondary Monk has no card (unchanged). The 2024 card is unchanged (27 options, no `group`).
- **Verified.** The Monk is the only class / background / race / subrace / trait proficiency envelope with a nested choice (test); equipment nested choices go through `build_equipment_choice` and are untouched.
- **Deviation from the plan text.** The SRD nested artisan's list has 19 entries, not 17: it also lists Disguise Kit and Forgery Kit. The catalog tool table does not class them as artisan's tools (they are "Other Tools" in the books), so they are not offered: 17 + 10 = 27, the same ids and order as the 2024 card. Pinned by test.
- **Rebaseline.** `REBASELINES["RP7"]`: cases `*/src-*/monk[0-9]*` and `ks/src-*/monk14` (the multiclass Barbarian 3 / Monk 2 has Monk as the secondary class and is not matched); paths (written from the rule matrix: the new card, the filler's pick in the draft, the picked tool): `^result\.choices\[class:monk:1:prof-choice-1\]$`, `^draft\.classes\[0\]\.choices\.class:monk:1:prof-choice-1$`, `^result\.sheet\.proficiencies\.tools$`. The dry run showed exactly these three paths per case and no disallowed path; no `sheet.features` row changed. Printed summary of the real run (same as the dry run's header):

```
rebaseline RP7 (SRD Monk tool or instrument choice (E11c))
  matching cases changed: 18 of 231
     18 changed path(s) match ^result\.choices\[class:monk:1:prof-choice-1\]$
     18 changed path(s) match ^draft\.classes\[0\]\.choices\.class:monk:1:prof-choice-1$
     18 changed path(s) match ^result\.sheet\.proficiencies\.tools$
wrote 231 cases, 752291 bytes
```

  The 18 cases: monk 1 / 3 / 11 / 20 under mixed and 2014, each with `src-all` and `src-srd` (16), plus `ks/src-all/monk14` and `ks/src-srd/monk14`. The other 213 cases are byte-identical. `test_c_series_golden.py` pins `REBASELINES` as append-only (`("RP7",)`) and that the 18 matched cases hold the resolved card while the multiclass case has none.
- **Second golden.** `tests/golden/g2_resolve_baseline.json.gz` (guard of `test_worn_wielded.py`, not covered by `--rebaseline`) holds the unfilled `ks-monk14` draft; its only difference is the new pending card. The test asserts that card (pending, 27 options) and removes it before the comparison; the gz is unchanged.
- **Not changed.** No new output keys (`group` is already in `types.ts`; `export_fields.py`, the frontend and `npm run build` untouched). 2024 output is byte-identical.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_srd_monk_tool_choice.py` (19, rule matrix in the docstring), 2 added in `test_c_series_golden.py`, 1 test edited there (the old "REBASELINES is empty" pin).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20): mixed Fighter 3.1, Wizard 3.2, Monk 2.3; 2024 Fighter 2.0, Wizard 3.2, Monk 1.6 (limit 50).
- **Totals.** Server suite 4098 -> 4118 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP8 (D) 2024 pack contents and carried weight (2026-10-23)

Ruleset 2024 only; mixed / 2014 are byte-identical (`test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated). Source: PHB 2024 ch. 6, "Equipment Packs" table and the per-pack item lists; stored as item ids and numbers, no sentences.

- **Data.** `reference_data.PACKS_2024 = {pack id: {"weight": lb, "contents": [(item id, qty), ...]}}` for the 7 packs (Burglar's 42, Diplomat's 39, Dungeoneer's 55, Entertainer's 58.5, Explorer's 55, Priest's 29, Scholar's 22 lb). All 29 member ids exist in the catalog (test). The table weight wins over the member sum (Explorer's: the SRD rope weighs 10 lb, so the member sum is 60, the table says 55; the test documents it). Pack cost differs from the SRD record (Priest's 33 GP vs 19) and is not shown anywhere, so it is untouched.
- **Engine.** `resolve._with_pack_contents` (ruleset 2024 only) adds `contents: [{itemId, name, qty}]` to granted, chosen and added entries of a pack id; members missing from the catalog are skipped. The pack stays one inventory item. `derived.equipment_weight(entries, items, weights=None)` / `carrying_summary(..., weights=None)`: `weights` overrides the catalog weight; resolve passes the table weights under 2024 only, so `carryingCapacity.carried` counts the pack (it was 0 for every pack in every ruleset).
- **Exports / frontend.** `export_fields.pack_contents_suffix(entry)` gives " (съдържа: Backpack, Oil x2, ...)"; Markdown and PDF print it after the pack bullet; YAML keeps `contents` as part of the equipment entry. `types.ts` `SheetEquipmentEntry.contents?`; `EquipmentPage` prints the same line (plain text, no CSS) for granted, chosen and added entries. No new top-level sheet / derived key.
- **Kitchen sinks / deliberate test changes.** `test_export_fields._EXPECT["fighter4-2024"]` gains `"pack": "dungeoneers-pack"` (contents present and printed in Markdown). `test_f3a_fixes` Ranger A + Soldier carried weight moves 35 -> 90 (Explorer's Pack 55 lb now counts); test renamed `..._carried_weight_is_90`. Any 2024 character holding a pack now carries 7-58.5 lb more.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_packs_2024.py` (17: table, member ids, SRD weight difference, per-pack contents and weight, qty 2, mixed / 2014 unchanged, Fighter package A +55, override only when given, degrade with missing members, suffix text, MD / PDF / YAML). Without the resolve wiring 11 of them fail.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20 with an Explorer's Pack): mixed Fighter 3.2, Wizard 3.3; 2024 Fighter 2.1, Wizard 2.7 (limit 50).
- **Totals.** Server suite 4118 -> 4135 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green.
- **Known gap closed.** The "2024 pack contents differ" limit (item 8 of the open list) now holds for ruleset 2024; mixed / 2014 still show SRD packs with weight 0 and no contents (Extras RPX2).

## RP9 (C, E12) 2024 background starting equipment: real items and quantities (2026-10-23)

Ruleset 2024 only (decision in the plan: the user's byte-identical rule for mixed, the C9b / E8 precedent); mixed / 2014 are byte-identical (`test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated). Governing text: the `Equipment:` line of every ruleset-2024 background record, "Choose A or B: (A) ..., N GP; or (B) 50 GP". Tests first (`tests/test_background_equipment_2024.py`, 46; the module failed to import before the change).

- **Data.** New `catalog/official/background_equipment.py`: `starting_equipment_options_2024_from_5etools(raw, proficiency_choices)` builds SRD-shaped A/B envelopes flagged `keep_counts` with the `desc` of the record's text line. `backgrounds._background_from_5etools` stores them under the ADDITIVE key `starting_equipment_options_2024` for the 60 backgrounds with ruleset 2024 (the plan said 26: those 26 carry placeholders, the other 34 only need the quantities and the gold fix). The old key is untouched.
- **Member rules.** Quantities kept; "Robe" -> `robes`, "20 Arrows" -> ONE `arrow`, "20 Bolts" -> ONE `crossbow-bolt`, "Iron Spikes" -> `spike-iron` (`reference_data.BACKGROUND_2024_EQUIPMENT_ITEM_MAP`; the catalog spike weighs 5 lb = the 10-pack, so one unit). "Holy Symbol" -> nested `holy-symbols` pick (Amulet / Emblem / Reliquary), but "Holy Symbol (reliquary)" (Vampire Survivor) is the Reliquary. "Arcane Focus (Crystal or Wand)" (Spellfire Initiate) -> nested `arcane-foci` pick narrowed by a new optional `from.only` list (`build_equipment_choice` filters the nested options; the id stays `any-arcane-foci`). Gaming set / artisan's tools / musical instrument: the sentinel `reference_data.BACKGROUND_TOOL_SENTINEL` (`background-tool`) when the background has an `any_tool` proficiency pick of that category (the text says "same as above"), otherwise a nested pick of that category (new `_item_in_category` indexes `gaming-sets`, `artisans-tools`). Any other unmapped name raises ValueError (strict, like C9a / C9b).
- **Text wins over data (found while verifying).** (1) Mulhorandi Tomb Raider: the data has no gold value, the "26 GP" sits in a display name ("Waterskin 26 GP"); the gold member comes from the text. (2) Scribe: the data has no quantities for "Oil (3 flasks)" and "Parchment (12 sheets)" (every other record carries them in a display name or `quantity`); the quantities come from the text tokens when their number equals the data member count (a test checks that without the text the data-only fallback gives the same counts for the other 59). (3) Gold amounts come from the text for every record (same rule as C9b, E9).
- **Catalog gaps (no item added; RPX4 covers extra gaming sets).** "Map" has no catalog item: the old converter matched it to "Case, map or scroll" by word subset (Archaeologist, House Orien Heir got that case twice). The 2024 key makes it a narrative `flavor-map` item and keeps the real "Map or Scroll Case". The catalog has only two gaming sets (Dice Set, Playing Card Set), so the Wayfarer nested card and the gaming-set proficiency cards list those two (Dragonchess, Three-Dragon Ante are not in the catalog; the tool table `reference_data.TOOLS` has no other sets).
- **Carouser (ABH) proficiency (fixed in RPF).** The raw `{"gaming set": true}` is now converted to a one-pick Gaming Sets card in every ruleset (see RPF), so the "same as above" item is the RP9 sentinel like the other backgrounds; only Wayfarer (text says "(any)") keeps the nested gaming-set pick.
- **Engine.** `resolve()` swaps `starting_equipment_options` for the 2024 key under ruleset 2024 before `_equipment_choice_templates` (same cid `background:<id>:equipment-choice-N`, same A/B structure; nested cards are `<cid>:<slot>`). `_background_tool_pick(choices, bg_id, items)` reads the selected tool of `background:<id>:proficiency-choice-*` (None when unpicked or when the tool has no catalog item); `_chosen_equipment(..., background_tool)` hands out its item for the sentinel, `_selected_gold_and_narrative_items(..., background_tool)` shows the sentinel label ("Gaming Set (same as the tool proficiency)") as a narrative item while it cannot resolve. The pick is read after the choices are applied, so changing the proficiency pick changes the item.
- **Ruleset switch.** The cid and the B option id (`gp-50`) are the same in every ruleset, and so is package A for 31 of the 60 backgrounds (quantity changes do not change option ids). For the other 29 (the 26 with placeholders plus Archaeologist and House Orien Heir for "Map", Mulhorandi Tomb Raider for the recovered gold; pinned by a test) a stored A is not an option under mixed, so the card falls back to pending (the existing stale-selection discipline, non-blocking); a test pins both outcomes.
- **Kitchen sinks / deliberate test changes.** The Soldier (XPHB) kitchen sinks fill the first option (A), which now brings the 20-pack Arrow, the Quiver and a Dice Set (the filled tool pick): `test_export_fields._EXPECT["fighter4-2024"]` gains `bg_items: ["Arrow", "Dice Set", "Quiver"]` (and asserts that no "Gaming Set" / "Arrows (20)" narrative item is left). `test_f3a_fixes` Ranger A + Soldier A carried weight 90 -> 91 (the Soldier's arrow pack, 1 lb, was a narrative placeholder); test renamed `..._carried_weight_is_91`. `carryingCapacity.carried` of any 2024 character holding such a package changes by the weight of the newly real items.
- **Not changed.** No new output keys (`ChoiceOption.quantities` already typed), so `types.ts`, `export_fields.py`, the frontend and `npm run build` are untouched. The proficiency cards, `starting_gold` of fixed lists and the 2014 / mixed envelopes are as before.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20, 2024 with Soldier package picked / mixed with the SRD-style background): mixed Fighter 2.9, Wizard 3.0; 2024 Fighter 2.7, Wizard 3.0 (limit 50).
- **Totals.** Server suite 4135 -> 4181 passed (46 new; F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched, no `npm run build` needed.

## RP10 (B1) 2024 class-feature numbers as notes (2026-10-23)

Ruleset 2024 only; mixed / 2014 are byte-identical (`test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated). Tests first (`tests/test_class_feature_numbers_2024.py`, rule matrix and the governing sentences in the docstring); with the `_attach_notes_2024` call removed the attached-note tests fail, the note-only entries are data-driven through the R9 tail.

- **Data.** `reference_data.RESOURCE_TAIL_2024` gained 10 note-only entries (`uses: None`, `recharge: None`, R9 style) and the new `RESOURCE_NOTES_2024` holds 8 notes added to EXISTING resources (`target` = resource id). Every RP10 entry carries `quotes` = (feature id, verbatim substring of the record's name + desc); `test_quotes_match_catalog` pins all of them, and `DIVINE_SPARK_DICE_2024 = ((7, 2), (13, 3), (18, 4))` against "7 (2d8), 13 (3d8), and 18 (4d8)". Entries may carry `extras` (feature id, text; appended with "; " once that record's level is reached, R9 mechanism) and `vars` (name -> (default, ((feature id, value), ...)): the value of the last listed record whose level the class has reached; used for the Brutal Strike die / effect count and the Cunning Strike effect count). No level is duplicated in code (all levels come from the XPHB records), except the Divine Spark table whose levels exist only in the text.
- **Engine.** `class_resources._note_vars` (level, twice_level, five_level, wis / dex / cha, wis_min1, cha_min1, dex_level, dc_wis / dc_cha / dc_dex = 8 + PB + modifier, ma_die, spark_dice), `_render_note` (shared by the R9 tail and the new tables; R9 output unchanged), `_attach_notes_2024` called per class entry in `resources_for` after the builder and the tail and before the subclass builder. An entry exists only under ruleset 2024, with its XPHB record in the catalog, the class level at the record's level and (attached notes) the target resource present; an existing note is kept and extended with "; ". An SRD-only catalog gives nothing.
- **New note-only resources** (ids `<class>:<slug>`): Brutal Strike (Barbarian 9; 1d10 -> 2d10 and one -> two different effects from 17, Staggering / Sundering Blow from 13), Relentless Rage (11; DC 10, +5 per later use, Hit Points become 2 x level), Sear Undead (Cleric 5; max(1, WIS) d8), Deflect Attacks (Monk 3; 1d10 + DEX + Monk level, redirect 2 Martial Arts dice + DEX at DC 8 + PB + WIS, any damage type from 13), Slow Fall (4; 5 x level), Stunning Strike (5; DC), Heightened Focus (10; Patient Defense Temporary Hit Points = 2 Martial Arts dice), Superior Defense (18; 3 Focus Points), Abjure Foes (Paladin 9; max(1, CHA) targets, DC 8 + PB + CHA), Cunning Strike (Rogue 5; DC 8 + PB + DEX, die costs 1d6, two effects from 11, Devious Strikes Daze 2d6 / Obscure 3d6 / Knock Out 6d6 from 14).
- **Notes attached to existing resources:** Bardic Inspiration (Superior Inspiration 18), Channel Divinity of the Cleric (Divine Spark dice by level + WIS modifier, Turn Undead, save DC 8 + PB + WIS; only the Cleric pool, the Paladin pool is untouched), Wild Shape (Evergreen Wild Shape, Archdruid 20), Indomitable (+ Fighter level to the reroll), Second Wind (Tactical Mind), Focus Points (Perfect Focus 15), Lay on Hands (5 Hit Points of the pool to remove Poisoned; Restoring Touch 14: 5 per condition), Favored Enemy (Foe Slayer 20: d10 for Hunter's Mark).
- **Verified, no change needed.** Monk Focus Points already carry `dc` = 8 + PB + WIS (the plan's candidate "save DC" note); the Lay on Hands pool (5 x level) is already shown as `pool`; a guard test pins the Focus Points DC. Divine Spark scaling verified in the catalog text (above).
- **Scanned and skipped** (all 173 `xphb-*` class features re-read for numbers; nothing modelled when the text gives no verifiable number or use rule beyond what is already shown): Rage (numbers are table-driven, R5), Reckless Attack, Danger Sense, Primal Knowledge, Feral Instinct, Instinctive Pounce (half Speed), Indomitable Might, Superior Inspiration neighbours (Font of Inspiration is R4, Countercharm "30 feet" has no use count), Jack of All Trades, Expertise, Magical Secrets, Words of Creation, Divine Order, Thaumaturge (WIS minimum +1), Turn Undead alone (in the Channel Divinity note), Blessed Strikes (C6c-2), Wild Shape known forms (R3), Tactical Shift and Tactical Master, Studied Attacks, Action Surge (R2), the Fighter Indomitable duplicates at 13 / 17 (same text, one resource), Monk Martial Arts die / Unarmored Movement (tables), Self-Restoration, Disciplined Survivor (the Focus Point reroll has no count of its own), Evasion, Divine Sense (Channel Divinity effect, range only), Aura of Protection / Courage / Expansion (auras), Radiant Strikes, Paladin Weapon Mastery, Hunter's Mark riders other than Foe Slayer, Precise Hunter, Relentless Hunter, Roving, Rogue Steady Aim, Cunning Action, Uncanny Dodge, Reliable Talent, Slippery Mind, Elusive. Rage damage / Brutal Strike effect text beyond the four named effects is not shown.
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys: `details.note` is already printed by `export_fields.resource_line` and `ClassResourcesList.describeResource`, so `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no `npm run build` needed). The SPA shows the longer notes on the existing resource rows (plain text).
- **Kitchen sinks.** `test_export_fields._EXPECT` is unchanged (it lists resource names a superset must contain, and the 2024 Fighter 4 sink gains no new resource at level 4; the Fighter Second Wind note is added to an existing row).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20): mixed / 2024 Fighter 3.0 / 2.0, Wizard 3.1 / 2.5, Bard 3.5 / 3.6, Monk 2.2 / 1.6, Rogue 2.6 / 1.8, Barbarian 2.3 / 1.6, Paladin 2.9 / 2.1, Cleric 2.8 / 2.2 (limit 50; also asserted in the tests).
- **Edited test.** `test_blessed_strikes_2024::test_resource_order_follows_the_class`: Cleric 7 now also lists Sear Undead after Channel Divinity / Divine Strike (deliberate). No `_EXPECT` change was needed.
- **Tests / totals.** `tests/test_class_feature_numbers_2024.py` (102). Server suite 4181 -> 4283 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP11 (B3) 2024 subclass resource tail (2026-10-23)

Ruleset 2024 only (decision D2); mixed / 2014 are byte-identical (`test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated). Tests first (`tests/test_subclass_resources_2024.py`, 133, rule matrix and the use rules in the docstring); with the `_subclass_tail_2024` call removed 123 of them fail, with the 2024 gate or the subclass match removed the gating tests fail.

- **Data.** `reference_data.SUBCLASS_TAIL_2024` (55 resource entries) and `SUBCLASS_NOTES_2024` (23 sentences on existing resources), 35 XPHB subclasses. Spec keys: `subclass`, `class`, `feature` (XPHB record id; its own `level` is the first level, no level is duplicated in code), `name`, `uses` (int or a `_note_vars` name: `wis_min1`, `cha_min1`, `int_min1`, `level_plus1`, `zealot_dice`), optional `die`, `recharge`, `uses_stages` / `recharge_stages` / `vars` (value of the last listed record whose level the class has reached: Portent 2 -> 3 at Greater Portent, Warding Flare long -> short-or-long at Improved Warding Flare, Dreadful Strike 2d6 -> 2d8 at Stalker's Flurry), `extras`, `note`, `quotes`. `test_quotes_match_catalog` pins 192 verbatim substrings (name + desc) plus the two tables that live only in text: `ZEALOT_WARRIOR_DICE_2024 = ((3,4),(6,5),(12,6),(17,7))` ("pool of four d12s ... levels 6 (5 dice), 12 (6 dice), and 17 (7 dice)") and `LANDS_AID_DICE_2024 = ((3,2),(10,3),(14,4))`. Notes are paraphrases with the computed number.
- **Engine.** `class_resources._subclass_tail_2024(ctx, rc, resources)`, called from `resources_for` after the class builder, the R9 / RP10 tail and the R6 subclass builder (so order is: class entries, R6 Psionic / Superiority dice, new subclass entries). New entries have `source.kind "subclass"`; notes are added to the matching resource of the class or of the R6 builders (an existing note is kept and extended with "; "; `shortRestRegains` is untouched). Only for the class entry's selected subclass; an SRD-only catalog (no `*-xphb-*` feature records) gives nothing. `_note_vars` gained int / int_min1 / dc_str / dc_int / pb / level_plus1 / third_level / three_level / half_level_up / cha_pb / moon_ac / ward_hp / zealot_dice / lands_aid / cha_creatures / int_creatures / cha_dice; `_render_note` now also formats the `extras` templates and uses the new `_staged` helper (RP10 output unchanged).
- **Resources (uses, recharge):** Barbarian Berserker Intimidating Presence (1, long), Zealot Warrior of the Gods (d12 pool 4/5/6/7, long), Zealous Presence, Rage of the Gods; Bard Glamour Beguiling Magic, Mantle of Majesty (1, long), Unbreakable Majesty (1, short-or-long); Cleric Light Warding Flare (WIS min 1, long; short-or-long from 6) and Corona of Light, War War Priest (WIS, short-or-long); Druid Moon Moonlight Step, Stars Star Map and Cosmic Omen (WIS, long), Land Natural Recovery (Circle Spell) and (Spell Slots) (1, long); Fighter Battle Master Know Your Enemy, Psi Warrior Telekinetic Movement / Psi-Powered Leap (short-or-long), Bulwark of Force (INT min 1), Telekinetic Master; Monk Open Hand Wholeness of Body, Mercy Flurry of Healing and Harm (WIS), Hand of Ultimate Mercy; Paladin Glory Glorious Defense (CHA) and Living Legend, Devotion Holy Nimbus, Ancients Undying Sentinel and Elder Champion, Vengeance Avenging Angel; Ranger Fey Wanderer Fey Reinforcements and Misty Wanderer (WIS), Gloom Stalker Dread Ambusher (WIS); Rogue Arcane Trickster Spell Thief, Soulknife Psychic Veil and Rend Mind; Sorcerer Clockwork Restore Balance (CHA), Trance of Order, Clockwork Cavalcade, Aberrant Warping Implosion, Draconic Dragon Wings and Dragon Companion, Wild Magic Tides of Chaos and Tamed Surge; Warlock Archfey Steps of the Fey (CHA) and Beguiling Defenses, Celestial Healing Light (d6 pool of 1 + level) and Searing Vengeance, Fiend Dark One's Own Luck (CHA) and Hurl Through Hell, Great Old One Clairvoyant Combatant (short-or-long); Wizard Abjurer Arcane Ward (HP maximum 2 x level + INT in the note), Diviner Portent (d20, 2 / 3) and The Third Eye (short-or-long), Illusionist Phantasmal Creatures (2) and Illusory Self (short-or-long). The "unless you expend / spend ..." restore options are in each note.
- **Notes on existing resources (feature spends that resource, no new row):** Bardic Inspiration (Glamour Mantle of Inspiration), Channel Divinity (Life Preserve Life 5 x level, Light Radiance of the Dawn 2d10 + level, War Guided Strike +10; Paladin: Devotion Sacred Weapon, Glory Inspiring Smite), Wild Shape (Moon Circle Forms, Land's Aid, Sea Wrath of the Sea), Superiority Dice (Relentless), Psionic Energy Dice (Protective Field, Psionic Strike, Telekinetic Thrust DC; Soulknife Psychic Whispers, Psychic Teleportation), Focus Points (Mercy Hand of Harm / Hand of Healing, Open Hand Quivering Palm, Elements Elemental Burst, Shadow Cloak of Shadows), Sorcery Points (Bastion of Law, Bend Luck, Psionic Sorcery).
- **Scanned (all 309 features of the 48 XPHB subclasses: rest / uses / times / expend / Initiative / Proficiency Bonus text, then a second pass for dice, DC and "equal to" numbers) and skipped:**
  - R6 already: Psionic Energy Dice and Superiority Dice themselves.
  - Spends a resource but gives no number or use rule: Cutting Words, Peerless Skill, Dance (Agile Strikes, Bardic Damage, Inspiring Movement, Tandem Footwork), Trickery Invoke Duplicity, War God's Blessing, Nature's Wrath, Vow of Enmity, Glory Peerless Athlete, Stars Starry Form, Nature's Sanctuary, Oceanic Gift, Guarded Mind, Elemental Attunement, Shadow Darkness and Improved Shadow Step, Gloom Stalker option effects.
  - Numbers but no use count or rest rule (a note would only restate a per-turn rider): Berserker Frenzy, Zealot Divine Fury and Fanatical Focus (once per Rage), World Tree Vitality of the Tree and Travel Along the Tree (once per Rage), Dreadful Strikes, Assassin Assassinate and Death Strike, Fiend Dark One's Blessing, Celestial Resilience and Radiant Soul, Awakened Mind / Telepathic Speech, Create Thrall, Otherworldly Glamour, Sculpt Spells, Blessed Healer, Improved Duplicity, Stars constellations (Archer, Chalice, Dragon), Survivor, Bestial Fury, Open Hand Technique, Evoker Overchannel (escalating damage, no count), Wild Magic Surge table, Revelation in Flesh.
  - One-rest picks (RP13 option cards or notes): Aspect of the Wilds, Hunter's Prey, Defensive Tactics, Land's Circle Spells type, Fiendish Resilience, Elemental Epitome, Starry Form, Third Eye option, Primal Companion.
  - Fixed effects (RP2), spell lists (RP17 / RP18), spellcasting (PART 2): Eldritch Knight / Arcane Trickster Spellcasting, War Bond, Circle Spells and Spell tables, Glory Aura of Alacrity.
- **Mixed / 2014.** No change: the tail returns nothing outside ruleset 2024, and a 2014 twin subclass (`light-phb`) under 2024 shows nothing new. World Tree, Dance and Sea (visible under mixed) stay as before under mixed (D2).
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys: `details.note`, `die`, `uses`, `recharge` print through `export_fields.resource_line` and `ClassResourcesList.describeResource`; `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no build). Mobile list length (up to 3 new subclass rows at one level) is for the G check at 375 px.
- **Kitchen sinks.** No kitchen sink selects an XPHB subclass, so `test_export_fields._EXPECT` is unchanged.
- **Edited test.** `test_class_resources_subclass::test_xphb_psionic_dice_table_under_2024`: the 2024 Psi Warrior / Soulknife dice `details` was asserted `== {"shortRestRegains": 1}`; it now also allows the new `note` key (deliberate).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20, mixed / 2024): Fighter 2.9 / 1.9, Wizard 3.0 / 2.4, Bard 3.3 / 3.5; with the subclass: Psi Warrior 3.2 / 3.4, Diviner 3.2 / 2.5, Glamour 3.1 / 4.1, Light 2.8 / 2.2, Celestial 3.1 / 6.5, Mercy 2.5 / 1.7, Land 3.0 / 2.2, Clockwork 3.1 / 2.4, Glory 2.8 / 2.3 (limit 50).
