## RP12 (F1, E13) 2024 subclass proficiency picks (2026-10-23)

Ruleset 2024 only (decision D2); mixed / 2014 are byte-identical (`test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated). Tests first (`tests/test_subclass_picks_2024.py`, rule matrix and the governing sentences in the docstring); each wiring step was removed once to check that the tests fail without it (templates 21 fail, positioned gate 8, save gate 7, note hook 1, multiclass position 3).

- **Data.** `reference_data.SUBCLASS_PICKS_2024` (5 entries, 4 XPHB features), each with a verbatim `quote` pinned by `test_quotes_match_catalog` (the feature's level, class and subclass come from the catalog record; no level is duplicated):
  - Lore Bard 3 Bonus Proficiencies: 3 of the 18 skills;
  - Battle Master 3 Student of War: TWO cards, one Artisan's Tool (the 17 `TOOLS` of that category) and one skill from the 2024 Fighter level 1 list (`classes_2024`, 9 skills);
  - Fey Wanderer 3 Otherworldly Glamour: 1 of Deception / Performance / Persuasion, plus a note-only subclass resource "Charisma checks +{max(1, WIS)}" (`class_resources._subclass_pick_notes_2024`, after the RP11 tail; its `note_quote` is pinned too);
  - Gloom Stalker 7 Iron Mind: the WIS save is fixed, a card INT / CHA (count 1) only when WIS is already proficient at that position.
- **Card ids.** `class:<cls>:<lvl>:feature:<feature id>` for every pick but Student of War's tool pick, `class:fighter:3:tool-choice:<feature id>` (a feature with two picks needs two cids); `sheet_features._CLASS_CHOICE_ID` gained `tool-choice`, so both attach to the one feature row. Built by `class_choices_2024.subclass_pick_templates`; `pick_spec_for_card` maps a card back to its entry. `is_new_2024_class_card` covers exactly these cids, so an empty card is `unavailable` (never blocks export). They are not count-variable cards (their options do not follow the level; stale picks go through the gates' own `pruned` entries and the generic "no longer applies" entry when the subclass or level goes away).
- **Positioned proficiency picks (generalised C6b-2 / F3a-4).** `class_choices_2024.is_positioned_proficiency_card` = Primal Knowledge (`is_primal_knowledge_card` stays, unchanged) + the skill / tool picks above. `resolve._gate_positioned_proficiencies` walks them by (class order, class level). Each sees `_proficiency_grants` (background, species traits, origin feats at (-1, 0); a class's fixed proficiencies at (class, 1); `SUBCLASS_PROFICIENCIES` at (class, 3); ASI / Lessons feats at their build position; the RP2 XPHB effects, so Mercy's Insight / Medicine / Herbalism Kit, at their record's level) plus the proficiency cards at or before its position and the kept picks of earlier positioned cards. Options held are disabled "already proficient" and a stored duplicate is dropped with a `pruned` entry (Primal Knowledge keeps its own wording through `gate_primal_knowledge`). Then `gate_after_positioned_pick` (the old `gate_after_primal_knowledge`, now generic) applies the pick to every LATER skill / tool card. A grant that comes later never converts a pick. The first-pass `proficient_skills` loop skips all positioned cards; their skills are added after the walk, the Student of War tool through `_categorize_proficiency`.
- **Multiclass position.** `proficiency_position` = `card_position` plus `class:<cls>:multiclass-prof-choice-N` at (class, 1) (the cid has no level). Before RP12 those cards had no position and were ignored by the Primal Knowledge walk in both directions; now they take part (Primal Knowledge and Lore see them, and they see the picks before them). Only `resolve._gate_positioned_proficiencies` / `_gate_save_picks` use it; `card_position` itself (Expertise walk, FE6 priors, language gate) is untouched. No existing test changed.
- **Iron Mind.** The card is always templated at Gloom Stalker 7; `resolve._gate_save_picks` (after the class-feature saves, before `derived.savingThrows`) collects the saves held at its position (`_save_grant_positions`: first class's saves at (0, 1), feat fixed saves and Resilient-style `save-choice` cards at their position, Diamond Soul / Slippery Mind / Disciplined Survivor at their record's level). WIS not held: the card is removed from `choices`, WIS is added outright, a stored pick gets a `pruned` entry. WIS held (Cleric / Druid / Paladin / Wizard first, an earlier Resilient (WIS), Monk 14 first): the card stays, saves already held are disabled "already proficient" (both held: `unavailable`), the pick is added to `sheet.proficiencies.savingThrows`. A WIS gained after the position (Cleric as second class gives no saves anyway; Resilient (WIS) at Ranger 8) does not make a card.
- **Known limits.** (1) A Resilient (WIS) card taken AFTER Iron Mind does not see the fixed WIS grant (the Resilient card has no "already proficient" gate in any ruleset). (2) Tool duplicates are compared by the catalog tool ids; a background's "Gaming Set" or "Artisan's Tools" PICK counts once it is chosen (card position (-1, 0)). (3) `SUBCLASS_PROFICIENCIES` grants are placed at class level 3 for this walk (every 2024 subclass starts at 3). (4) Invocation skills (Beguiling Influence) are not in the prior set, as for Primal Knowledge.
- **Open design question (not guessed).** Mercy: the XPHB Implements of Mercy text ("You gain proficiency in the Insight and Medicine skills and proficiency with the Herbalism Kit.") has no replacement clause, so RP12 adds no replacement card when a class / Lore pick already took Insight or Medicine (a Mercy grant after an earlier pick of the same skill is simply listed once, as in RP2). Later picks do see Mercy's skills. A "choose another skill" card would need a rule that is not in the catalog text.
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys (`types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched; `ChoiceStatus.unavailable` already existed; no `npm run build`). The cards render through the generic ClassPage list (class source, cid prefix `class:<cls>:`).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 30, ms, level 20, mixed / 2024): Fighter 4.4 / 4.4, Wizard 5.0 / 3.3, Bard 4.0 / 4.4; Bard 20 Lore 4.4, Fighter 20 Battle Master 3.1, Ranger 20 Fey Wanderer 3.0, Gloom Stalker 2.9, Cleric 1 / Gloom Stalker 7 1.8, Barbarian 3 / Lore Bard 3 1.2 (limit 50; the tests assert it too; measured while the full suite ran).
- **Tests / totals.** `tests/test_subclass_picks_2024.py` (32). Server suite 4416 -> 4448 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP13 (F2, E13) 2024 subclass option cards and their effects (2026-10-23)

Ruleset 2024 only (decision D2); mixed / 2014 are byte-identical (`test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated). Tests first (`tests/test_subclass_options_2024.py`, 52, rule matrix and governing texts in the docstring); each wiring step was removed once to check that the tests fail without it (templates 34 fail, effects 10, resource notes 12, attack-row note 1, 2024 gate 9).

- **Why a data table and not `FEATURE_TEXT_CHOICES`.** The converter flattens the XPHB entries (verified by script): these features are plain text lines ("Owl. You have Darkvision ..."), not 5etools `options` blocks, so no `feature_specific.text_options` exists and the catalog loader (and its cache) is untouched. `reference_data.SUBCLASS_OPTIONS_2024` is keyed by the XPHB feature record id; the options are checked against the record text when the card is built (a changed catalog gives no card, never a wrong one).
- **Data.** Six features, each with verbatim `quotes` and per-option `quote`s pinned by `test_quotes_match_catalog`: Hunter 3 Hunter's Prey (Colossus Slayer, Horde Breaker), Hunter 7 Defensive Tactics (Escape the Horde, Multiattack Defense), Wild Heart 6 Aspect of the Wilds (Owl, Panther, Salmon), Draconic 6 Elemental Affinity (Acid, Cold, Fire, Lightning, Poison), Beast Master 3 Primal Companion (Beast of the Land / Sea / Sky, label only: the stat blocks are not in the data), Fey Wanderer 3 Fey Wanderer Spells = the Feywild Gifts table (6 flavour options, `gift-1` .. `gift-6`, label = the table text). An option marked `line` takes its description from its own "Name. text" line.
- **Cards.** `engine/subclass_options_2024.py` (pure; resolve only wires it): `templates` (class `cls_id` + selected subclass match the entry, class level at the record's level, ruleset 2024), `spec_for_card`, `selected`, `effects`. Cid `class:<cls>:<lvl>:feature:<feature id>`, option ids `<feature id>--<key>`, count 1, required (pending until picked, like Divine Order). `_feature_choice_templates`' generic attach (`sheet_features._CLASS_CHOICE_ID`) puts the pick on the feature row (the Gifts pick on "Fey Wanderer Spells"). `is_new_2024_class_card` covers them, so an empty card is `unavailable` (not reachable today). Not count-variable: the option sets do not follow level, a stale pick falls in the generic `pruned` passes (below the level or without the subclass the card is gone and the stored cid is reported).
- **Effects** (keyed by the picked option, read by the existing RP2 readers; `resolve` appends `subclass_options_2024.effects(choices)` to the class effects after the choices are resolved): Owl `senses_additive` darkvision 60 (60, or +60 on the final range, e.g. Dwarf 120 -> 180), Panther `climb_equals_walk`, Salmon `swim_equals_walk` (both equal the FINAL walk speed, after Fast Movement), Affinity `resist` of the picked type (merged without duplicates in `sheet.damageModifiers`). Switching the stored option moves the effect.
- **Notes.** `class_resources._subclass_option_notes_2024` (after the RP12 notes): note-only subclass resources "Hunter's Prey: Colossus Slayer" / "Horde Breaker", "Defensive Tactics: Escape the Horde" / "Multiattack Defense" and "Elemental Affinity: <Type>" ("Add your Charisma modifier (+N) to one damage roll of a <type> damage spell", N from the final CHA modifier). Colossus Slayer also adds "Colossus Slayer: +1d8 once per turn if the target is missing any Hit Points" to every weapon attack row (`annotate_attacks`, the Divine Strike pattern; the unarmed strike has no catalog weapon and gets none).
- **Notes only, no card (as planned).** Elemental Epitome, Fiendish Resilience, Starry Form (the pick lasts a turn, a rest or one use): their text stays the note (`CLASS_FEATURE_NOTES_ONLY_2024`; Starry Form was added with its pinned wording, Aspect of the Wilds was removed because it is now a card). The Third Eye already had no card (S4).
- **Deferred (not guessed).** Land's Circle Spells type and Nature's Ward: they depend on the four named spell blocks, so they stay with RP17 (the plan's RP13 goal list does not include them either). **Superseded by RP17 (2026-10-23): done, see "RP17" below.**
- **Design notes.** (1) The Feywild Gifts card is required like every other card; the text says "Choose it from the table or determine it randomly", so a pending card blocks export until one gift is picked. If a purely cosmetic pick should not block, that is a decision for the user (one-line change: `count` 0 is not supported by the card pipeline, it would need an optional-card flag). (2) The two Hunter cards store one pick per Rest in the draft ("Whenever you finish a Short or Long Rest, you can replace the chosen option"): the editor is the card itself.
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys (`types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend untouched, no `npm run build`; drift tests green with an empty `UNTYPED_ALLOWLIST`): cards use `choices[]`, notes `derived.classResources[].details.note` (printed by `export_fields.resource_line`), effects `sheet.senses`, `derived.speeds`, `sheet.damageModifiers`.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, mixed / 2024): Fighter 20 3.2 / 2.9, Wizard 20 3.2 / 3.2, Bard 20 3.6 / 4.4; Hunter 7 1.3 / 1.6, Wild Heart 6 1.0 / 1.1, Draconic 6 1.5 / 1.7 (limit 50; the tests assert it too).
- **Tests / totals.** `tests/test_subclass_options_2024.py` (52). Server suite 4448 -> 4500 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP14 (X3) SPA shows senses, damage modifiers and innate spells (2026-10-23)

Frontend only; no server, `types.ts` or `export_fields.py` change (the types were already present).

- **Component.** `frontend/src/components/SheetTraitsSummary.tsx`, rendered by `ExportPage` right after the problems/choices blocks and before Attacks. Plain `<div><h2/><ul>` blocks, no CSS, each absent when empty.
  - "Сетива": `<sense name> <n> фута` per `sheet.senses` entry, names from the same table as `server/export_fields.py` (`_SENSE_NAMES`, incl. `devilsSight`).
  - "Съпротиви и имунитети": "Устойчивост / Имунитет / Уязвимост към щета: ..." per `damageModifiers` list plus "Имунитет към състояния: ..." from `conditionImmunities` (same labels and `title` formatting as the exports' "Съпротиви и имунитети" block).
  - "Вродени заклинания": `name (uses[, от ниво N]) - note [source]`, the Markdown line plus the origin label (`source`, e.g. Eldritch Invocation) which the Markdown line omits.
- **Placement decision.** Only the Export page (the sheet preview). The Species/Class pages were left alone because senses and resistances come from many sources (species, subclass, invocations, feats) and the plan names the Export preview.
- **Verification.** `npm run build` green. Browser, 375 px, Vite + Flask, draft in `localStorage["dnd-builder-draft-v2"]`: Tiefling (PHB) / Draconic (XPHB) Sorcerer 6 shows darkvision 60, resistances Fire and Acid, innate Hellish Rebuke (1/day, from level 3) and Darkness (1/day, from level 5); Warlock 17 (Devil's Sight, Mask of Many Faces, Armor of Shadows) shows devil's sight 120 and four at-will invocation spells with the source label. `scrollWidth` was 375 in both, no console errors. No frontend test setup exists (no test runner in `package.json`), so no frontend test was added.
- **Totals.** Server 4500 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped (unchanged, no server code touched).

## RPF Review-fix batch after RP14 (2026-10-23)

Nine review findings. Items 1-3, 5-9 change behaviour; item 4 is test infrastructure. Mixed / 2014 output is untouched except where stated (items 5, 8: the golden rebaseline below). Each item has its own test.

1. **Dance Unarmored Defense excludes a Shield** (2024). `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS_2024["dance-xphb-3-unarmored-defense"]` now has `allows_shield: False`. The child record states no condition, but its parent Dazzling Footwork says "While you aren't wearing armor or wielding a Shield, you gain the following benefits."; the sentence is pinned through a new `parent_quotes` field (checked against the parent record like `quotes`). Body armor still replaces the formula through `armor_class_from_gear`. The former "shield allowed" design note (RP2) was corrected in place.
2. **Unconditional condition immunities.** Effect key `condition_immune` (`features.class_condition_immunities`, merged without duplicates into `sheet.conditionImmunities` after the species / feat immunities): Archfey 10 Beguiling Defenses (Charmed) and Land 10 Nature's Ward (Poisoned only; its land-dependent damage Resistance is no longer in `CLASS_FEATURE_NOTES_ONLY_2024` wording but stays out of the effect). Conditional ones stay notes (Aura of Courage / Devotion, Mindless Rage). 2024 only, level-gated, subclass-gated.
3. **Gloom Stalker Initiative.** Effect key `initiative_ability: "WIS"` on `gloom-stalker-xphb-3-dread-ambusher`; `features.class_initiative_bonus(effects, modifiers)` adds `max(0, WIS modifier)` (never negative) to `derived.initiative` through the existing `feat_init_bonus` argument. 2024, Ranger 3+ Gloom Stalker only.
4. **Kitchen-sink filler** (`tests/kitchen_sink._store`) routes a feat card by its cid prefix the way the frontend does (`class:<id>:` into that class's choices, `species:` into the species choices, any other into the background choices), so feat-dependent cards (Magic Initiate spells) are filled. New kitchen sink `magic-initiate-2024` (Fighter 1, Human / Acolyte XPHB) and an export test. Tests only.
5. **Carouser (ABH) gaming set is a pick card.** The raw `{"gaming set": true}` was converted to a fixed grant with the generic id `gaming-set`. `catalog/official/proficiencies._GENERIC_TOOL_KEYS` now excludes it from the fixed grants and `_background_tool_choice_envelopes_from_5etools` emits a one-pick `any_tool` / "Gaming Sets" envelope (every ruleset; the converter output changes, so a catalog cache rebuild applies). The RP9 "same as above" equipment sentinel now resolves from that pick; only Wayfarer keeps the nested gaming-set pick. A scan test asserts that no other background or race has a generic tool key as a fixed grant.
6. **PHB 2024 weights.** `reference_data.ITEM_WEIGHTS_2024 = {"rope-hempen-50-feet": 5, "warhammer": 5}` (lb) is merged under `PACKS_2024` weights when `resolve` calls `carrying_summary` and the ruleset is 2024. All other gear / weapon / armor / tool weights were compared with the PHB 2024 tables and are equal. Mixed / 2014 keep the SRD weights (rope 10, warhammer 2). Packs keep their table weight (the pack total already counted 5 lb of rope).
7. **Feywild Gifts is optional.** `SUBCLASS_OPTIONS_2024` entry has `optional: True`; `subclass_options_2024.templates` copies it onto the card (`Choice.optional`). It stays selectable and `pending` while unpicked, but `app._pending_export_problems` skips optional cards, and the frontend mirrors that: `ExportPage` pending list, `StepNav` badge count, `ChoiceCard` pending dot and `firstPendingId` (prefers a non-optional pending card). This answers the RP13 design note "(1)" for the Gifts card; every other card is still required. `Choice.optional?: boolean` in `types.ts`.
8. **Disabled ASI pick and duplicate skill picks.** (a) In `resolve`, a stored pick on an ASI card (`_ASI_CID_RE`) whose option is offered but `disabled` (cap 20 after a ruleset / species switch, an unmet feat prerequisite) now also gets a non-blocking `pruned` entry with the option's own `disabledReason` ("... is not available (<reason>) and was ignored."); before, the card silently turned pending. A valid pick, an unpicked card and a pick not offered at all (the existing "no longer offered" entry) are unchanged. This is why `test_grappler_own_chosen_bonus_cannot_satisfy_its_own_prerequisite_at_an_asi_level` now expects two `pruned` entries (the ASI card for the disabled Grappler pick plus the stale `ability-bonus` sub-choice); the rule it protects (a feat's own chosen bonus does not satisfy its own prerequisite: STR stays 11, no feat choices cascade, the prerequisite problem is raised) is asserted as before. (b) `_gate_duplicate_skill_picks` (all rulesets, PHB "you can't gain the same proficiency twice"): the ordinary class / species / multiclass skill pick cards (`prof-choice-` / `proficiency-choice`, not expertise, not `profOrExpertise`, not the RP12 positioned cards, which have their own gate) are walked in (class order, class level) order, species first and a multiclass card at (its class, 1). A card keeps the skills held by the cards before it out of its options ("already proficient", disabled) and drops a stored duplicate with a `pruned` entry; status is recomputed. It runs before the derived skill set is built.
9. **`SheetInnateSpell.level` is `number | null`** in `types.ts` (an innate spell without a catalog record has no level); `ExportPage` and `SheetTraitsSummary` already tolerated it. Guarded by a text test in `test_types_drift.py` (also pins `Choice.optional`).

**Golden rebaseline `RPF`** (`REBASELINES["RPF"]`, cases `*/src-*/multi-rogue3-bard2`). The golden's multiclass Rogue 3 / Bard 2 held a real duplicate: the Bard multiclass skill card (entered second) was filled with Acrobatics like the Rogue's card. The filler re-picks the first enabled skill (Animal Handling). `--rebaseline RPF` changed 4 of 231 cases (2014 and mixed, SRD-only and all sources) and 14 paths each, 56 in total: `draft.classes[1].choices.class:bard:multiclass-prof-choice-0[0]` (1), `result.choices[class:bard:multiclass-prof-choice-0]` options Acrobatics / Athletics / Deception / Intimidation `disabled` + `disabledReason` and `selected[0]` (9), `result.choices[class:rogue:1:expertise-choice:rogue-expertise-1].options[skill-animal-handling].disabled|disabledReason` (2, a now proficient skill becomes eligible for Expertise), `result.sheet.proficiencies.skills` (1), `result.derived.skills.skill-animal-handling` (1). Verified after the fact by diffing the gz at `cfbfa8f^` against HEAD with the module's `_norm` / `diff_paths`: exactly these 4 cases changed, every path matches the allowlist, no other case moved. `test_rpf_cases_are_the_multiclass_rogue_bard_only_and_hold_no_duplicate_skill_pick` pins the case set and the absence of duplicates; the append-only `REBASELINES` tuple is now `("RP7", "RPF")`.

**Surfaces.** `types.ts`: `Choice.optional`, `SheetInnateSpell.level` nullable. No `export_fields.py` change. Tests: `test_xphb_feature_effects_2024.py` (items 1-3), `test_background_gaming_set_pick.py` (5), `test_item_weights_2024.py` (6), `test_subclass_options_2024.py` (7), `test_asi_disabled_pruned.py` and `test_skill_pick_duplicates.py` (8), `test_export_fields.py` (4), `test_types_drift.py` (9).

**Tests / totals.** Server suite 4500 -> 4536 passed over the batch (final run after this commit: 4536; the golden was rebaselined only as listed above), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green.

## RP15 (E11b) 2014 Spell Mastery / Signature Spells (2026-10-23)

Second slice that deliberately changes mixed / 2014 output, through the `--rebaseline` policy (never `--regenerate`). Tests first (33 of 38 new tests failed before the wiring), then the implementation.

**Rules (SRD, `catalog.features`, pinned verbatim by test).** `spell-mastery` (Wizard 18): "Choose a 1st-level wizard spell and a 2nd-level wizard spell that are in your spellbook. You can cast those spells at their lowest level without expending a spell slot when you have them prepared." `signature-spell` (Wizard 20, singular id and name): "Choose two 3rd-level wizard spells in your spellbook as your signature spells. You always have these spells prepared, they don't count against the number of spells you have prepared, and you can cast each of them once at 3rd level without expending a spell slot. When you do so, you can't do so again until you finish a short or long rest."

- **Deviation from the task text.** The SRD Spell Mastery has no casting-time condition (the "1 action" rule is XPHB 2024 only); the catalog text and the plan agree, so the 2014 cards offer every book spell of the level (a reaction such as Shield and a 1-minute spell such as Identify are valid). Pinned by test (`"casting time" not in` the text).
- **Cards.** Under mixed and 2014 a Wizard (class level, multiclass-aware) gets `class:wizard:18:spell-mastery-1` / `-2` (one level 1 / one level 2 book spell) and `class:wizard:20:signature-spells` (two level 3 book spells): the SAME cids as 2024. `reference_data.WIZARD_SPELL_PICKS_2014` is the SRD spec (same keys as the 2024 table plus `always_prepared` and `feature_name`; the 2024 entries default `always_prepared` True); `reference_data.wizard_spell_picks(ruleset)` selects the table, and `class_choices_2024.wizard_spell_pick_templates` (rename skipped) now runs for every ruleset: no card without its feature record (`spell-mastery` / `signature-spell` for 2014, the `xphb-` ones for 2024). The 2024 cards, their casting-time rule and tests are unchanged.
- **Prepared state.** Spell Mastery picks are NOT always prepared ("when you have them prepared"): they are not in `alwaysPrepared` and the prepared card does not disable them; they list under `sheet.spells[wizard].spellMastery`. Signature picks are always prepared (reason "Signature Spells", prepared-card option disabled "always prepared (Signature Spells)"), listed under `signatureSpells` and in `alwaysPrepared`. `resolve.py` builds both keys from `wizard_spell_picks(ruleset)` and only extends `always_ids` for `always_prepared` specs (`WIZARD_SPELL_PICKS_2024` import replaced).
- **Resource.** `wizard:signature-spells` (name "Signature Spells", uses = picked spells, `short-or-long`, note "Cast each of ... once at 3rd level without a spell slot") for 2014: `reference_data.SIGNATURE_SPELLS_2014`; `class_resources._wizard` appends `_signature_spells` outside 2024 (2024 keeps building it in `_tail_2024`), after Arcane Recovery.
- **sheet.features.** `_spell_pick_labels(choices, ruleset)` now serves every ruleset; the non-overlay (SRD) branch of `build_features` attaches the picked names to the rows "Spell Mastery" and "Signature Spell" (the SRD names; 2024 keeps "Signature Spells").
- **Cascades never block.** `_is_count_variable_choice` already covered the cids (level-down, a pick that left the book, more picks than the count are non-blocking `pruned`). `class_choices_2024.mark_unavailable` now also runs outside 2024, but only for the Wizard pick cids (`_WIZARD_PICK_CARD_RE`): an empty book or a book without a spell of that level gives an option-less `unavailable` card that does not block export, while the empty spellbook card still does.
- **Surfaces.** No new keys: `spellMastery` / `signatureSpells`, `SPELL_PICK_LINES` exports, `ClassPage.isSpellChoiceId` and `types.ts` already cover the cids and keys (C6d). Frontend untouched, `npm run build` not needed. No `export_fields.py` change.

**Golden rebaseline `RP15`** (`REBASELINES["RP15"]`, cases `*/src-*/wizard20`; paths written from the rule matrix: the three new cards at their roots, the filler's picks in the draft, `sheet.spells[N].spellMastery|signatureSpells|alwaysPrepared`, the `wizard:signature-spells` resource, the `sheet.features[N].choice` rows, the prepared card's `disabled|disabledReason` flags). VERIFIED: the golden Wizard 20 books hold level 1 and level 2 spells only (43 + 1), so Spell Mastery is filled (Absorb Elements, Acid Arrow) and the Signature card is `unavailable` ("no option is offered yet", never blocking); hence the resource and the prepared-flag paths match 0 changes (kept in the allowlist because the rule produces them for a book with level 3 spells; the new kitchen sink covers them). Printed summary of the real run (the dry run printed the same):

```
rebaseline RP15 (SRD Wizard Spell Mastery / Signature Spell cards (E11b))
  matching cases changed: 4 of 231
      8 changed path(s) match ^result\.choices\[class:wizard:18:spell-mastery-[12]\]$
      4 changed path(s) match ^result\.choices\[class:wizard:20:signature-spells\]$

      8 changed path(s) match ^result\.sheet\.spells\[\d+\]\.(spellMastery|signatureSpells|alwaysPrepared)$
      0 changed path(s) match ^result\.derived\.classResources\[wizard:signature-spells\]$

      0 changed path(s) match ^result\.choices\[class:wizard:spells-known\]\.options\[[^\]]+\]\.(disabled|disabledReason)$

```

The 4 cases: `mixed` and `2014`, each `src-all` and `src-srd`, `wizard20`. Verified afterwards by diffing the gz at HEAD against the working tree with `_keyed` / `diff_paths`: exactly those 4 cases moved (apart from the stripped `derived.featResources`), every path matches the allowlist, the other 227 cases are byte-identical. Then `--append` added the kitchen sink `wizard20-2014` (the plan said `wizard18-2014`; level 20 also covers Signature, the resource and the prepared flags): stored book with Shield, Magic Missile, Detect Magic, Web, Haste, Fireball, Counterspell, Fly; Mastery Shield (a reaction) and Web, Signature Fireball and Counterspell. New case names: `ks/src-all/wizard20-2014`, `ks/src-srd/wizard20-2014` (233 cases). The append-only `REBASELINES` tuple is now `("RP7", "RPF", "RP15")`; `test_rp15_cases_are_the_wizard_20_cases_and_hold_the_srd_pick_cards` pins the case set and the content.

**Edited existing tests (intended reversal of "2024 only").** `test_class_spell_grants_2024.py::test_no_mastery_or_signature_cards_outside_2024` became `test_outside_2024_the_cards_are_the_srd_ones_with_the_same_cids`; `test_f3a_fixes.py::test_f2b_helper_marks_only_new_2024_cards_with_zero_enabled_options` now expects the Wizard pick card to be marked `unavailable` outside 2024 and every other card untouched. `test_export_fields.py`: `_EXPECT` and the stage-lines spot check gained `wizard20-2014`. All 2024 assertions are untouched.

**Tests.** `tests/test_wizard_picks_2014.py` (38, rule matrix in the docstring): text pins, cards and options (no casting-time rule), not-always-prepared Mastery, always-prepared Signature, resource, features rows, prune and unavailable cascades, scope (non-Wizard, multiclass level), 2024 unchanged, patched catalog without the SRD features, Markdown / PDF lines, timing.

**Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, Wizard 20 with all picks): mixed 3.4, 2014 2.5, 2024 2.8 (limit 50).

**Totals.** Server suite 4536 -> 4582 passed (38 new in `test_wizard_picks_2014.py`, 2 new golden cases, 1 new golden pin, 2 export-sweep parametrizations, minus nothing: the two edited tests keep their counts), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP16 (E11a) SRD Bard Magical Secrets under mixed / 2014 (2026-10-23)

Third slice that deliberately changes mixed / 2014 output, through the `--rebaseline` policy (never `--regenerate`). Tests first (21 of 33 new tests failed before the wiring), then the implementation.

**Rules (SRD, `catalog.features`, pinned verbatim by test).** `magical-secrets-1/2/3` (Bard 10 / 14 / 18, all three carry the same text): "Choose two spells from any class, including this one. A spell you choose must be of a level you can cast, as shown on the Bard table, or a cantrip. The chosen spells count as bard spells for you and are included in the number in the Spells Known column of the Bard table. You learn two additional spells from any class at 14th level and again at 18th level." The Lore Bard's Additional Magical Secrets (level 6, "don't count against the number of bard spells you know") is RPX1, not this slice.

- **Card.** The existing `class:bard:spells-known` card (same cid, so picks survive a ruleset switch) gains, from Bard 10, every spell that sits on some class list but not on the Bard's, of level 1 .. the highest Bard slot level (`_magical_secrets_options`, ruleset / sources aware, a stored pick hidden by `sources` stays offered), plus the other classes' cantrips. The Bard's own cantrips stay on the Cantrips card. Options are labelled "<Spell> (Magical Secrets)" (`class_choices_2024.label_magical_secrets`, now also active for the SRD feature), groups stay `spell-level-N` (the other-class cantrips are `spell-level-0`, shown first under "Трикове"). A spell that is on no class list is not "from a class" and is not offered. The description gains " Magical Secrets: up to N of them may be spells of any class's list, or another class's cantrips; they count in this number."
- **Decided point (cantrips count).** "included in the number in the Spells Known column": the card count is unchanged (Bard 10: 14) and a cantrip taken this way uses one of the known slots (tested: 13 own spells + Fire Bolt fills 14, the next pick is `pruned` over the count). It lists in `sheet.spells[bard].known` with level 0.
- **Cap** (`_apply_secrets_cap`, the `_apply_school_limit` pattern): picks off the Bard list <= `class_choices_2024.magical_secrets_cap_2014` = 2 x the SRD features reached (10: 2, 14: 4, 18: 6; 0 for non-Bards, under 2024 and when the catalog lacks the feature records). At the cap the other off-list options are disabled "Magical Secrets: N of N used"; a stored off-list pick over the cap is disabled too, so the generic pass drops it as a non-blocking `pruned` entry (`:spells-known` is already count-variable) and it does not take a count slot (the cap walks the stored picks in order, like the generic pass). Level-down 14 -> 10 keeps two, below 10 drops all. No new problem, never blocks export.
- **Approximation (as in C6d).** No level-up history: the rule says each feature's two spells are chosen when it is gained (and at the level they could be cast); the engine only counts the total off-list picks against the features reached, so the per-feature spell-level limit is not enforced and a pick may later be swapped by editing the card.
- **Scope.** Bard class level (multiclass: the Bard's own level and Bard table; the other class's card is untouched, tested with Bard 10 / Wizard 3 and Wizard 8 / Bard 6). 2024 keeps its XPHB `expanded` mechanism and tests unchanged. `resolve()` only wires `secrets_cap=` into `_spell_choice_templates`.
- **Surfaces.** No new keys, `types.ts` / exports / frontend untouched (`npm run build` not needed). Phone check by reading the existing picker (`ChoicePicker`): a filter box appears above 20 options and matches the label, so "Fire Bolt" or "(Magical Secrets)" filters; level headings come from the `spell-level-N` groups; disabled options are hidden behind "Показване на недостъпните (N)". Option counts of the card, mixed all sources: Bard 10 405, Bard 20 528 (2014 497, 2024 462 already before this slice); `sources ["srd"]` Bard 10 228.

**Golden rebaseline `RP16`** (`REBASELINES["RP16"]`, cases `*/src-*/bard11` and `*/src-*/bard20`; paths written from the rule matrix before the dry run: an added option at its root, a changed option's label / group / disabled / disabledReason, the card description). VERIFIED by the dry run and by diffing the gz at HEAD against the working tree with `_norm` / `diff_paths`: exactly these 8 cases moved, every path matches the allowlist, no `draft` path moved (the stored picks are the old list's first spells), the other 225 cases are byte-identical. The stored golden picks are not changed, so no label / flag path fired (0 matches, kept in the allowlist because the rule produces them for a stored Magical Secrets pick; the new kitchen sink covers that). Printed summary of the real run (the dry run printed the same):

```
rebaseline RP16 (SRD Bard Magical Secrets (E11a))
  matching cases changed: 8 of 233
   2068 changed path(s) match ^result\.choices\[class:bard:spells-known\]\.options\[[^\]]+\]$
      0 changed path(s) match ^result\.choices\[class:bard:spells-known\]\.options\[[^\]]+\]\.(label|group|disabled|disabledReason)$
      8 changed path(s) match ^result\.choices\[class:bard:spells-known\]\.description$

```

## RPX1 (Extra 1, D4) SRD Lore Bard: Bonus Proficiencies and Additional Magical Secrets under mixed / 2014 (2026-10-23)

Fourth slice that deliberately changes mixed / 2014 output, through the `--rebaseline` policy (never `--regenerate`). Tests first (`tests/test_lore_bard_2014.py`, 34 tests, rule matrix in the docstring); each wiring step was removed once to check the tests fail without it (gate 2 fail, count-variable 1, sheet known 4, SRD card builder 8, spec lookup 3, `mark_unavailable` 1). Ruleset 2024 keeps its XPHB Lore card (RP12) unchanged; under 2024 a stored SRD `lore` subclass shows neither card.

**Rules (SRD, `catalog.features`, subclass `lore`, pinned verbatim by test).** `bonus-proficiencies` (Bard 3): "When you join the College of Lore at 3rd level, you gain proficiency with three skills of your choice." `additional-magical-secrets` (Bard 6): "At 6th level, you learn two spells of your choice from any class. A spell you choose must be of a level you can cast, as shown on the Bard table, or a cantrip. The chosen spells count as bard spells for you but don't count against the number of bard spells you know."

- **Bonus Proficiencies.** `reference_data.SUBCLASS_PICKS_2014` (one entry, same shape as `SUBCLASS_PICKS_2024`, with a pinned `quote`). `class_choices_2024.subclass_pick_templates` uses it outside 2024 and `pick_spec_for_card` searches both tuples, so the card `class:bard:3:feature:bonus-proficiencies` (count 3, the 18 skills) is a RP12 positioned proficiency card with no further wiring: `_gate_positioned_proficiencies` runs under every ruleset, so a skill held at or before (class order, level 3) is disabled (background: the generic "already granted by Acolyte" reason; class cards, an earlier class' skills: "already proficient" with a non-blocking `pruned` for a stored duplicate), later multiclass skill cards see the picks, and the Bard Expertise cards (3 / 10) offer them. `mark_unavailable` now also covers these SRD cards outside 2024 (like the Wizard pick cards; the XPHB pick cards stay 2024-only). Attaches to the "Bonus Proficiencies" row of `sheet.features` (existing `_CLASS_CHOICE_ID`). Not count-variable (the options do not follow the level).
- **Additional Magical Secrets.** `reference_data.ADDITIONAL_SECRETS_2014`; card `class:bard:6:feature:additional-magical-secrets` (count 2) from `resolve._additional_secrets_templates` (only the Bard with the SRD subclass `lore`, ruleset mixed / 2014, Bard level >= the record's level, the feature record present). Options (`_additional_secrets_options`): every spell that sits on SOME class list (the Bard's own included, "any class"; a spell on no class list is not offered), ruleset / sources aware (a stored pick hidden by `sources` stays offered), of level 0 .. the highest slot level of the BARD table (the Bard's own level row, also in a multiclass), so cantrips of every class too; groups `spell-level-N`. Count-variable (`_is_count_variable_choice`): a pick that stops being offered or exceeds the count is a non-blocking `pruned`. Frontend: `ClassPage.isSpellChoiceId` also matches `:feature:additional-magical-secrets`, so the card sits in the Spells tab (`npm run build` green; no other frontend change).
- **Not counted, and the interplay.** The known-spells card keeps its count (Bard 6: 9 for Lore and non-Lore alike); the RP16 Magical Secrets cap (10 / 14 / 18) counts only the known card's off-list picks, so these two picks do not use it (tested at Bard 10: both cards full, `Magical Secrets: 2 of 2 used` intact). Duplicates are gated by `class_choices_2024.gate_additional_secrets`, run after the generic pass: a spell kept on the Cantrips / Spells Known card is disabled here ("already known") and a stored duplicate is dropped with a `pruned` entry (the earlier cards win); then a spell kept here is disabled on those two cards ("chosen as Additional Magical Secrets"). A spell picked as a Magical Secret at 10 / 14 / 18 sits on the known card, so it is the same duplicate case.
- **Sheet.** The picks list in `sheet.spells[bard].known` (a cantrip with level 0, as the RP16 secrets cantrips) after the ordinary known spells; PDF / Markdown / JSON exports read that list (a Markdown line per spell, tested), so no export key changed. `sheet.features` shows the picked spell names on the "Additional Magical Secrets" row. Bard is a "known" caster, so there is no prepared list to touch.
- **Approximation.** As for RP16: no level-up history, so a pick is not tied to the level at which it was chosen (only "a level you can cast now"); the picks are not removed when the slot level grows or the Bard level shrinks unless they stop being offered.
- **Open points left as they are.** (1) A stored duplicate occupies one of the two count slots before the gate drops it (so with three stored picks, one a duplicate, the third is truncated first). (2) A Wizard multiclass' own spell cards are not part of the duplicate gate (a Bard 6 / Wizard 3 may take Fireball as Additional Magical Secret and on the Wizard's cards; they are separate classes' spell lists).

**Golden rebaseline `RPX1`** (`REBASELINES["RPX1"]`, cases `*/src-*/bard3`, `*/src-*/bard11`, `*/src-*/bard20`; the paths were written from the rule matrix above before the dry run, then compared with it: the dry run showed one path I had not listed, the picks in `sheet.spells[bard].known` as keyed paths per added spell, which I fixed in the regex; I removed a passive-score path I had listed that no case produced). VERIFIED by diffing the gz at HEAD (copy before the run) against the working tree with `_norm` / `diff_paths`: exactly these 12 cases moved (the Lore Bard 3 / 11 / 20 cases under mixed and 2014, each `src-all` and `src-srd`), every path matches the allowlist, no other case moved, 235 -> 237 cases. ## RPX2 (Extra 2, D4) SRD packs weigh their contents under mixed / 2014 (2026-10-23)

**Rule.** The SRD 5.1 / PHB 2014 "Equipment Packs" list a cost and the items inside, no weight of their own, so a pack weighs the sum of its contents. The catalog pack records (`burglars-pack` ... `scholars-pack`) carry `contents` ([{item: {index}, quantity}]) and `weight: None`; VERIFIED by script: all 7 have complete contents, every member exists in the catalog with a numeric weight. The only other records with `weight: None` are the 9 mounts and 6 vessels (not carried gear, no `contents`), which stay 0; the catalog has no other container with a missing weight (Backpack 5 lb, Chest 25 lb, Pouch 1 lb are set). `saddle-pack` is a saddle (15 lb), not an equipment pack.

**Sums (lb).** Burglar's 47.5, Diplomat's 36, Dungeoneer's 61.5, Entertainer's 38, Explorer's 59, Priest's 18, Scholar's 10 (2024 keeps the RP8 table: 42 / 39 / 55 / 58.5 / 55 / 29 / 22; the SRD sum is never used under 2024).

**Engine.** `derived.pack_weights_from_contents(items)` returns `{pack id: lb}` for records with `weight` None and non-empty, fully numeric `contents` (a pack with an unknown or weightless member is left out rather than guessed; a record with a weight keeps it). `resolve` passes it as the `weights` override of `carrying_summary` for every ruleset except 2024 (2024 keeps `ITEM_WEIGHTS_2024` + `PACKS_2024`). The catalog item record is untouched and the sheet entries gain no `contents` (not requested; mixed / 2014 still do not list pack contents). No new output key, so `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched.

**Deliberate test changes.** `test_packs_2024.py::test_mixed_and_2014_are_unchanged` (renamed `..._have_no_contents_and_the_srd_sum_weight`) asserted the SRD pack counts 0; it now asserts +59 lb for the Explorer's Pack and still asserts no `contents`. `test_worn_wielded.py::test_drafts_without_worn_or_wielded_resolve_exactly_as_before_g2` (pre-G2 baseline, cases `ks-barbarian20-2014` and `srd-barbarian5-chain-in-pack`) now allows `carryingCapacity.carried` to differ from the baseline by exactly the SRD pack weights of the sheet's granted / chosen / added entries and nothing else. New: `tests/test_packs_srd_weights.py` (22: member data, sums, degrade, added / chosen / granted packs, qty, 2024 unchanged, mounts) and `test_rpx2_every_case_counts_its_srd_pack_weight` in the golden module; 19 of the 22 failed before the engine change.

**Golden rebaseline `RPX2`** (`REBASELINES["RPX2"]`, cases `*`: every one of the 237 golden cases holds an SRD pack, the fixed one or the filler's first package; paths `^result\.derived\.carryingCapacity\.carried$` and `...overCapacity$`, written from the rule matrix). The append-only `REBASELINES` tuple is now `("RP7", "RPF", "RP15", "RP16", "RPX1", "RPX2")`.

## RPX3 (Extra 3, D4) Counts of the starting-equipment text are real quantities under mixed / 2014 (2026-10-23)

**Data check (VERIFIED by script over the 12 SRD classes and every background).** The SRD / 5etools data keeps the counts: `counted_reference.count` in the class envelopes (Barbarian "two handaxes" 2, Fighter "two handaxes" 2, "20 arrows" 20, "20 bolts" 20, Paladin "five javelins" 5, Ranger "two shortswords" 2, Rogue "20 arrows", Cleric / Sorcerer / Warlock "20 bolts") and `quantity` on the fixed `starting_equipment` entries (Barbarian 4 javelins, Monk 10 darts, Rogue / Sorcerer / Warlock 2 daggers, Ranger 20 arrows). The converter never dropped them; the engine did: `keep_counts` (C9b) was set only on the 2024 envelopes, so `quantities` was missing for every other bundle, and a single counted option ("two handaxes" is a plain `counted_reference`, not a `multiple`) never got quantities even with the flag. Bard, Druid and Wizard have no counted phrase. The SRD Acolyte has none either (its SRD record has no incense / prayer book); the counts under mixed come from the 5etools backgrounds (e.g. Criminal XPHB: 2 daggers, 2 pouches; 27 backgrounds, the same counts RP9 reads for 2024), whose option labels already said "2x Pouch" while the inventory held one.

**Ammunition is one pack (the one real trap).** The catalog `arrow` / `crossbow-bolt` / `sling-bullet` (20) and `blowgun-needle` (50) are already packs (item `quantity`, F3a-6; the 2024 packages map "Arrows (20)" to ONE arrow). Applying "20 arrows" literally would give qty 20 (400 arrows, 20 lb). `catalog.choices.pack_units(item, count)` converts a counted phrase of a packed item to pack units (rounds up, never below 1) and `pack_quantities` drops quantities that become 1; so the Fighter's, Rogue's, Cleric's, Sorcerer's and Warlock's ammunition bundles stay qty 1. The same rule fixes the Ranger's FIXED arrows (`_fixed_equipment`), which were granted with qty 20 under mixed / 2014 (an existing defect of the same family, outside the strict "bundle" wording; deviation noted in the report).

**Engine.** `resolve._equipment_choice_templates` sets `keep_counts` on every envelope in every ruleset (it was set only on the 2024 ones; 2024 output is identical), then runs `pack_quantities` over the option quantities. `choices.build_choice_options` gives a single counted option `quantities` and the label "Nx Name" like the expanded path already did ("2x Handaxe", was "Handaxe"). Option ids and card ids are unchanged (quantities were never part of an id), so stored picks survive. `_chosen_equipment` already reads `quantities` (C9b). Result: Barbarian / Fighter "two handaxes" qty 2 (+4 lb), Paladin five javelins qty 5 (+10 lb), Ranger two shortswords qty 2 (+4 lb), the Ranger's fixed arrow qty 1 (was 20, -19 lb), backgrounds as above. No new output key (`ChoiceOption.quantities?` exists), so `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched.

**Deliberate test changes.** `test_class_equipment_2024.py::test_mixed_and_2014_keep_the_srd_packages_with_qty_one` (renamed `..._with_the_text_counts`): asserted that no mixed option has `quantities`; it now asserts exactly one, the Fighter "two handaxes" (`{"handaxe": 2}`), no ammunition quantity and no `keep_counts` in the data. `test_background_equipment_2024.py::test_mixed_and_2014_keep_the_old_envelope_with_qty_one` (renamed `..._with_the_text_counts`): Acolyte XPHB under mixed now has `quantities {"parchment-one-sheet": 10}` and 10 parchment in the inventory (was 1). `test_worn_wielded.py` pre-G2 baseline test: besides the RPX2 pack weights, `carried` may differ by exactly the quantity deltas times the item weights, and the touched `quantities` / `qty` / "Nx " label fields are neutralised on both sides; everything else must be identical. New: `tests/test_srd_bundle_quantities.py` (44; 23 failed before the engine change) and `test_rpx3_cases_hold_the_text_counts_and_no_ammunition_stack` in the golden module.

**Golden rebaseline `RPX3`** (`REBASELINES["RPX3"]`; cases `*/src-*/barbarian*`, `fighter*`, `paladin*`, `ranger*`, `feats-mixed-fighter14`, `psi-warrior11`, `totem-bear6`, `multi-barbarian3-monk2`; paths written from the rule matrix: the four cards' option `label` / `quantities`, `sheet.equipment.(granted|chosen)[n].qty`, `carryingCapacity.(carried|overCapacity)`). ## RPX4 (Extra 4, D4) All four PHB gaming sets on every gaming-set card (2026-10-23)

**Data check (VERIFIED).** The catalog items had two gaming sets (SRD `dice-set` 1 sp / 0 lb, `playing-card-set` 5 sp / 0 lb, `tool_category` "Gaming Sets"); `reference_data.TOOLS` (the tool-proficiency list behind every `any_tool`, `any_skill_or_tool` and Gaming Sets card) had the same two. The local 5etools data does NOT hold the missing sets as items (`items-base.json` has only weapons / armor `baseitem`s plus the `Gaming Set` item TYPE; no `Dragonchess` / `Three-Dragon Ante` record anywhere), so they come from the PHB Tools table (p. 154, gaming set rows; the PHB 2024 table lists the same four): Dragonchess set 1 gp, 1/2 lb; Three-Dragon Ante set 1 gp, no weight (Dice 1 sp, Playing card 5 sp, both 0). The SRD Dice Set text already names Three-Dragon Ante (pinned by a test).

**Change.** `reference_data.PHB_GAMING_SETS` (index, name, cost gp, weight) holds the two records; `TOOLS` gets `dragonchess-set` and `three-dragon-ante-set` (alphabetical: Dice, Dragonchess, Playing Card, Three-Dragon Ante). `loader._add_phb_gaming_sets` adds the item records (source `PHB`, ruleset `2014`, SRD-shaped, the SRD gaming-set description text reused; never replaces an existing record) right after the SRD set they sort next to, called from `_merge_official_items`, so it works without any 5etools data. The ids equal the TOOLS indexes, so a picked proficiency is also the inventory item for the RP9 `background-tool` sentinel and the Monk sentinel (`dragonchess-set` +0.5 lb). One rule had to give way: `choices.equipment_category_options` skips every non-SRD item ("imported sourcebook items never change a starting-equipment option set"), which would have kept the Wayfarer nested card at two sets; it now exempts exactly the PHB gaming-set ids for the `gaming-sets` category. The explicit two-way Soldier (PHB) card "bone dice or deck of cards" stays two options: that is the book's text, not a gaming-set pick (a test pins it).

**Where it shows.** Background tool picks (Noble PHB "any gaming set", Carouser ABH, Far Traveler / Inheritor / Knight of the Order SCAG musical-or-gaming, Urban Bounty Hunter, Waterdhavian Noble), the Skilled feat's skills-or-tools list, the 2024 Wayfarer nested equipment card, `/api/items` (with `sources` containing only `srd` the PHB sets are filtered out of the item BROWSER like every other non-SRD item, while the pick cards always list them), in all rulesets and for sources [] and ["srd", "phb"]. No new output key; `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (the frontend has no hard-coded tool list).

**Golden.** NOT rebaselined: a scan of all 237 golden results found no card that offers a gaming set (the golden drafts hold the SRD Acolyte, the SRD Monk card is artisan's tools and instruments only, no golden draft holds a Skilled feat), and `test_c_series_golden.py` is green on the unchanged gz. `REBASELINES` stays `("RP7", "RPF", "RP15", "RP16", "RPX1", "RPX2", "RPX3")`.

**Deliberate test changes.** Counts +2 items: `test_app.py::test_items_endpoint_lists_every_bundled_item`, `test_loader.py::test_items_indexed_by_srd_index_covers_all_categories` (237 + 2 + 5), `test_item_import.py::test_real_items_are_imported_and_srd_wins` (the non-SRD item set gains the two PHB sets). Option lists: `test_resolve.py::test_any_gaming_set_wildcard_tool_choice_on_a_background_offers_only_gaming_sets` (Noble PHB: four, was two), `test_background_equipment_2024.py::test_wayfarer_gaming_set_is_a_nested_card_with_the_catalog_sets` (four), `test_patched_catalog_without_gaming_sets_drops_only_the_option_that_needs_them` (pops all four). New: `tests/test_gaming_sets_phb.py` (19; 16 failed before the change).
