## RP17 (F3, E13) 2024 subclass spell picks: Savants, Improved Illusions, Magical Discoveries, Circle of the Land (2026-10-23)

Ruleset 2024 only (decision D2); mixed / 2014 are byte-identical (`test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated; no `REBASELINES` entry). Tests first (`tests/test_subclass_spell_picks_2024.py`, 59, rule matrix and governing texts in the docstring); 12 wiring steps were each removed once and every removal made at least one test fail (Savant join of the book, book closing a Savant pick, Minor Illusion override, the late gate, Discoveries' always-prepared hand-off, the Land variant lookup, Nature's Ward effect, count-variable hook, unavailable hook, the three sheet additions).

- **Converter** (`catalog/official/subclasses.py`, additive keys on XPHB subclass records only, six records): `spell_choices` = `[{level, mode "known" | "prepared", filter}]`, one entry per `{choose}` item of the single `known` / `prepared` block, filter parsed by the feat `_parse_spell_filter` (Abjurer / Diviner / Evoker / Illusionist: 9 entries each at 3, 3, 5, 7, ..., 17 with `school`, `class ["wizard"]`, level list; Lore: 2 prepared entries at 6, `class [cleric, druid, wizard]`). `spells_by_variant` = `{arid | polar | temperate | tropical: [spells entries]}` from the four NAMED blocks of Circle of the Land (key = slugged name without " Land"; entries have the `spells` shape, prerequisite `druid-N`, mode "always", 6 spells each). `_subclass_spell_entries` was split into `_block_spell_entries` (same output). The data filters include level 0, but the Savant text is "Wizard spells" and the 2024 spellbook holds level 1+ spells (C4 precedent, plan), so Savant options start at level 1.
- **Data** (`reference_data`): `SUBCLASS_SPELL_PICKS_2024` (6 entries, `kind` savant | cantrip | discoveries, verbatim `quotes`, pinned by `test_quotes_match_catalog`; the school word of each Savant is checked against its quote) and the Land entry in `SUBCLASS_OPTIONS_2024` (`land-xphb-3-circle-of-the-land-spells`: 4 options with `variant`, a `ward` block naming the Nature's Ward record and per option the table row `ward_quote` / `ward_resist`). `test_subclass_options_2024.py::test_quotes_match_catalog` pinned the SET of option features; it now also allows the Land key (the one edit of an existing test).
- **Savants.** `engine/subclass_spells_2024.py` (pure; the spell lookup is passed in as a callable, the module never imports resolve). Card `class:wizard:3:feature:<savant fid>` (labels "Abjuration / Divination / Evocation / Illusion Savant"), built by `resolve._spell_choice_templates` (new `subclass_id` argument) right after the spellbook card. Count = reached `known` entries of the school (2 at 3, then 3 at 5, 4 at 7, ..., 9 at 17; pinned against the levels at which the Wizard table opens a new slot level). Options = Wizard-list spells of the school, levels 1 .. min(highest slot level of the Wizard's own table, the levels of the reached filters), `sources` / ruleset aware, a stored hidden pick stays offered, grouped `spell-level-N`. Not counted against the spellbook card. A spell already in the effective book is disabled "already in your spellbook" (a stored duplicate: non-blocking `pruned`); the other way round the book card disables a Savant pick ("chosen as <Savant>"), so each spell is held once and the earlier (book) card wins a stored clash.
- **Cascade.** The valid Savant picks are appended to `wizard_book` (the effective book, what the book card resolves to): the prepared card offers them, Spell Mastery / Signature Spells options are built from them, `sheet.spells[wizard].spellbook` lists them after the book picks (exports print the line, test). Removing the subclass (or another school's subclass, or a level below the next Savant level) makes the book smaller on the next resolve: the prepared / Mastery / Signature picks that depended on a Savant spell are dropped through the existing count-variable `pruned` entries and the Savant card itself through the generic "no longer applies" entry; never a problem, never blocking (tested at Wizard 20 with a Savant Fireball as Signature spell and a Savant level 1 spell as Spell Mastery, for no subclass and for another school).
- **Improved Illusions** (Illusionist 3). "You also know the Minor Illusion cantrip" stays the existing `spells` entry (11b.4: listed in `alwaysPrepared`). The card `class:wizard:3:feature:illusionist-xphb-3-improved-illusions` (count 1, level 0 Wizard cantrips without Minor Illusion or its twin) is always templated for the Illusionist and removed by the late `subclass_spells_2024.gate_improved_illusions` (the Iron Mind pattern, run in `resolve` before the sheet spells are assembled) unless Minor Illusion is already known elsewhere: selected on any other card (the Wizard's Cantrips Known card, another class's, a Magic Initiate cantrip card, ...) or an innate spell of the species / feats (`_species_innate_spells`, `_species_trait_spell_choices`, `_feat_innate_spells`). Known: the card stays, a cantrip held elsewhere is disabled "already known" (a stored duplicate is dropped with a `pruned` entry). Not known: the card is removed and a valid stored pick gets a non-blocking `pruned` entry. To make the Wizard's own Cantrips card a reachable source, Minor Illusion is no longer closed on that card by the Illusionist grant (the subclass grant is dropped from the `always_prepared` map only for that cantrip; the map is otherwise unchanged); when the card exists, Minor Illusion is also removed from `alwaysPrepared` (not listed twice) and the extra pick is appended to `sheet.spells[wizard].cantrips`. It does not change `cantrips_known`. The card attaches to the "Improved Illusions" row of `sheet.features`. No 2024 species in the bundled data grants Minor Illusion innately, so the innate branch is tested through the pure gate function.
- **Magical Discoveries** (Lore 6). Card `class:bard:6:feature:lore-xphb-6-magical-discoveries`, count 2 (prepared entries reached), options = Cleric / Druid / Wizard spells of level 0 .. the highest BARD slot level (the Bard Features table; 3 at Bard 6, 5 at Bard 10), grouped by spell level. Built in `resolve` BEFORE the class spell cards: the valid picks go into the `always_prepared` map with the reason "Magical Discoveries", so the Bard's Cantrips and Prepared cards disable them (a stored duplicate is dropped, non-blocking) and `sheet.spells[bard].alwaysPrepared` lists them. A spell the class already always prepares (class grants such as Bard 20 Power Word Heal, subclass spells) is disabled on the card "always prepared (<source>)". The Bard-only spells are not on the three lists and are not offered.
- **Circle of the Land.** The Land entry makes a required editable card `class:druid:3:feature:land-xphb-3-circle-of-the-land-spells` (Arid / Polar / Temperate / Tropical, no default; blocks export while unpicked like every RP13 card). `subclass_options_2024.picked_variant` reads the pick (raw class choices for the templates, resolved cards for the sheet) and `resolve._subclass_spell_ids(..., variant)` adds the entries of `spells_by_variant[variant]` whose Druid level is reached: all of them "always prepared" with the reason "Circle of the Land", so the Druid's prepared card closes a spell it already has; without a pick there are no Circle Spells (the Druid's own Speak with Animals grant is unaffected). Nature's Ward (Druid 10): the Poisoned immunity is the existing unconditional RPF effect; the land Resistance comes from `subclass_options_2024.ward_effects` (the Nature's Ward record level, the picked option's table row): Arid Fire, Polar Cold, Temperate Lightning, Tropical Poison in `sheet.damageModifiers`; none below Druid 10 or without a pick.
- **Hooks.** `_is_count_variable_choice` and `class_choices_2024.is_new_2024_class_card` (so an empty card is `unavailable` and never blocks export; tested with a catalog that has no spell of the school) recognise the three card kinds through `subclass_spells_2024.is_pick_card`. The Land card is covered by the RP13 option-card hooks. Everything is gated on `ruleset == "2024"`, the XPHB feature record, the selected subclass and the record's level, so an SRD-only catalog, a catalog without the converter keys, mixed, 2014 and a stored hidden XPHB subclass give no card (tested).
- **Frontend.** `ClassPage.isSpellChoiceId` also matches `/:feature:[a-z0-9-]+-(?:savant|improved-illusions|magical-discoveries)$/` (the three card kinds go to the Spells tab; the Land card stays with the features); a server test reads the pattern out of the TSX and matches it against the real cids. Plain markup, no CSS, spell options use the existing `spell-level-N` groups. Browser check at 375 px (Vite + Flask, Evoker 5 draft): the "Evocation Savant" card (3 to pick) sits between Cantrips Known and Prepared Spells on the Spells tab, `scrollWidth` 375. `npm run build` green.
- **No new output keys.** `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` unchanged (cards use `choices[]`, the book / cantrips / alwaysPrepared lists use the existing `sheet.spells` keys, the Land resistance uses `damageModifiers`); `test_types_drift.py` green with an empty `UNTYPED_ALLOWLIST`; exports tested (Markdown spellbook line with the Savant spells).
- **Approximations / decisions.** (1) No level-up history: a Savant pick is not tied to the level it was added at; the options are the union of the reached filters' levels (capped by the slot level), and the count is the number of filters reached. (2) Savant slot level uses the Wizard's own class table, as the spellbook card does (multiclass). (3) Improved Illusions' "already known" looks at picks that are resolved cards plus species / feat innate lists; it does not look at the Wizard's `known` of other kinds (Pact of the Tome rituals are level 1, never a cantrip match). (4) Magical Discoveries' replace-on-level-up is the editable card. (5) The Land type is a build-time card (the book says it is replaced after a Long Rest); no resource note.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 30, ms, level 20, mixed / 2024, quiet machine): Fighter 20 4.4 / 2.9, Wizard 20 4.6 / 3.2, Bard 20 5.4 / 4.3; reference characters (2024): Evoker 17 with a Savant Fireball 3.2, Illusionist 20 with Minor Illusion 3.9, Lore 20 with both Discoveries 5.6, Land 10 Arid 2.0 (limit 50; the tests assert it too).
- **Tests / totals.** `tests/test_subclass_spell_picks_2024.py` (59). Server suite 4752 -> 4811 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green.

## RP18 (F4) 2024 subclass innate spells (2026-10-23)

Ruleset 2024 only (decision D2); mixed / 2014 are byte-identical (`test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated; no `REBASELINES` entry). Tests first (`tests/test_subclass_innate_2024.py`, 28, rule matrix and governing texts in the docstring); 9 wiring steps were each removed once and every removal made at least one test fail (innate assembly, Hex always-prepared hand-off, its ruleset gate, the innate ruleset gate, the level gate, the subclass gate, the Charisma uses, the converter key, the XPHB restriction of the key).

- **Converter** (`catalog/official/subclasses.py`, additive key `innate_spells` on XPHB subclass records only, seven records): `_subclass_innate_spells` turns the `innate` part of the single `additionalSpells` block into one record per spell `{level (None for "_"), spell (catalog id, -xphb first), kind "ritual" | "daily" | "resource" | "plain", uses (the daily / resource key: "1", "cha"), ability (the block's `ability`, upper case), resource (`resourceName`)}`. It only says which spell exists at which class level; what it means is the table below. `spells` of these records is unchanged (the converter still ignores `innate` there).
- **Data** (`reference_data.SUBCLASS_INNATE_2024`, 8 entries keyed by the XPHB feature record): `mode` innate | prepared, `spells`, `uses` (`{cha_min1}` = max(1, Charisma modifier)), `ability` + `ability_from` (text | data | class; the class ones are checked against the class's `spellcasting_ability`), `note`, verbatim `quotes` (pinned by `test_quotes_match_catalog`; every spell's catalog name is in a quote; each frequency word is pinned by `test_frequency_and_ability_are_pinned_to_the_text`; `test_table_agrees_with_converter_data` ties the table to the converter data).
  - Wild Heart 3 Animal Speaker: Beast Sense and Speak with Animals, "ritual only", WIS; Wild Heart 10 Nature Speaker: Commune with Nature, same.
  - Glamour 6 Mantle of Majesty: Command, "1/long rest", CHA, note (Bonus Action, no slot, regain by a level 3+ slot).
  - Psi Warrior 18 Telekinetic Master: Telekinesis, "1/long rest", INT, note (no slot or components, regain with a Psionic Energy Die).
  - Warrior of Shadow 3 Darkness: "1 Focus Point", WIS (the block's `ability`), note (no components).
  - Archfey 3 Steps of the Fey: Misty Step, "<max(1, CHA)>/long rest", CHA.
  - Diviner 10 The Third Eye: See Invisibility, "1/short or long rest", INT. The 5etools `innate` entry is the "See Invisibility" benefit of the pick-one-per-rest feature, not a free daily spell, hence the note and the rest wording.
  - Great Old One 10 Eldritch Hex: mode "prepared".
- **Engine.** New pure module `engine/subclass_innate_2024.py`: `innate_entries(catalog, resolved_classes, ruleset, modifiers)` returns catalog-shaped entries (needs the XPHB feature record, the selected subclass, the class level at the record's level, the spell in the subclass's converter data at that level); `resolve` turns them into `sheet.innateSpells` rows with `_innate_entry_to_sheet`, `source` = the subclass name, `level` = the class level of the feature ("from level N" in the exports, the same meaning as for races), and `note`, appended after the invocation rows. `prepared_spell_ids` feeds `_subclass_spell_ids` (new `ruleset` argument, default "mixed"; the Hex branch needs "2024", the four callers pass it), so Hex is in `sub_always` (the Warlock's prepared card disables it), in `owned_spells` and in `sheet.spells[warlock].alwaysPrepared` with the reason "Great Old One Patron" like the rest of the subclass table.
- **Great Old One Hex is not an innate spell** (deviation from the plan list, decision by the text): "You always have the Hex spell prepared" gives no free cast, so a sheet.innateSpells row would claim a free Hex. It is an always-prepared spell from Warlock 10 (before this slice it was listed nowhere).
- **One surface.** The RP11 resource rows (Steps of the Fey, Mantle of Majesty, Telekinetic Master, The Third Eye) stay the use trackers; RP18 adds no resource row (tested: `derived.classResources` equal with and without the converter key). The innate row only names the spell, the use rule and the ability. Archfey's `uses` is computed from the final Charisma modifier, like the RP11 resource.
- **Gates.** `ruleset == "2024"`, XPHB feature record, selected subclass (a stored hidden XPHB subclass under mixed / 2014 resolves its `spells` today, so the Hex hand-off carries an explicit ruleset gate; tested), class level, converter key present (SRD-only catalog and a catalog without the key give nothing; tested). Multiclass keeps each class's own level.
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys (`source` and `note` already exist for invocation rows); `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no build). Markdown / PDF print the row through the existing innate-spells block; the SPA preview (RP14 `SheetTraitsSummary`) shows it with the source label (both tested through the Markdown export).
- **Scanned and skipped.** All 48 XPHB subclasses' `additionalSpells` blocks: `innate` exists on exactly the seven records above. Not done: FRHoF (ruleset 2024, not XPHB) Banneret "Comprehend Languages" ritual, College of the Moon and Scion of the Three `innate` choices, Moon's `1e` daily Moonbeam (out of scope, the key is restricted to `source == "XPHB"`); Wild Heart's other casting mentions, Fey Wanderer / Gloom Stalker / Archfey `prepared` lists (already `spells`), Archfey 6 Misty Escape and 14 Bewitching Magic (extra casts of the same spell, left to the feature text), Shadow's `known` Minor Illusion (already `spells` since 11b.4). Beast Sense and Speak with Animals are not on the Barbarian's list, so there is no duplicate with a class card.
- **Not changed (pre-existing).** Invocation innate rows use the spell level in `level`; subclass rows use the class level of the feature. The exports label both "from level N".
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 30, ms, level 20, mixed / 2024, tests running in parallel): Fighter 20 4.5 / 2.9, Wizard 20 4.9 / 3.3, Bard 20 5.5 / 4.4; reference characters (2024): Wild Heart 10 1.5, Glamour 20 4.9, Psi Warrior 18 2.7, Shadow 3 0.8, Archfey 10 2.9, Great Old One 10 2.7, Diviner 10 2.4 (limit 50; the tests assert it too).
- **Tests / totals.** `tests/test_subclass_innate_2024.py` (28). Server suite 4811 -> 4839 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP19 (A2, open question 14, + Spells of the Mark) Slot-keyed feat spell tables (2026-10-23)

Decision D1: the feat's own text under every ruleset in which the character holds the feat (the standing FE edition policy); a character without these 20 feats is byte-identical. The F0 golden (`test_c_series_golden.py`) is green and not regenerated; a script over all 237 golden cases found none that holds an Arcane Adept feat, a Mark feat or a House (Mark) background (the 3 feat cases hold Elemental Adept, Keen Mind, Resilient, Weapon Master, Alert, Great Weapon Master, Lucky, Observant, Tough, Eldritch Adept, Martial Adept). No `REBASELINES` entry. Tests first (`tests/test_feat_slot_spells.py`, 135, rule matrix and governing sentences in the docstring); 8 of the 9 wiring mutations each made at least one test fail (the ninth, the class-slot-level cap inside `_add_mark_options`, is a redundant guard behind the call-site cap in `mark_spell_slugs`).

- **Converter** (`catalog/official/feats.py`, two additive keys on every feat record): `slot_spells` {slot level: [spell slug]} from `additionalSpells[].prepared` `sN` (the 8 AU Adept feats) and `expanded_spells` from `expanded` `sN` (the 12 EFA Marks). The slugs resolve against the catalog at engine time (`Catalog.resolve_spell_id`). Pins: `test_adept_table_matches_catalog_text` / `test_mark_table_matches_catalog_text` compare the keys with the rows of the table printed in the catalog `entries`; `feat_effects_tail.RP19_QUOTES` pins the two governing sentences; only these 20 feats carry a table; every slug resolves; a Mark's row N holds level N spells (Enchantment Adept lists Hold Person, a level 2 spell, at slot row 3: kept as printed).
- **Arcane Adept** (`engine/feat_spell_tables.py`, pure): the highest slot level is `max(shared pool, Pact Magic slot level)` (`highest_slot_level(derived.spellSlots, derived.warlockSpellSlots)`); every spell of rows 1..H becomes a `sheet.innateSpells` row (`uses` "always prepared", `featId`, `source` = the feat name, `level` None, `ability` None, `note` "<feat>: spell slot level N row"), appended right after the feat innate rows. No class attribution (it is not on a class card, so no multiclass question). A non-caster gets nothing (the feat stays on the sheet). Paladin 5 -> Shield + Lesser Restoration, Wizard 9 -> all five, Warlock 5 -> rows 1-3 (pact level 3), Warlock 5 / Wizard 3 -> rows 1-3, Cleric 9 / Warlock 2 -> rows 1-5. Two Adept feats list both tables. The FE10 note of the 8 feats is shortened to "the spells of the <School> Adept Spells table for your highest spell slot level and the lower levels are always prepared (listed in the innate spells)" (the table itself is gone from the note).
- **Spells of the Mark** (`resolve._feat_mark_spell_ids`, `_add_mark_options`, `_label_mark_options`): for each Spellcasting / Pact Magic class (and a third-caster subclass, list = Wizard) the table spells up to THAT class's highest castable level (`_highest_spell_slot_level(spell_dict)`, so Warlock = pact level, multiclass = each class's own) join the spell cards of the class: the known / prepared card (`class:<cls>:spells-known`) and the Wizard spellbook card (the text adds them to the feature's spell list and the C4 book draws from the Wizard list; the prepared card then draws from the book). They are labelled "(Spells of the Mark)" only when the class list does not offer them already; spells on the list are not duplicated. Visibility follows the ruleset / sources (`_spell_visible`; both editions of a spell are candidates, the filter picks one; a stored pick stays). A stored Mark pick is a normal pick: with the Mark gone the existing spell-card pass reports a non-blocking `pruned` entry. RP16 interplay: a Mark spell counts as on the Bard's list (not a Magical Secrets pick: not labelled and not counted in the 2014 cap).
- **Surfaces.** No new output key: `source` and `note` already exist on `SheetInnateSpell`; `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no build); Markdown / PDF print the rows through the innate-spells block ("Shield (always prepared)").
- **Left as notes (not deterministic or not modelled).** Potent Dragonmark "Dragonmark Preparation" (its spells of the Mark list always prepared, extra slot), Rune Shaper, Boon of Magic School Mastery, Magic Connoisseur, Greater Mark of Healing / Making / Shadow (RP5 notes), the Marks' fixed innate spells (already innate entries since FE11) and the Mark notes keep their printed table text. Adept rows do not take the 2024 twin of a spell (the sheet shows the same name; the id is the SRD one), and an Adept spell is not removed from a class card's pickable options (the spell is simply also prepared; no "already prepared" disabling).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 30, ms, mixed / 2024): Fighter 20 3.2 / 2.1, Wizard 20 3.7 / 3.0, Bard 20 4.5 / 3.7, Wizard 20 with two Adept feats 4.1 / 3.1, Warlock 12 / Wizard 8 with an Adept feat 4.5 / 4.1, Wizard 20 with Mark of Healing 4.2 / 3.2, Bard 20 with Mark of Storm and two Adept feats 5.5 / 4.4 (limit 50; the test asserts it too).
- **Totals.** Server suite 4839 -> 4974 passed (the F0 golden green, not regenerated); root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP20 (A3, open question 15) 2024 pools for the option-pick feats (2026-10-23)

Rule: PHB 2024 line 68 "the versions of things in this book replace versions from older books" (plan decision, not re-asked): under ruleset `2024` the TCE / PHB option-pick feats draw from the 2024 class lists. `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (the F0 golden `test_c_series_golden.py` is green and was never regenerated; no `REBASELINES` entry). Built in three commits in the plan's order: A3a (pools and prerequisites), A3b (dependents), A3c (Lessons of the First Ones through the feat). Tests first (`tests/test_option_feats_2024.py`, 78, rule matrix and governing sentences in the docstring); before the wiring 17 (A3a), 11 (A3b) and 11 (A3c) of them failed, and further wiring mutations (dependent pruning, feat Tome in the Warlock gate, feat Tome innate rows, pact weapon notes, count-variable status) each made at least one fail.

- **Catalog check (VERIFIED).** The catalog has no 2024 record of Eldritch Adept, Metamagic Adept, Martial Adept or Fighting Initiate (each exists once, TCE / PHB, `ruleset` 2014; XPHB has no counterpart feat), so the feat records and their effects are unchanged and only the OPTION POOLS change. XPHB has 28 invocations (`feature_types` EI, `prerequisite_2024` {level, optional_features, cantrip}), 10 Metamagic (MM, includes Seeking and Transmuted Spell, `cost`) and 20 maneuvers (the options of `battle-master-xphb-3-battle-master`). Each governing sentence of the feats and each pooled option text is pinned against the catalog (`test_quote_matches_catalog_text`, `test_xphb_option_text_matches_catalog`).
- **A3a pools** (`resolve._option_pick_options(catalog, kind, ruleset)`; `_feat_choice_templates` and `_feat_extra_templates` take `ruleset`, passed at all five feat sites): Eldritch Adept = the 28 XPHB invocations (no `~k` repeat instances: the feat takes one), Metamagic Adept = the 10 XPHB options labelled "(N SP)" (`class_choices_2024.metamagic_records` / `metamagic_options`, shared with the Sorcerer card), Martial Adept = the 20 XPHB maneuvers. A catalog without the XPHB records keeps the FE9 pools (tested with a patched catalog). Fighting Initiate is unchanged (see the open question below).
- **Prerequisites** (`resolve._gate_feat_invocation_prereqs`, 2024 branch for `opt-*-xphb` ids): `class_choices_2024.invocation_unmet` (= the C7a check) with the Warlock level (0 for a non-Warlock), the Warlock's selected invocations and cantrips plus Pact of the Tome cantrips. A non-Warlock is left with Armor of Shadows, Eldritch Mind and the three Pacts; Agonizing Blast / Eldritch Spear / Repelling Blast / Lessons read "requires warlock level 2"; a Warlock needs the damaging / attack-roll cantrip, the Pact for Thirsting Blade and so on. An unmet stored pick is a `pruned` entry. The FE9 duplicate walk compares by name, so the XPHB and SRD editions are one option; stored SRD ids (invocations, Metamagic, maneuvers) under 2024 are `pruned` and the card goes back to `pending`. The effects needed no change (`invocation_effect` already reads XPHB ids: Armor of Shadows AC and Mage Armor, Devil's Sight, Pact of the Chain's Find Familiar, with the "Eldritch Adept" label and the feat's ability card).
- **A3b dependents** (`class_choices_2024`, the C7c builders take a holder `{prefix, source, stored}` next to the Warlock default, so the C7c tests are untouched): cids `<feat card cid>:<invocation id>:cantrip | cantrips | rituals | weapon` (`dependent_cid(..., prefix)`, `FEAT_DEPENDENT_RE`, `is_feat_dependent`), source = the feat card's own source. `feat_invocation_holders` reads the RAW stored pick (valid option, Warlock level), `feat_dependent_templates` builds them (resolve calls it after `all_selections`, because every feat card exists then; the Warlock's known cantrips and owned spells come from `warlock_spell_ctx` captured in the spell loop). Targets: the Warlock's known cantrips plus Tome cantrips with the C7c reasons; a Warlock with the pick and a Warlock class card holding the same invocation is blocked by the duplicate walk, so the two never share a target card. Pact of the Tome: cantrip and ritual cards for ANY class, shown as `sheet.innateSpells` rows (`uses` "always prepared", `source` the feat name, ability = the feat's own ability card; `features.feat_tome_entries`); Tome cantrips held through the feat count for the Warlock's cantrip prerequisites while the class does not hold the Tome (`gate_invocations(..., feat_tome_cantrips)`). Pact of the Blade: weapon card (all Simple / Martial Melee weapons), the pact weapon row (CHA, proficient, note) and the Thirsting Blade / Eldritch Smite / ... row notes (`selected_pact_weapon` and `features.selected_feat_invocation_ids`). `class_resources._warlock` reads the feat card's target through `instance_target(..., prefix)`. The cards are count-variable (`_is_count_variable_choice` via `is_dependent_choice`, a stale pick is a non-blocking `pruned` entry) and in the `mark_unavailable` set (`is_new_2024_class_card`). `prune_feat_invocation_dependents` runs after the feat and duplicate gates and drops the cards of a pick that is no longer selected. The feat may come from another class than the Warlock (Fighter ASI, Warlock multiclass).
- **A3c Lessons of the First Ones** (`resolve._FeatLessonsResolver`, `_feat_lessons_templates`; `_lessons_card` is shared with the Warlock's own Lessons cards): `_resolve_asi_selected_feats(..., after_feat=)` resolves the Origin feat right after the Eldritch Adept ASI feat is accepted, against the same `seen` map (so a background feat, an earlier pick or a later ASI pick is judged in walk order, duplicates give the usual `feat-duplicate-*` problem). Needs Warlock level 2 (final level) and no Lessons on the Warlock card (the gate disables the same option, so the walk and the card agree). cid `<feat card cid>:opt-lessons-of-the-first-ones-xphb:feat`, the feat's own choices cascade under `...:feat:<index>` exactly as for the Warlock's Lessons (Skilled, Magic Initiate spells, Alert initiative reach the sheet), origin `"invocation"`, `_inv_class` places it at the holder's ASI for the language ordering (`_feat_build_position`). A prerequisite-blocked Eldritch Adept (preference on) removes its Lessons feat too (`_ea_asi_cid`); changing the pick away removes the feat and prunes the card.
- **Deviations from the brief / plan (listed, not guessed).** (1) Fighting Initiate keeps the six SRD styles under 2024 (the plan: "no 2024 style option list exists"). The brief said "Fighting Initiate offers 2024 Fighting Style feats"; the 2024 Fighting Style FEATS do exist (category FS, XPHB) but they are feats with their own effects and prerequisites, not option records, so offering them inside a feat is a design question and was NOT done. The six SRD styles' effects are the same-named FS effects. (2) Repeatable 2024 invocations (Agonizing Blast, Eldritch Spear, Repelling Blast, Lessons) cannot be doubled through the feat when the Warlock holds them: the FE9 name-based duplicate walk is kept (plan: "FE9 name compare"), although the invocation text says "Repeatable". (3) Edited FE9 tests (shape only, no rule changed): `test_feat_option_picks.py::test_every_other_feat_site_emits_the_card` (the maneuver pool under 2024 has 20, not 23 options) and `test_mma_duplicate_of_the_2024_sorcerer_metamagic_by_name` (the pick is now the XPHB id).
- **Limits.** The Warlock's 2024 invocation card is a holder only (C7a): an Eldritch Adept pick that comes EARLIER in the walk than the Warlock card (a species feat; an ASI feat of an earlier class) does not block the later Warlock pick of the same invocation, and the Warlock card is not narrowed by it. The Tome cards of a non-Warlock are checked against the Warlock's own spells only (nothing for a non-Warlock), like the C7c Tome cards. A Tome held through the feat counts for the Warlock's cantrip prerequisites only when the class does not hold the Tome. Origin-feat holders other than an ASI feat (species / background) get the target, Tome and Blade cards (tested at the background site with a patched effect table) but not Lessons (the Eldritch Adept is neither an Origin feat nor a Variant Human option under ruleset 2024).
- **Surfaces.** No new output key (cards, `innateSpells`, `derived.attacks`, `sheet.feats` origin `"invocation"` all exist); `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no build; `ClassPage.isSpellChoiceId` already routes `:opt-pact-of-the-tome-xphb:` cards to the Spells tab, and the class page lists feat cards whose cid starts with `class:<cls>:`). Drift and export-key tests green.

## RP21 (X2, open question 5) Invocation repeat-instance renumbering (2026-10-23)

Rule (decision D3, user-approved): the repeat instances of a repeatable XPHB invocation (Agonizing Blast, Eldritch Spear, Repelling Blast, Lessons of the First Ones; ids `<id>`, `<id>~2`, ...) are interchangeable ("Each time you do so, choose a different ..."), so when the base instance is removed from the STORED picks the stored picks FOLLOW the instance: `~2` becomes the base, `~3` becomes `~2`, and the dependent picks keyed by the instance move with it. Before this slice the repeat was dropped ("take Agonizing Blast first") and its target / Lessons feat was pruned. Ruleset `2024` only (the `~k` options exist only on the 2024 Warlock card); `mixed` and `2014` are byte-identical (F0 golden `test_c_series_golden.py` green, not regenerated, no `REBASELINES` entry).

- **Where.** `class_choices_2024.renumber_invocation_instances(catalog, rc, ruleset, class_choices)` (pure, returns the input itself when nothing changes, otherwise a shallow copy; the draft is never mutated). `resolve()` calls it once per Warlock class entry right after the ruleset is validated (`if ruleset == "2024"`), BEFORE any card is built, so the invocation card, the C7c target cards, the Lessons walk (`lessons_instances`), the resources and the sheet all see the renumbered picks. The stored draft is not rewritten (resolve is pure): the response shows the renumbered selection and the client echoes it back; resolving the echo gives the same result (tested).
- **Rule.** Per repeatable base, over the stored ids that are VALID options of the card (`~k` up to the Warlock's invocation count): if the instance numbers are not `1..n`, they are compacted in order (`[~2, ~3]` -> `[base, ~2]`; a middle gap `[base, ~3]` -> `[base, ~2]` closes the same way). List positions are kept, only the ids change. Dependent keys: every key `class:warlock:invocation:<instance>:...` (target `:cantrip`, Lessons `:feat` and the whole cascade `:feat:<index>:...`) of a moving instance is renamed with it; the keys of an instance that is renumbered away (the removed base's target, a vacated slot) are dropped, so the removed base's pick never becomes the next instance's. A removed base whose successor had no target leaves the base card open (pending).
- **Not renumbered (by design).** An id beyond the count (`~4` at Warlock 2, count 3) is not an offered option, so it is neither renumbered into range nor counted as an instance (it is ignored as before). A pick the LATE gate removes (the cantrip prerequisites, "needs another eligible cantrip", the last damaging cantrip going) is not a stored removal: nothing renumbers, the dependents are pruned as in C7c. Level-down drops the instances beyond the count / the level prerequisite as before.
- **Safe-to-renumber limit.** No stored shape needed a `pruned` fallback: the move is a rename of cid prefixes, which is shape-agnostic (a malformed non-list invocation value is left as it is, the card ignores it as before). No new `pruned` entry is emitted for the replaced stale base target (it belonged to an instance the user removed).
- **Eldritch Adept (feat-held).** Instances do not apply: the RP20 pool offers one invocation with no `~k` option (the feat takes one), so nothing renumbers there; a stored `~2` in the feat card stays invalid and its dependents are not moved (tested). The feat-held Lessons is one Origin feat, never a repeat.
- **Tests first.** `tests/test_invocation_renumber_2024.py` (36, rule matrix in the docstring; helper matrix of 11 stored shapes, dependent-key moves, `resolve()` for Agonizing / Spear / Repelling, middle gap, several invocations at once, Lessons feat and cascade, level-down with and without a removed base, late-gate drop, duplicate target, echo stability, no mutation, mixed / 2014 / SRD-only untouched, Eldritch Adept, timing). Before the wiring 11 of the `resolve()` tests failed (the helper tests failed with the helper missing). Two old tests encoded the dropped behaviour and were changed (rule change, approved D3): `test_invocations_2024.py::test_repeat_instances_need_previous` (a lone `~2` / `[~3, ~2]` now renumbers) and `test_lessons_of_the_first_ones_2024.py::test_a_repeat_instance_needs_the_base_instance` (renamed `..._becomes_the_base`).
- **Surfaces.** No new output key, no frontend change (no `npm run build`), `types.ts` / `export_fields.py` untouched.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 50, ms, Warlock 20 with 10 invocations incl. Tome, Chain, 2x Agonizing, Spear, Repelling, 2x Lessons, run while a test suite was also running): 2024 intact 4.9 / base removed 5.0, mixed 3.4 (limit 50, asserted in the new tests).
- **Totals.** Server suite 5052 -> 5088 passed (+36; F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched (no `npm run build`).

  Skip reasons in short: subclass intros and restrictions (flavor); no number or use limit (Ancestral Protectors, Battlerager Charge, Shielding Storm, Unstable Backlash, Controlled Surge, Unfailing Inspiration, Mystical Connection, Battle Magic); passive numbers without a use limit (Giant's Havoc, Storm Soul, Silver Tongue, Spiritual Focus, Bestial Soul); picks (Totem Warrior: Totem Spirit, Aspect of the Beast, Totemic Attunement; the Elk DC exists only for one pick and the 2014 picks have no note wiring, deferred); already derived (Extra Attack, proficiencies, Fighting Style card); Combat Inspiration only uses the Bardic Inspiration die of the creature holding it.
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys; `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no `npm run build`).
- **Golden: no rebaseline.** The golden's primary cases hold the first SRD subclass of the class, and the two book-subclass sinks (psi-warrior11, totem-bear6) hold unmodelled features (R6 and picks), so none of the 243 cases moved: every stored result re-resolved identically (the `--append` run asserts it, and the full golden-driven tests pass), no `REBASELINES` entry was added and the gz was not rebaselined. Instead `--append` added the kitchen sink `whispers-bard14-2014` (Bard 14, College of Whispers, 2014; `ks/src-all` and `ks/src-srd`): 243 -> 245 cases, the 243 existing cases byte-identical (checked by loading the gz before and after). It holds Words of Terror, Mantle of Whispers, Shadow Lore and the Psychic Blades note and is listed in `_EXPECT` of `test_export_fields` so Markdown / PDF / YAML carry it.
- **Edited tests (deliberate).** `test_worn_wielded` (the pre-G2 neutraliser also removes the book resources and notes), `test_c_series_golden` (the W6 sweep exempts the appended Bard 14 sink, which legitimately holds Song of Rest; a W8a guard test).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20, mixed): Storm Herald 2.4, Glamour Bard 4.5, Creation Bard 4.5 (limit 50; also asserted for five subclasses under both rulesets).
- **Totals.** Server suite 5733 -> 5974 passed (233 new + 1 golden test + the 2 new sink cases through the parametrized sweeps), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched.
- **Uncertain / deferred.** (1) Totem Warrior (and any other pick-dependent feature) needs RP13-style 2014 pick notes. (2) Storm Aura lists all three environments because the pick is not read. (3) "Once per rage" Fanatical Focus has no recharge value (a rage is not a rest). **Superseded by X2: recharge `rage`.** (4) The Blessed Strikes / Divine Strike TCE optional-feature swap is not a pick under 2014 (relevant for W8b clerics).

### W8b: Cleric, Druid and Fighter (2026-10-24)

Second commit of W8 part 1 (the catalog holds no Artificer; Monk to Wizard are part 2, a later slice). Same machinery as W8a: the two book tuples are extended (`_W8B_TAIL` / `_W8B_NOTES` concatenated to `SUBCLASS_TAIL_2014_BOOKS` / `SUBCLASS_NOTES_2014_BOOKS`, built with the small helpers `_w8_res`, `_w8_note`, `_w8_cd`, `_w8_divine_strike` in `reference_data`), no engine change except new `_note_vars` use. Tests first (`tests/test_subclass_features_2014_w8b.py`, 395, rule matrix in the docstring); with only the W8a entries kept 334 of them fail, the 2024 independence test pins the gate.

- **Data.** 55 resources (SUBCLASS_TAIL_2014_BOOKS now 83) and 34 sentences on existing resources (SUBCLASS_NOTES_2014_BOOKS now 40), 12 more records ride along as `extras`. Seven more level tables that live only in the text (Divine Strike dice, Arcane Abjuration banishment CR, Halo of Spores dice, Circle Forms CR and limits, Starry Form dice, Runes Known, Giant's Might dice, Fighting Spirit temporary hit points) are pinned in `BOOK_TABLE_PINS_2014`.
- **Cleric (31 resources, 25 Channel Divinity sentences).** Warding Flare (Ambition, Light; WIS modifier (min 1) uses, long rest; Improved Flare as an extra), War Priest, Priest of Zeal, Solidarity's Action, Wrath of the Storm, Eyes of the Grave, Sentinel at Death's Door, Embodiment of the Law (same uses), Emboldening Bond and Steps of Night (PB uses), Blessing of the Forge, Eyes of Night, Blaze of Glory (1, long rest), Visions of the Past (Knowledge PHB and PSA; 1, short or long rest), note-only Keeper of Souls, Corona of Light, Dampen Elements, Order's Wrath; Divine Strike (11 domains, `dice` 1d8 / 2d8 from 14, ids `cleric:divine-strike-<domain>-domain`, never `cleric:divine-strike`, so the 2024 attack-row notes are not fed). Channel Divinity sentences on `cleric:channel-divinity` (after the W6 / W7 ones): Invoke Duplicity (+ Improved Duplicity), Cloak of Shadows, Arcane Abjuration (banishment CR 1/2 to 4 at 5 / 8 / 11 / 14 / 17), Touch of Death (5 + twice the level), Artisan's Blessing, Path to the Grave, Knowledge of the Ages, Read Thoughts, Radiance of the Dawn (2d10 + level), Charm Animals and Plants, Order's Demand, Balm of Peace (2d6 + WIS), Preserve Life (5 x level), Oketra's / Rhonas's / War God's Blessing, Feat of Strength, Guided Strike (+10), Destructive Wrath, Consuming Fervor, Twilight Sanctuary (1d6 + level temporary hit points).
- **Druid (11 resources, 6 Wild Shape sentences).** Balm of the Summer Court (`uses` = Druid level, `die` d6, long rest, half the level spendable), Hidden Paths, Fungal Infestation (WIS), Walker in Dreams, Faithful Summons, Blazing Revival, Spirit Totem (short or long rest; bear 5 + level, unicorn level, Guardian Spirit half level), Halo of Spores (`dice` 1d4 / 1d6 / 1d8 / 1d10, DC), Star Map and Cosmic Omen and Cauterizing Flames (PB uses). Sentences on `druid:wild-shape`: Combat Wild Shape, Circle Forms (CR and limits from the table), Elemental Wild Shape, Symbiotic Entity (+ Spreading Spores DC), Starry Form (1d8 / 2d8 + WIS), Summon Wildfire Spirit (+ Enhanced Bond).
- **Fighter (13 resources, 3 sentences).** Unwavering Mark (STR modifier uses, half the level), Warding Maneuver (CON), Ferocious Charger (DC 8 + PB + STR), Manifest Echo (AC 14 + PB), Unleash Incarnation (CON uses, Legion of One), Echo Avatar, Shadow Martyr, Reclaim Potential (2d6 + CON), Rune Carver (runes 2 / 3 / 4 / 5, DC 8 + PB + CON, Master of Runes), Giant's Might (PB uses, `dice` 1d6 / 1d8 / 1d10, Runic Juggernaut), Runic Shield, Fighting Spirit (3 uses, 5 / 10 / 15 temporary hit points, Tireless Spirit), Strength before Death. Purple Dragon Knight sentences on Second Wind (Rallying Cry), Action Surge (Inspiring Surge, two allies from 18) and Indomitable (Bulwark). Battle Master, Psi Warrior and Arcane Archer stay with their R6 builders.
- **Scan (all 258 feature records of the 33 Cleric, Druid and Fighter book subclasses). 101 modelled (55 resources + 34 sentences + 12 extras), 157 skipped:**

  Skip reasons in short: subclass intros and domain-spell tables (flavor); the 18 TCE Blessed Strikes records (an optional feature that replaces Divine Strike or Potent Spellcasting; there is no optional-feature pick under 2014, so both would be shown, and it has no use limit); passive numbers without a use limit (Thunderbolt Strike, Stormborn, Hearth of Moonlight and Shadow, Elegant Courtier, Royal Envoy); no number (Reaper, Spell Breaker, Voice of Authority, Hold the Line, Vigilant Defender, War Magic, Rapid Strike ...); spell lists, cantrips and proficiencies; already modelled by R6 (Battle Master, Psi Warrior, Arcane Archer).
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys; `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no `npm run build`).
- **Golden: no rebaseline.** The golden's primary Cleric / Druid / Fighter cases hold Life / Land / Champion and the book-subclass sinks hold unmodelled features, so no case moved (the `--append` run asserts every stored result re-resolves identically and it is byte-compared before / after). No `REBASELINES` entry. `--append` added the kitchen sink `stars-druid10-mixed` (Circle of the Stars Druid 10, mixed; Star Map, Cosmic Omen, the Starry Form sentence on Wild Shape): 245 -> 247 cases, the 245 existing cases byte-identical; `_EXPECT` lists it.
- **Edited tests (deliberate).** `test_subclass_resources_2024::test_no_subclass_entry_at_all_without_the_xphb_subclass_under_mixed` (the 2014 Light Domain now holds its own Warding Flare and Corona of Light, 2014 text, but still no XPHB entry), `test_c_series_golden` (a W8b guard test).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20, mixed / 2014): Twilight Cleric 3.1 / 2.4, Zeal Cleric 3.1 / 2.4, Stars Druid 3.2 / 2.6, Moon Druid 3.2 / 2.4, Rune Knight 3.3 / 2.2, Echo Knight 3.3 / 2.2, Samurai 3.8 / 2.2 (limit 50; also asserted).
- **Totals.** Server suite 5974 -> 6377 passed (395 new + 1 golden test + the 2 new sink cases through the parametrized sweeps + 1 edited), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched.
- **Uncertain / deferred.** (1) Visions of the Past prints "Wisdom score" instead of a number (resources only see ability modifiers). (2) Blessed Strikes / Divine Strike are both shown for a Cleric of a domain with Divine Strike (no optional-feature pick). (3) The Channel Divinity sentences are long for a note; the merged Channel Divinity of a multiclass keeps only the winning class's note (as in W6). (4) Totem Warrior and the Eldritch Knight / Arcane Archer picks stay feature lines.

### W8c: Monk, Paladin, Ranger and Rogue (2026-10-24)

First commit of W8 part 2 (the catalog holds 108 non-SRD 2014 subclasses; part 1 covered 48, part 2 covers the other 60: W8c the 32 of Monk, Paladin, Ranger and Rogue, W8d Sorcerer, Warlock and Wizard). Same machinery as W8a / W8b: the two book tuples are extended (`_W8C_TAIL` / `_W8C_NOTES` concatenated to `SUBCLASS_TAIL_2014_BOOKS` / `SUBCLASS_NOTES_2014_BOOKS`, same helpers `_w8_res` / `_w8_note` plus `_w8_pcd` for Paladin Channel Divinity sentences). Rulesets mixed / 2014 only, only the selected imported book subclass, source kind "subclass"; 2024 output is byte-identical (pinned by the independence test). Tests first (`tests/test_subclass_features_2014_w8c.py`, 349, rule matrix in the docstring); with the two W8c concatenations removed 274 of them fail, the remaining 75 are the negative and gate tests.

- **Method (rule used for the scan).** MODELLED when the record text has a use limit or rest rule, dice, a save DC, a number computed from the character (ability modifier, PB, level) or a resource cost (ki points, a spell slot, a Psionic Energy die) with a numeric effect, each with a pinned quote. SKIPPED when the text is flavor, a spell list, a proficiency, a pick (deferred) or a passive effect with no cost, limit or character-derived number (ranges, durations and flat speed or AC bonuses alone).
- **Data.** 71 resources (`SUBCLASS_TAIL_2014_BOOKS` now 154), 20 sentences (`SUBCLASS_NOTES_2014_BOOKS` now 60) and 8 extras that ride along. Seven more level tables that live only in the text are pinned in `BOOK_TABLE_PINS_2014` (Breath of the Dragon rolls, the Four Elements ki cap by level, the Paladin aura range 10 / 30 feet at 18, Drake's Breath, Dreadful Strikes, Planar Warrior and Gathered Swarm dice). New `_note_vars`: `wis_level_min1`, `five_plus_wis`, `wails` (half the Sneak Attack dice rounded up, as `Nd6`).
- **Monk (25 resources, all under `monk:`).** Ascendant Dragon: Breath of the Dragon (3; PB uses, long rest, two Martial Arts dice, three from 11, ki save DC, 2 ki to use it again, Augment Breath as an extra from 17), Draconic Presence (3; 1, long), Wings Unfurled (6; PB, long), Aspect of the Wyrm (11; 1, long or 3 ki, Explosive Fury as an extra from 17). Astral Self (note-only): Arms (1 ki, two Martial Arts dice), Visage (1 ki), Body (1d10 + WIS modifier, Empowered Arms), Awakened (5 ki, +2 AC). Drunken Master: Tipsy Sway (1 ki), Drunkard's Luck (2 ki). Four Elements: Disciple of the Elements (the ki cap per spell 3 / 4 / 5 / 6 at 5 / 9 / 13 / 17; the discipline picks are not tracked). Kensei: Kensei's Shot (1d4), Deft Strike (1 ki, Martial Arts die), Sharpen the Blade (up to 3 ki). Long Death: Touch of Death (WIS modifier + Monk level temporary hit points, minimum 1), Mastery of Death (1 ki), Touch of the Long Death (1 to 10 ki, 2d10 each, ki DC). Mercy: Hand of Harm, Hand of Healing (1 ki, one Martial Arts die + WIS modifier), Hand of Ultimate Mercy (1, long; 5 ki, 4d10 + WIS modifier). Shadow Arts (2 ki). Sun Soul: Radiant Sun Bolt, Searing Arc Strike (at most half the Monk level in ki), Searing Sunburst (2d6 + 2d6 per ki up to 8d6, ki DC), Sun Shield (5 + WIS modifier radiant). The ki save DC is pinned from the Monk `ki` record (`("ki", "Ki save DC = 8 + ...")`, as W7 did for Quivering Palm) for the four ki features whose subclass text only says "ki save DC" or forces a save while spending ki.
- **Paladin (15 resources, 16 Channel Divinity sentences).** 1 use, long rest: Undying Sentinel (Ancients 15), Elder Champion (20), Invincible Conqueror (Conquest 20), Exalted Champion (Crown 20), Living Legend (Glory 20, or a 5th-level slot), Dread Lord (Oathbreaker 20; 4d10 psychic, 3d10 + CHA modifier necrotic), Avenging Angel (Vengeance 20), Mortal Bulwark (Watchers 20; spell save DC, or a 5th-level slot); Glorious Defense (Glory 15; CHA modifier (min 1) uses, long rest). Note-only: Aura of Conquest (half the Paladin level psychic damage, 10 feet, 30 from 18), Scornful Rebuke (CHA modifier, min 1), Aura of Hate (CHA modifier, min 1; 10 / 30 feet), Protective Spirit (1d6 + half the level), Aura of the Sentinel (PB initiative; 10 / 30 feet), Vigilant Rebuke (2d8 + CHA modifier). Sentences on `paladin:channel-divinity` after the W6 note (the DC is the one it already names): Nature's Wrath, Turn the Faithless, Conquering Presence, Guided Strike (+10), Champion Challenge, Turn the Tide (1d6 + CHA modifier, min 1), Inspiring Smite (2d8 + level), Peerless Athlete, Control Undead (a CR at or above the level is immune), Dreadful Aspect, Emissary of Peace (+5), Rebuke the Violent, Abjure Enemy, Vow of Enmity, Abjure the Extraplanar, Watcher's Will (CHA modifier, min 1, creatures).
- **Ranger (19 resources).** Ranger's Companion (Beast Master 3; hit points 4 x the Ranger level or the normal maximum, PB on AC, attacks, damage and saves), Drake Companion (Drakewarden 3; 1, long or a spell slot; Magic Fang and Empowered Bite as extras), Drake's Breath (11; 1, long or a 3rd-level slot, `dice` 8d6 / 10d6 from 15, spell save DC), Reflexive Resistance (15; PB, long), Dreadful Strikes (Fey Wanderer 3; `dice` 1d4 / 1d6 from 11), Otherworldly Glamour (WIS modifier, min 1), Beguiling Twist (spell save DC), Fey Reinforcements (11; 1, long), Misty Wanderer (15; WIS modifier (min 1), long), Dread Ambusher (Gloom Stalker 3; WIS modifier initiative, 1d8), Detect Portal (Horizon Walker 3) and Ethereal Step (7): 1, short or long, Planar Warrior (`dice` 1d8 / 2d8 from 11), Hunter's Sense (Monster Slayer 3; WIS modifier (min 1), long), Slayer's Prey (1d6, Supernatural Defense 1d6 as an extra from 7), Magic-User's Nemesis (11; 1, short or long, spell save DC), Gathered Swarm (Swarmkeeper 3; `dice` 1d6 / 1d8 from 11, spell save DC, Mighty Swarm as an extra), Writhing Tide (7) and Swarming Dispersal (15): PB, long.
- **Rogue (12 resources, 4 sentences).** Spell Thief (Arcane Trickster 17; 1, long; spell save DC with INT), Death Strike (Assassin 17; DC 8 + DEX modifier + PB), Insightful Fighting (Inquisitive 3; Eye for Weakness 3d6 as an extra from 17), Unerring Eye (13; WIS modifier (min 1), long), Wails from the Grave (Phantom 3; PB, long; `dice` half the Sneak Attack dice rounded up; Death's Friend as an extra from 17), Tokens of the Departed (9; PB soul trinkets), Ghost Walk (13; 1, long), Psychic Blades (Soulknife 3), Psychic Veil (13; 1, long or a die), Rend Mind (17; 1, long or three dice; DC 8 + PB + DEX modifier), Rakish Audacity (Swashbuckler 3; CHA modifier initiative), Master Duelist (17; 1, short or long). The Soulknife `rogue:psionic-energy-dice` resource already exists from R6 (twice PB, d6 to d12, deliberately not duplicated): Psi-Bolstered Knack, Psychic Whispers, Homing Strikes and Psychic Teleportation are sentences on it.
- **Scan (all 217 feature records of the 32 Monk, Paladin, Ranger and Rogue book subclasses; "phrase" = the use / number words the regex found, `-` = none). 95 feature records modelled (71 resources + 20 sentences + 8 extras, some records host two entries), 122 skipped (of which 6 are DEFERRED picks or stat blocks, plus the kensei weapon picks noted on the Kensei record):**

  Skip reasons in short: subclass intros, tenets, oath spell lists and the Channel Divinity headers (flavor or granted elsewhere); no number, cost or limit (Hour of Reaping, Shadow Step, Opportunist, Divine Allegiance, Soul of Vengeance, Stalker's Flurry, Slayer's Counter, Magical Ambush ...); passive numbers without a cost, limit or character-derived value (Aura of Warding, Aura of Alacrity, Aura of the Guardian, Umbral Sight, Superior Mobility, Drunken Technique, Intoxicated Frenzy, Agile Parry); downtime rules (Infiltration Expertise, Impostor); proficiencies and languages; already derived or already modelled (Soulknife's Psionic Energy Dice by R6).
- **Deferred (picks and stat blocks, not guessed).** (1) Way of the Four Elements: the elemental disciplines (`Elemental Disciplines` and the three Extra Elemental Discipline records) need discipline pick wiring; only the numeric ki cap is modelled. (2) Kensei weapon picks (kensei weapons, calligraphy or painting supplies). (3) Arcane Trickster Spellcasting (wizard cantrips and enchantment / illusion spells). (4) Beast Master Primal Companion (the Beast of the Land / Sea / Sky stat block uses PB inside the stat block, nothing in the text to compute). (5) Fey Wanderer and Phantom skill / tool picks stay feature lines.
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys; `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no `npm run build`).
- **Golden: no rebaseline.** The golden's primary Monk, Paladin, Ranger and Rogue cases hold the SRD subclasses (Open Hand, Devotion, Hunter, Thief) and the only book-subclass Ranger sink is 2024, so no case moved: the full golden-driven tests pass against the unchanged stored results (a dry run is unnecessary when nothing moves; the `--append` run asserts every stored result re-resolves identically). No `REBASELINES` entry. `--append` added the kitchen sink `watchers-paladin20-2014` (Oath of the Watchers Paladin 20, 2014; `ks/src-all` and `ks/src-srd`): 247 -> 249 cases, the 247 existing cases byte-identical (checked by loading the gz before and after); `_EXPECT` of `test_export_fields` lists it so Markdown / PDF / YAML carry Aura of the Sentinel, Vigilant Rebuke, Mortal Bulwark and the Channel Divinity sentences.
- **Edited tests (deliberate).** `test_subclass_features_2014_w8a::test_quotes_match_catalog` (a quote may come from a record of the class itself, no subclass, for the Monk `ki` rule), `test_subclass_features_2014_w8b::test_every_entry_is_a_non_srd_2014_book_subclass_of_the_three_classes` (the allowed class set now lists all twelve classes), `test_class_resources_subclass::test_subclass_entries_follow_their_class_entries` (a Soulknife 3 now also holds the note-only Psychic Blades after the R6 die resource), `test_c_series_golden` (the W6 sweep exempts the appended Paladin 20 sink, which legitimately holds Divine Smite and Cleansing Touch; a W8c guard test).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20, mixed / 2014): Astral Self Monk 2.6 / 1.9, Ascendant Dragon Monk 2.6 / 1.9, Watchers Paladin 3.1 / 2.4, Oathbreaker Paladin 3.1 / 2.3, Drakewarden Ranger 3.0 / 2.3, Swarmkeeper Ranger 3.1 / 2.3, Soulknife Rogue 2.9 / 1.9, Phantom Rogue 2.8 / 1.9 (limit 50; also asserted).
- **Totals.** Server suite 6377 -> 6734 passed (349 new + 1 golden test + the 2 new sink cases through the parametrized sweeps + 1 edited), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched.
- **Uncertain.** (1) The ki save DC is applied to three ki features whose subclass text gives no DC (Arms of the Astral Self, Touch of the Long Death, Searing Sunburst; Breath of the Dragon and Aspect of the Wyrm name "your ki save DC" themselves), following the W7 Quivering Palm precedent and the Ki record's "Some of your ki features require your target to make a saving throw" rule. (2) Avenging Angel's frightened save names no DC in the text, so none is printed. (3) The Paladin Channel Divinity sentences are long for a note, as the W8b Cleric ones were.


### W8d: Sorcerer, Warlock and Wizard (2026-10-24)

Second commit of W8 part 2 and the last of W8 (28 non-SRD 2014 subclasses: 8 Sorcerer, 8 Warlock, 12 Wizard; with W8a-W8c that is all 108). Same machinery as W8a-W8c: the two book tuples are extended (`_W8D_TAIL` / `_W8D_NOTES` concatenated to `SUBCLASS_TAIL_2014_BOOKS` / `SUBCLASS_NOTES_2014_BOOKS`, same helpers). Rulesets mixed / 2014 only, only the selected imported book subclass, source kind "subclass"; 2024 output is byte-identical (pinned by the independence test). Same rule as W8c for what is modelled and skipped. Tests first (`tests/test_subclass_features_2014_w8d.py`, 328, rule matrix in the docstring); with the two W8d concatenations removed 242 of them fail, the remaining 86 are the negative and gate tests.

- **Data.** 81 resources (`SUBCLASS_TAIL_2014_BOOKS` now 235), 1 sentence (`SUBCLASS_NOTES_2014_BOOKS` now 61) and 2 extras that ride along. Two more level tables that live only in the text are pinned in `BOOK_TABLE_PINS_2014` (Tentacle of the Deeps / Guardian Coil dice 1d8 and 2d8 from 10; Portent d20s two, three from 14 through Greater Portent). New `_note_vars`: `half_level_min1`, `three_plus_cha`, `cha_min0`, `level_plus_pb`, `two_pb`, `one_plus_cha_min1`, `level_plus_cha`, `half_level_plus_cha`.
- **Sorcerer (26 resources, 1 sentence).** Aberrant Mind: Telepathic Speech (CHA modifier (min 1) miles, Sorcerer level minutes), Revelation in Flesh (14; 1+ sorcery points), Warping Implosion (18; 1, long or 5 sp; 3d10, spell DC), Psionic Sorcery (a sentence on Sorcery Points after the W6 / W7 ones). Clockwork Soul: Restore Balance (PB, long), Bastion of Law (1 to 5 sp, d8s), Trance of Order (1, long or 5 sp), Clockwork Cavalcade (1, long or 7 sp; 100 hit points). Divine Soul: Favored by the Gods (1, short or long; `dice` 2d4), Empowered Healing (1 sp), Unearthly Recovery (1, long). Lunar: Lunar Embodiment (1, long), Lunar Boons (PB, long), Waxing and Waning (1 sp), Lunar Phenomenon (3d8, 3d10, spell DC, 5 sp). Pyromancer: Heart of Fire (half the level, min 1), Pyromancer's Fury (the level). Shadow: Eyes of the Dark (120 feet, 2 sp), Strength of the Grave (1, long; DC 5 + damage), Hound of Ill Omen (3 sp, half the level temporary hit points), Umbral Form (6 sp). Storm: Heart of the Storm (half the level), Storm's Fury (the level, spell DC), Wind Soul (1, short or long; 3 + CHA modifier creatures). Wild Magic: Tides of Chaos (1, long), Wild Magic Surge (d20 on a 1), Bend Luck (2 sp, 1d4).
- **Warlock (25 resources).** Archfey: Fey Presence (1, short or long; 10-foot cube, spell DC), Misty Escape (1, short or long), Beguiling Defenses (spell DC), Dark Delirium (1, short or long). Celestial: Healing Light (`uses` 1 + Warlock level, `die` d6, long; CHA modifier (min 1) dice per use), Radiant Soul, Celestial Resilience (level + CHA modifier, half the level + CHA modifier), Searing Vengeance (1, long; 2d8 + CHA modifier). Fathomless: Tentacle of the Deeps (PB, long; `dice` 1d8 / 2d8 from 10), Guardian Coil, Grasping Tentacles (1, long; level temporary hit points), Fathomless Plunge (1, short or long). Genie: Bottled Respite (1, long; 2 x PB hours, vessel AC = spell DC, hit points level + PB; Sanctuary Vessel as an extra from 10), Genie's Wrath (PB damage), Elemental Gift (PB, long), Limited Wish (1 use, "1d4 long rests" so no recharge value). Great Old One: Entropic Ward (1, short or long). Hexblade: Hexblade's Curse (1, short or long; PB damage, level + CHA modifier hit points), Accursed Specter (1, long; half the level). Undead: Form of Dread (PB, long; 1d10 + level), Necrotic Husk (1, no recharge value; 2d10 + level), Spirit Projection (1, long). Undying: Among the Dead (spell DC), Defy Death (1, long; 1d8 + CON modifier), Indestructible Life (1, short or long; 1d8 + level).
- **Wizard (30 resources).** Abjuration: Arcane Ward (1, long; maximum 2 x level + INT modifier), Improved Abjuration (+PB). Bladesinging: Bladesong (PB, long; AC and concentration +INT modifier, min 1), Song of Defense (5 x slot level), Song of Victory. Chronurgy: Chronal Shift (2, long), Temporal Awareness (INT modifier initiative), Momentary Stasis (INT modifier (min 1), long; spell DC), Arcane Abeyance (1, short or long). Conjuration: Benign Transposition (1, long). Divination: Portent (two d20s, three from 14, long), The Third Eye (1, short or long). Enchantment: Hypnotic Gaze, Instinctive Charm (spell DC), Alter Memories (1 + CHA modifier hours). Graviturgy: Violent Attraction (INT modifier (min 1), long), Event Horizon (1, long; 2d10, spell DC). Illusion: Illusory Self (1, short or long). Necromancy: Grim Harvest (twice / three times the spell level), Undead Thralls (level, PB), Command Undead (spell DC). Scribes: Awakened Spellbook (1, long), Manifest Mind (1, long; PB casts per day), Master Scrivener, One with the Word (1, long). Transmutation: Shapechanger (1, short or long), Master Transmuter (1, long). War Magic: Arcane Deflection (Deflecting Shroud half the level as an extra from 14), Tactical Wit, Power Surge (INT modifier (min 1) surges, half the level).

  Skip reasons in short: subclass intros, expanded spell lists and spell tables (flavor or granted elsewhere); halved spellbook copy costs (the five Savant features); passive resistances, immunities, flat speeds and flat bonuses with no cost, limit or character-derived number (Psychic Defenses, Fire in the Veins, Fiery Soul, Otherworldly Wings, Gift of the Sea, Oceanic Soul, Thought Shield, Undying Nature, Inured to Undeath, Spell Resistance, Durable Summons, Durable Magic, Lunar Empowerment); at-will actions with only ranges or durations (Shadow Walk, Tempestuous Magic, Storm Guide, Minor Conjuration, Adjust Density, Gravity Well, Illusory Reality, Awakened Mind); no number (Wild Magic's Controlled Chaos and Spell Bombardment, Create Thrall, Armor of Hexes, Master of Hexes, Grave Touched, Projected Ward, Expert Divination, Split Enchantment); proficiencies, cantrips and languages; downtime (Minor Alchemy, Wizardly Quill); Convergent Future (cost is a level of exhaustion, no count).
- **Deferred (picks, not guessed).** (1) Divine Soul Divine Magic (the cleric spell access and the affinity pick). (2) Transmuter's Stone (the stone benefit is chosen at creation). (3) Hex Warrior's weapon pick and the Lunar Embodiment phase pick (the phase is not tracked: Lunar Embodiment, Lunar Boons and Waxing and Waning describe all three). (4) The patron expanded spell lists stay with the existing spell logic.
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys; `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no `npm run build`).
- **Golden: no rebaseline.** The golden's primary Sorcerer, Warlock and Wizard cases hold the SRD subclasses (Draconic, Fiend, Evocation) and no existing sink holds a book subclass of these classes, so no case moved: the full golden-driven tests pass against the unchanged stored results. No `REBASELINES` entry. `--append` added the kitchen sink `celestial-warlock14-mixed` (Celestial Warlock 14, mixed; `ks/src-all` and `ks/src-srd`): 249 -> 251 cases, the 249 existing cases byte-identical (checked by loading the gz before and after); `_EXPECT` of `test_export_fields` lists Healing Light, Radiant Soul, Celestial Resilience and Searing Vengeance so Markdown / PDF / YAML carry them.
- **Edited tests (deliberate).** `test_c_series_golden` only (the W6 sweep exempts the appended Warlock 14 sink, which legitimately holds Mystic Arcanum; a W8d guard test).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20, mixed / 2014): Lunar Sorcerer 3.5 / 2.6, Shadow Sorcerer 3.5 / 2.6, Genie Warlock 3.5 / 2.7, Celestial Warlock 3.5 / 2.8, Undead Warlock 3.5 / 2.7, Scribes Wizard 3.5 / 2.7, Graviturgy Wizard 3.9 / 2.6, War Magic Wizard 3.5 / 2.6 (limit 50; also asserted).
- **Totals.** Server suite 6734 -> 7070 passed (328 new + 1 golden test + the 2 new sink cases through the parametrized sweeps), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched. W8 as a whole (a-d): all 108 non-SRD 2014 subclasses scanned (341 feature records in part 1, 383 in part 2: 217 + 166), the book tables hold 235 resources and 61 sentences (part 2 added 152 resources and 21 sentences; W8c and W8d together also 10 extras).
- **Uncertain.** (1) Limited Wish and Necrotic Husk are rolled in long rests (1d4); they are one-use resources without a recharge value, unlike W8a's Fanatical Focus, which is note-only (uses None, recharge None). (2) Form of Dread's Wisdom save names no DC in the text, so none is printed. (3) Manifest Mind has two limits in the text (conjuring once per long rest, PB casts per day); the resource tracks the conjuring and the note states the casts. (4) Power Surge is a note-only entry because its pool resets to one, not to the maximum, on a long rest.


## W7 (F4) The 2014 SRD subclass use-limit tail (2026-10-24)

Rulesets mixed / 2014 only and the 12 SRD subclass ids only (the 117 book subclass features are W8); 2024 output is byte-identical (the 2014 subclass tables are never read under "2024", also for a held SRD subclass, pinned by a test that empties them). Tests first (`tests/test_subclass_features_2014_w7.py`, 130, rule matrix in the docstring); with the `_subclass_tail_2014` call removed 90 of them fail, with the ruleset gate removed the 2024 independence test fails.

- **Data.** `reference_data.SUBCLASS_TAIL_2014` (16 resources) and `SUBCLASS_NOTES_2014` (7 sentences on existing resources), same spec keys as the W6 tables plus `subclass`; one level table that lives only in the text, `DIVINE_STRIKE_DICE_2014 = ((8, "1d8"), (14, "2d8"))`, pinned against `divine-strike` (rendered through `level_vars`). `test_quotes_match_catalog` pins every quote against an SRD record that belongs to the named SRD subclass and class.
- **Engine.** `class_resources._subclass_tail_2014(ctx, rc, resources)` (reuses the W6 `_entry_2014` / `_add_note`), called from `resources_for` after `_subclass_tail_2024`: class entries, W6 tail, R6 builders, then the new subclass entries (source kind "subclass"). Needs the selected subclass id, the SRD feature record and the class level at its level; nothing under "2024".
- **New resources (id, first level, uses / recharge).** Berserker Frenzy (3; note-only, bonus-action attack, one level of exhaustion when the rage ends), Intimidating Presence (10; DC 8 + PB + CHA, 24 hours); Life Disciple of Life (1; 2 + the spell's level, plus Blessed Healer from 6), Divine Strike (Life Domain) (8; `dice` 1d8, 2d8 from 14; id `cleric:divine-strike-life-domain`, deliberately not `cleric:divine-strike`, which feeds the 2024 attack-row notes); Land Natural Recovery (2; `pool` = half the Druid level rounded up, long rest), Nature's Sanctuary (14; DC 8 + PB + WIS); Champion Survivor (18; 5 + CON); Open Hand Wholeness of Body (6; 1, long rest, 3 x Monk level), Tranquility (11; sanctuary DC 8 + WIS + PB); Devotion Holy Nimbus (20; note-only: 30-foot radius, 10 radiant damage); Draconic Elemental Affinity (6; CHA modifier); Fiend Dark One's Blessing (1; CHA modifier + Warlock level, minimum 1 temporary hit points), Dark One's Own Luck (6; 1, short or long rest, d10), Hurl Through Hell (14; 1, long rest, 10d10 psychic); Evocation Empowered Evocation (10; INT modifier), Overchannel (14; 1 free use, long rest; 2d12 necrotic per spell level after).
- **Notes on existing resources.** Bardic Inspiration (Lore Cutting Words 3, Peerless Skill 14: each expends a use), Cleric Channel Divinity (Life Preserve Life: 5 x Cleric level hit points), Paladin Channel Divinity (Devotion Sacred Weapon: CHA modifier, minimum +1; Turn the Unholy), Monk Ki (Open Hand Quivering Palm: 3 ki, days = Monk level, 10d10 necrotic, DC 8 + PB + WIS), Sorcery Points (Draconic Presence: 5 sorcery points). They follow the W6 notes of the same target ("; "-joined).
- **Scanned (all 88 SRD subclass records: berserker 4, lore 4, life 5, land 17, champion 5, open-hand 4, devotion 5, hunter 15, thief 5, draconic 15, fiend 4, evocation 5) and skipped:**
  - No number or use rule: Mindless Rage, Retaliation (Berserker); Bonus Proficiency (Life); Land's Stride, Nature's Ward, the Circle of the Land variants and Circle Spells (spell lists), Bonus Cantrip (Land); Additional Fighting Style (a card); Aura of Devotion, Purity of Spirit (Devotion); Fiendish Resilience (a pick on every rest, no number); Evocation Savant (halved copy cost).
  - Already cards (RPX1 / RP12): Lore Bonus Proficiencies and Additional Magical Secrets.
  - Numbers but passive or derived elsewhere: Improved Critical (19-20) and Superior Critical (18-20) (the crit range is not tracked), Remarkable Athlete (half PB on checks), Open Hand Technique (effects, no DC in the text), Second-Story Work (DEX feet), Supreme Sneak, Use Magic Device, Thief's Reflexes (initiative - 10), Draconic Resilience (HP / AC effect), Dragon Ancestor and its ten ancestry records, Dragon Wings (fly speed equals the walk speed, no use count), Sculpt Spells (1 + the spell's level creatures, no character number), Potent Cantrip (half damage on a success, no number).
  - One-of picks without a use limit (deferred, see below): Hunter's Prey (Colossus Slayer 1d8, Giant Killer, Horde Breaker), Defensive Tactics (Multiattack Defense +4 AC), Multiattack, Superior Hunter's Defense (the whole Hunter row, 15 records).
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys; `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no `npm run build`).
- **Golden: `--rebaseline W7`** (`REBASELINES["W7"]`; the golden's primary cases hold the class's first SRD subclass from level 3; `cases` from the rule matrix by class and level plus the multiclass Barbarian 3 / Monk 2 and Warlock 5 / Sorcerer 3 and the Lore Bard kitchen sinks; `paths` = the 16 new resource roots, the `details`/`details.note` of the five attached targets, and the whole `classResources` list when it was empty (Warlock 3 without picks)). Dry run first, then the real run; the gz was diffed before / after with `_norm` / `diff_paths` (112 matched cases moved, 131 unmatched byte-identical, no path outside the allowlist). Printed summary:
```

  matching cases changed: 112 of 243
    130 changed path(s) match ^result\.derived\.classResources\[(barbarian:(frenzy|intimidating-presence)|cleric:(disciple-of-life|divine-strike-life-domain)|druid:(natural-recovery|nature-s-sanctuary)|fighter:survivor|monk:(wholeness-of-body|tranquility)|paladin:holy-nimbus|sorcerer:elemental-affinity|warlock:(dark-one-s-blessing|dark-one-s-own-luck|hurl-through-hell)|wizard:(empowered-evocation|overchannel))\]$
     48 changed path(s) match ^result\.derived\.classResources\[(bard:bardic-inspiration|cleric:channel-divinity|paladin:channel-divinity|monk:ki|sorcerer:sorcery-points)\]\.details(\.note)?$

  The kitchen sinks psi-warrior11, totem-bear6 (Totem Warrior is a book subclass) and every kitchen sink without an SRD subclass did not move; bard6-lore-2014 / -mixed did. `sheet.features` did not move.
- **Edited tests (deliberate).** `test_worn_wielded` (the pre-G2 neutraliser also removes the W7 resources and notes on both sides), `test_c_series_golden` (REBASELINES tuple, a W7 case test).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20, with the SRD subclass, mixed / 2014): Fighter (Champion) 3.4 / 2.2, Wizard (Evocation) 3.6 / 2.7, Bard (Lore) 5.7 / 5.0, Cleric (Life) 3.2 / 2.4, Warlock (Fiend) 3.7 / 2.7 (limit 50; also asserted).
- **Totals.** Server suite 5602 -> 5733 passed (130 new + 1 golden test), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched.
- **Uncertain / deferred.** (1) The Hunter's picks (Colossus Slayer 1d8 once per turn, Multiattack Defense +4 AC ...) need RP13-style pick notes on the 2014 feature-text cards; no use limit, so left for W8 or a user decision. (2) Holy Nimbus: the plan listed "long rest", but the catalog text of `holy-nimbus` has no rest sentence, so no recharge is modelled (pinned by `test_holy_nimbus_has_no_invented_rest_rule`). (3) The book subclasses with the same feature names (Oathbreaker, Ancients ...) get nothing until W8. (4) The Divine Strike resource shows the dice but, unlike the 2024 Blessed Strikes, adds no attack-row note (an attack-row change is outside this slice's allowlist).

## W6 (F4) The 2014 SRD class-feature use-limit tail (2026-10-24)

Rulesets mixed / 2014 only; 2024 output is byte-identical (the 2014 tables are never read under "2024", pinned by a test that empties them and compares all 12 classes at 20). Tests first (`tests/test_class_features_2014_w6.py`, 103, rule matrix in the docstring); with the two wiring calls removed 97 of them fail, with the ruleset gate removed the two 2024 independence tests fail. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-24-gap-audit-plan.md`, F4 / W6 (class half; W7 is the SRD subclass half).

- **Data.** `reference_data.RESOURCE_TAIL_2014` (14 resources) and `RESOURCE_NOTES_2014` (9 sentences on existing resources), SRD feature ids only (the 2014 API ids, e.g. `stroke-of-luck`, `song-of-rest-d6`). Spec keys: `class`, `feature` (the SRD record; its own `level` is the first level, no level is duplicated in code), `name`, `uses` / `recharge` / `die` / `dice` / `pool` (a literal, an "@variable" of `_note_vars` / `vars` / `level_vars`, or a staged tuple (default, ((feature id, value), ...)): the value of the last listed record whose level the class has reached; Mystic Arcanum 1 -> 4 uses, Song of Rest d6 -> d12), `note` (template), `vars`, `level_vars` (tables that live only in the text; none needed in W6), `extras`, `quotes`. `test_quotes_match_catalog` pins every quote against name + desc of an SRD record (and that every referenced record is an SRD record); the staged record levels are pinned too (Brutal Critical 13 / 17, Song of Rest 9 / 13 / 17, Mystic Arcanum 13 / 15 / 17).
- **Engine.** `class_resources._tail_2014` (new resources, after the class's own entries) and `_attach_notes_2014` (notes on existing resources, "; "-joined in table order), both called from `resources_for` right after the 2024 tail, both returning nothing under ruleset "2024". New helpers `_field_2014`, `_values_2014`, `_entry_2014`, `_add_note`; `_note_vars` gained `con`, `survivor` (5 + CON), `blessing` (max(1, CHA + level)) and `pact_slots` (Warlock only); `_render_note` accepts `level_vars` (unused by 2024 specs, W7 uses it). The feature record must be in the catalog and the class level at its level, like the 2024 tail.
- **New resources (id, first level, uses / recharge).** Barbarian Brutal Critical (9; note-only, one / two / three additional dice at 13 / 17), Relentless Rage (11; note-only, DC 10, +5 per use, reset on a short or long rest); Bard Song of Rest (2; note-only with `die` d6 / d8 / d10 / d12 at 9 / 13 / 17); Cleric Divine Intervention (10; 1, long rest; roll <= Cleric level, 7 days after a success, automatic at 20); Monk Deflect Missiles (3; 1d10 + DEX + Monk level, 1 ki to catch and attack), Slow Fall (4; 5 x level), Stunning Strike (5; 1 ki, DC 8 + PB + WIS, the same DC as the Ki resource), Empty Body (18; 4 / 8 ki); Paladin Divine Smite (2; 2d8, +1d8 per slot level, maximum 5d8, +1d8 undead / fiend; Improved Divine Smite +1d8 from 11), Cleansing Touch (14; max(1, CHA) uses, long rest); Ranger Foe Slayer (20; WIS modifier added); Rogue Stroke of Luck (20; 1, short or long rest); Warlock Mystic Arcanum (11; 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 uses at 11 / 13 / 15 / 17, long rest), Eldritch Master (20; 1, long rest, all Pact Magic slots with the maximum in the note).
- **Notes on existing resources.** Bardic Inspiration (Superior Inspiration 20), Cleric Channel Divinity (Turn Undead, save DC 8 + PB + WIS), Druid Wild Shape (a beast shape lasts half the Druid level hours; the R3 numbers are untouched), Monk Ki (Flurry of Blows / Patient Defense / Step of the Wind cost 1 ki at 2, Diamond Soul at 14, Perfect Self at 20), Paladin Lay on Hands (5 hit points of the pool cure a disease or poison) and Channel Divinity (DC 8 + PB + CHA), Sorcerer Sorcery Points (Sorcerous Restoration 4 points on a short rest). The ids `barbarian:relentless-rage`, `monk:slow-fall`, `monk:stunning-strike`, `cleric:divine-intervention` and `rogue:stroke-of-luck` now exist under every ruleset (same names in both editions); each carries its own edition's text.
- **Scanned (all 319 SRD class-feature records of the 12 classes: use / rest / times / expend / spend text, then dice, DC and "equal to" numbers) and skipped:**
  - Already a resource (R2-R6, RP15, R8): Rage, Sneak Attack, Second Wind, Action Surge, Indomitable (all three records), Martial Arts, Ki, Bardic Inspiration (four dice records), Font of Inspiration, Channel Divinity (Cleric 1 / 2 / 3 per rest records, Paladin), Destroy Undead (five CR records), Wild Shape (the CR records at 2 / 4 / 8) and Archdruid, Lay on Hands, Divine Sense, Sorcery Points / Font of Magic / Flexible Casting costs, Arcane Recovery, Signature Spell, Spell Mastery (pick card), Eldritch Invocation records (C7b / R8), Mystic Arcanum picks (choice cards).
  - No number or use rule: Unarmored Defense (both), Reckless Attack, Danger Sense, Primal Path / Bard College / Divine Domain / Sacred Oath / Roguish Archetype / Ranger Archetype / Arcane Tradition / Sorcerous Origin / Otherworldly Patron / Druid Circle / Martial Archetype / Monastic Tradition and their "feature" records, Feral Instinct, Persistent Rage, Spellcasting records, Domain Spells / Oath Spells, Druidic, Beast Spells, Timeless Body (both), Extra Attack records, Fighting Style records (feat cards), Expertise, Magical Secrets, Countercharm, Divine Health, Evasion (both), Stillness of Mind, Purity of Body, Tongue of the Sun and Moon, Ki Empowered Strikes, Thieves' Cant, Cunning Action, Uncanny Dodge, Blindsense, Slippery Mind, Elusive, Vanish, Feral Senses, Land's Stride, Favored Enemy, Natural Explorer, Pact Boon and the three pacts, Pact Magic, Supreme Healing.
  - Numbers but passive or already derived elsewhere: Jack of All Trades (half PB, a check bonus), Fast Movement / Unarmored Movement (speed tables), Aura of Protection / Courage / Aura improvements (auras), Indomitable Might (STR score as a floor), Primal Champion (+4 STR / CON, ability effect), Hide in Plain Sight (+10 Stealth while still), Primeval Awareness (1 minute per slot level, 1 / 6 miles), Spell Mastery, Metamagic and its eight options (costs ride on the 2014 pick, which has no resource wiring; deferred, see below), ASI records.
- **Surfaces.** No new output keys: `details.note`, `die`, `uses`, `recharge` print through `export_fields.resource_line` and `ClassResourcesList.describeResource`; `types.ts`, `export_fields.py` and the frontend are untouched (no `npm run build`).
- **Golden: `--rebaseline W6`** (`REBASELINES["W6"]`, cases written from the rule matrix: the classes at or above their feature levels, the two multiclass cases that hold a Bard / Monk, the kitchen sinks of those classes; `paths` = the new resource roots, the `details`/`details.note` of the nine attached targets, and the whole `classResources` list when it was empty). Dry run first (no violation), then the real run; the gz was diffed before / after with `_norm` / `diff_paths` (116 matched cases moved, 127 unmatched cases byte-identical, no path outside the allowlist). Printed summary:
```
