  `sheet.features` did not move (nothing in it reads a resource). The kitchen sinks psi-warrior11, totem-bear6, wizard*, dragonborn6, feats-* did not move; barbarian20-2014, bard10-2014, bard6-lore-*, cleric6, monk14, paladin6 and warlock17 did.
- **Edited tests (deliberate).** `test_class_feature_numbers_2024::test_nothing_outside_ruleset_2024` and `test_attached_notes_only_under_2024`, `test_class_resources_tail_2024::test_nothing_outside_ruleset_2024` (the shared ids now exist under 2014 with their own text: they assert the 2024 note is absent instead of the id), `test_class_resources_bard_sorcerer_wizard_ranger::test_multiclass_bard_wizard_gets_one_entry_each_in_class_order` (Song of Rest), `test_class_resources_cleric_paladin_druid::test_druid_srd_wild_shape` (the hours note), `test_worn_wielded::test_drafts_without_worn_or_wielded_resolve_exactly_as_before_g2` (the pre-G2 baseline neutralises the W6 resources and notes on both sides), `test_c_series_golden` (REBASELINES tuple, a W6 case test).

- **Uncertain / deferred.** (1) Metamagic option costs for the 2014 Sorcerer (the SRD records are verifiable; the 2014 picks have no resource wiring like the 2024 C6c-1 one). (2) The merged Channel Divinity (PHB 164, Cleric + Paladin multiclass) keeps only the winning class's note. (3) Divine Intervention uses 1 / long rest as the base recharge; the 7-day lockout after a success is in the note only.

## W5b (F2) Species trait resources for the non-2024 species (2026-10-24)

Builds on W5a: the same `derived.speciesResources` key, shape, exports and SPA list; no new key, no `types.ts` / frontend change. The table for the 2014 records is `reference_data.SPECIES_RESOURCES_2014` (trait id -> spec, same fields as `SPECIES_RESOURCES`), looked up after the W5a table by `engine/species_resources.py`; `resources()` gained a `choices=` argument (the resolved cards) because the 2014 ancestry picks are cards, not species variants.

### Commit 1: SRD species

- **Modelled (3).** Dragonborn Breath Weapon (`breath-weapon`), Half-Orc Relentless Endurance, Halfling Lucky (note only). Quote-pinned against the SRD trait text (tags stripped) in `tests/test_w5b_species_resources.py`.
- **SRD Breath Weapon.** `uses` 1, `recharge` "short-or-long" ("you cannot use it again until you complete a short or long rest"), `dc` 8 + CON + PB, `dice` by total level 2d6 / 3d6 at 6 / 4d6 at 11 / 5d6 at 16 (all from the text). The damage type, save and area come from the picked Draconic Ancestry subtrait's structured `trait_specific.breath_weapon` (card `species:dragonborn:trait:draconic-ancestry`): `details.damageType` ("acid" ...), `details.save` ("DEX" / "CON"), `details.shape` ("a 30-foot line" / "a 15-foot cone"), `details.note`. A test asserts that structured data (dice progression, once per rest, half on success) equals the pinned text for all ten colors. Unpicked: the note says to choose the ancestry (`damageType` None, no `shape` / `save` keys). Never an attack row (D-2).
- **Scanned and skipped (SRD).** Tiefling Infernal Legacy (innate spells, already modelled as innate spells), Elf Trance (4-hour rest, no uses), Rock Gnome Tinker (no use count or rest rule), the remaining SRD traits have no use or rest text. A test scans every SRD race and subrace trait for a use / rest pattern and fails on a trait that is neither in the table nor in that skip list.
- **Golden.** All 241 existing cases are the SRD Human (verified by script), so mixed / 2014 output does not move and no rebaseline was needed (`derived.speciesResources` is already in `STRIP`). `--append` added two cases for the new kitchen sink `dragonborn6-2014` (SRD Dragonborn, Fighter 6, Acolyte; `ks/src-all` and `ks/src-srd`): 241 -> 243, the 241 existing entries asserted unchanged and rewritten byte for byte. `_EXPECT` in `test_export_fields.py` has the new sink (Breath Weapon in every export).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_w5b_species_resources.py`: quote pins, structured-data agreement, SRD scan, ten ancestry colors, dice by level at the boundaries, unpicked ancestry, 2014 and mixed rulesets, empty lists for species without table traits, degradation with a patched catalog (missing ancestry trait, missing trait), export block. Verified to fail when the `choices` wiring in `resolve()` is removed (11 failures).

### Commit 2: book species (PHB, MPMM, VGM, FTD, AAG, ...)

- **Scan.** Every non-SRD, non-2024 species record (race trait refs plus the racial traits of its subspecies) whose trait text matches a use / rest pattern (`SCAN_PATTERN` in the test: long / short rest, "expended", "times equal", "can't do so again", "once per", ...) is either in `SPECIES_RESOURCES_2014` or in the test's `SKIPPED_BOOK_TRAITS` with a reason; a new trait that is neither fails the scan, and so does a stale skip entry. 137 traits matched: **56 modelled, 81 skipped**. The full table is below (modelled entries: uses, recharge, extras).
- **Rule for what is modelled.** Only numbers and rules that the trait text states: PB uses ("a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus ... long rest"), a single use with a short or long rest recharge, dice (Breath Weapon progression, d4 / d6 / d12), DC (8 + PB + the ability the text names), level gates ("Starting at 3rd / 5th level"), and note numbers derived from the character (PB, level, CON modifier, 5 x PB feet). A trait whose casting ability is chosen when selecting the race (Eladrin Fey Step, Kender Taunt) gets no `dc`. The notes paraphrase; every entry's `quotes` are verbatim substrings of the catalog text, pinned per entry.
- **Breath Weapon-likes (D-2).** PHB Dragonborn: `uses` 1, short or long rest, DC 8 + CON + PB, 2d6 / 3d6 / 4d6 / 5d6 at 1 / 6 / 11 / 16; the damage type is the species resistance pick (the catalog has no ancestry table for the PHB Dragonborn, so no save or area is stated and `details.save` is absent). FTD Chromatic / Gem / Metallic: PB uses, long rest, DC 8 + CON + PB, 1d10 / 2d10 / 3d10 / 4d10 at 1 / 5 / 11 / 17, Dexterity save, damage type from the variant (`resist`), area from the text (30-foot line for Chromatic, 15-foot cone for Gem and Metallic). Metallic Breath Weapon (the second breath at 5th level) has a DC and 1 long-rest use but no dice or save (the options list is missing from the catalog text). None of these is an attack row.
- **Skipped, by reason.** (a) 56 traits that grant innate spells (Drow Magic, tiefling legacies, Mark of ... magic, genasi, gith, MPMM Fairy / Firbolg / Triton ..., Elf Eladrin DMG Fey Step "cast misty step"): the uses belong to the spell. (b) 9 rest-duration traits (Trance, Astral Trance, Sentry's Rest, Sleepless). (c) Per-turn / per-combat / movement-recharged riders (Hobgoblin Martial Advantage, Bugbear Surprise Attack "once per combat", Tabaxi Feline Agility "until you move 0 feet"): the resource vocabulary is `long`, `short`, `short-or-long` only. (d) Flight (armor wording), Gift of the Aetherborn, Astral Knowledge, Lucky Footwork, Cunning Artisan, Blood Thirst, Black Blood Healing: no use count.
- **Trait text that is cut in the catalog.** Several traits lose their option lists (Dhampir Vampiric Bite, Aasimar Celestial Revelation, Hobgoblin Fey Gift level 3 options, Eladrin Fey Step seasons, Hexblood Eerie Token actions, Metallic second breath options); the notes say only what remains in the text.
- **Golden.** Unchanged by this commit (243 cases after commit 1; every golden draft is the SRD Human or the appended SRD Dragonborn); no rebaseline, no `--append` needed. Existing 241 cases verified unchanged by `--append`'s own assertion (run again, 0 added) and `test_c_series_golden.py` (260 passed).
- **Timings** (`resolve()`, mean of 30, ms, fully chosen drafts, a Dragonborn species at level 20): Fighter 20 mixed 4.29 / 2014 2.64 / 2024 2.94, Wizard 20 mixed 3.73 / 2014 2.65 / 2024 3.42 (all well under 50 ms; the species table lookup is one dict get per trait).
- **Totals.** Server suite 5301 -> 5438 passed (commit 1: 5340), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped (`tests/test_chargen_isolation.py` untouched), frontend untouched (no build needed).
- **Deferred (uncertain, user-owned).** Whether a per-combat / per-turn rider should become a resource kind; whether innate-spell uses should be tracked on the sheet; the Giant Ancestry-style option lists missing from the catalog for the book species.

## W5a (F2) Species trait resources: `derived.speciesResources` (2026-10-24)

**Superseded by W5b (and WF):** the 2014 / non-2024 species are no longer empty: `SPECIES_RESOURCES_2014` models the SRD and book species (see W5b below); the statements here that the key is `[]` for every non-2024 species describe W5a only.

- **What.** New derived list key `speciesResources`, the class / feat resource shape minus `classId`, plus `speciesId` and `traitId`, `source = {kind: "species", id, label}`, id `species:<raceId>:<slug of trait name>`. Built by the pure `server/engine/species_resources.py` (`resources(catalog, race, race_id, subrace, variant, total_level, pb, modifiers, walk_speed)`), called from `resolve()` next to `featResources`. Data: `SPECIES_RESOURCES` in `catalog/reference_data.py`, keyed by catalog trait id; each entry carries `quotes` (verbatim substrings of the catalog trait text, tags stripped) and a paraphrased `note` template. Only ruleset-2024 species records are listed; the key is `[]` for every other species (the table gates on the species id, not on the draft ruleset, so decision D-1 holds: a 2024 species picked under mixed gets them too; no golden case holds one).
- **Breath Weapon-like traits (D-2).** Resources with `dc` (8 + CON mod + PB), `dice` (1d10 / 2d10 at 5 / 3d10 at 11 / 4d10 at 17), and `details` (`save`, `shape`, `damageType` from the picked Draconic Ancestry, `note`); never attack rows. Giant Ancestry puts the picked boon (name + effect, Stone's Endurance with the CON modifier) in the note, or "choose a Giant Ancestry" while unpicked. Level-gated traits (Draconic Flight 5, Large Form 5, Celestial Revelation 3) appear from their level.
- **Modelled (15).** Dragonborn Breath Weapon, Draconic Flight; Dwarf Stonecunning; Goliath Giant Ancestry, Large Form; Orc Adrenaline Rush (short or long rest), Relentless Endurance; Aasimar Healing Hands (PB d4s), Celestial Revelation; Halfling Luck (note); Human Resourceful (note); plus found by the catalog scan: Lupin Howl (rhw), Reborn Knowledge from a Past Life (rhw), Shifter Shifting (efa), Khoravar Lethargy Resilience (efa). **Scanned, skipped** (rest rule but no use count, or already modelled): Elven Lineage / Fiendish Legacy (innate spells, done), Trance, Everlasting, Sentry's Rest (rest length), Severed from Dreams, Fey Gift, Skill Versatility (swap per Long Rest); Human Skillful (a proficiency, no use or rest rule). `test_scan_every_2024_species_trait_with_a_rest_or_use_rule_is_modelled_or_listed_skipped` fails when a new such trait appears.
- **Surfaces.** `export_fields.DERIVED_FIELDS` (YAML / Markdown / PDF via the same `resource_line`, h2 "Ресурси от расата" in `extra_blocks`), `types.ts` `SpeciesResource` + `Derived.speciesResources?`, `ExportPage.tsx` renders it with the existing `ClassResourcesList` (its `describeResource` mirror accepts the new type; no CSS). Kitchen sinks `dragonborn11-2024`, `orc5-2024`, `goliath5-2024` run through all three exporters.
- **Giant Ancestry quotes.** The catalog trait text drops the boon list, so `GIANT_ANCESTRY_2024` quotes are pinned against the raw 5etools `races.json` Goliath (XPHB) entry.
- **Golden.** The 241 cases are unchanged and the gz untouched; `derived.speciesResources` is added to the `STRIP` tuple as an additive key like `featResources` (every golden draft is the SRD Human, so it is `[]`; verified for all 241). No rebaseline.
- **Tests.** `server/tests/test_w5a_species_resources.py` (48): quote pins per entry, raw Giant Ancestry pins, catalog scan, dice / DC / PB scaling, level gating, variants, empty for non-table species and 2014 records, export blocks; verified to fail when the resolve() wiring is disabled.

## W4 (F5) Backgrounds without an origin feat (2026-10-24)

- **What the raw data held** (`~/.claude/dnd/.data/official/backgrounds.json`, verified by script): **Genie Touched (FRHoF)** has no `feats` field at all, but its entry reads "Feat: {@feat Magic Initiate|XPHB} (Wizard)" (a data gap in the file). **Mist Wanderer (RHW)** and **Spirit Medium (RHW)** carry only `feats: [{"anyFromCategory": {"category": ["DG"]}}]` and the text "A Dark Gift feat of your choice" (Mist Walker / Gathered Whispers "is recommended"): a pick, not a fixed feat, which the converter dropped (it keeps `is True` entries only). So outcome (a), in two shapes. Haunted One and Investigator carry the same wildcard beside a plain grant ("Survivor or a Dark Gift feat"): an alternative, the plain grant stays and they get no card (deferred).
- **Genie Touched.** `reference_data.BACKGROUND_FEATS_FROM_TEXT["genie touched|frhof"]` restores the missing `feats` as `[{"magic initiate; wizard|xphb": True}]` (the shape the four other spell-list backgrounds use), so `feats = ["magic-initiate"]`, `feat_refs = ["magic-initiate-xphb"]` and the hint "Wizard Spells" follow; the record's own sentence is the pinned `quote`. Applied only when the raw record has no `feats`.
- **Dark Gift pick.** `_background_from_5etools` now emits `feat_grants` (`[{"count": 1, "category": "DG"}]`, built by the race converter `_species_feat_grants_from_5etools`) only when the record has no plain feat and no alternatives. `resolve()` builds one card `background:<id>:feat-<i>` (kind `background`, label "Feat", description "Choose a Dark Gift feat.") through the SAME species feat machinery (`_species_feat_choice_template` got a `kind` parameter; `_background_feat_grant_slots` lists the slots). The card runs before the species cards (precedence background, species, ASI), its picked feat is a `species_feats` entry tagged `_background_grant`, so the S2 `taken` map, the ASI pre-walk, the feat effects and the cascaded choices (`<cid>:feat:<index>:...`, read from `background.choices`, not `species.choices`) work unchanged; `sheet.feats[].origin` is `background`. `class_choices_2024.mark_unavailable` also covers the background card (W3 rule): sources without RHW give `unavailable`, never a blocking `pending`. The warning `ruleset-2024-background-no-origin-feat` no longer fires for them. The 9 Dark Gifts all have `ruleset` 2014 and a Ravenloft campaign prerequisite (not checked by the engine), so they are offered under every ruleset.
- **Not affected.** The other 57 backgrounds, the species feat cards, `types.ts`, the exports and the frontend (BackgroundPage already lists `background`-kind cards by cid). The Dark Gifts are in no other feat pool (Origin / General / category-less / ASI), so the duplicate map only matters in principle (a test widens the Human pool to prove the order); Magic Initiate is `repeatable`, so Genie Touched plus a Human Origin pick of Magic Initiate is allowed.
- **Tests.** `server/tests/test_w4_background_feats.py` (26): raw record pins, converter output, card, pick, cascaded choices, taken map, sources, hidden pick, unrelated backgrounds. Golden unchanged (241, no regeneration); a 81-draft sweep of the three backgrounds x rulesets x sources x species ended with no pending card and 200 on all exports; `FUZZ_N=500` passes.
- **Deferred.** Haunted One / Investigator "or a Dark Gift feat of your choice" alternative (needs an either-or card; Rewarded / Ruined `feat_options` are likewise not a card).

## W3 (F9) Fuzz / property harness and the non-settling fill (2026-10-24)

- **Harness.** `server/tests/test_resolve_fuzz.py`: `gen_draft(seed, catalog)` is a pure function of `random.Random(seed)` (ruleset 2014 / 2024 / mixed, 1-3 distinct classes with levels summing to at most 20, a random subclass at any level, species with an optional subspecies and a background allowed by the ruleset, half the drafts with a random `sources` subset, base scores 3-18), filled by `kitchen_sink.fill` through a tracing resolve. Properties: resolve never raises (filled and unfilled drafts), resolve twice is byte-identical (`json.dumps(sort_keys=True)`), `fill` settles within 10 rounds (the failure message has the seed, the full draft and the cards still pending), 3 random mutations per settled draft (level-down, class reorder, ruleset / species / background switch) never raise and every stored pick without a card, or selected id outside its options, is listed in `pruned`, yaml / markdown / pdf exports return 200 on up to 10 problem-free settled drafts, the slowest resolve is under 50 ms (a slow draft is re-timed warm, best of 3: the first resolve with a new `sources` set builds the spell-visibility cache; one cold run of 8000 took 66 ms, warm 2.8 ms). Default N=60 adds about 3 s.
- **Bigger runs.** `cd server && FUZZ_N=3000 ../.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_resolve_fuzz.py -q` (3000 drafts about 80 s, 8000 about 190 s); `FUZZ_SEED0=<n>` starts at another seed. No new dependency and no marker (the env var is the switch).
- **Volume run.** Seeds 0..2999 (before the fix) and 0..7999 (after): 0 exceptions, 0 non-idempotent resolves, 0 mutation or export failures. Only F9 below failed.
- **F9 root cause.** The planner's non-settling case was not reproducible with `sources: []` (a targeted sorcerer 5 + wizard 10 scan over 12000 seeds and all three rulesets settled every time). The fuzz found it with a restricted `sources`: seeds 763 and 1366 (mixed, `human-xphb`, sources without any book that has Origin feats). `_species_feat_pool` keeps only feats from the allowed books, so the Human Origin feat card had zero options but stayed `pending`; `fill` stored `[]` forever and export could never succeed (nothing to flip: one card, never fillable). Fix (`class_choices_2024.mark_unavailable`): a pending species feat card with no options at all becomes `unavailable` with a reason, like the F3a cards; cards whose options are merely all disabled (prerequisite / already taken) are untouched. Regression: `test_f9_fuzz_seeds_settle[763|1366]` and a hand-built draft test. Golden unchanged (default sources never hit it). A scan of 1500 seeds for other pending cards with no enabled option found no other source.
- **Not found.** No oscillating pair of cards turned up in 8000 drafts; if the planner's seed 11 was a different (flip) case it is still unreproduced.

## W2 (F6, F7, F8) Input bounds: ability clamp, level audit, non-object bodies (2026-10-24)

- **Ability scores (F6, D-4).** `normalize_draft` clamps each base score to 1..30 and reports `ability-out-of-range-<AB>` (problem, same id family as the existing final-score check; resolve skips its own duplicate when normalize already reported that ability). Before: `99999999999999999999` flowed through unclamped (huge modifier, one problem from the final-score check). Non-numeric stays `ability-invalid-<AB>` -> 10 (unchanged). New non-blocking warning `ability-above-20-<AB>` (in `warnings`, base score 21..30). Point buy / standard array / roll rules untouched.
- **Class levels (F7).** VERIFIED before any change: resolve already reports level 999 / 21 / 0 / -1 as `level-unsupported` and falls back to level 1 (never crashes, never inflates); total over 20 is already reported as `total-level-exceeded`. Existing tests pin both (including "garbage 25 does not trigger total-level-exceeded"), so they were NOT changed: no clamp-to-20, no new `level-total-over-20` id. Pinned further in `tests/test_w2_bounds.py`.
- **Non-object body (F8).** `/api/resolve` and `/api/export/<fmt>` answer a parsed JSON list / string / number / bool with 400 `{"error": "draft must be a JSON object"}`; an empty, unparseable or `null` body is still an empty draft. These are the only endpoints taking a draft. The SPA (`useResolution.ts`) already shows the `error` text for a non-OK resolve and its drafts are always objects, so the frontend is untouched.
- **Output keys.** Only new warning / problem ids; `types.ts` and exports unchanged. Golden unchanged (no regeneration). resolve() Fighter 20 about 1.4 ms, Wizard 20 about 2.0 ms.

## W1 (F1, F3) Dwarven Toughness for official dwarves and the heavy-armor speed penalty (2026-10-24)

Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-24-gap-audit-plan.md` (decision D-3: the penalty applies under every ruleset).

- **Dwarven Toughness (F1).** `SRD_TRAIT_EFFECTS` gained `hp_per_level` entries for the official traits `dwarf-xphb-trait-2`, `dwarf-phb-hill-phb-trait-0`, `dwarf-(kaladesh)-psk-trait-6` (1 per level) and Stensia "Tough" `human-(innistrad)-psi-stensia-psi-trait-1`. **Deviation from the plan:** Stensia's catalog text is "increases by 2, and it increases by 2 every time you gain a level", so the entry is **2 per level** (Fighter 5: +10), not +level. Each entry has a quote in the new `reference_data.TRAIT_EFFECT_QUOTES` (pinned against `catalog.traits`), and a test scans the whole trait catalog for the sentence "maximum increases by N, and it increases by N": exactly the five mapped traits (SRD + four official) match, so a new book cannot add an unmapped one.
- **Heavy-armor speed (F3).** `equipment.armor_speed_penalty(equipped_items, final_scores)` returns 10 when any worn body armor lists a Strength score above the wearer's (PHB 2014 armor table and PHB 2024 ch. 6: "that armor reduces the wearer's speed by 10 feet unless the wearer has a Strength score equal to or higher than the listed score"; verified in the 2024 book text). `resolve()` applies it to the walk speed after Unarmored Movement, Fast Movement, Aura of Alacrity and the feat walk bonuses, floored at 0 (`_heavy_armor_speed_penalty`), and BEFORE every "equal to your walking speed" mode is set: the class / feat ones (Gift of the Depths, Thief, Roving, Athlete) AND, since WF, the species ones (`_species_equal_walk_modes`; they used to be copied from the species BASE walk and never followed the final walk). It uses the G2 effective worn armor (`equipped_items` after `worn.apply`): armor in the pack does not slow.
- **Exemption.** `HEAVY_ARMOR_SPEED_EXEMPT_TRAITS` (`dwarf-phb-trait-2`, `dwarf-(kaladesh)-psk-trait-3`, text "Your speed is not reduced by wearing heavy armor.", pinned; a test asserts these are the ONLY catalog traits with the sentence) and `HEAVY_ARMOR_SPEED_EXEMPT_RACES` (`dwarf`, the SRD dwarf: the SRD record has no trait for it, so it is pinned by race id and cannot be quote-pinned against the catalog). Dwarf (XPHB) has no such trait (verified) and is slowed. For an exempt dwarf the `armor-strength-<item>` warning is no longer raised (nothing happens); for everyone else the text is now "... below that, speed is reduced by 10 ft." (the "(not applied to the sheet)" tail is gone).
- **Existing tests adjusted** (they put a heavy armor on a STR 10-12 character and asserted the old unreduced speed): `test_attacks_resolve` (expects 20), `test_feat_effects_numeric`, `test_save_speed_rage_effects` (3), `test_xphb_feature_effects_2024` (2) now give the character enough STR so their original intent (Mobile, Fast Movement, Aura of Alacrity, Roving in heavy armor) is still what they test.
- **New tests:** `server/tests/test_w1_dwarven_toughness_heavy_armor.py` (34): quote pins, catalog scan, HP cases (Fighter 5 CON 14 dwarf-xphb = SRD hill dwarf = 49, dwarf-phb hill +5, Kaladesh, Stensia +2/level, empty-trait degrade), speed cases (plate STR 13 walk 20; STR 15 walk 30; chain mail 13; SRD dwarf plate STR 10 unchanged; dwarf-xphb plate STR 10 walk 20; 2024; other modes keep their value; Monk Unarmored Movement unaffected; Fast Movement; Mobile; pack armor; worn id; floor at 0; warning text). Verified to fail (24 of 34) with the engine and catalog changes stashed.
- **Golden.** The 241 cases are unchanged (`test_c_series_golden.py` passes untouched): no kitchen-sink or matrix draft holds an official dwarf with the trait or a heavy armor below its requirement. No rebaseline, the gz was not touched.
- **Totals and timing.** Server suite 5132 -> 5166 passed (+34), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, no frontend change. `resolve()` mean of 30, ms: Fighter 20 in plate 3.11 (mixed) / 2.11 (2024), Wizard 20 3.41 (2024) / 2.60 (2014), Barbarian 20 (2014) 1.68, Monk 14 1.65.
- **Output keys.** None new (`derived.speed(s)`, `hitPointMax`, warnings only), so `types.ts` and the exports are unchanged; frontend untouched.

## Where to find what (orientation for a future session)

- **Start here, then read in this order:** the headline and "Known gaps after RP22" at the top, "Open design questions" (status of every question), "RP overview" (what each slice did and which commit), then the dated section of the slice you touch. The plan with the decisions is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-22-remaining-program.md`; the older plans are `2026-10-10-2024-chassis-remaining-stages.md` and `2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`. The device checks nobody could do here are in `docs/superpowers/specs/REAL-DEVICE-CHECKLIST.md`. The gap-audit plan (W1-W12, WF, WG; findings F1-F9, decisions D-1..D-7, what is deferred) is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-24-gap-audit-plan.md`; its slices have one dated section each at the end of this file (W1 ... W12, WF, WG) and the "W overview" table maps them to commits.
- **Data bundle (B1-B6).** The official 5etools data is vendored at `server/data/official/**` with `MANIFEST.json` (sha256, record counts, url / pulled_at); builder and CLI `server/catalog/official/bundle.py` (`python -m catalog.official.bundle --from <dir> [--check]`); precedence in `server/catalog/official/paths.py` (`DND_5ETOOLS_DATA` > `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none|<dir>` > explicit `DND_CAMPAIGN_ROOT` with data > bundle); where each file came from and which source codes it holds: `DATA-SOURCES.md` (guarded by `server/tests/test_data_sources_doc.py`); how to refresh: README "Refreshing the official data". SRD-only test run: `env DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none`. Loader precedence and `data_source()` ("srd-only"|"explicit"|"campaign"|"bundled"): `server/catalog/official/paths.py`, tests `server/tests/test_data_precedence.py`. Hosting: `scripts/start.sh` (waitress; builds the frontend when `dist` is missing), `scripts/setup.sh`, the untested `Dockerfile`, guards in `server/tests/test_deployment.py`, README "Hosting for friends". Clean-clone proof: `scripts/smoke_clean_clone.py [--frontend] [--full-suite]` (README "Test"). Load-robust 50 ms checks: `server/tests/perf_helpers.py` (`measure`, `assert_fast`: warm, best of N, best and median in the failure message).
- **Engine** (`server/engine/`): `resolve.py` (the pipeline, choice templates, gates, pruning), `class_choices_2024.py` (XPHB class cards, invocations, Metamagic, subclass proficiency picks), `class_resources.py` (resources, notes, subclass tails), `feat_effects.py` and `feat_spell_tables.py` (feat numbers, Adept / Mark tables), `subclass_options_2024.py`, `subclass_spells_2024.py`, `subclass_innate_2024.py` (RP13 / RP17 / RP18, pure modules wired by resolve), `worn.py` (G2), `attacks.py`, `derived.py`, `equipment.py`, `spells.py`, `sheet_features.py`.
- **Data tables** (`server/catalog/`): `reference_data.py` (hand-kept rules facts: `SUBCLASS_PICKS_2024`, `SUBCLASS_OPTIONS_2024`, `RESOURCE_TAIL_2024`, `RESOURCE_NOTES_2024`, the 2014 use-limit tables `RESOURCE_TAIL_2014` / `RESOURCE_NOTES_2014` (W6, SRD classes), `SUBCLASS_TAIL_2014` / `SUBCLASS_NOTES_2014` (W7, SRD subclasses), `SUBCLASS_TAIL_2014_BOOKS` / `SUBCLASS_NOTES_2014_BOOKS` (W8, book subclasses) and `BOOK_TABLE_PINS_2014` (quote pins of the level tables that live only in the text), `WIZARD_SPELL_PICKS_2014`, pack contents, repeatable feats, ...; every entry has a quote pin test), `feat_effects_data.py` / `feat_effects_tail.py` (one entry per feat), `official/` (5etools converters per kind: classes, subclasses, feats, backgrounds, equipment, spells), `loader.py` (SRD merge). The 5etools data lives outside the repo (`chargen/sync.py --official`); without it many tests skip.
- **Exports:** `server/export_fields.py` (shared field list and Bulgarian text blocks), `export.py` (YAML), `markdown_export.py`, `pdf_export.py`. A new sheet / derived key needs `export_fields.py` AND `frontend/src/types.ts` in the same commit.
- **Tests:** `server/tests/`, one module per slice (`test_<topic>_2024.py`, `test_<topic>_2014.py`); `kitchen_sink.py` + `test_export_fields.py` (fully chosen drafts through all three exporters), `test_types_drift.py` (types guard), `test_c_series_golden.py` + `golden/c_series_mixed_2014_baseline.json.gz` (mixed / 2014 never changes except through a logged rebaseline), `test_worn_wielded.py` (G2 golden). Run `cd server && ../.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q` (about 105 s, 126 s when another suite runs in parallel; with `DND_CAMPAIGN_ROOT=/nonexistent` about 10 s and 0 failures, the data-dependent tests skip) and `.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q` at the root; `cd frontend && npm run build` when `frontend/` changes.
- **Rules of thumb that have held:** 2024 work is gated on `ruleset == "2024"`; mixed / 2014 change only through a rebaseline entry; every new pick card must be count-variable-aware (`_is_count_variable_choice`), covered by `mark_unavailable` and, for spell cards, by `ClassPage.isSpellChoiceId`; a card that cannot be filled is `unavailable` and never blocks export; new catalog texts are pinned with short verbatim quotes, not copied sentences; stage plans say "tests first".
- **Frontend** (`frontend/src/`): `ClassPage.tsx` (Способности / Заклинания tabs, card routing by cid), `BackgroundPage.tsx`, `EquipmentPage.tsx`, `ExportPage.tsx` (export gate mirrors the server's), `DraftContext.tsx` (reducer, `localStorage["dnd-builder-draft-v2"]`), `types.ts` (hand-written, guarded by the drift test).

## W9 Export completeness audit (2026-10-24)

`server/tests/test_export_completeness.py` (109 tests): for every KITCHEN_SINK draft (44, all rulesets) plus five long-content drafts (Bard 20, Wizard 20 with a 44-spell book, Paladin 20 Watchers, Warlock 20 with 10 invocations, Fighter 19 with 7 feat resources) it walks every leaf of `sheet` and `derived` and asserts the leaf reaches the Markdown AND the PDF text (pypdf; whitespace-collapsed, case-folded). Engine untouched, golden untouched.

- **Method.** Strings must occur verbatim, or through a `FORMATTED` translator when the exporters print a label instead of the id (skills, languages, armor/weapon/tool proficiencies, recharge words, damage types). Numbers must occur as a token (`+3`, `x8`, `-1`), and inside a list item with a `name` within 40 characters before to 400 after the name; ten scalars (AC, HP, speed, passives, carrying, level, starting gold) are checked against their exact labelled line (`STRICT_SCALARS`). Quantity 1 is implied (`IMPLIED_NUMBER`). `False` and `None` print nothing by design and are skipped; a `True` flag must print a fixed phrase (`FLAG_TEXT`, 8 entries) or be listed. A bare number match is weak by nature; the labelled checks and test_export_fields.py carry the precision.
- **`EXPORT_OMITTED` (47 entries, path pattern -> "category: reason").** 42 ids (feat/spell/item/species/background/subclass ids, resource and feature source ids, grantedFeats), 2 flags (`attacks[].proficient`, `slots.hands[].twoHanded`), 2 implied (cantrip level 0, the shield in use follows `(екипиран)` / `Носено`), 1 slot picker field (`slots.hands[].kind`). The test fails on an unlisted missing leaf and on a stale entry (one matching no leaf in any audited draft; also checked for the `FORMATTED`, `FLAG_TEXT` and `STRICT_SCALARS` tables).
- **Gaps found and fixed.** (1) `sheet.species.size` was in no export: new "Размер" block in `extra_blocks`. (2) `derived.carryingCapacity.pushDragLift` was in no export: now printed after the carried/capacity line. (3) PDF headings used `cell`, so a long character name (18 pt) ran off the right edge: `h1`/`h2`/`h3` use `multi_cell` (same look when the text fits). Markdown and PDF still share `extra_blocks`.
- **PDF layout checks.** A spy on `FPDF.cell` asserts no cell is wider than the room to the right margin, for every sink and long draft, and with an 150-character name; the long drafts span 3-4 pages and every resource line, spell name, feature and feat name is in the extracted text (no page-break loss); no fpdf2 missing-glyph warning, and Cyrillic, `×`, `’`, `–` render in the long drafts.
- **Remaining gaps.** `attacks[].proficient` and the hand-slot flags are UI data only (listed, not printed). Number matching is token-level, not per-field, outside the ten labelled scalars. Hit-dice spent, ability-check proficiency markers and similar play-time state are not in `sheet`/`derived`, so out of scope. Legacy root exports untouched.
- **Totals.** Server suite 7077 -> 7186 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, golden unchanged (no rebaseline), frontend untouched.

## WF Review fixes for W1-W5b (2026-10-24)

Fixes for the read-only review of commits 020f069..HEAD. Tests: `server/tests/test_wf_review_fixes.py`.

- **Species "equal to your walking speed" modes.** `_species_speeds` still seeds a `true` mode with the base walk (key order), but `resolve()` now recomputes every such mode (`_species_equal_walk_modes`: Aarakocra / Fairy / Owlin fly, Sea Elf / Giff / Lizardfolk / Triton / Water Genasi swim, Dhampir / Hadozee / Tabaxi climb, 13 records) from the FINAL walk speed, after Unarmored Movement, Fast Movement, Aura of Alacrity, the feat walk bonuses and the W1 heavy-armor penalty. Sea Elf Fighter 5 in chain mail with STR 12: walk 20, swim 20. Numeric modes are untouched. Two older tests that pinned the old behavior (a Triton's swim staying 30 beside a raised walk: `test_speed_bonuses_raise_only_the_walking_speed`, `test_triton_barbarian_with_mobile_...`) were updated: the swim now equals the walk. Fly in medium / heavy armor is NOT zeroed (deferred, see Known gaps). **Superseded by X1: the banned fly speeds are now removed.**
- **Magic Initiate (XPHB) "different spell list each time".** Catalog text, entry "Repeatable": "You can take this feat more than once, but you must choose a different spell list each time." The PHB 2014 Magic Initiate has `repeatable` false and no such sentence (unchanged, not repeatable). The `:spell-variant` card of a repeatable feat with more than one list carries `distinctGroup` (the FE7 mechanism, `_gate_distinct_choices`): a later instance (walk order background, species, then ASI by class and level) gets the lists already taken disabled with "already chosen for Magic Initiate (n)"; a stored duplicate is dropped with a non-blocking `pruned` entry, together with that instance's `:spell-choice-N` cards. A background-fixed list (Genie Touched: Wizard) is held even before it is clicked (`distinctFixed`, internal like `distinctGroup`, stripped from the output).
- **Lucky.** `SPECIES_RESOURCES_2014` gained the note-only `halfling-phb-trait-2` and `kor-psz-trait-7` (word for word the SRD Lucky). A catalog scan found no other trait whose text equals a modelled one. Brave is not modelled anywhere (no use or rest rule).
- **Clamped ability plus bonus.** A base score clamped to 30 that a species bonus raises again (31 + 2) now makes the one `ability-out-of-range-<AB>` message add "With the species, background and feat bonuses the final <AB> score is 32, still outside 1-30." The final score is not clamped (decision: smallest consistent fix, the sheet shows the real arithmetic).
- **Breath Weapon note before the ancestry is picked.** The damage type is dropped from the sentence and the note ends "; choose your Draconic Ancestry to see the damage type" (SRD: "..., area and save"). Picked states are unchanged.
- **Docs.** Headline, W1 order line, W5a pointer, Known gaps, `mark_unavailable` docstring (the W3 rule applies under every ruleset).
- **Golden.** Unchanged and green (243 cases, `STRIP` unchanged, no rebaseline, no `--regenerate`).
- **Totals and timing.** Server suite 5438 -> 5498 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped (unchanged), frontend untouched (no build). `resolve()` mean of 30, ms, mixed / 2024: Fighter 20 3.2 / 2.1, Wizard 20 3.3 / 3.0, Bard 20 4.4 / 3.8, Dragonborn Fighter 20 3.2 / 2.2.

## WG Review fixes for W6-W8 (2026-10-24)

Fixes for the read-only review of commits 20fc18b..HEAD. Tests added to the W8 modules (no new module).

- **"Minimum of 1" on the total.** Alter Memories (`enchantment-phb-14-alter-memories`): the text is "1 + your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1)", so the hours are `max(1, 1 + CHA)` (`one_plus_cha_min1`), CHA 6 gives 1, not 2. The same wording puts the minimum on the whole roll in Defy Death (1d8 + CON), Balm of Peace (2d6 + WIS), Body of the Astral Self Deflect Energy (1d10 + WIS, minimum reduction 1) and Mote of Potential (roll + CHA, minimum 1 temporary hit point): their notes now print the dice with the plain signed modifier and "minimum ... in total". Turn the Tide ("1d6 + your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1)") is ambiguous; it is read the same way (minimum on the total) and the note says the wording is ambiguous. Left unchanged because the minimum is on the modifier or the count: uses = modifier, creatures = modifier, Touch of Death (WIS modifier + Monk level, minimum 1, total anyway), Healing Light dice count, Scornful Rebuke, Sacred Weapon, Glorious Defense, Otherworldly Glamour, Watcher's Will, Telepathic Speech.
- **Cosmetic.** The W8d Celestial (XGE) Healing Light prints "1 die" / "N dice" (`cha_dice`). Performance of Creation at level 14+ no longer prints the gp cap next to Creative Crescendo: the note's `gp_cap` level variable says "worth up to N gp (20 x Bard level)" below 14 and "of any gp value (no gp limit)" from 14 (the text: "You are no longer limited by gp value"); `_render_note` now formats string `level_vars` with the earlier variables.
- **Golden.** Unchanged and green (251 cases, `STRIP` unchanged, no rebaseline, no `--regenerate`).
- **Totals.** Server suite 7070 -> 7077 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend untouched.

## W10 Frontend functional audit (2026-11-03)

No visual work. An earlier executor left uncommitted partial work; it was reviewed line by line, kept where correct, trimmed where cosmetic. Measured in the browser pane at 375 px and desktop, with Warlock 7 (2024), Bard 10 (2014) and Wizard 5 (2024 Evoker) drafts, including the Flask server STOPPED.

- **1 /api/resolve failure: fixed (kept from partial work).** `useResolution` aborts the previous request (AbortController plus a request-id guard, 30 s timeout, 150 ms debounce) so an older response cannot overwrite a newer one. New `StatusBanner` (outside the routes) shows a Bulgarian error line with "Опитай пак"; the last good resolution stays on screen (the per-page error early returns that blanked pages were removed); export is disabled while the error stands. Verified: server stopped (502 line, retry recovers), mocked 500 with `{error}` body, slow-first race (AAA then BBB: only BBB applied, no stuck loading).
- **2 Draft import/download: ok plus fixed.** Invalid JSON, array, `null`, `{foo:1}` (no draft key, new check in `mergeDraft`), `draftVersion` 99 all give a Bulgarian message ending "Текущата чернова не е променена" and leave the stored draft untouched; an older `draftVersion` merges over defaults (the migration). A stored draft that fails to load at boot is copied to `dnd-builder-draft-v2.unreadable-backup` before the empty draft replaces it, with a dismissible notice.
- **3 ErrorBoundary: fixed.** It already wrapped the whole app; added a per-route boundary in `App.tsx` keyed by pathname (a crash keeps the nav and clears on navigation) and a "Опитай пак" button. A hostile draft (non-array choices, null values) did not crash any page; the boundary itself was verified by reading only.
- **4 Labels/semantics: fixed.** Added aria-labels to the unlabeled selects/search boxes (spell library, items, category, background, subspecies, ability scores, point-buy steppers, manual number input via `CommitNumberInput`). Disabled ChoicePicker options now carry `aria-disabled` and `aria-describedby` pointing at the disabledReason span (checked: 100-option ASI picker). Browser audit: 0 unlabeled controls and 0 `<button>` without `type` on all 7 routes.
- **5 Stale selections: ok.** Ruleset 2024 to 2014 on the Warlock draft reports "Отпаднали избори: 8" in the banner (server `pruned`, already listed on Home). SET_CLASS/SET_BACKGROUND/SET_SPECIES reducers already clear anchored choices. Deferred: SET_BACKGROUND also clears class equipment choices (existing design).
- **6 Refresh/back/forward/localStorage: ok plus fixed.** Draft survives reload and history back. A failing `setItem` (quota/private mode, simulated) shows "Черновата не може да се запази..." instead of failing silently.
- **7 Long lists: ok, no change.** Spells Known (116 options): open 22-60 ms, toggle about 60 ms; ASI picker (100 options): open 22 ms, toggle 21 ms. No memoization needed; the filter box exists.
- **8 Tap targets: fixed (min-height only).** Baseline at 375 px (partial CSS removed): text input 25 px, select 22 px, summary 19 px, number input 31x25, Home source checkbox labels 39 px. After: 0 controls under 44 px on all 7 routes, `scrollWidth` 375 everywhere. Reverted from the partial CSS: `display:flex` on checkbox/radio labels (it overrode the existing `inline-flex` rows) and the `.status-banner` margin; kept `min-height:44px` for inputs/select/textarea/summary/checkbox labels and `min-width:44px` for number inputs.
- **9 Flows/export: ok plus fixed.** Required pending card (Warlock cantrips removed) disables all three export buttons and lists the unresolved choice; optional/unavailable/equipment cards do not block (unchanged server mirror). Mocked export 500 (HTML body), 400 (`problems`) and a network failure each show a Bulgarian `role="alert"` line.
- **Console:** only the expected 502s from the stopped-server phase.
- **Deferred / product-level:** on a failed first load pages without a resolution still say "Няма избори за този ..." under the banner (wording); the Home pruned link points at Home itself; no frontend test runner exists (no vitest), so no unit tests were added.
- **Gate:** `npm run build` green; server and root suites not run (no server/root file touched); golden unchanged.

### Open questions for the user

Q1-Q5 of the gap audit, answered 2026-10-24: Q1 stack (keep), Q2 per-class mastery cards allowed (keep), Q3 Mercy no replacement card (keep, question 23), Q4 Fighting Initiate under 2024: pending / explained, the default (six SRD styles) stays (question 24), Q5 per-turn / per-combat riders stay notes (keep). Deferred and waiting for the user: Haunted One / Investigator either-or Dark Gift card; per-turn / per-combat riders as a resource kind; innate-spell use tracking; PHB Dragonborn save / area (needs a real ancestry pick); the Ravenloft prerequisite on Dark Gifts; fly speeds in medium / heavy armor for Aarakocra / Fairy / Owlin; taking `speciesResources` out of the golden `STRIP`; D-5 (legacy v1 root files), D-6 (xdist), D-7 (rate limiting / auth), pushing, the real-device check.

## W12 Test-suite health and final consolidation (2026-11-03)

- **Tests without the official data.** The W11 README note (229 failed, 5 errors with `DND_CAMPAIGN_ROOT=/nonexistent`) was a real gap: 63 test functions in 19 files assumed the 5etools data without a skip marker, plus the whole fuzz module (its generator draws from the full catalog). They now carry a `needs_official` skipif (`has_official() and has_official_classes() and has_official_spells()` from `catalog.official.paths`), `test_resolve_fuzz.py` a module-level `pytestmark`. No assertion was changed. Server: **with data 7213 passed** (7196 + the 17 new unit tests; no test became a skip), **without data 1460 passed, 5503 skipped, 0 failed, 0 errors**; root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped both ways.
- **Timing.** Server suite about 105 s on this machine, 126 s when another suite runs in parallel (`--durations=30`): the slowest test is 1.85 s (`test_export_fields::test_kitchen_sink_2024_stage_lines_reach_every_export`), then `test_types_drift` 1.78 s, `test_subclass_import::test_resolve_stays_as_fast_as_with_the_srd_only_catalog` 1.55 s, `test_feat_consolidation::test_every_feat_key_on_the_output_is_typed_in_types_ts` 1.32 s, `test_resolve_fuzz::test_exports_return_200_on_settled_drafts` 1.13 s; everything else is under 0.7 s. The 30 slowest add up to about 19 s of the 105 s: the rest is thousands of tests of 20-60 ms each, dominated by `resolve()` and catalog loading, so no `slow` marker is worth adding. pytest-xdist (D-6, NOT added) would split that long tail across cores: on a 4-8 core machine a plausible 105 s -> 30-40 s, bounded by the per-worker module-scoped `load()` of the catalog (about 0.3-0.7 s per worker) and by the 1.85 s single-test floor.
- **Modules without a direct test (checked by an import and name scan).** Not imported by any test: `catalog/official/languages.py`, `catalog/official/innate_spells.py`, `catalog/official/races.py`, `catalog/official/backgrounds.py`, `engine/worn.py`, `engine/subclass_innate_2024.py`. Of these `worn.py` is covered by `test_worn_wielded.py` (G2, through resolve, name differs), `subclass_innate_2024.py` by `test_subclass_innate_2024.py`, `races.py` / `backgrounds.py` by the converter and species tests through `load()`. `paths.py` and `items.py` ARE imported by tests. W12 added `server/tests/test_converter_units.py` (17 pure unit tests) for `languages.py`, `innate_spells.py` and `worn.py`; `races.py` and `backgrounds.py` stay tested only through the loaded catalog (their helpers need real 5etools records, the data-dependent tests already pin them).
- **Gate.** Server 7196 -> 7213 passed, root 405 passed / 8 skipped, golden 251 unchanged, `npm run build` green.

## WH Fixes from the final read-only review of the W series (2026-11-03)

- **Heavy-armor speed follows the counted suit.** `equipment.counted_body_armor_id(equipped_items)` is the first body armor, the suit AC and `slots.current.armor` use. `armor_speed_penalty` and `armor_warnings` take it as `counted_body`: a second, uncounted suit no longer lowers speed and gets no `armor-strength-*` warning (its proficiency warning and `armor-multiple-body` stay). With an explicit `equipment.worn.armor` pick `equipped_items` already holds only that suit, so behavior is unchanged there. Tests (`test_w1_dwarven_toughness_heavy_armor.py`): Fighter 5 mixed, chain mail then plate, STR 14, no pick: walk 30, no `armor-strength-plate-armor`, AC 16; worn pick plate: walk 20.
- **Docs.** W2 only verified class levels (no clamp); README skip reasons (two) and one timing figure (about 105 s, 126 s when another suite runs); `_srd_trait_effects` docstring.
- **Client error messages.** `frontend/src/httpErrors.ts` maps 400 / 413 / 500 / 502-504 / other statuses to Bulgarian; `useResolution` and `ExportPage` use it, the server `error` text follows only as "Технически детайл: ...". Export 400 with `problems` keeps the problem messages after a Bulgarian lead. Server JSON unchanged.
- **Search inputs** back to `type="text"` with `aria-label`, `inputMode="search"`, `enterKeyHint="search"`; redundant `box-sizing` in the W10 `summary` rule removed (the `input/select/textarea` rule had no extra line).
- **Boot backup.** `loadInitial` returns `unsavedRaw` when `setItem(BACKUP_KEY)` fails; `StatusBanner` then shows a different notice with a download link (data URL) and a copy button. Happy path unchanged. Not done: the stored draft is still overwritten by the empty one on boot (the offered download is the safeguard).
- **Gate.** Frontend build clean; server 7215 passed (7213 + 2); root 405 passed, 8 skipped; golden 274 passed, no regeneration.

## X1 Flying speed unusable in medium / heavy armor (2026-11-03)

Approved by the user on 2026-11-03 (it was deferred item (f) of the gap audit).

- **Scan (script over `catalog.traits`, all 1086; `catalog.races` / `subraces` with a `fly` movement mode).** Nine records say the flying speed cannot be used in armor; each is pinned in the new `reference_data.FLY_ARMOR_BAN` (trait id -> `race`, optional `subrace`, display `name`, banned `categories`, verbatim `quote`):
  `aarakocra-eepc-trait-3` ("To use this speed, you can't be wearing medium or heavy armor."), `aarakocra-mpmm-trait-0`, `fairy-mpmm-trait-2`, `fairy-wbtw-trait-1`, `owlin-scc-trait-2` ("You can't use this flying speed if you're wearing medium or heavy armor."), `aven-psa-trait-3`, `aven-psd-trait-3`, `siren-psx-trait-3` ("You can't use your flying speed while you wear medium or heavy armor."), and the **Winged Tiefling** (SCAG subspecies) `tiefling-phb-variant;-winged-scag-trait-1` ("You have a flying speed of 30 feet while you aren't wearing heavy armor.": **Heavy only**, medium armor keeps the speed). All nine are ruleset 2014; no 2024 species has a fly mode. The Aven and Siren records were not named in the request but carry the same sentence.
- **Not banned.** `aarakocra-dmg` (fly 50) has no Flight trait in the catalog (its three traits say nothing about armor), so by the rule "only where the text says so" it keeps its fly speed (a test pins that it is the only flier outside the table). Harengon, Kenku, Gith have no fly mode; Aasimar wings (Celestial Revelation) have no armor sentence.
- **Rule.** `equipment.fly_armor_bans(race, subrace, equipped_items, counted_body)` returns the table records the species holds (race trait refs plus subrace racial traits, `race` / `subrace` must match) whose categories contain the `armor_category` of the COUNTED body armor (WH: the suit AC uses; with a worn pick only that suit is in `equipped_items`; armor in the pack, a shield alone and `worn.armor: null` never count). `resolve()` drops the `fly` key from `derived.speeds` (omitted, not 0: the key means "has this mode"; walk stays a key even at 0) AFTER the W1 heavy-armor penalty and the WF equal-to-walk recomputation, so the order is: walk bonuses, W1 penalty, WF equal modes, X1 removal. Other modes (swim, climb) and Unarmored Movement are untouched. Unarmored (no suit) keeps the fly speed and follows the final walk.
- **Warning.** One non-blocking warning per record, id `fly-armor-<subrace or race id>`, text "<name> flying speed unusable in medium/heavy armor." (Winged Tiefling: "... unusable in heavy armor."), same style as `armor-strength-*`. Warnings are not a sheet key, so `types.ts` and the exports are unchanged; frontend untouched.
- **Tests.** `server/tests/test_x1_fly_armor.py` (51): quote pins per record, catalog scan equals the table, fliers outside the table are exactly `aarakocra-dmg`, light / medium / heavy per record (Winged Tiefling heavy only), shield only, shield plus armor, `worn.armor: null`, worn pick, two suits, unequipped armor, fixed vs equal-to-walk fly, Fast Movement and Monk interplay, W1 penalty plus removal, mixed / 2014 / 2024, empty table, a mismatching table entry, timing. Verified to fail (31 of 51) with the engine and catalog changes stashed. Two older tests that pinned the old behavior were adjusted: `test_equal_mode_follows_heavy_armor_penalty` (fly modes: walk lowered, fly removed) and `test_speeds_other_modes_keep_their_values` (now a Bullywug swim speed; the Aarakocra case asserts the removal).
- **Golden.** 274 test cases of `test_c_series_golden.py` pass untouched (no rebaseline, `STRIP` unchanged, no `--regenerate`): no golden draft holds a flying species.
- **Totals and timing.** Server suite 7215 -> 7266 passed (+51), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, no frontend change. `resolve()` mean of 30, ms, mixed / 2014: Fighter 20 Aarakocra in plate 3.3 / 2.2, Wizard 20 Fairy 3.6 / 2.7.
- **Uncertain / left.** Whether `aarakocra-dmg` should be banned (the DMG text is not in the catalog); encumbrance variants named in the Aven / Siren text ("if you are encumbered") are not modelled.

## X2 Per-turn / per-combat / per-rage recharges as a resource kind (2026-11-03)

Approved by the user on 2026-11-03 (it was deferred item (b) of the gap audit, question Q5).

- **Vocabulary.** `reference_data.RESOURCE_RECHARGES` = `long`, `short`, `short-or-long` plus **`turn`** ("Once per turn" / "once on each of your turns"), **`combat`** ("only once per combat"), **`rage`** ("only once per rage") and **`other`** (any other stated condition, written out in `details.note`). A limited feature is a resource with `uses` 1 and that recharge. One list, four consumers, kept in step by `test_x2_turn_combat_rage_recharges.py`: `export_fields._RECHARGE_WORDS` (Bulgarian: "на ход", "на битка", "на ярост", "виж бележката"; so `resource_line` prints `Sneak Attack: 1, 3d6 (на ход)`; `test_export_completeness` formats `recharge` through the same dict), `describeResource`'s `RECHARGE` map in `ClassResourcesList.tsx`, the `ResourceCore.recharge` union in `types.ts`, and the shape docstring of `engine/class_resources.py`. No new output key (only new values of `recharge`), so the export audit and `test_types_drift` stay as they were. Frontend build clean.
- **Species (5, `SPECIES_RESOURCES_2014`, quote-pinned per entry).** Hobgoblin (DMG) Martial Advantage ("Once per turn, you can deal an extra 2d6 damage ...") `turn`; Bugbear (ERLW) and Bugbear (VGM) Surprise Attack ("You can use this trait only once per combat.") `combat`; Tabaxi (MPMM) and Tabaxi (VGM) Feline Agility ("... you can't use it again until you move 0 feet on one of your turns.") `other`, the condition is in `details.note`. They leave `SKIPPED_BOOK_TRAITS` of the W5b scan test (the stale-entry check forced that). Species resources appear only for characters of those species.
- **Existing note-only rows that gain `uses` 1 + recharge (24, `reference_data.X2_RECHARGE_UPGRADES`).** `turn`: Ranger Foe Slayer (2014), 2024 Stunning Strike, the Divine Strike of the Life domain and of the 11 book domains (Death, Forge, Nature, Order, Solidarity, Strength, Tempest, Trickery, Twilight, War, Zeal), Order's Wrath, Cavalier Ferocious Charger, Kensei Deft Strike, TCE Mercy Hand of Harm, TCE Fey Wanderer Dreadful Strikes, Swarmkeeper Gathered Swarm, Divine Soul Empowered Healing, Genie's Wrath, Necromancy Grim Harvest; `rage`: Zealot Fanatical Focus. `_x2_upgrade` (applied at import) rewrites the row (asserting it was note-only) and appends the pinned sentence to its quotes when no quote has it. The older note texts are unchanged.
- **New rows (8).** 2014: Samurai Rapid Strike, Kensei Unerring Accuracy, Gloom Stalker Stalker's Flurry, Wild Magic Spell Bombardment (`turn`). 2024 (`SUBCLASS_TAIL_2024`): Fey Wanderer Dreadful Strikes, Celestial Radiant Soul, Hunter Superior Hunter's Prey (`turn`), World Tree Travel Along the Tree ("once per Rage, you can increase the range of that teleport to 150 feet", `rage`).
- **Engine builders with dice (3).** Sneak Attack (`_rogue`, every ruleset, "Once per turn"), 2024 Divine Strike and Primal Strike (`_class_strikes`, "Once on each of your turns"): `uses` 1, `recharge` "turn", dice unchanged.
- **Left as notes, by reason (the `LEFT` dict of the new test scans every catalog feature whose text has the phrase; a new one fails the test).** Already a resource with its own count (Wild Resurgence, Emboldening Bond, Arcane Shot, Psionic Power / Strike, Giant's Might, Flurry of Healing and Harm, Living Legend, Dread Ambusher, Form of Dread, Hurl Through Hell, Portent); a note on a resource it spends (Relentless on Superiority Dice, 2024 Hand of Harm on Focus Points, Psychic Blades on Bardic Inspiration, Eldritch Smite / Lifedrinker on the pact weapon); pick-dependent (Hunter's Prey options); a sub-clause of a multi-part feature (Form of the Beast, Bestial Soul, Body of the Astral Self, Elemental Epitome, Lunar Form, Grave Touched, Spirit Projection, Spreading Spores); table-triggered (Wild Magic Surge 2014 / 2024); Power Surge (its pool resets to one, W6-W8 deferral (g)); FRHoF features (Moon's Inspiration, Frigid Explorer, Spellfire Burst, Crown of Spellfire: ruleset 2024 but not XPHB, not read). Species left: Lucky Footwork (reaction d4, no limit text), Gift of the Aetherborn, Astral Knowledge, Cunning Artisan, Blood Thirst, Black Blood Healing (no use count or recharge text); the 56 innate-spell and 9 rest-duration traits as before. Zealot / Berserker "first creature you hit on each of your turns" (Divine Fury, Frenzy) is not the phrase "once", left. **Feats are not touched:** about 30 feats carry a "once per turn" rider as notes (Crusher, Piercer, Slasher, Savage Attacker, Charger ...); the request named the W6-W8 and RP10 / RP11 skip lists, and the feat notes live in `feat_effects_*`, a separate change.
- **Golden (rebaseline `X2`, never `--regenerate`).** The existing SRD resources that now carry `uses` 1 / `recharge` "turn" are in 33 of the 251 cases (mixed and 2014, src-all and src-srd): Rogue 1 / 3 / 11 / 20 and the multiclass Rogue 3 / Bard 2 (Sneak Attack), the 2014 feats Rogue 16 kitchen sink, Cleric 11 / 20 (Life Domain Divine Strike), Ranger 20 (Foe Slayer). Allowlist written from the rule matrix: `^result[.]derived[.]classResources[(rogue:sneak-attack|cleric:divine-strike-life-domain|ranger:foe-slayer)][.](uses|recharge)$`. The dry run reported 66 changed paths, all matching (two per case); the other 218 cases re-resolved to their stored result. The species, book-subclass and 2024 rows are in no golden case. `test_x2_cases_hold_the_turn_recharge` pins it. `STRIP` unchanged. Golden 274 tests pass.
- **Existing tests updated consciously.** Sneak Attack / Divine Strike (2024) / Foe Slayer / Stunning Strike / the W7 Life Divine Strike / W8a-d rows: expected `uses` and `recharge`; the W8b-d and 2024 subclass matrices got the new rows; `test_worn_wielded` neutralises Sneak Attack's new fields against its pre-G2 baseline; the W5b scan test lost the 5 skip entries (Tabaxi and Bugbear moved out of "species without a modelled trait").
- **Tests.** `server/tests/test_x2_turn_combat_rage_recharges.py` (127): vocabulary (constant, export words, tsx map, types.ts union, every table value), species pins and resolve, 24 + 8 rows quote-pinned against `catalog.features`, resolved resource per row (mixed / 2014 or 2024, below the level absent, other subclass absent), engine builders, the feature scan with the `LEFT` reasons, timing. 106 of the 127 fail with the engine and catalog changes stashed.
- **Totals and timing.** Server suite 7266 -> 7415 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, `npm run build` green. `resolve()` mean of 30, ms, mixed / 2014 / 2024: Fighter 20 Hobgoblin (Samurai) 3.3 / 2.1 / 2.1, Wizard 20 Tabaxi 3.5 / 2.6 / 2.9, Rogue 20 Bugbear 2.8 / 1.9 / 1.9.
- **Uncertain / left.** The meaning of `uses` 1 for a per-turn limit is "one per turn": nothing tracks the spending. Divine Fury / Frenzy wording; feat per-turn riders; a `LEFT` feature promoted to a resource needs a golden check (SRD ids) first.

Vite proxies only `/api`. The v2 PDF export draws its own sheet (fonts in `server/assets/fonts`), so the root `assets/character-sheet-template.pdf` (used only by `pdf_fill.py`) went too; root `static/favicon.ico` and `templates/builder.html` were used only by v1 `app.py`. The v1 `app.py` had once shadowed `server/app.py` (see Phase 2 notes): that hazard is gone.

- **Kept.** `chargen/` (the SRD JSON and `sync`/`sources` that v2 reads), `tests/test_chargen_*.py` (build, catalog, cli, normalise, rules, sync, isolation), `tests/conftest.py` (campaign-root isolation; docstring updated), root `pytest.ini` and `requirements.txt` (still `-r server/requirements.txt`; no v1-only dependency existed: flask, flask-cors, PyYAML, pypdf, fpdf2 are all used by `server/`).
- **Edits.** `tests/test_chargen_isolation.py`: `app` is now listed in `SERVER_LOCAL_MODULES` (it had been allowed only because the root `app.py` made it a root stem), comments updated. README: v1 paragraph replaced by a history note. Comments in `paths.py`, `pdf_export.py`, `markdown_export.py`, `requirements.txt`, `tests/conftest.py`; "Superseded" pointers in the deferred list and the root-suite sentence above, the gap-audit plan, and the two 2026-09-13 design/plan documents.

## D6 pytest-xdist (2026-11-03)

Approved by the user 2026-11-03. No behavior change; golden unchanged.

- **Install and pins.** `pytest-xdist==3.8.0` and `execnet==2.1.2` (pip's pinned dependency) added to `server/requirements.txt`; `pip install --dry-run -r requirements.txt` resolves. Serial `pytest -q` is still the default: `-n` is NOT in any `addopts`; README documents `-n auto` as opt-in.
- **Audit and result: no test needed a fix.** Every server test that writes files uses `tmp_path`/`tmp_path_factory` (the 5etools fixtures in `test_class_2024_*`, `test_class_resources`, `test_subclass_import`, `test_spell_import`); the golden file is written only by its CLI `rebaseline`/`--append` functions, and the tests of those functions monkeypatch `GOLDEN` to a `tmp_path`; no test binds a port (the Flask test client is used); env changes go through `monkeypatch`; the fuzz module draws from `random.Random(seed)` per parametrized seed, so the seeds are the same on every worker; the `lru_cache`/module-level catalog caches are per process and read-only. The repo-root `tests/conftest.py` already gives each test its own `DND_CAMPAIGN_ROOT`.
- **Counts (identical to serial).** With data: serial 7415 passed; `-n 4` 7415 passed; `-n auto` 7415 passed (two runs). Without data (`DND_CAMPAIGN_ROOT=/nonexistent`): serial 1460 passed / 5705 skipped; `-n auto` 1460 passed / 5705 skipped. (The W12 figure 5503 skipped was before the 202 tests added since.) Root suite 356 passed / 8 skipped serial and with `-n auto`. `git status` after the parallel runs: no tracked file changed.
- **Timing (8 CPUs, darwin).** Server serial 107-112 s; `-n 4` 40 s; `-n auto` (8 workers) 45 s (the per-worker catalog load and the 1.85 s test floor bound it; more workers than 4 do not help on this machine). Without the data: serial 10.3 s, `-n auto` 12.4 s (worker start-up dominates, not worth it). Root suite about 2 s either way.

## Y1 long option lists (2026-11-03)

Frontend only (`ChoicePicker.tsx`, a few structural rules appended to `index.css`); server, golden and root untouched. Real-phone feedback: the Class page ASI card listed 130+ feats (about 250 under mixed with all sources).

- **Switch.** `ChoicePicker` renders the new `LongPicker` when a card has more than `LONG_LIST_THRESHOLD` (30) options; otherwise the old code runs as `ShortPicker`, unchanged (filter box above `FILTER_THRESHOLD` 20, consecutive-group headings, "Показване на недостъпните").
- **Groups (1, 6).** The server's `group` buckets (order of first appearance; options with no group go to "Други") become a `<button aria-expanded aria-controls>` header: "<име> — N налични[, M недостъпни][, избрани K]". Only a group holding a selected option, or the first group with options, starts open; a collapsed group does not mount its rows (the `<ul>` is rendered only when open). With a single bucket the list stays flat. Open/closed overrides are local state; typing in search or changing the source resets them.
- **"Избрано" block (2).** Always on top: "Избрано (K/count)" plus one row per `choice.selected` id with a "Премахни" button (`aria-label` "Премахни: <label>", calls the same `onToggle`). Label falls back to the id when the option is not in the list.
- **Unavailable (3).** Disabled / `hiddenByFilters` and not selected: kept behind the existing "Показване на недостъпните (M)" toggle (M = matching ones over all groups) and counted in each group header. With the toggle on they appear inside their group.
- **Search (4).** Same input as before; in long mode matching groups open automatically, groups without matches disappear, and a `role="status"` line shows "Намерени: N (още M недостъпни)" or "Няма съвпадения.". Selected rows are not force-kept in the groups during a search (the Избрано block shows them).
- **Source filter (5).** The server gives options NO `source` field; the only source signal is the book code the server appends to a label when names collide (`Athlete (XPHB)`, regex ` \(([A-Z][A-Z0-9]{1,7})\)$`, `optionSource`). A native `<select class="picker-source">` ("Всички източници", `CODE (n)`, "Без етикет (n)") appears when 2+ distinct codes exist; so it shows on feat cards (PHB / TCE / XPHB under mixed) and not on spell cards ("(Magical Secrets)" is not a book code). It is a heuristic; a real `source` on options would be the clean fix (server change, not done).
- **Measured at 375px** (before -> after; rows = `.choice-picker li` on the page; ASI card expanded):

  | case | rows | page height | DOM nodes |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Fighter 4, 2024, ASI card | 119 -> 21 | 7604 -> 2980 px | 507 -> 218 |
  | Fighter 8, mixed, all sources, first ASI card | 186 -> 21 | 11283 -> 3510 px | 742 -> 259 |
  | Bard 10, 2014, Spells tab (Spells Known incl. Magical Secrets, both cards open) | 12+381 -> 12+32 | 21409 -> 4782 px | 1840 -> 801 |

  scrollWidth 375 in all; no console errors; selecting inside a collapsed group (open, pick, collapse: the pick stays in Избрано and the header shows "избрани 1"), search ("wea" -> 8 results over two groups), the source select (PHB -> 33 + 5 unavailable) and Премахни all work; every new control is at least 44px high. A short card (Bard Cantrips Known, 12 rows) has no Избрано block and the same markup. At 1024px width: no overflow, headers 740px wide.
- **Gate.** `npm run build` green; no frontend test runner exists (build + browser verification only).
- **Open.** A single huge ungrouped list is still flat (none exists today). Native `details` was not used because it keeps children mounted.

## B1 bundle: vendored data (2026-11-03)

- **What.** The raw 5etools files the converters read are now vendored at `server/data/official/**`: 40 files (5,868,057 bytes, 5.6 MiB) plus `MANIFEST.json`. It is a byte-exact copy of `~/.claude/dnd/.data/official` (not re-synced; `meta.json` is not copied, its url/pulled_at are folded into the manifest). No behaviour change: the loader (`catalog/official/paths.py`) is unchanged and does not read the bundle yet (B2).
- **Builder.** `server/catalog/official/bundle.py` (stdlib only): `required_files(src)` (5 top-level files + `spells/` and `class/` index, `sources.json`, and every plain `*.json` named by those indexes; the regex is copied from `chargen/sync.py`), `build(src, dest)` (shutil.copyfile, mode 0644, built in a temp sibling then swapped in, stale files dropped, missing/invalid JSON raises with dest untouched), `check(src, dest)`. CLI from `server/`: `python -m catalog.official.bundle --from <dir> [--check] [--dest <dir>]`; `--check` prints added/removed/changed and exits 1 on drift, writes nothing.
- **Manifest.** `{format: 1, files: {rel: {bytes, sha256, records: {top-level list key: len}, url, pulled_at}}, bundle_sha256}`, sorted keys, deterministic (no timestamp); `bundle_sha256` is the sha256 of the sorted `"rel sha\n"` lines.
- **Repo hygiene.** `.gitattributes` marks `server/data/official/**` as `linguist-vendored`; `Resurses/` is in `.gitignore`.
- **Tests.** `server/tests/test_data_bundle.py` (10): builder on fixture trees (exact set, hostile index ignored, bytes and mode, deterministic manifest, stale removal, missing file, invalid JSON, `--check` exit codes) and the real bundle (integrity against the manifest; record counts: race 160, subrace 98, background 161, feat 305, optionalfeature 213, baseitem 230, 17 spell books with 936 spells, 15 class files).
- **Gate.** Server 7425 passed; `DND_CAMPAIGN_ROOT=/nonexistent` 1470 passed / 5705 skipped; root 356 passed + 8 skipped; golden untouched (275 passed).

## B2 loader precedence (2026-11-03)

- **What.** `server/catalog/official/paths.py` now defaults to the vendored bundle. `BUNDLED_DIR = <repo>/server/data/official`; `official_dir()` is evaluated at call time, in this order: `DND_5ETOOLS_DATA` (per-file dev override in `spells_dir`/`class_dir`/`optionalfeatures_file`/`items_base_file`, unchanged, always first) > `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` (SRD-only: returns the guaranteed-absent `BUNDLED_DIR/__disabled__`) > `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=<path>` > `DND_CAMPAIGN_ROOT` explicitly set and non-blank AND its `.data/official` exists and is non-empty (only this branch calls `chargen.sources.official_dir()`) > `BUNDLED_DIR`. The implicit `~/.claude/dnd/.data/official` is never read by the server any more. `has_official()` is now a local non-empty check on the new `official_dir()`; `data_source()` returns `'srd-only' | 'explicit' | 'campaign' | 'bundled'`. `DND_CAMPAIGN_ROOT=/nonexistent` therefore now means "use the bundle". `server/app.py` logs one stderr line when started as a script (`catalog data: bundled (<sha[:12]>)`). `chargen/` is untouched.
- **Tests.** `server/tests/test_data_precedence.py` (9 cases: default bundle with the pinned full counts under a fake empty HOME; `none` gives SRD counts and all `has_official*` False; explicit path; campaign root with data; empty/missing/blank campaign root falls to the bundle; `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA` beats the campaign root; `DND_5ETOOLS_DATA` still wins per file; decoy under HOME/.claude ignored; catalog equality bundle vs the real synced copy). Two older tests that read machine paths (`Path.home()/.claude/...` in `test_feat_defense.py`, `~/Documents/5etools-src` in `test_background_gaming_set_pick.py`) now read `official_dir()`, so they pass on a clean machine.
- **Suite semantics.** Skip markers evaluate at collection, so they now RUN against the bundle by default (0 data skips). To get the SRD-only subset put `env DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` in front of pytest. README updated; the root suite's `tests/conftest.py` only isolates chargen's own campaign root (chargen.sources), which the server ignores unless it holds data, so it needed no change.
- **Gate.** Server `-n auto`: 7434 passed, 0 skipped (default); `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none`: 1479 passed / 5705 skipped, 0 failed; `HOME=<empty dir>`: 7433 passed + 1 skip (the bundle-vs-synced comparison, which needs a real synced copy). Root 356 passed + 8 skipped. Golden untouched (275 passed). Wall-clock perf tests (`*_resolves_under_50_ms`, `*_stays_fast*`) can flake under heavy machine load (seen at load average above 100); they pass on rerun.

## B4 deployment (2026-11-03)

- **What.** `waitress==3.0.2` pinned in `server/requirements.txt` (the root requirements include it). `scripts/start.sh` (bash, `set -euo pipefail`, root resolved from the script path): fails with a clear message when `.venv` (waitress) is missing; builds the frontend (`npm ci && npm run build`) when `frontend/dist/index.html` is missing or `--rebuild` is given; then `cd server && exec ../.venv/bin/waitress-serve --listen=${HOST:-127.0.0.1}:${PORT:-8000} --threads=${THREADS:-4} app:app`. It never sets `FLASK_DEBUG`. `scripts/setup.sh` does venv + pip + npm ci + build. `Dockerfile` (node slim builds the frontend; python slim installs requirements, copies `server/`, `chargen/` and the built dist to `frontend/dist`, which is where `app.py` looks) and `.dockerignore`: both UNTESTED (no Docker here). README "Hosting for friends": Tailscale `tailscale serve` (recommended) or Caddy on a VPS, no authentication, 1 MB body limit, drafts in localStorage per origin, Docker untested.
- **Import guard.** `ALLOWED_THIRD_PARTY` unchanged: nothing in Python imports waitress (only the start script runs its CLI).
- **Tests.** `server/tests/test_deployment.py`: app is a WSGI callable serving index.html (FRONTEND_DIST tmp dist; SPA serving itself is covered by `test_spa_serving.py`); thread-safety guard: deep hash (`json.dumps(default=str, sort_keys=True)`) of every attribute of the shared `app._catalog` except the lazy `_row_index`, 20 fuzz drafts resolved in 4 threads through the test client, all 200, hash unchanged (no shared-catalog mutation found); `bash -n` and executable bit for both scripts; start.sh has no FLASK_DEBUG; opt-in `RUN_WAITRESS_TEST=1` runs the real start script on a free port and fetches `/` and POSTs `/api/resolve`.

## B5 clean-clone smoke (2026-11-03)

`scripts/smoke_clean_clone.py` (stdlib only) clones HEAD with `git clone --no-local` into a temp dir and runs the app from
there with a minimal environment (PATH, empty HOME, PYTHONNOUSERSITE=1, no `DND_*`), using the main checkout's `.venv`
interpreter (cwd = clone/server) and with non-loopback sockets blocked. It asserts: app and `official_dir()` are inside the
clone, `data_source()=='bundled'`, the pinned catalog counts, a fixed non-SRD draft (Wizard 5, Scribes TCE, Hobgoblin VGM,
XGE spells, PHB Alert) resolves with no problems, and yaml/markdown/pdf exports return 200 (PDF > 10 KB, fonts load).
`--frontend` builds the SPA in the clone (symlinked node_modules) and checks `GET /`; `--full-suite` runs `pytest -q -n 4`
in the clone (0 failures, no official-data skips). It refuses tracked changes. Tests: `server/tests/test_clean_clone_smoke.py`
(env builder, network blocker, tracked-change guard, a clone missing races.json fails; full run only with `RUN_CLONE_SMOKE=1`).
Results at B5: default 1.2 s, `--frontend` 2.5 s, `--full-suite` 46 s (7444 passed, 2 skipped, none data-related). No defect found
in the committed tree.
