## B6 consolidation (2026-11-03)

- **Timing tests.** 56 test functions in 55 files (61 `assert_fast` call sites, before: single or mean `perf_counter` measurements of resolve() against 50 ms) were rewritten so a loaded machine cannot fail them (it did at load average above 100). `server/tests/perf_helpers.py`: `measure(fn, runs=10, warmup=1)` times each call separately after a warm-up, `assert_fast(laps, what)` requires the BEST run under 50 ms and on failure reports best and median. The bound was not raised. `c7c_helpers.time_resolve` became `resolve_laps`; the `_mean_ms` helpers became `_laps`. `test_resolve_fuzz.test_slowest_resolve_under_50_ms` re-times a slow draft 7 times instead of 3. `test_subclass_import` (relative comparison against the SRD-only catalog, already best of 4) is unchanged. Sensitivity check (throwaway, not committed): with `resolve` wrapped in a 60 ms sleep, the checks fail with e.g. "best of 20 runs 73.0 ms, median 80.3 ms".
- **`.claude/launch.json`.** The backend entry uses the relative `.venv/bin/flask` (verified: the preview starts the server on 8000); ports unchanged.
- **Found by the gates and fixed.** `test_deployment.py::test_concurrent_resolves_do_not_mutate_shared_catalog` uses the fuzz generator, which needs the official data, and failed under `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none`; it now skips there like the fuzz module. The root import guard knows the new `perf_helpers` module.
- **Final gates.** See the headline at the top.

## SK1 frontend harness + logic modules (2026-11-04)

- **What.** First slice of the SK (skeleton) series: a frontend unit-test harness and the pure logic of the pages moved into `frontend/src/logic/`, with NO behaviour change and NO visual change. `vitest` 5.1.0 is a pinned (exact) devDependency; `npm test` = `vitest run`; the `test` block lives in `frontend/vite.config.ts` (`defineConfig` from `vitest/config`, `environment: "node"`, `include: ["src/**/*.test.ts"]`), so `npm run build` is unchanged (`tsc -b && vite build`, test files are type-checked, not bundled).
- **Modules (code moved, not rewritten).** `exportReadiness.ts` (`exportReadiness(resolution, error)` -> `{missingParts, pendingChoices, pendingEquipment, problems, canExport}`, from ExportPage; mirrors `server/app.py` `_pending_export_problems`), `steps.ts` (`STEPS`, `stepStatus(resolution, draft?)` -> per-path `{pending, problems, missing, done}`, `stepBadgeCount`; from StepNav; `missing` is always 0 and an empty step still has no badge: that change is SK3), `choiceRouting.ts` (`isSpellChoiceId`, the class/species/background cid filters, `classChoicesFor`, `splitClassChoices`, `speciesChoicesOf`, `backgroundChoicesOf`; from ClassPage, StepNav, SpeciesPage, BackgroundPage), `sources.ts` (`optionSource` from ChoicePicker, `sourceOn`, `toggleSource(draftSources, allSourceIds, id, on)` from HomePage), `files.ts` (`safeFilename` replaces the two copies of the unsafe-character regex, `downloadBlob`, `shareOrDownload` replaces the two copies of the share-sheet-then-download code in HomePage and ExportPage), `abilities.ts` (`ABILITIES`, `STANDARD_ARRAY`, `POINT_BUY_COST`, `POINT_BUY_BUDGET`, `pointBuySpent`, `canIncrement`, `setStandardScore`, `scoreFromDice`, `rollAbilityScore`, `formatModifier`; from AbilitiesPage), `draft.ts` (`DRAFT_VERSION`, `emptyDraft`, `Action`, `reducer`, `totalLevel`, `MAX_TOTAL_LEVEL`, `mergeDraft`, `sanitizeDraft`; from DraftContext, which keeps only the provider, storage and boot-load code and re-exports `DRAFT_VERSION`, `MAX_TOTAL_LEVEL`, `emptyDraft`, `mergeDraft`, `totalLevel`, so existing imports are unchanged), `testFixtures.ts` (test builders).
- **Tests.** 108 tests in 7 files next to the modules (`*.test.ts`): export gate parity (equipment, optional and unavailable cards never block; missing class/species/background, resolve problems and a failed re-resolve block), `stepStatus` (current badge behaviour, feat cards counted once by cid, unmapped problems not badged), `isSpellChoiceId` over every cid pattern (spells-cantrips, spells-known, spellbook, mystic-arcanum, spell-mastery, signature-spells, additional-magical-secrets, Pact of the Tome cards, savant, improved-illusions, magical-discoveries) plus 14 feature cards that must stay on "Способности", `toggleSource`, `sourceOn`, `optionSource`, `safeFilename`, `pointBuySpent`/`canIncrement`/`setStandardScore`/`scoreFromDice`, `mergeDraft` (newer version and non-drafts throw, null class entries dropped, level clamp, malformed equipment dropped), and the reducer. Mutation check (throwaway, not committed): 21 single-line mutations of the logic were applied one at a time; 20 made a test fail, the 21st (`score < 15 &&` removed in `canIncrement`) is an equivalent mutant (`POINT_BUY_COST[16]` is undefined).
- **Server test touched.** `server/tests/test_subclass_spell_picks_2024.py::test_frontend_routes_the_new_spell_cards_to_the_spells_tab` read the `isSpellChoiceId` source from `ClassPage.tsx`; it now reads `frontend/src/logic/choiceRouting.ts` (same assertions). It, `test_types_drift.py` and `test_x2_turn_combat_rage_recharges.py` pass (191).
- **No-change verification.** Dev servers (Vite + Flask) at 375 px: the 7 routes (`/home /class /background /species /abilities /equipment /export`) were captured (`#root` innerHTML, innerText, `scrollWidth`) for four localStorage drafts (Warlock 7 2024 and Bard 10 2014 from the kitchen-sink builder, an empty draft, a Fighter 4 / Wizard 2 draft with no background and a narrowed `sources`) from a clean `git archive` of HEAD (Vite on a second port) and from the working tree: all 28 captures are byte-identical (HTML, text and `scrollWidth`), `scrollWidth === 375` on all, no `error` events or `console.error` calls in any iframe. A live check on the working tree: the source checkbox toggle (one off -> 41 sources, back on -> `[]`) and the point-buy "+" (15 -> 14 points remaining) behave as before; no console errors. Servers stopped, viewport reset.
- **Gate.** `cd frontend && npm run build` green, `npm test` 108 passed. Server/root suites were not re-run in full (only the three server test files above, 191 passed); baselines unchanged: server 7449 passed + 2 skipped, root 356 passed / 8 skipped, golden untouched.

## SK2 CI workflow (2026-11-04)

- **What.** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` for the private repo. Triggers: `push` to `main` and every `pull_request`; `permissions: contents: read`; no secrets. Four independent jobs on `ubuntu-latest`: `server-bundle` (`pip install -r server/requirements.txt`, then `cd server && pytest -q -n auto`), `server-srd-only` (same install, `cd server && env DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none pytest -q -n auto`), `root` (same install, `pytest -q` from the repo root) and `frontend` (`cd frontend && npm ci && npm run build && npm test`). Python 3.14 (the version of `.venv`, 3.14.7; the Dockerfile uses `python:3.14-slim`), Node 22 (vite 8 needs `^20.19 || >=22.12`). pip cache keyed on `server/requirements.txt`, npm cache on `frontend/package-lock.json`.
- **Pinned actions.** `actions/checkout` v4.2.2 (`11bd7190...`), `actions/setup-python` v5.6.0 (`a26af69b...`), `actions/setup-node` v4.4.0 (`49933ea5...`), each by full commit SHA with the tag in a comment; the SHAs were resolved with `gh api repos/<action>/git/ref/tags/<tag>` (lightweight tags, so the ref is the commit).
- **Test.** `tests/test_ci_workflow.py` (root suite, 7 tests; PyYAML is already in the isolation allowlist): exact job set, triggers, each job's last `run` equals the expected command, the python jobs install the requirements first, the commands match the README "Test" section (after normalising the venv interpreter prefix; CI adds `npm ci` and `-n auto`, which the README documents separately), no secrets or `${{ }}` expressions, every `uses` is `actions/*@<40-hex>`, setup steps have a cache, `permissions` is read-only.
- **Not verified.** GitHub Actions cannot run here: the first push shows whether the workflow is green (user checks on GitHub). Only the YAML parse and the job commands were run locally (see the gate). Risk to watch on the first run: the wall-clock "resolve() under 50 ms" tests (best of N, warm) on a 2-vCPU shared runner while `-n auto` runs 2 workers.
- **Gate.** Job commands run locally with the venv on PATH: server 7449 passed + 2 skipped (`-n auto`), SRD-only 1493 passed / 5708 skipped, root 363 passed / 8 skipped (356 + 7 new), `npm ci && npm run build && npm test` green (108 tests). Golden untouched.

## SK3 step cues (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Functional only, no visual design (existing class names, plain markup). (1) `stepStatus(resolution, draft?)` now fills `missing`: 1 for an empty Class, Species or Background step (read from the draft when given, else from `resolution.sheet`, the export gate's test); `done` includes it; with no resolution nothing is counted (no badge while the first resolve loads). StepNav passes the draft and the badge carries `role="img"` + `aria-label="неизбрани: N"`. (2) `nextUnfinishedStep(resolution, draft)` in `logic/steps.ts` (first step in `STEPS` order with a badge count > 0, else `/export`, `null` without a resolution) rendered by the new `components/NextStepLink.tsx` ("Следваща незавършена стъпка →", `<p className="next-step">`) under StepNav in `App.tsx`, on every page. Equipment cards count (a pending equipment card sends the link to `/equipment` although it never blocks export). (3) `stepForChoice(cid)` (equipment-choice cids first, then `class:`, `species:`, else background); Export's "Unresolved choices" items are links to `<step>#<cid>`; `ChoiceCard` root has `id={cid}` and opens + scrolls (`document.getElementById(..).scrollIntoView`, never a CSS selector, since cids contain colons) when `cidFromHash(location.hash)` equals its id, on mount and on hash change (`logic/hashTarget.ts`); `ClassBlock` switches to the Способности/Заклинания tab that holds the hashed card. (4) `<Route path="*">` redirects unknown paths to `/home`. (5) ClassBlock h2 shows the sheet's class name, else the catalog name, else the id.
- **Tests.** vitest 108 -> 126 (written first: 14 failed before the change): missing counts (empty draft, partial, additive with pending, no resolution, complete), `nextUnfinishedStep` (empty -> /class, class picked -> /background, class+background -> /species, pending/problem/equipment in step order, all done -> /export, null), `stepForChoice` (class, ASI feat cascade, species, species feat, background, background feat, class and background equipment cids), `cidFromHash`. The old "empty step gets no badge" test became its SK3 opposite.
- **Browser check** (Vite + Flask dev servers, 375 px, localStorage draft): empty draft shows badges 1 on Клас/Background/Species with accessible text "неизбрани: 1" and the next-step link `-> /class`; the link navigates to `/class`; an unknown path (`/nope/xyz`) lands on `/home`; a Warlock 7 2024 kitchen-sink draft with `class:warlock:spells-known` removed lists "Prepared Spells" on Export linking to `/class#class:warlock:spells-known`; clicking it (and a hard load of that URL) selects the Заклинания tab, expands the card and scrolls it to the top; the h2 reads "Warlock"; `scrollWidth === 375` throughout; no console errors. Servers stopped, viewport reset, test draft removed.
- **Gate.** `npm run build` green, `npm test` 126 passed; server `-n auto` 7449 passed + 2 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped; golden untouched.

## SK4 character library (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Findings A1/A21 closed: the app keeps several characters in `localStorage`. Functional only, no visual design (plain markup, existing classes). Server and draft shape untouched (`DRAFT_VERSION` 2).
- **Storage.** `dnd-builder-library-v1` = `{version:1, activeId, items:{[id]:{id,name,createdAt,updatedAt,seenChoiceIds:[]}}}` (+ optional `legacyId`, the item the legacy key was migrated into); one draft per character at `dnd-builder-draft-v2:<id>`; `createdAt`/`updatedAt` are epoch milliseconds; `item.name` is kept equal to `draft.name` on every save. Backups of unreadable text: `dnd-builder-draft-v2.unreadable-backup` (legacy key, unchanged), `dnd-builder-draft-v2:<id>.unreadable-backup` (per item), `dnd-builder-library-v1.unreadable-backup` (index).
- **Code.** `frontend/src/library.ts`: pure functions over an injected `LibraryEnv {storage, now, newId}` (storage adapter = `getItem/setItem/removeItem/keys`; `browserEnv()` wraps localStorage): `bootLibrary`, `saveDraft`, `createCharacter`, `openCharacter`, `renameCharacter`, `duplicateCharacter`, `deleteCharacter`, `exportLibrary`, `importDrafts`, `summarize`, plus `activeDraftRaw`/`resetActiveDraft` for the ErrorBoundary. `LibraryContext.tsx` (`LibraryProvider`, `useLibrary`) wraps them with React state and sits above `DraftProvider` in `main.tsx`. `DraftProvider` now renders an `ActiveDraft` keyed by the active id (switching remounts the reducer from the newly active draft) and saves through `library.persistActive` after each real change (an unchanged draft is not rewritten, so opening or reloading does not bump "updated"). `useDraft()` keeps its interface, so every page and `StatusBanner` work with the active character only; `storageFailed` = the draft write failed OR a library operation failed.
- **Boot.** No library key: the legacy `dnd-builder-draft-v2` is read through `mergeDraft` into the first item (new id, active, draft written first, then the index; if the index cannot be written the draft key is removed again and the legacy key stays the source of truth, storageFailed is raised). An unreadable/newer legacy text keeps the old behaviour (copied to the legacy backup key, Bulgarian notice, `unsavedRaw` when even the backup fails) and an empty character starts. The legacy key is never modified; it is removed when a second character is added (new, duplicate or import) and only if its content is safe elsewhere (migrated draft key exists, or an identical backup exists). Booting twice migrates once (idempotent: the second boot reads the library). With a library: every item draft is read; a corrupt one is copied to its backup key and its draft key removed (it becomes an empty character with the same name; the notice names the backup key); if the backup write fails the corrupt text is left untouched and the notice says so; a missing draft key loads as an empty character with the item name; an unreadable or newer-version index is backed up and rebuilt from the `dnd-builder-draft-v2:<id>` keys; an unknown `activeId` falls back to the most recently updated item.
- **Operations.** New (adds, activates, deletes nothing, no confirm), open, rename (non-active: rewrites that draft's name and the index; active: `SET_NAME`), duplicate (deep copy through JSON, new id, name `<name> (копие)`, `Без име (копие)` when empty, inherits `seenChoiceIds`, stays non-active, uses the live in-memory draft for the active one), delete (UI confirm; deleting the active one opens the most recently updated other one, deleting the last creates an empty one), export all (`{kind:"dnd-builder-library", version:1, drafts:[Draft...]}`, unreadable items are skipped and named), import (single draft or bundle; each entry through `mergeDraft`; accepted entries become NEW characters with fresh ids, regenerated while an id collides with an item or a stored draft key; never overwrites, never changes the active one; a per-entry report `{index,name,status,id?,message}` with Bulgarian messages; bundle newer than version 1, bundle without `drafts`, empty bundle and more than 200 entries are rejected). Writes are all try/catch; the draft is written before the index, so a failed draft write leaves the index and the other characters untouched.
- **Home.** "Герои" list (name, classes with catalog names and levels, total level, updated time; "(активен)" marker; buttons Отвори / Преименувай (window.prompt) / Копирай / Изтрий (window.confirm)), "Нов герой", "Изтегли всички (.json)", "Качи герой или пакет (.json)" (replaces the old "replace the current draft" upload), "Изтегли този герой (.json)", import report list. The old "Нов герой = delete the current draft" is gone. ErrorBoundary's download/reset now act on the active character only.
- **Tests.** vitest 126 -> 165 (`src/library.test.ts`, 39 tests with an in-memory adapter, fake clock and fake ids): fresh boot, legacy migration (draft preserved, active set, legacy untouched), idempotent migration, unreadable/newer/backup-failing legacy, library not writable during migration, new/open/rename/duplicate (deep copy, live draft)/delete (active, non-active, last) , legacy removal rules, quota failure on one item (index unchanged, others intact), corrupt item (non-active, active, backup failure), rebuilt index, newer index, unknown activeId, missing draft key, export-all -> import round trip, mixed valid/invalid bundle report, id collision regeneration, single draft import, bad inputs, import under quota, `summarize` ordering.
- **Browser check** (Vite + Flask dev servers, 375 px, Warlock 7 2024 as the legacy key): reload -> 1 item, legacy key kept, steps work (`/class` shows Warlock); "Нов герой" -> 2 items, legacy key removed, name typed and Fighter picked on the new one; reload -> both persist; switching, editing the active name, renaming a non-active one, duplicate, delete of a copy and of the active one (another activated), export all (bundle captured from the Blob: 3 drafts) and import (3 added; a bundle with a junk entry gave 2 added + 1 rejected "Файлът не прилича на чернова на герой."); one item's JSON corrupted in localStorage + reload -> banner with the backup key, backup key present, the other rows intact, the corrupt one listed as "няма запазена чернова"; `scrollWidth === 375` throughout; no console errors.
- **Deviations / notes.** (1) The bundle holds plain drafts (the draft has no id field and its shape must not change), so import always assigns new ids; "regenerate on collision" is implemented in the id generator. (2) The list is ordered by `updatedAt`, so the active character moves to the top while it is edited. (3) Opening a character does not bump `updatedAt`. (4) Sheet-level summary data (classes) come from the draft, class names from the catalog, so a not-yet-loaded catalog shows class ids. (5) `seenChoiceIds` is stored but unused until SK8.
- **Gate.** `cd frontend && npm run build` green, `npm test` 165 passed. No server/root files touched, so those suites were not re-run (baselines unchanged).

## SK5 sheet page (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Findings A3 (no in-app sheet), A4 (Abilities page showed base next to the final modifier) and the A16 part "per-route title and focus". New route `/sheet` ("Лист") between Equipment and Export (`logic/steps.ts` STEPS has 8 entries; it has no badge: `kind: null`, no `problemStep`; `nextUnfinishedStep` still ends at `/export`). NO visual design: plain semantic markup (`<section data-section="<key>" aria-labelledby>` + `h2`, `dl`, `table` with `th scope`, `ul`), existing class names only (the one new class is the functional `no-print`).
- **Logic.** `logic/sheetSections.ts`: `sheetSections(resolution, base?)` returns `[{key, data}]` in page order for the 23 keys in `SHEET_SECTION_KEYS` (identity, abilities, proficiencyBonus, armorClass, hitPoints, initiative, speeds, senses, passives, savingThrows, skills, proficiencies, languages, attacks (incl. weapon masteries), resources (class / feat / species), features, feats, spells (per caster + shared and pact slots), innateSpells, equipment (+ worn / wielded, gold, narrative items), carryingCapacity, defenses (damage modifiers + condition immunities), details); a section with nothing to show is left out. `abilityRows(totals, modifiers, base?)` gives base / bonus (= total - base) / total / modifier and is shared by the sheet and the Abilities page (columns Основа, Бонус, Общо, Модификатор; base comes from the draft, total from `sheet.abilities`, modifier from `derived.abilityModifiers`). `logic/labels.ts` holds the id -> name tables the exports print (skills, languages, proficiency slugs; game content, not translated). `logic/spellAbility.ts`: the resolution has the DC and attack bonus but not the casting ability, so the class's ability is looked up in a small table and shown only if `8 + PB + mod` equals the server's DC (and PB + mod its attack bonus); otherwise no ability line. `logic/resourceText.ts`: `describeResource` moved out of `ClassResourcesList.tsx` (same text; now reads the copy module; adds the `details.damageTypes` fragment "типове щета: ..." that the exports only print by luck). `logic/routeTitle.ts` + `components/RouteA11y.tsx`: `document.title` = "<page> — Конструктор на герои" per path and, when the PATH changes (not on first load or hash-only changes), focus moves to `main h1` (fallback `main`, `tabindex=-1`).
- **Components.** `components/sheet/*` (SectionFrame, Identity, Abilities, StatSections (PB, AC, HP, initiative, speeds, senses, passives), ProficiencySections (saves, skills, proficiencies, languages), Attacks, Resources, Features (features + feats), Spells (spells + innate), Equipment (equipment + carrying + defenses), Details, SheetView): typed props, no fetching, no router. `pages/SheetPage.tsx` renders `SheetView`, a "Печат" button (`window.print()`), a link to Export and a "Листът е непълен" note while parts or choices are outstanding (the sheet works on an unfinished draft). `ExportPage` keeps its gate and downloads and links to `/sheet`. `print.css` (`@media print`): hides `.step-nav`, `.next-step`, `.status-banner`, `.no-print` and buttons. `index.css`: `[data-section] table` shares the `.ability-table` scroll rule and `[data-section]` wraps long words (without it the attacks table was 685 px wide at 375 px).
- **Copy module (language preparation).** `frontend/src/copy/bg.ts` (Bulgarian, one object structured by area: `nav`, `titles`, `common`, `abilitiesPage`, `sheet.<area>`, `exportPage`; templates and plurals as functions, e.g. `t.common.feet(n)`, `t.sheet.incomplete(n)`) and `copy/index.ts` (`type Copy = typeof bg`, `export const t: Copy = bg`). A future `en.ts` is `const en: Copy = {...}` and the switch picks the object in `index.ts`; en.ts is NOT created. Every user-visible string of the new sheet components, the new route titles, the new nav label and the new Abilities columns is in copy. Not moved (SK6): the other inline strings of the old pages, `SheetTraitsSummary`, the other step labels. Game content (class / species / feat / spell / item names, features, rule texts, skill / language / proficiency names via `labels.ts`) is shown as the data gives it.
- **Tests.** vitest 189 passed (was 165): `sheetSections.test.ts` (section keys and content per fixture, base + bonus = total, expertise marks, details / defenses / worn-wielded with synthetic resolutions, `spellcastingAbility`, `routeTitle`, and a leaf check that every resolution leaf assigned by `sheetCoverage.json` appears in its section data and in the text of the rendered page (`renderToStaticMarkup`)); `steps.test.ts` updated to eight routes. Fixtures: `frontend/src/__fixtures__/{warlock7-2024,bard10-2014,fighter4-2024,wizard20-2024,rogue6-2024}.json`, dumped by `server/tests/test_sheet_fixtures.py` (`{id, ruleset, base, resolution}` with `choices` emptied; the test asserts the committed files equal a fresh dump; regenerate with `SHEET_FIXTURES_REGEN=1 python -m pytest tests/test_sheet_fixtures.py`; skipped without the 5etools data, a second test checks the files exist and parse). `logic/sheetCoverage.json` is the single list "section key -> resolution path patterns"; `server/tests/test_sheet_coverage.py` takes every leaf the W9 export audit checks (all `ALL_SPECS` drafts plus a fighter 4 with worn / wielded / details set; leaves in `EXPORT_OMITTED` are ids and are skipped) and requires it to be assigned in the coverage file or listed in `SHEET_OMITTED` (also: no leaf in both, no stale entry, the coverage keys equal the TypeScript `SHEET_SECTION_KEYS`). `SHEET_OMITTED` has 12 entries: `derived.classResources.[].classId` (id), `*Resources.[].source.label` x3 (dup: the resource name already names the feature), `speciesResources.[].details.{damageType,save,shape}` (dup: in the Breath Weapon note), `sheet.equipment.slots.{armor,shield,hands}.[].name` (ui: picker candidates), `sheet.equipment.{worn,wielded}.manual` (flag). `UNSEEN_OK` (2 coverage patterns no audited draft produces: narrative items, subspecies name). Side edits: `tests/test_chargen_isolation.py` `SERVER_LOCAL_MODULES` += `test_export_completeness`; `server/tests/test_x2_turn_combat_rage_recharges.py` now reads the recharge words from `copy/bg.ts` instead of `ClassResourcesList.tsx`.
- **Browser check** (dev servers, legacy-key drafts migrated into the library; 375 px and 1024 px; no console errors; viewport reset, servers stopped, test drafts removed from localStorage). Warlock 7 2024 (21 sections: pact slots, innate Mage Armor, devil's sight, Pact of the Tome picks, Alert note), Bard 10 2014 (18 sections: expertise on Acrobatics / Animal Handling, shared slots 4/3/3/3/2, DC 15 / +7), Wizard 5 Evoker 2024 (19 sections: spellbook, INT, DC 13 / +5), Fighter 4 2024 with chain mail (18 sections: AC 16 with the stealth note, attacks table, pack contents, carrying capacity): `scrollWidth === 375` on all four after the table/wrap rule; focus moved to the h1 and the title changed on every nav click; Abilities page shows base / bonus / total / modifier; at 1024 `/abilities`, `/sheet`, `/export` have no overflow. Print preview not exercised.
- **Deviations / notes.** (1) The spellcasting ability is derived client-side with a cross-check (see Logic) because the resolution does not carry it; a server field would move golden. (2) Skill and language names stay in English as the exports print them. (3) The `Fighter 4` section list has no `senses` / `spells` / `innateSpells`, and `defenses` / `details` appear in no kitchen-sink fixture (tested synthetically). (4) The Export page's resource lists now come from the copy module (identical text, plus the damage-types fragment). (5) The step label keeps the Bulgarian "Лист"; the old English step labels are SK6.
- **Gate.** `cd frontend && npm run build && npm test` green (189 passed); server suite 7466 passed + 2 skipped (7449 + 17 new); SRD-only run of the new tests: 9 passed, rest skipped; root suite 363 passed / 8 skipped; golden untouched.

## I0 glossary (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Slice I0 of the bilingual plan (docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-04-bilingual-and-names.md). No behaviour change: nothing reads the glossary yet (SK6-BI / I-series will). `server/i18n/` (new package; `i18n` added to `SERVER_LOCAL_MODULES` in `tests/test_chargen_isolation.py`): `glossary.json` = `{termId: {en, bg, cite: {src, locator}}}` (40 terms: the plan's starter list plus blindsight / tremorsense / truesight), `glossary.py` (loader, `validate`, `check_cite`, `phb_locator_found`, generators, CLI). Generated from the json: `docs/GLOSSARY.md` (table) and `frontend/src/copy/glossary.gen.ts` (`glossary` const `{id: {en, bg}}` + `GlossaryId`). Regenerate with `cd server && ../.venv/bin/python -m i18n.glossary`; `--check` exits 1 when a generated file is stale (also run by the test suite).
- **Conventions.** `en` = wording of the source (2024 PHB first, SRD second), in the source's case; `bg` = the Bulgarian the app prints today (frontend copy / exports), in the case the app uses (sense, rest and unit words are lower case there). Only terms and locators are stored, never book text. Locator forms: `phb2024`: a heading line, a bold / bold-italic run-in or a Rules Glossary entry name (case-insensitive), or `text:<literal>`; `srd` / `5etools`: `file.json::<exact JSON string>` (relative to `chargen/data/srd` / `server/data/official`). PHB cites are verified only when `/Users/yoanpetrov/Downloads/Player's Handbook (2024).md` exists (outside the repo, skipped in CI); SRD and 5etools cites are always verified (darkvision -> SRD Traits, weaponMastery -> 5etools fighter class feature).
- **Tests.** `server/tests/test_glossary.py` (18 tests plus the parametrised cite checks): structure, starter terms, validator negatives, JSON and PHB locator checks (PHB matcher also tested on synthetic text), bg consistency with `export_fields` (senses, rests, resistance / immunity / vulnerability) and every bg wording must occur in the exports or `copy/bg.ts` (`originFeat` excepted: new wording), generated files up to date, `--check` drift behaviour and CLI. 
- **Deviations from the plan's starter list.** (1) `feet` (en "feet") instead of "ft.": the 2024 PHB writes "feet", it has no "ft." abbreviation. (2) `spellAttackBonus` has en "Spell Attack Modifier" (PHB wording; the id keeps the plan's name). (3) `senses` has en "Special Senses" (the PHB heading). (4) `passiveInsight` / `passiveInvestigation` cite the PHB "Passive Perception" entry (the PHB defines only that one; the others follow its formula). (5) `originFeat` bg "Талант от произхода" is new (no BG label exists today).
- **Gate.** Server `-n auto` 7484 passed + 2 skipped (7466 + 18); `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1520 passed / 5716 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped; `npm run build` green, vitest 189 passed. Golden untouched, no REBASELINES.

## SK6-BI bilingual UI copy catalog (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Slice SK6-BI of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-04-bilingual-and-names.md. All user-facing UI text in `frontend/src` (pages, components, hooks, logic modules, the SK4 library and SK5 sheet copy) now comes from the copy catalog; there is an English catalog; the language is a per-device preference. NO switch UI yet (that is LANG-SW); the server is NOT changed (engine messages stay as delivered; the `lang` request parameter comes with the I-series). The Bulgarian UI is byte-identical to before.
- **Catalog.** `copy/bg.ts` (352 texts + 120 templates, structured by area: `nav`, `titles`, `common`, `abilitiesPage`, `sheet.*`, `status`, `errorBoundary`, `home`, `classPage`, `backgroundPage`, `speciesPage`, `equipmentPage`, `exportPage`, `choice`, `spellLibrary`, `traits`, `slots`, `msg`, `library`), `copy/en.ts` = `const en: Copy = {...}` (a missing key is a tsc error), `copy/index.ts` (`Copy`, `Lang`, `COPY`, `copyFor(lang)`; the static `t` export is gone, so no module can use a language-blind catalog). Rules terms in `en.ts` are taken from `glossary.gen.ts` (`g.armorClass.en`, ...); a test pins every mapped bg/en term to the glossary (spell slots stay "Клетки за заклинания" in bg, "Spell Slots" in en).
- **Language.** `LanguageContext.tsx`: `LanguageProvider` (localStorage `dnd-builder-lang-v1` = bg|en, default bg, anything else or a blocked storage = bg; sets `document.documentElement.lang`; mounted above `ErrorBoundary` and `LibraryProvider` in `main.tsx`), `useLang()` -> `{lang, setLang}`, `useT()` -> the catalog. Without a provider (unit tests of single components) the default bg catalog applies. Pure modules take `t` as an argument (`routeTitle(t, path)`, `describeResource(t, r)`, `groupLabel(t, g)`), `STEPS` carries a `key` into `t.nav.steps`.
- **Messages of pure modules and hooks.** `logic/messages.ts`: `Msg = {code, params?}` (params may be strings, numbers or nested messages), `msg()`, `MsgError` (an Error carrying a Msg; its `.message` is the code), `toMsg(err)`, `renderMsg(t, msg)`; the texts live in `t.msg[code]` (templates take one params object). Converted: `mergeDraft` errors (`draft.*`), `library.ts` (boot/open/delete notices, import report entries; the stored words "Без име" / "(копие)" come in through a `LibraryNames` argument of `duplicateCharacter`, `exportLibrary().skipped` holds the raw names), `httpErrorMessage` (now returns a Msg), `exportReadiness().missingParts` (now `"class"|"species"|"background"`), and the `error` state of `useResolution`/`useCatalog`/`useItems`/`useSpells` (now `Msg | null`). Server-written texts (problems, warnings, pruned reasons, choice and option labels) are data and are shown as delivered; a non-coded `Error` is shown with its own technical text (`raw`).
- **Tests (vitest 189 -> 247).** `bgRender.test.ts`: the render guard. 8 routes x 10 states (the 5 fixture characters, empty+loading, empty+failed, and three "busy" states with a multi-character library incl. empty/corrupt rows, choice cards short and long, problems, warnings, pruned, resources, notices, storage failure, other ability methods, manual hit points) are rendered with `renderToStaticMarkup` through the real providers (hooks mocked with fixture data, in-memory library storage, fixed clock, TZ=UTC) and compared with the FULL HTML (aria-label/placeholder/title included) of `src/__snapshots__/bg-render.json.gz`, captured and committed BEFORE the conversion (commit 1058f93); React `useId` values are normalised. It also renders the English UI with Latin user data and asserts no Cyrillic on any route. `copyGuards.test.ts`: key parity, leaf kind and arity, placeholder parity (each template is sampled with distinct sentinels and must print the same parameters in both languages; `msg.*` templates through a recording Proxy), no Cyrillic in `en.ts` (source and every rendered output), Latin words in bg only from an allowlist (permanent: AC, DC, CR, SRD, PDF, YAML, JSON/json, Markdown, MB, ability codes, `d`, `lb`; LEGACY list = the English words the Bulgarian UI already showed, kept so the BG rendering stays identical, with a "no stale entries" check), bg/en pinned to the glossary, and an AST scan (`rolldown/parseAst`, already installed as a dependency of vite) of every non-test source: letters in JSX text, JSX child expressions, `aria-label`/`placeholder`/`title`/`alt`/`label` attributes, `alert/confirm/prompt` arguments and `document.title`, and any Cyrillic literal outside `copy/` (the exception list is empty; negative tests prove the scan catches each case). `LanguageContext.test.ts`, `logic/messages.test.ts` (BG wording of the HTTP and library messages pinned to the old text). Existing tests adapted: `library.test.ts` (messages rendered with bg), `draft.test.ts` (codes + BG text), `exportReadiness.test.ts` (codes), `sheetSections.test.ts` (`bg` for `t`, `routeTitle(t, ...)`). `nodeShim.d.ts` types the few Node APIs the tests use (the project has no @types/node).
- **Browser check** (Vite + Flask dev servers, 375 px). The old commit (1058f93, git worktree) and the new code ran side by side on ports 5174/5173 with the same localStorage draft (Fighter 5 / Wizard 3, manual hit points, Acolyte, Human): the `innerText` of all 8 routes is identical (same lengths and SHA-256), including the parts that load by effect (subclass list, higher-level features, resources). With `dnd-builder-lang-v1=en` and a reload: `<html lang="en">`, English titles on all 8 routes (only the draft's own Cyrillic name/faith remains), no horizontal overflow, no console errors. localStorage, viewport, servers and the worktree were reset/removed.
- **Deviations / notes.** (1) The Bulgarian UI already showed many English words (page titles `Home`, `Background`, `Species`, `Abilities`, `Equipment`, `Export`, `Character Name`, ability-method labels, the whole Export attack/carrying lines, ...). They are kept (byte-identical rule) and listed in the LEGACY allowlist; translating them is a deliberate later change. (2) Two latent quirks are preserved: the Export attack line prints the literal text `null` for an attack with damage but no damage type, and senses there always say "фута" (never "фут"). (3) `exportLibrary().skipped` returns raw names (empty string for unnamed); the Home page applies the "Без име" fallback. (4) Date locale is a catalog value (`bg-BG` / `en-GB`). (5) The catalog still contains the pre-existing unused keys of SK5 (`common.level`, `common.levelOf`, `sheet.features.level`, ...). (6) Not covered by a pixel/DOM test: nothing in the render guard runs effects, so the effect-loaded sections (class detail, spell library) were compared in the browser only.
- **Gate.** `cd frontend && npm run build && npm test` green (247 passed); server `-n auto` 7484 passed + 2 skipped; `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1520 passed / 5716 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped. Golden untouched, server and tests/ untouched.

## LANG-SW language switch (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Slice LANG-SW of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-04-bilingual-and-names.md. `components/LanguageSwitch.tsx`: a plain two-button control (`role="group"`, `aria-label` = `t.lang.label`, buttons `type="button"` with `lang`, `aria-pressed`, label `t.lang.bg` / `t.lang.en`), mounted in a new `<header className="app-header">` in `App.tsx` just before `StepNav`; it uses `useLang()`. CSS: `.app-header` (right-aligned row) and `.lang-switch button { min-width/min-height: 44px }`, nothing else. Persistence and `<html lang>` are the existing LanguageProvider behaviour.
- **Requests.** `logic/langQuery.ts` `withLang(path, lang)` appends `?lang=` (or `&lang=`). `useResolution` posts to `/api/resolve?lang=<lang>` and has `lang` in its effect dependencies (a language change re-resolves); `ExportPage` posts to `/api/export/<fmt>?lang=<lang>`. Body, headers and everything else are unchanged; the server ignores the parameter for now. Catalog, items and spells requests are NOT changed (not asked for).
- **Copy.** `lang: {label, bg, en}`: bg catalog "Език" / "БГ" / "EN"; en catalog "Language" / "BG" / "EN".
- **Snapshot.** `bg-render.json.gz` updated for exactly one change: in all 80 entries (10 states x 8 routes) the string `<header class="app-header"><div class="lang-switch" role="group" aria-label="Език"><button type="button" lang="bg" aria-pressed="true">БГ</button><button type="button" lang="en" aria-pressed="false">EN</button></div></header>` is inserted between `<div class="app">` and `<nav class="step-nav"`; verified by a script (old snapshot with that insertion == new snapshot for every key).
- **Tests (vitest 247 -> 252).** `LanguageSwitch.test.ts`: markup and pressed state in both languages, 44px rule in the stylesheet, `withLang`, and source-level checks that `useResolution` / `ExportPage` use `withLang` and `lang` is an effect dependency (the project has no DOM test library, so the re-resolve itself is covered in the browser). `copyGuards.test.ts`: `EN` added to the PERMANENT Latin allowlist.
- **Browser check** (375 px, Vite + Flask): buttons 44x44, no horizontal overflow; clicking EN gives `<html lang="en">`, localStorage `en`, title "Home — Character Builder" and a new `POST /api/resolve?lang=en`; clicking БГ back gives `lang=bg` and `/api/resolve?lang=bg`; clicking the already-pressed button makes no request. Storage reset afterwards.
- **Deviations / notes.** (1) The plan says labels "БГ"/"EN"; the English catalog must contain no Cyrillic (SK6-BI guard), so the English UI shows "BG"/"EN". (2) The header sits outside `<main>`, above the step nav. (3) The control is not styled further (designer's job in the SK7 hooks).
- **Gate.** frontend build + vitest 252 passed; server `-n auto` 7484 passed + 2 skipped; `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1520 passed / 5716 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped. Golden, server and tests/ untouched.

## SK7 theming hooks (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Slice SK7 of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-04-skeleton-and-verification-campaign.md (A14). NO visual change. `src/index.css` is now only a list of `@import`s of `src/styles/*.css`: `tokens.css` (`:root` placeholder variables equal to the old literals: `--font-sans`, `--space-1..5` = 4/8/12/16/20 px, `--radius-sm/pill/round`, 17 colour tokens, `--color-error: red`), then `base`, `layout`, `status`, `choice`, `abilities`, `lists`, `spells`, `forms`, `touch`, `hooks`. The old file was cut into contiguous chunks in the original order, so the cascade is unchanged; existing rules reference the tokens (colours everywhere; spacing in `padding`/`margin`/`gap`; `border-radius`; `font-family`). Literals kept on purpose: 1/2/3/6/10 px spacing, 44 px touch targets, font sizes, widths, negative margins. `hooks.css` holds 11 empty rules so every class the markup uses exists in a stylesheet. `docs/FRONTEND-STRUCTURE.md` is the designer's guide (stylesheet map, `data-status` / `data-state` values, component map, state table).
- **Markup.** The inline red style of the Export error line became `className="status--error"` (`styles/status.css`, `color: var(--color-error)`). New `data-state` hooks (existing `data-status` kept): choice card `expanded|collapsed`, picker option rows `available|disabled|hidden-by-filters`, status banners `error|notice|storage-failed|pruned`, Home pruned items `pruned`, export buttons `ready|busy|disabled`, export error `error`, language buttons `active|inactive`.
- **Snapshot.** `bg-render.json.gz` updated; verified by script that the new snapshot equals the previous one once every ` data-state="..."` attribute is removed (80 of 80 entries; counts of added attributes: active 80, inactive 80, available 138, disabled 9, ready 15, expanded 9, collapsed 9, error 16, notice 24, pruned 30). The red-error line is not rendered by any snapshot state.
- **Tests (vitest 252 -> 256).** `styles.test.ts`: no `style={{` in any TSX, every class named in a `className` literal exists in a stylesheet, `index.css` contains only `@import`s with `tokens` first and every file in `styles/` imported, only `tokens.css` defines custom properties and every `var(--x)` is defined, the language switch 44 px rule. The 44 px check moved out of `LanguageSwitch.test.ts`.
- **Browser check** (375 px emulation not needed for a no-change slice; same pane viewport). The previous commit (git worktree, port 5174) and the new code (5173) ran against the same server and localStorage draft (Fighter 5 / Wizard 3, Acolyte, Human, BG): for all 8 routes (choice cards expanded) the computed style of every element under `#root` (all properties except size/position-derived ones such as width, height, inset, transform-origin, which follow loading timing) is identical: 0 differences over 2570 elements. Worktree, servers and storage cleaned up.
- **Ambiguities, minimal reading.** (1) "data-status/data-state on every stateful element": applied to the elements that already have a state in code (list above); the react-router step-nav link keeps its `active` class (NavLink cannot set a data attribute from its state), the library list keeps `data-active`, and the "new" state has no markup (it only exists as library data). (2) "every class used in TSX exists in some stylesheet": satisfied with empty hook rules instead of removing classes. (3) "per-component-group" files are contiguous slices, not a regrouping, to keep the cascade byte-for-byte. (4) `status--error` is the only class added; `data-theme` is documented but not used.
- **Gate.** frontend build + vitest 256 passed; server `-n auto` 7484 passed + 2 skipped; `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1520 passed / 5716 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped. Golden, server and tests/ untouched.

## SK8 level-up marker (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Finding A8 closed. A pending choice card that appeared through a level-up gets `data-new="true"` and the text marker `<span class="choice-card__new">` ("ново" in bg, "new" in en, `t.choice.newMarker`); under the step nav a link `<p class="new-choices" data-new="true">` "Нови избори: N" / "New choices: N" (`t.nav.newChoices`) points to the first new card (`stepForChoice` + `#cid` anchor). Hooks only: `.choice-card__new` and `.new-choices` are empty rules in `styles/hooks.css`; `docs/FRONTEND-STRUCTURE.md` (data-new convention, state table row "new") updated.
- **Model.** `library.ts`: `seenChoiceIds` (SK4 field, now used) = the choice ids the app has acknowledged; new optional item field `seenBaselined` (true after the first sync, so old characters do not light up); `setSeenChoices` (writes the index only, does not touch the draft or `updatedAt`); duplicate inherits both. `logic/newChoices.ts` (pure): `syncSeen(state, tracker, resolution)` acknowledges every non-pending card (resolving clears for good), everything on the first-ever sync, and afterwards any pending card that appears without a rise of `sheet.level` (class/species/subclass picks); a pending card that appears together with a level rise (tracker level >= 1) stays unseen, also across reloads (the first sync of a session absorbs nothing). `newChoiceIds` = pending and unseen. `NewChoicesContext.tsx` (`NewChoicesProvider` inside `ResolutionProvider`, which is remounted per character; `useNewChoices`), `components/NewChoicesLink.tsx`, `ChoiceCard` reads the context.
- **Snapshot.** `bg-render.json.gz` NOT changed (no state renders a new card: every library item is baselined at the first sync and the static render shows none; the existing guard passes byte for byte).
- **Tests (vitest 256 -> 271).** `logic/newChoices.test.ts` (8: first sync absorbs, level raise marks the new card, persists across syncs and reload, resolving clears for good, no marker for class/subclass/species picks, second class at level 1 is a raise, unavailable never new, unchanged resolution no change), `NewChoices.test.ts` (3: card markup bg/en, resolved/acknowledged card, header link count/target/empty), `library.test.ts` (4: `setSeenChoices` incl. reboot and `updatedAt` kept, quota failure, duplicate inherits list and flag, old index loads not baselined).
- **Browser check** (Vite + Flask, 375 px, fresh storage, restored afterwards): Fighter picked at level 1 -> no marker; level set to 4 -> link "Нови избори: 1" to `/class#class:fighter:4:asi`, that card `data-new="true"` with "ново", the two level-1 cards unmarked; reload -> marker persists; picking an ASI option -> card resolved, marker and link gone, id in `seenChoiceIds`; `scrollWidth` 375, no console errors.
- **Deviations / notes.** (1) A new optional library field `seenBaselined` was needed (the plan names only `seenChoiceIds`). (2) "New" is defined by a level rise seen in the same session (resolution `sheet.level` compared with the previous resolution); editing the stored draft's level outside the app does not mark anything. (3) Lowering a level and raising it again makes the cards new again only if they were never acknowledged.
- **Gate.** frontend build + vitest 271 passed; server and root suites unchanged (not touched), see the SK10 gate run at the end.

## SK9 API version and contract docs (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Findings A10/A11 closed. `GET /api/health` now returns `{ok, apiVersion: 1, draftVersion: 2, dataSource, bundleSha256}`: `ok` is unchanged (the deployment test and the SPA tests only read it; the two old `== {"ok": True}` asserts in `test_app.py` / `test_spa_serving.py` now check `["ok"] is True`), `dataSource` = `catalog.official.paths.data_source()` (`bundled | srd-only | explicit | campaign`), `bundleSha256` = the full manifest hash when bundled, else `null`. `server/app.py` holds `API_VERSION` and `DRAFT_VERSION`; a test pins `DRAFT_VERSION` to the constant in `frontend/src/logic/draft.ts`.
- **Client.** `logic/serverHealth.ts` (`parseHealth`, `serverIsOlder`) and `useServerHealth.ts` (one fetch at start, failures silent); `StatusBanner` shows `<p class="status-banner" data-state="server-older">` (copy `status.serverOlder`, bg and en) when the server's `draftVersion` is older than the build's or not reported (an old `{ok:true}` server). `logic/migrations.ts`: `MIGRATIONS` table (v1 -> v2 adds `ruleset: "mixed"`), `migrateDraft(draft, target, table)` applies the chain one version at a time (a draft without `draftVersion` is v1, a newer one is rejected, a hole in the table throws); `mergeDraft` runs it before merging defaults (behaviour for existing drafts unchanged).
- **Docs.** `docs/API.md` (English): conventions, the two error shapes, status table, every endpoint with parameters and bodies, the export gate. A test fails if an `/api/` route is missing from it.
- **Tests.** Server `tests/test_api_contract.py` (17): health keys and versions, draftVersion = frontend, dataSource bundled / srd-only / explicit with the sha rule, non-object bodies (5 payloads x 2 routes), 404 unknown class, 404 export format, 400 level, JSON 404 for an unknown API path, 413, export-gate 400 body shape and first ids, API.md covers all routes. vitest 271 -> 280 (`logic/migrations.test.ts`: complete chain, version reading, v1 -> v2, idempotent current draft, synthetic 4-step chain, newer/hole errors, `mergeDraft` through the chain, health parsing and the banner rule).
- **Snapshot.** `bg-render.json.gz` unchanged by this slice (effects do not run in the static render, so no banner is rendered).
- **Pre-existing environment failure.** On this machine (Node v26.13.0, Homebrew) `bgRender.test.ts` fails on a clean HEAD: `toLocaleString("bg-BG")` now returns `... ч. ч.` (the ICU data already appends the "ч." that the catalog template adds), so the 10 entries that show the Home list time differ. Verified by regenerating the snapshot into the working tree and comparing with HEAD: the only differences are those 10 entries, and they vanish after replacing ` ч. ч.` by ` ч.`; the snapshot file was restored. Not changed by SK8-SK10 (the date format is a deliberate later fix: the test and the app need a locale-stable formatter).
- **Gate.** See the SK10 section (one final run of all suites after SK10, plus per-slice runs recorded there).

## SK10 Y2 option `source` (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Finding A12 closed. Every option dict that traces to a catalog record carries `"source": <book code>` ("SRD" when the record has none); labels, ids, order and cards are unchanged byte for byte. `server/engine/option_source.py` (pure) `annotate_choices(catalog, choices)` runs once at the end of `resolve()` (after `mark_unavailable`), copies only the options it changes. Rules by option id, equipment cards skipped (their item ids collide with spell ids such as "shield"): `feat:<index>` -> the feat (ASI, background/species feat grants, Adept/Initiate pools); an ASI step (`...:asi` card, not a feat) -> "SRD"; `<feature>--<key>` -> the text option's own `source` (Totem Spirit Elk/Tiger = SCAG), else the feature record (2014 and 2024 subclass-option cards); an id in `catalog.features` -> that record (invocations, metamagic, maneuvers and other optional features, Circle of the Land); an id in `catalog.spells` (not `skill-*`) -> the spell (every spell card). Skills, languages, abilities, damage types, equipment and the like get no `source`.
- **Client.** `types.ts` `ChoiceOption.source?: string` (same commit); `ChoicePicker` uses `o.source ?? optionSource(o.label)` for the source select and filter, so the "Без етикет (177)" bucket of the mixed ASI card now splits by real book (the ASI steps are "SRD").
- **Golden.** `REBASELINES["Y2"]` = cases `*`, paths `^result[.]choices[[][^]]+[]][.]options[[][^]]+[]][.]source$` (written from the rule before the dry run); `--rebaseline Y2 --dry-run` showed no DISALLOWED path; then the real run. Summary as printed:

```
rebaseline Y2 (option source field (SK10 / Y2))
  matching cases changed: 215 of 251
  77656 changed path(s) match ^result[.]choices[[][^]]+[]][.]options[[][^]]+[]][.]source$
wrote 251 cases, 1097682 bytes
```
  The other 36 cases (levels 1 and 3 without a feat, spell or feature option) are byte-identical (the tool prints a "pattern too wide" warning for each, expected for `*`). Verified afterwards by diffing the gz at HEAD with the working tree: drafts identical, every result identical except the 77656 added `options[*].source` keys and the rewritten fresh results' `derived.speciesResources` of 72 changed cases (a `STRIP` path, stripped on both sides at compare time; the rebaseline writes the fresh result of a changed case, as in earlier rebaselines). The REBASELINES tuple pin test now ends `"X2", "Y2"`; a new golden test pins the 215 cases and that no skill/equipment option carries a source. `--regenerate` was not used. The second golden (`g2_resolve_baseline.json.gz`, guard of `test_worn_wielded.py`) is not covered by `--rebaseline`: `strip_g2` drops `options[*].source` from the fresh result, the gz is unchanged.
- **Tests.** Server `tests/test_option_source.py` (13): every option of the mixed Fighter 4 ASI card has a source, the unlabelled feat options carry the record's real code (PHB, XPHB, ...), labelled options agree with their label suffix, ASI steps are SRD, SRD-only sources, spell cards (SRD and book spells), Warlock invocations and Sorcerer metamagic, Totem Warrior (PHB; Elk SCAG) and 2024 Ranger Hunter option cards, skills and equipment have none, labels unchanged by the pass, input not mutated, a wide sample with no book option left without a source. vitest +1 (`sources.test.ts`: the picker expression). Totals: server 7515 passed + 2 skipped; `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1537 passed / 5730 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped; vitest 280 passed + the environment failure noted in SK9.
- **Deviations / notes.** (1) ASI steps (STR +2, ...) get `source: "SRD"` so that "every option of the Fighter 4 ASI card has a source" holds literally; they now appear under the SRD source filter. (2) The server tests of the new module were written right after the module (not before); the golden rebaseline was the failing-first check (215 cases moved before the allowlist was applied). (3) Under pytest the allowlist regex raises a `FutureWarning` ("Possible nested set") at compile time, harmless, the regex is the one in the plan.
- **Gate.** See totals above (server `-n auto` once showed `test_resolve_stays_as_fast_as_with_the_srd_only_catalog` failing under load during SK9; it passes alone and in the final run).

## I1 i18n core and frozen English message snapshot (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Slice I1 of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-04-bilingual-and-names.md. Two commits: (a) the message zoo and the frozen snapshot, taken from the UNMODIFIED engine and committed alone; (b) the i18n core. No behaviour change for English (every engine message is still a bare English string; no emission site is converted: that is I2-I5).
- **Snapshot (commit a).** `server/tests/i18n_zoo.py` builds 1163 drafts: every golden draft as stored (251) in four variants (`g/` as is, `clear/` all selections cleared, `half/` class levels halved with selections kept, `r2024/` ruleset switched to 2024 with selections kept = 1004), the 23 2024 kitchen sinks as built (`ks2024/`) and switched to 2014 (`ks2024-as-2014/`) = 46, and 113 hand-written triggers (`t/<name>`; three of them resolve against a degraded catalog: no 2024 class/subclass tables, or envelopes the engine drops). For each case `leaves()` records, as plain strings: `problems` and `warnings` as [id, message], `pruned` as [choiceId, reason], `disabled` {"card | option": disabledReason}, `cards` {card: [label, description]}, and `exportGate` (`app._pending_export_problems`). Stored in `tests/golden/messages_en_snapshot.json.gz` (259 KB; 952 problem/warning leaves, 2070 pruned, 11972 disabledReason). `tests/test_i18n_snapshot.py` re-resolves every stored case and requires equality (8 chunks for xdist); skipped without the 5etools data, like the golden. Policy: the file only grows through `python tests/i18n_zoo.py --append` (re-resolves all stored cases first and requires them unchanged, adds new case names only); there is no regenerate (`--freeze` refuses when the file exists; the test checks it). Any snapshot diff in I2-I5 is an executor error: stop and report. `--coverage` prints which problem/warning message sites the zoo reaches.
- **Core (commit b).** `server/i18n/messages.py`: `Msg(str)` carries `.key` and `.params`, equal to and serialized as its English text (`json.dumps` writes it as a plain string), `startswith`/`==`/`hash`/`repr` unchanged; `__reduce__` makes copy, deepcopy and pickle keep key and params (params are deep-copied). String operations (`+`, f-strings, `.strip()`, `join`) return a plain `str` and drop the tag (the localizer then leaves English; I2 tests must check BG coverage over the zoo). `msg(key, **params)` renders the catalog's English template; `render(m, lang)` (non-Msg values pass through; a key without a template in the language falls back to the stored English); `localize(resolution, lang)`: "en" returns the input object itself, another language returns a copy-on-change deep walk (containers without a Msg are shared, the input is not modified). Templates use `str.format` fields plus the plural filter `{n|one|many}` (`one` when n == 1; two forms exactly; the forms may not contain `{ } : ! [ ] |`). `validate_catalog` (English required, optional Bulgarian, same placeholder names, templates format) and `template_fields` are there for the I2 completeness tests. `server/i18n/catalog/` = `problems`, `warnings`, `pruned`, `disabled`, `labels` modules (each an empty `MESSAGES`) merged by `build()` into `MESSAGES` (a duplicate key raises). `parse_lang`: missing = "en", "bg"/"en" exact, anything else ValueError.
- **API.** `app.py` `_request_lang()`: `/api/resolve` and `/api/export/<fmt>` read `?lang=` (missing = en, other = 400 `{"error": "'lang' must be 'bg' or 'en'"}`, checked before the body; an unknown export format is still 404 first); the resolution and the export 400 `{problems}` body go through `localize` (a no-op today). The `{error}` texts stay English; the export files are not language dependent yet (XL). `server/service.py` does not exist (the Pyodide worker comes later and will call `localize` itself). `docs/API.md` updated (Conventions, error table, export gate).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_i18n_messages.py` (53): Msg text/key/params, string behaviour, JSON, tag loss, deepcopy/copy/pickle (protocols 2 and default), nesting (including in list params), plural filter (parametrized, several fields, malformed), `render` fallback, `localize` (en identity, bg rendering, input unchanged, shared subtrees, tuples), lang parsing unit and HTTP (both routes, all three export formats, 404 precedence, localized `problems` body with a patched resolver), catalog wiring/merge/duplicates, `validate_catalog`. `tests/test_i18n_snapshot.py` (19). Root `tests/test_chargen_isolation.py`: `i18n_zoo` added to `SERVER_LOCAL_MODULES`.
- **Zoo coverage of the problem/warning message sites (AST scan of the engine and app: dict literals with "id" and "message").** 81 of 85 sites are reached. Not reachable with the real catalog (listed as `KNOWN_GAPS` in the snapshot test): resolve.py's own `level-unsupported` ("isn't a valid number") and `ability-invalid-<AB>` ("isn't a valid number") re-checks, dead after `normalize_draft` coerces first; the two `feat-prerequisite-unmet` sites for species/background feat picks ("...so the <label> pick isn't applied" and the plain origin-feat one), because no Origin feat in the data has a structured prerequisite. Pruned reasons (37 sites) and disabledReason (~49 sites) have no ids; the zoo yields 176 distinct pruned-reason shapes and 117 disabledReason shapes (quoted names and digits normalized), which is a rough, not exact, coverage measure. After I2 changes the emission sites the AST scan in `i18n_zoo.declared_families` will see `msg(...)` calls instead of dict literals and must be adapted by that slice (the test asserts at least 85 sites today).
- **Deviations / notes.** (1) The plan names `server/service.py` for lang parsing; it does not exist. (2) The snapshot test is skipped under `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` (all cases need the 5etools data or the golden file's drafts); the SRD-only run therefore does not exercise it. (3) The gz header carries a timestamp, so a rewrite by `--append` changes the file bytes even when the content is identical.
- **Gate.** Server `-n auto` 7587 passed + 2 skipped; `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1590 passed / 5749 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped; `npm run build` green, vitest 281 passed. Golden untouched, no REBASELINES.

## I2 problems and warnings as tagged messages (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Slice I2 of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-04-bilingual-and-names.md. Every problem/warning emission site is `{"id": ..., "message": msg("<key>", **params)}`; the English text is the catalog's `en` template filled in and is byte-identical to the former f-string (frozen snapshot: 1163 cases identical; golden untouched; the existing English assertions pass untouched). Sites: `normalize.py` 16 (16 keys), `resolve.py` 51, `worn.py` 10, `equipment.py` 4 (incl. `fly_armor_warning`), `class_resources.py` 2, `app.py` the export-gate tuple (3 rows) and `unresolved-choice-*`. 88 keys: `catalog/problems.py` 52, `catalog/warnings.py` 36. Sites with the same wording share a key (`feat-duplicate-at-level` x5, `feat-hidden-by-filters` x4, `source-hidden` x3, `equipment-envelope-dropped` x2); the id of the dict is unchanged and is NOT the key.
- **Parameters.** Draft-supplied values and names are params (`{value!r}` keeps the Python repr for odd values). Counts use the plural filter in Bulgarian (`{n|избор|избора}`; English keeps `choice(s)`/`selection(s)` as before). Pieces that are English words inside a sentence are nested messages: the banned armor category of `fly-armor` (`armor-category-*`) and the slot words of `slot-unknown-item`/`slot-not-in-inventory` (`slot-kind-*`, `slot-armor/main/off`). The 2-school list of `subclass-spell-school-limit` is two params (`school_a`, `school_b`; the data has exactly two). `msg(key, /, **params)` now takes the key positional-only so a param may be called `key`.
- **Engine comparisons.** The `"final" not in reported["message"]` composition (WF) is now `reported["message"].key == "ability-out-of-range-clamped"` and a tagged rebuild `msg("ability-out-of-range-clamped-final", final=score, **old.params)` instead of `+=` (a `+=` would return a plain str and drop the tag); same text, same behaviour. No other problem/warning message is read by the engine (grep: only that one). `startswith("already taken")` on `disabledReason` is I4.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_i18n_problems.py` (118): every `msg` key used in code is in the catalog with en+bg; every problems/warnings catalog key is used in code; call-site kwargs equal the template fields; en/bg placeholder parity (`validate_catalog(require_bg=True)`); Cyrillic only in bg; each template renders in both languages; AST guard (no `"message": <str/f-string/concat>` dict, no `["message"] +=` in engine/app/export modules; the guard is itself tested); zoo tag-loss (every problem/warning/export-gate message is a `Msg`) and zoo Bulgarian coverage (each localizes to Cyrillic text different from the English), both in 8 chunks; the WF rebuild; `/api/resolve?lang=bg` and the export 400 body through the Flask client. `i18n_zoo.declared_families` now scans `msg("<key>")` dict values and `("<id>", msg(...))` tuples and builds each regex from the key's English template: 87 sites, 83 reached, the same 4 known gaps; the floor in `test_problem_and_warning_families_are_covered_except_the_known_gaps` is 87. `test_i18n_messages.py`: the two "empty catalog" tests were adapted (problems and warnings filled; pruned/disabled/labels still empty).
- **Zoo reach.** 75 of 88 keys appear as top-level messages in the zoo; 9 are nested pieces (armor category, slot words) reached as params; the 4 other unreached keys are the known gaps (`ability-invalid-number`, `level-invalid-number`, `feat-prerequisite-unmet`, `feat-prerequisite-unmet-pick`) and are covered by the per-template render test only.
- **Unchanged on purpose.** Pruned reasons, `disabledReason`, card labels/descriptions stay English (I3-I5); `{error}` transport texts stay English. A label that the engine builds as text (a card label inside `over-selected`, `unresolved-choice`, `feat-duplicate-at-source`) stays an English param until I5.
- **Gate.** Server `-n auto` 7705 passed + 2 skipped; `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1692 passed / 5765 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped; `npm run build` green, vitest 281 passed.

## I3 pruned reasons as tagged messages (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Slice I3 of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-04-bilingual-and-names.md. The `reason` of every `pruned` entry `{choiceId, reason}` is a `msg("pruned-<kind>", **params)`; the English text is the catalog `en` template filled in and is byte-identical to the former f-string (frozen snapshot: 1163 cases identical, golden untouched, no REBASELINES, existing English assertions untouched). 37 catalog keys in `server/i18n/catalog/pruned.py`; 45 `msg("pruned-...")` calls (AST count; the commit message says 38 emission sites, which counted dict sites, not branches) in `class_choices_2024.py` (11: languages x3, proficiencies x4, Bard known, invocation prerequisites, invocation count overflow, dependent invocation cards x2), `features.py` (2: ability bonus at max / capped), `subclass_spells_2024.py` (2), `worn.py` (1), `resolve.py` (29). Sites with the same wording share a key (`pruned-proficient` x2, `pruned-language-known` x2, `pruned-invocation-prereq` x2, `pruned-count-overflow` x3, `pruned-ability-at-max` x3, `pruned-invocation-dependent` x2).
- **List parameters.** New in the i18n core: a list/tuple parameter with a format spec is joined by the spec: `{names:, }` joins with ", ", `{items: or }` would join with " or " (without a spec a list still formats as its repr, so the I1 behaviour is unchanged). The engine used to join option/spell/feat names itself (`", ".join(...)`, a plain str that drops any tag); it now passes the list, so a name that is a nested `Msg` after I5 still localizes. `list_fields(template)` lists the join fields; `validate_catalog` samples them with a list.
- **Parameters.** Card labels (`'{label}'`), choice ids and names are params (plain English strings until I5). The "this class"/"this feat" wording of the mastery message is two keys (`pruned-mastery-unavailable-class/-feat`), the wizard spellbook message with and without its "Also N over the allowed ..." tail is two keys (the former `reason +=` is gone: it would have dropped the tag), the ability-bonus closed-option list is a list of `pruned-ability-closed-item` messages. `o.get("disabledReason", "not available")` inside two pruned messages is a param: a plain str until I4 turns `disabledReason` into messages (then it is a nested message and renders in the requested language; the fallback text is converted in I4).
- **Bulgarian.** Counts use the plural filter (`{n|...|...}`); English keeps `selection(s)`. Owned proficiency is "владее", Weapon Mastery follows the glossary. The `names` follow the sentence head ("вече се владее Arcana, History") so one wording fits one or many names.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_i18n_pruned.py` (56; the AST helpers `server_trees`/`msg_calls` live in `i18n_zoo.py`, which the root isolation test already allows): every `pruned-` key used in code is in the catalog with en+bg and every catalog key is used; placeholder parity; Cyrillic only in bg; call-site kwargs equal the template fields; every template renders in both languages with 1/2/5 samples (list fields as lists); AST guard (no `"reason": <str/f-string/concat>`, no plain `reason =`/`reason +=` in a function that builds pruned dicts; itself tested); zoo tag-loss and BG coverage in 8 chunks (all 2070 pruned reasons are `Msg` and localize to Cyrillic text different from the English); `/api/resolve?lang=bg` through the Flask client. Zoo reach: 12 of 37 keys occur in the zoo; the engine suite builds every key but `pruned-mastery-unavailable-feat` (measured with a recording plugin). `test_i18n_messages.py`: the catalog-wiring test now expects `pruned` filled.
- **Unchanged on purpose.** `disabledReason` (I4), card labels/descriptions and `unavailableReason` (I5), `{error}` texts.
- **Gate.** Server `-n auto` 7761 passed + 2 skipped; `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1738 passed / 5775 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped; `npm run build` green, vitest 281 passed.

## I4 disabledReason as tagged messages (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Slice I4 of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-04-bilingual-and-names.md. The `disabledReason` of every choice-card option is a `msg("disabled-...", **params)`; the English text is the catalog `en` template filled in and is byte-identical to the former string (frozen snapshot: 1163 cases identical, all 11972 zoo `disabledReason` leaves; golden untouched, no REBASELINES, existing English assertions untouched). 49 keys in `server/i18n/catalog/disabled.py` (36 `disabled-*` whole reasons, 13 `prereq-*` pieces of a feat-prerequisite reason); 69 `msg(...)` calls: `class_choices_2024.py` 20, `subclass_spells_2024.py` 3, `resolve.py` 46 (30 reasons, 16 prerequisite parts). All the "reason" helpers return a `Msg`: `_taken_reason` (`disabled-already-taken` / `disabled-already-taken-from`), `_target_reason`, `_mastery_filter_reason`, `_invocation_unmet` and `_invocation_unmet_2024`, `_feat_prerequisite_unmet_reason`; the module constant `_LESSONS_REASON` is a `Msg` too.
- **Prerequisite reason.** The former `reason = "prerequisite not met"; reason += ": requires " + "; ".join(parts)` (which would have dropped the tag) is two messages: `disabled-prereq-unmet`, and `disabled-prereq-unmet-requires` with `parts` = a list of nested `prereq-*` messages, joined by `{parts:; }`. The " or " joins (ability alternatives, race entries, feat names, the two schools of the school limit) are `{items: or }` / `{schools: or }`. The armor proficiency part nests the existing `armor-category-light/medium/heavy` messages (`_armor_category_msg`); a race entry's `displayEntry` text from the data stays a plain English item.
- **`startswith("already taken")`.** `resolve.py` (`_resolve_species_selected_feats`) still decides between `feat-duplicate-at-source` and `feat-prerequisite-unmet-pick` with `str(option.get("disabledReason")).startswith("already taken")`. A `Msg` is a `str` whose text is the English wording, and the engine only ever sees the English text (localization runs on the finished resolution), so the comparison is unchanged; a comment says not to rebuild a reason with `+`/f-strings. Regression tests (tests/test_i18n_disabled.py): `_taken_reason` yields `Msg`s whose text starts with "already taken" (both shapes, and a repeatable/none case), a duplicate species feat pick is still a `feat-duplicate-at-source` problem (not a prerequisite problem) and is the same under `?lang=bg`, a prerequisite reason is not mistaken for a taken one.
- **Pruned reasons that quote a disabledReason.** `pruned-asi-option-closed` (`why`) and `pruned-ability-closed-item` (`why`) now receive the option's `Msg` and nest it (the fallback "not available" is `disabled-not-available`).
- **Bulgarian.** A reason is a phrase under the option: "вече се знае", "вече се владее", "вече взето ({where})", "изисква ниво N на warlock"; a count of spells uses the plural filter (`disabled-school-limit`). Cantrip = "трик", proficiency = "владеене" (glossary vocabulary); feature/feat/spell names and ability codes stay English.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_i18n_disabled.py` (74): used-in-code vs catalog both ways, parity, Cyrillic only in bg, call-site kwargs, per-template render (list fields as lists), nested prerequisite parts, a pruned reason nesting a disabled reason, AST guard (plain `disabledReason` dict/keyword/subscript, `reason`/`*_REASON` plain or `+=` in a function that sets a disabledReason, plain `return` in the reason helpers, plain `parts.append`; itself tested), zoo tag loss + BG coverage in 8 chunks (all 11972 leaves are `Msg` and localize to Cyrillic text different from the English), the `startswith` regression tests, and `/api/resolve?lang=bg` (taken reasons, nested prerequisite parts, nothing else changes). Zoo reach: 28 of 49 keys occur in the zoo; the whole engine suite builds every key but `prereq-feat-category` and `prereq-martial` (measured with a recording plugin); those two are covered by the render test only. `test_i18n_problems.py` / `test_i18n_pruned.py`: the "rest of the resolution is identical in both languages" assertion now ignores the options' `disabledReason` (localized).
- **Unchanged on purpose.** Card labels/descriptions and `unavailableReason` (I5); `{error}` texts. Card labels inside reasons (`chosen as {label}`, pruned `'{label}'`) are params (plain English until I5).
- **Gate.** Server `-n auto` 7835 passed + 2 skipped; `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1798 passed / 5789 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped; `npm run build` green, vitest 281 passed.


## I5 card labels, descriptions and unavailableReason as tagged messages (2026-11-04)

- **What.** Slice I5 of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-04-bilingual-and-names.md. The `label` and `description` of every choice card the engine WRITES, and a card's `unavailableReason`, are `msg(...)` tagged messages; the English text is the catalog `en` template filled in and is byte-identical to the former string (frozen snapshot: 1163 cases identical, including every card label/description leaf; golden untouched, no REBASELINES, existing English assertions untouched except the "rest of the resolution is identical" comparisons, see Tests). 107 keys in `server/i18n/catalog/labels.py` (37 `label-*`, 57 `desc-*`, 10 `noun-*` pieces of a "Choose N <noun>." sentence, 3 `unavailable-*`); 148 `msg(...)` calls: `resolve.py` 112 (label/desc keys only: lineage, size, bonus language, ASI, species/background/feat cards, spell cards, Weapon Mastery, Mystic Arcanum, equipment), `class_choices_2024.py` 23, `reference_data.py` 9 (Spell Mastery, Student of War, Circle Spells labels), `choices.py` 2 (nested equipment picks), `subclass_spells_2024.py` 1, `feat_effects_data.py` 1 (the Giant Strike description; the table imports `msg`).
- **Composition.** `label-prefixed` is the "{head}: {tail}" label of a feat / pact card (the feat or feature name is a param, the tail a nested message or a rules term); `label-feature-level` is "{name} (level N)". Variable-built descriptions are nested pieces, not f-strings: the spell sentence of `_spell_filter_description` is `desc-choose-noun` over `noun-cantrip` / `noun-spell-levels` / `noun-spell` wrapped by `noun-school`, `noun-from-list`, `noun-ritual` (so Bulgarian can order the words its own way); Spells Known / Prepared Spells / Spellbook use `noun-spell`, `noun-spell-upto`; "data text + engine suffix" descriptions (granted automatically, Magical Secrets suffix, additional-secrets, savant free spells, equipment "Note:") are `desc-*` keys with the data text as the `text` param. The `.strip()` of an empty base is two keys (`desc-granted-automatically[-after]`, `desc-equipment-note[-only]`). Counts use the plural filter.
- **Engine label comparison.** `resolve.py` compared `tpl["label"] == "Skill Proficiencies"` twice (relabel a class's non-skill proficiency envelope as Tool Proficiencies). It is now `_label_is(tpl["label"], "label-skill-proficiencies")`, a comparison by Msg key (`getattr(label, "key", None)`), and the relabel sets `msg("label-tool-proficiencies")`; the first label comes from `_provisional_choice_label`. Audit of every other label read in the engine: `labels = {o["id"]: o.get("label", o["id"])}` and `unmet[o["id"]] = o["label"]` read OPTION labels (data); `label=c["label"]` / `tpl["label"]` are params of pruned / problem messages (now nested Msg, so they localize); `sheet_features` reads option labels and the Divine / Primal Order card label (a data feature name); `startswith` / `split(" + ")` act on option labels; no other card label is compared with text. Two test guards keep it so: an AST guard (`comparisons_with_label_text`: no `==` / `!=` of a `label` subscript or `.get("label")` with a string) and the regression tests for `_label_is` and the multiclass / class relabel.
- **Nested card labels.** The card labels quoted inside problems, pruned reasons and the export gate (`over-selected`, `unresolved-choice`, `feat-duplicate-at-source`, every `pruned-*` `{label}`) were plain English params; they now carry the card's own `Msg`, so they render in the requested language through the existing nesting (no call site changed). `disabled-chosen-as` (`label=spec["label"]`) quotes a data label and stays English.
- **Stays English on purpose.** (1) Labels and descriptions the DATA supplies: class feature / trait names (Divine Order, Fighting Style, Metamagic, Expertise, Thieves' Cant, Extra Language, ...), 5etools feature descriptions, an envelope's `desc` (starting-equipment "(a) ... or (b) ...", "Choose two skills from ..."); a test proves each plain label/description in the zoo is a string the loaded catalog or the reference tables contain. (2) Rules terms with no translation in any slice, kept as the English tail of `label-prefixed`: Expertise, Fighting Style, Maneuvers, Metamagic, Eldritch Invocation ("Skill Expert: Expertise" is a Msg whose Bulgarian text equals the English one). (3) OPTION labels (spell/feature/item names; skill and ability names; "(N SP)", "(Magical Secrets)", "(Spells of the Mark)", "Nx Item", size and damage-type words, feat groups): out of scope, they are not card labels. (4) E4 rule paraphrases (notes, resource text). (5) `instance_label` ("Agonizing Blast (2)") is language-neutral and stays an f-string. (6) The `{error}` transport texts.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_i18n_labels.py` (133): used-in-code vs catalog both ways (a key kept in a table counts through its string constant), parity, Cyrillic only in bg, call-site kwargs, per-template render (list fields as lists), English spot checks of the former strings, nested rendering, AST guard (card dicts with `options`, `tpl["label"|"description"] =`/`+=`, builder arguments, the local `card(...)` helper, `unavailableReason`; itself tested; data tables checked for `unavailableReason` only), the label-comparison guard and `_label_is` / relabel regressions, zoo tag loss and BG coverage in 8 chunks (every card label, description and unavailableReason is a `Msg` that localizes to Cyrillic text different from the English, or a plain string present in the data), the `unavailableReason` shapes, and `/api/resolve?lang=bg` through the Flask client (fixed cards, spell cards, Spell Mastery, problems and the export gate quoting a card label). The "rest identical in both languages" assertions of I2-I4 now compare through `i18n_zoo.without_localized` (drops card label / description / unavailableReason and each `disabledReason`). `i18n_zoo.server_trees(with_catalog=True)` adds `catalog/choices.py`, `reference_data.py` and `feat_effects_data.py` (the older guards keep scanning the engine files only). Reach: the zoo shows 81 of the 107 keys as card texts; the whole engine suite builds all 107 (measured with a recording plugin); the rest are covered by the render test.
- **One English fix.** `Choose {n} ability{s} to gain saving throw proficiency in.` produced "abilitys" for n > 1; the plural filter now says "abilities" (n = 1 unchanged; the data has only n = 1, so no snapshot or golden leaf changes).
- **Frontend.** No UI code matches on engine label or description text (grep: `choice.label` is only displayed, the option filter searches option labels, `sheetSections` compares source labels = names). `types.ts` is unchanged.
- **Gate.** Server `-n auto` 7967 passed + 3 skipped (clean-clone smoke, 2024 PHB markdown, waitress: opt-in); `DND_OFFICIAL_DATA=none` 1917 passed / 5803 skipped; root 363 passed / 8 skipped; `npm run build` green, vitest 281 passed.
